===== tests/Dig.Tests/AuthoritativeXyzSaveTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Xunit;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Tests
    13	{
    14	
    15	public sealed class AuthoritativeXyzSaveTests
    16	{
    17	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("xyz-save.air");
    18	    private static readonly ItemId Plank = new ItemId("xyz-save.plank");
    19	    private static readonly EntityId AgentId = EntityId.Parse(
    20	        "a1000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	    private static readonly EntityId BuildingId = EntityId.Parse(
    22	        "b1000000000000000000000000000001");
    23	
    24	    [Fact]
    25	    public void Agent_position_round_trip_preserves_exact_depth()
    26	    {
    27	        AgentState agent = new AgentState(
    28	            AgentId,
    29	            "Deep Dwarf",
    30	            AgentTestFactory.CreateNeeds(8_000, 8_000, 8_000, 10_000),
    31	            AgentTestFactory.CreateWorkSchedule(),
    32	            skills: null,
    33	            traits: null,
    34	            initialPosition: new CellId(3, 4, 2));
    35	        SaveGameDocument document = CreateBuilder().Build(CreateContext(
    36	            new BuildingsState(),
    37	            new[] { agent }));
    38	
    39	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = CreateLoader().Load(
    40	            document,
    41	            CreateMaterials(),
    42	            CreateItems());
    43	
    44	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess);
    45	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(3, 4, 2), loaded.Value.AgentPositions[AgentId]);
    46	        AgentPositionSaveData saved = Assert.Single(document.AgentPositions.Agents);
    47	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.Z);
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    [Fact]
    51	    public void Building_origin_footprint_and_work_position_round_trip_on_depth_two()
    52	    {
    53	        BuildingDefinition definition = CreateBuildingDefinition();
    54	        CellId origin = new CellId(4, 4, 2);
    55	        CellId workPosition = new CellId(4, 3, 2);
    56	        BuildingsState buildings = new BuildingsState();
    57	        Assert.True(buildings.Place(
    58	            BuildingId,
    59	            definition,
    60	            origin,
    61	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    62	            BuildingPlacementResult.Success(
    63	                definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, BuildingOrientation.North),
    64	                workPosition),
    65	            tick: 1).IsSuccess);
    66	        SaveGameDocument document = CreateBuilder().Build(CreateContext(
    67	            buildings,
    68	            Array.Empty<AgentState>()));
    69	
    70	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = CreateLoader().Load(
    71	            document,
    72	            CreateMaterials(),
    73	            CreateItems(),
    74	            new BuildingCatalog(new[] { definition }));
    75	
    76	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess);
    77	        BuildingSnapshot restored = Assert.Single(loaded.Value.Buildings.GetAll());
    78	        Assert.Equal(origin, restored.Origin);
    79	        Assert.Equal(workPosition, restored.WorkPosition);
    80	        Assert.All(restored.Footprint, cell => Assert.Equal(2, cell.Z));
    81	        BuildingSaveData saved = Assert.Single(document.Buildings.Buildings);
    82	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.OriginZ);
    83	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.WorkPositionZ);
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    private static SaveGameContext CreateContext(
    87	        BuildingsState buildings,
    88	        AgentState[] agents)
    89	    {
    90	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
    91	            new WorldSize(8, 8),
    92	            chunkSize: 4,
    93	            CreateMaterials(),
    94	            Air,
    95	            explored: true).Value;
    96	        return new SaveGameContext(
    97	            new SaveMetadataData
    98	            {
    99	                SlotId = "xyz-save",
   100	                DisplayName = "XYZ save",
   101	                SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-22T10:00:00Z",
   102	                SimulationTick = 2,
   103	                WorldSeed = 88,
   104	                GeneratorVersion = 1,
   105	            },
   106	            world,
   107	            new InventoryState(CreateItems()),
   108	            new JobSystem(),
   109	            buildings,
   110	            agents);
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    private static BuildingDefinition CreateBuildingDefinition()
   114	    {
   115	        return new BuildingDefinition(
   116	            new BuildingDefinitionId("xyz-save.workshop"),
   117	            "XYZ Workshop",
   118	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0), new CellOffset(1, 0) },
   119	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, -1) },
   120	            new[] { new BuildingMaterialRequirement(Plank, 1) },
   121	            requiredWork: 1,
   122	            maximumDurability: 100);
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    private static SaveGameBuilder CreateBuilder()
   126	    {
   127	        return new SaveGameBuilder(new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   128	            {
   129	                new DigJobDefinitionSaveCodec(),
   130	            }));
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    private static SaveGameLoader CreateLoader()
   134	    {
   135	        return new SaveGameLoader(
   136	            new SaveMigrationPipeline(Array.Empty<ISaveMigration>()),
   137	            new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   138	            {
   139	                new DigJobDefinitionSaveCodec(),
   140	            }));
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   144	    {
   145	        return new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   146	        {
   147	            new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   148	        });
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    private static ItemCatalog CreateItems()
   152	    {
   153	        return new ItemCatalog(new[]
   154	        {
   155	            new ItemDefinition(Plank, "Plank", maximumStackSize: 100, isTool: false),
   156	        });
   157	    }
   158	}
   159	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelAttackApplicationTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Application.WorldObjects;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     8	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BarrelAttackApplicationTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    17	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    18	    private static readonly ItemId Stone = new ItemId("material.stone");
    19	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("material.ore");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId BarrelId = Id("c1000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	    private static readonly EntityId FirstJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	    private static readonly EntityId SecondJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000002");
    23	    private static readonly EntityId FirstWorkerId = Id("c3000000000000000000000000000001");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId SecondWorkerId = Id("c3000000000000000000000000000002");
    25	    private static readonly CellId Target = new CellId(5, 6, 0);
    26	
    27	    [Fact]
    28	    public void One_hit_destroys_barrel_and_creates_one_world_unit_without_progression_side_effects()
    29	    {
    30	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
    31	        EntityId output = Id("c4000000000000000000000000000001");
    32	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    33	            FirstJobId,
    34	            BarrelId,
    35	            FirstWorkerId,
    36	            new CellId(4, 6, 0),
    37	            priority: 900,
    38	            tick: 1)).IsSuccess);
    39	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
    40	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
    41	
    42	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> completed = harness.Complete.Handle(
    43	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(FirstJobId, output, tick: 4));
    44	
    45	        Assert.True(completed.IsSuccess, completed.Error?.ToString());
    46	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId)!.Status);
    47	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
    48	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Destroyed, harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    49	        ItemStackSnapshot unit = harness.Inventory.GetStack(output)!;
    50	        Assert.Equal(Stone, unit.ItemId);
    51	        Assert.Equal(1, unit.Quantity);
    52	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(Target), unit.Location);
    53	        Assert.Single(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    [Fact]
    57	    public void Concurrent_attacks_are_allowed_but_only_first_commit_creates_contents()
    58	    {
    59	        CellId firstWorkPosition = new CellId(4, 6, 0);
    60	        CellId secondWorkPosition = new CellId(6, 6, 0);
    61	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Ore);
    62	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    63	            FirstJobId,
    64	            BarrelId,
    65	            FirstWorkerId,
    66	            firstWorkPosition,
    67	            900,
    68	            1)).IsSuccess);
    69	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    70	            SecondJobId,
    71	            BarrelId,
    72	            SecondWorkerId,
    73	            secondWorkPosition,
    74	            900,
    75	            1)).IsSuccess);
    76	
    77	        ReservationSnapshot[] reservations = harness.Jobs.GetReservations().ToArray();
    78	        Assert.Equal(3, reservations.Count(value => value.JobId == FirstJobId));
    79	        Assert.Equal(3, reservations.Count(value => value.JobId == SecondJobId));
    80	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    81	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    82	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForJob(FirstJobId));
    83	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    84	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    85	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(FirstWorkerId));
    86	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    87	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    88	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForPosition(firstWorkPosition));
    89	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    90	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    91	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForJob(SecondJobId));
    92	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    93	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    94	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(SecondWorkerId));
    95	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    96	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    97	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForPosition(secondWorkPosition));
    98	        Assert.DoesNotContain(reservations, value =>
    99	            value.Key == ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(BarrelId));
   100	
   101	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
   102	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(SecondJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
   103	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
   104	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(SecondJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
   105	
   106	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> first = harness.Complete.Handle(
   107	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(
   108	                FirstJobId,
   109	                Id("c4000000000000000000000000000001"),
   110	                4));
   111	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> second = harness.Complete.Handle(
   112	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(
   113	                SecondJobId,
   114	                Id("c4000000000000000000000000000002"),
   115	                4));
   116	
   117	        Assert.True(first.IsSuccess, first.Error?.ToString());
   118	        Assert.Equal(BarrelApplicationErrors.GenerationConflict, second.Error);
   119	        Assert.Single(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   120	        Assert.Equal(Ore, harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks.Single().ItemId);
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    [Fact]
   124	    public void Cancel_before_hit_preserves_supported_barrel_and_contents()
   125	    {
   126	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
   127	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
   128	            FirstJobId,
   129	            BarrelId,
   130	            FirstWorkerId,
   131	            new CellId(4, 6, 0),
   132	            900,
   133	            1)).IsSuccess);
   134	
   135	        Assert.True(harness.Cancel.Handle(new CancelBarrelAttackCommand(
   136	            FirstJobId,
   137	            "player_cancelled",
   138	            tick: 2)).IsSuccess);
   139	
   140	        BarrelSnapshot barrel = harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
   141	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, barrel.Lifecycle);
   142	        Assert.Equal(Stone, barrel.ContentsItemId);
   143	        Assert.False(barrel.ContentsMaterialized);
   144	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    [Fact]
   148	    public void Reserved_worker_is_rejected_without_exception_or_barrel_mutation()
   149	    {
   150	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
   151	        EntityId existingJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000099");
   152	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Add(new DigJobDefinition(
   153	            existingJobId,
   154	            new DigJobTarget(new CellId(1, 1, 0)),
   155	            priority: 500,
   156	            createdTick: 0,
   157	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default)).IsSuccess);
   158	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.MakeAvailable(existingJobId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   159	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Claim(existingJobId, FirstWorkerId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   160	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Start(existingJobId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   161	
   162	        Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult> started = harness.Start.Handle(
   163	            new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
   164	                FirstJobId,
   165	                BarrelId,
   166	                FirstWorkerId,
   167	                new CellId(4, 6, 0),
   168	                priority: 900,
   169	                tick: 1));
   170	
   171	        Assert.True(started.IsFailure);
   172	        Assert.Equal(JobErrors.AgentUnavailable, started.Error);
   173	        Assert.Null(harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId));
   174	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, harness.Jobs.Get(existingJobId)!.Status);
   175	        Assert.All(harness.Jobs.GetReservations(), reservation =>
   176	            Assert.Equal(existingJobId, reservation.JobId));
   177	        BarrelSnapshot barrel = harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
   178	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, barrel.Lifecycle);
   179	        Assert.Equal(Stone, barrel.ContentsItemId);
   180	        Assert.False(barrel.ContentsMaterialized);
   181	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    private static Harness CreateHarness(ItemId contents)
   185	    {
   186	        BarrelState barrels = new BarrelState(new BarrelCatalog(new[]
   187	        {
   188	            new BarrelDefinition(DefinitionId, new[] { Stone, Ore }),
   189	        }));
   190	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, Target, contents, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   191	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
   192	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[]
   193	        {
   194	            new ItemDefinition(Stone, "Stone", 100, isTool: false),
   195	            new ItemDefinition(Ore, "Ore", 100, isTool: false),
   196	        }));
   197	        InMemoryExecutionJournal events = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
   198	        InMemoryBarrelRepository barrelRepository = new InMemoryBarrelRepository(barrels);
   199	        InMemoryJobRepository jobRepository = new InMemoryJobRepository(jobs);
   200	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(inventory);
   201	        return new Harness(
   202	            barrels,
   203	            jobs,
   204	            inventory,
   205	            new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler(barrelRepository, jobRepository, events),
   206	            new ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler(jobRepository, events),
   207	            new CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler(jobRepository, events),
   208	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler(
   209	                barrelRepository,
   210	                jobRepository,
   211	                inventoryRepository,
   212	                events),
   213	            new CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler(jobRepository, events));
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    private static EntityId Id(string value) => EntityId.Parse(value);
   217	
   218	    private sealed class Harness
   219	    {
   220	        public Harness(
   221	            BarrelState barrels,
   222	            JobSystem jobs,
   223	            InventoryState inventory,
   224	            StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler start,
   225	            ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler arrive,
   226	            CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler hit,
   227	            CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler complete,
   228	            CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler cancel)
   229	        {
   230	            Barrels = barrels;
   231	            Jobs = jobs;
   232	            Inventory = inventory;
   233	            Start = start;
   234	            Arrive = arrive;
   235	            Hit = hit;
   236	            Complete = complete;
   237	            Cancel = cancel;
   238	        }
   239	
   240	        public BarrelState Barrels { get; }
   241	        public JobSystem Jobs { get; }
   242	        public InventoryState Inventory { get; }
   243	        public StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler Start { get; }
   244	        public ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler Arrive { get; }
   245	        public CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler Hit { get; }
   246	        public CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler Complete { get; }
   247	        public CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler Cancel { get; }
   248	    }
   249	}
   250

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelSaveRoundTripTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
    10	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    11	using Xunit;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Tests
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class BarrelSaveRoundTripTests
    17	{
    18	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("terrain.air");
    19	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    20	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    21	    private static readonly ItemId Stone = new ItemId("material.stone");
    22	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("ore.iron");
    23	    private static readonly EntityId BarrelId = Id("e1000000000000000000000000000001");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId JobId = Id("e2000000000000000000000000000001");
    25	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerId = Id("e3000000000000000000000000000001");
    26	    private static readonly CellId BarrelCell = new CellId(3, 3, 1);
    27	    private static readonly CellId WorkCell = new CellId(2, 3, 1);
    28	
    29	    [Fact]
    30	    public void Active_attack_round_trip_preserves_contents_version_and_job()
    31	    {
    32	        MaterialCatalog materials = Materials();
    33	        ItemCatalog items = Items();
    34	        BarrelCatalog barrelCatalog = Barrels();
    35	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
    36	            new WorldSize(8, 8), 4, materials, Air, explored: true).Value;
    37	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    38	        BarrelState barrels = new BarrelState(barrelCatalog);
    39	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(
    40	            BarrelId,
    41	            DefinitionId,
    42	            BarrelCell,
    43	            Ore,
    44	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    45	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
    46	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition definition = new BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
    47	            JobId,
    48	            BarrelId,
    49	            BarrelCell,
    50	            WorkCell,
    51	            barrelVersion: 0,
    52	            contentsGeneration: 0,
    53	            priority: 900,
    54	            createdTick: 1,
    55	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    56	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
    57	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(JobId, 1).IsSuccess);
    58	        Assert.True(jobs.Claim(JobId, WorkerId, 1).IsSuccess);
    59	        Assert.True(jobs.Start(JobId, 1).IsSuccess);
    60	
    61	        SaveGameDocument document = Builder().Build(new SaveGameContext(
    62	            Metadata(),
    63	            world,
    64	            inventory,
    65	            jobs,
    66	            new BuildingsState(),
    67	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
    68	            barrels: barrels));
    69	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec json = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    70	        SaveGameDocument decoded = json.Deserialize(json.Serialize(document));
    71	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = Loader().Load(
    72	            decoded,
    73	            materials,
    74	            items,
    75	            buildingCatalog: null,
    76	            terrainDepositCatalog: null,
    77	            mushroomCatalog: null,
    78	            barrelCatalog: barrelCatalog);
    79	
    80	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    81	        BarrelSnapshot restored = loaded.Value.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
    82	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, restored.Lifecycle);
    83	        Assert.Equal(Ore, restored.ContentsItemId);
    84	        Assert.False(restored.ContentsMaterialized);
    85	        Assert.Equal(0, restored.Version);
    86	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, loaded.Value.Jobs.Get(JobId)!.Status);
    87	        Assert.Contains(
    88	            loaded.Value.Jobs.GetReservations(),
    89	            value => value.Key == ReservationKey.ForPosition(WorkCell));
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    [Fact]
    93	    public void Version_six_migration_adds_empty_barrel_section()
    94	    {
    95	        SaveGameDocument document = new SaveGameDocument
    96	        {
    97	            FormatVersion = 6,
    98	            Barrels = null!,
    99	        };
   100	        SaveMigrationPipeline pipeline = new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
   101	        {
   102	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   103	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   104	            new SaveVersionEightAgentRuntimeMigration(),
   105	            new SaveVersionNineCombatSpatialMigration(),
   106	        });
   107	
   108	        Result<SaveMigrationReport> migrated = pipeline.Apply(document);
   109	
   110	        Assert.True(migrated.IsSuccess, migrated.Error?.ToString());
   111	        Assert.Equal(SaveFormat.CurrentVersion, document.FormatVersion);
   112	        Assert.NotNull(document.Barrels);
   113	        Assert.Empty(document.Barrels.Barrels);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    private static SaveGameBuilder Builder() => new SaveGameBuilder(
   117	        new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   118	        {
   119	            new BarrelAttackJobSaveCodec(),
   120	        }));
   121	
   122	    private static SaveGameLoader Loader() => new SaveGameLoader(
   123	        new SaveMigrationPipeline(Array.Empty<ISaveMigration>()),
   124	        new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   125	        {
   126	            new BarrelAttackJobSaveCodec(),
   127	        }));
   128	
   129	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata() => new SaveMetadataData
   130	    {
   131	        SlotId = "barrel-active",
   132	        DisplayName = "Barrel checkpoint",
   133	        SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-28T10:00:00Z",
   134	        SimulationTick = 2,
   135	        WorldSeed = 42,
   136	        GeneratorVersion = 1,
   137	    };
   138	
   139	    private static MaterialCatalog Materials() => new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   140	    {
   141	        new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   142	    });
   143	
   144	    private static ItemCatalog Items() => new ItemCatalog(new[]
   145	    {
   146	        new ItemDefinition(Stone, "Stone", 100, isTool: false),
   147	        new ItemDefinition(Ore, "Iron ore", 100, isTool: false),
   148	    });
   149	
   150	    private static BarrelCatalog Barrels() => new BarrelCatalog(new[]
   151	    {
   152	        new BarrelDefinition(DefinitionId, new[] { Stone, Ore }),
   153	    });
   154	
   155	    private static EntityId Id(string value) => EntityId.Parse(value);
   156	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelStateTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     7	using Xunit;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Tests
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class BarrelStateTests
    13	{
    14	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    15	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    16	    private static readonly ItemId Stone = new ItemId("material.stone");
    17	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("material.ore");
    18	    private static readonly EntityId BarrelId =
    19	        EntityId.Parse("b1000000000000000000000000000001");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId JobOne =
    21	        EntityId.Parse("b2000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	    private static readonly EntityId JobTwo =
    23	        EntityId.Parse("b2000000000000000000000000000002");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerOne =
    25	        EntityId.Parse("b3000000000000000000000000000001");
    26	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerTwo =
    27	        EntityId.Parse("b3000000000000000000000000000002");
    28	
    29	    [Fact]
    30	    public void First_hit_destroys_barrel_and_materializes_saved_contents_once()
    31	    {
    32	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
    33	        CellId cell = new CellId(4, 5, 1);
    34	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, cell, Stone, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    35	        BarrelSnapshot before = barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
    36	
    37	        Result<BarrelDestructionCommit> first = barrels.Destroy(
    38	            BarrelId,
    39	            before.Version,
    40	            JobOne,
    41	            WorkerOne,
    42	            tick: 1);
    43	        Result<BarrelDestructionCommit> duplicate = barrels.Destroy(
    44	            BarrelId,
    45	            before.Version,
    46	            JobTwo,
    47	            WorkerTwo,
    48	            tick: 1);
    49	
    50	        Assert.True(first.IsSuccess, first.Error?.ToString());
    51	        Assert.Equal(Stone, first.Value.ContentsItemId);
    52	        Assert.Equal(cell, first.Value.Cell);
    53	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Destroyed, barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    54	        Assert.True(barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.ContentsMaterialized);
    55	        Assert.Equal(BarrelErrors.NotAttackable, duplicate.Error);
    56	        Assert.Empty(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells());
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    [Fact]
    60	    public void Concurrent_jobs_do_not_reserve_the_barrel_target()
    61	    {
    62	        CellId target = new CellId(5, 5, 0);
    63	        CellId firstWork = new CellId(4, 5, 0);
    64	        CellId secondWork = new CellId(6, 5, 0);
    65	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition first = CreateJob(JobOne, firstWork);
    66	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition second = CreateJob(JobTwo, secondWork);
    67	
    68	        ReservationKey[] firstReservations = first.CreateReservationKeys().ToArray();
    69	        ReservationKey[] secondReservations = second.CreateReservationKeys().ToArray();
    70	
    71	        Assert.Single(firstReservations);
    72	        Assert.Single(secondReservations);
    73	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForPosition(firstWork), firstReservations);
    74	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForPosition(secondWork), secondReservations);
    75	        Assert.DoesNotContain(firstReservations, value => value == ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(BarrelId));
    76	        Assert.Equal(new[]
    77	        {
    78	            JobStageKind.TravelToTarget,
    79	            JobStageKind.PerformWork,
    80	            JobStageKind.Finalize,
    81	        }, first.Stages);
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    [Fact]
    85	    public void Support_loss_relocates_barrel_without_damage_or_contents_output()
    86	    {
    87	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
    88	        CellId source = new CellId(3, 2, 0);
    89	        CellId landing = new CellId(3, 8, 0);
    90	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, source, Ore, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    91	
    92	        Assert.True(barrels.BeginFall(BarrelId, landing, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
    93	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Falling, barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    94	        Assert.True(barrels.Land(BarrelId, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
    95	
    96	        BarrelSnapshot landed = barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
    97	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, landed.Lifecycle);
    98	        Assert.Equal(landing, landed.Cell);
    99	        Assert.Equal(Ore, landed.ContentsItemId);
   100	        Assert.False(landed.ContentsMaterialized);
   101	        Assert.Equal(landing, Assert.Single(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells()));
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    [Fact]
   105	    public void Supported_barrel_blocks_buildings_but_job_has_no_movement_occupancy()
   106	    {
   107	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
   108	        CellId cell = new CellId(8, 4, 2);
   109	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, cell, Stone, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   110	
   111	        Assert.Equal(cell, Assert.Single(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells()));
   112	        Assert.True(barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.IsAttackable);
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    private static BarrelState CreateState()
   116	    {
   117	        return new BarrelState(new BarrelCatalog(new[]
   118	        {
   119	            new BarrelDefinition(DefinitionId, new[] { Stone, Ore }),
   120	        }));
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    private static BarrelAttackJobDefinition CreateJob(EntityId jobId, CellId workPosition)
   124	    {
   125	        return new BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
   126	            jobId,
   127	            BarrelId,
   128	            new CellId(5, 5, 0),
   129	            workPosition,
   130	            barrelVersion: 0,
   131	            contentsGeneration: 0,
   132	            priority: 900,
   133	            createdTick: 0,
   134	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   135	    }
   136	}
   137	
   138	}

   118	    private static int Count(string source, string value)
   119	    {
   120	        int count = 0;
   121	        int index = 0;
   122	        while ((index = source.IndexOf(value, index, StringComparison.Ordinal)) >= 0)
   123	        {
   124	            count++;
   125	            index += value.Length;
   126	        }
   127	
   128	        return count;
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    private static string Read(string relativePath)
   132	    {
   133	        DirectoryInfo? directory = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   134	        while (directory != null && !File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   135	        {
   136	            directory = directory.Parent;
   137	        }
   138	
   139	        Assert.NotNull(directory);
   140	        return File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(directory!.FullName, relativePath));
   141	    }
   142	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionPolicyTests.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     2	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionPolicyTests
    15	{
    16	    [Fact]
    17	    public void World_box_requires_worker_at_source_before_start()
    18	    {
    19	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateAssigned();
    20	        BuildingSnapshot building = Building(harness);
    21	        ItemStackSnapshot box = Box(harness);
    22	
    23	        Assert.Equal(
    24	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.None,
    25	            Evaluate(harness, building, box, new CellId(0, 0)));
    26	        Assert.Equal(
    27	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.StartJob,
    28	            Evaluate(harness, building, box, harness.SourceCell));
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    [Fact]
    32	    public void Box_already_carried_by_worker_skips_external_pickup()
    33	    {
    34	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateAssigned(carriedByResident: true);
    35	        CellId arbitraryWorkerCell = new CellId(0, 0);
    36	
    37	        Assert.Equal(
    38	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.StartJob,
    39	            Evaluate(harness, Building(harness), Box(harness), arbitraryWorkerCell));
    40	        Assert.True(Advance(harness, tick: 450).IsSuccess);
    41	        Assert.Equal(
    42	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage,
    43	            Evaluate(harness, Building(harness), Box(harness), arbitraryWorkerCell));
    44	        Assert.Equal(1, Box(harness).ReservedQuantity);
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    [Fact]
    48	    public void Policy_drives_box_to_completed_building_without_duplication()
    49	    {
    50	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateAssigned();
    51	        long tick = 500;
    52	        for (int step = 0; step < 20 && !harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.IsTerminal; step++)
    53	        {
    54	            JobSnapshot job = harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!;
    55	            BuildingSnapshot building = Building(harness);
    56	            ItemStackSnapshot? box = harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId);
    57	            CellId workerCell = ResolveWorkerCell(job, building, box);
    58	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind action =
    59	                Evaluate(harness, building, box, workerCell);
    60	            Assert.NotEqual(BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.None, action);
    61	            Execute(harness, action, workerCell, tick++);
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Completed, Building(harness).Status);
    65	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed, Building(harness).BoxPlan!.CommitState);
    66	        Assert.Null(harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId));
    67	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(harness.BoxItemId));
    68	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.Status);
    69	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
    70	        Assert.Equal(
    71	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.None,
    72	            Evaluate(harness, Building(harness), null, Building(harness).WorkPosition));
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    [Theory]
    76	    [InlineData(
    77	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.StartJob,
    78	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.ContinueCurrentTick)]
    79	    [InlineData(
    80	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage,
    81	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.ContinueCurrentTick)]
    82	    [InlineData(
    83	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.CommitBoxToSite,
    84	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.StopCurrentTick)]
    85	    [InlineData(
    86	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AddWork,
    87	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.StopCurrentTick)]
    88	    [InlineData(
    89	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.CompleteAssembly,
    90	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.Completed)]
    91	    public void Tick_boundary_policy_preserves_observable_assembly_states(
    92	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind step,
    93	        BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition expected)
    94	    {
    95	        Assert.Equal(expected, BuildingBoxAssemblyTickBoundaryPolicy.AfterSuccessfulStep(step));
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    [Fact]
    99	    public void Carried_box_at_site_exposes_five_fast_states_and_finalizes_on_next_tick()
   100	    {
   101	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateAssigned(carriedByResident: true);
   102	        CellId workPosition = Building(harness).WorkPosition;
   103	        long tick = 700;
   104	
   105	        ExecuteDemoTick(harness, workPosition, tick++);
   106	        AssertAssemblyState(harness, BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild, completedWork: 0);
   107	        ItemStackSnapshot siteBox = Box(harness);
   108	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InBuilding(harness.BuildingId), siteBox.Location);
   109	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.DepositItem, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.Stage);
   110	
   111	        ExecuteDemoTick(harness, workPosition, tick++);
   112	        AssertAssemblyState(harness, BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction, completedWork: 1);
   113	
   114	        ExecuteDemoTick(harness, workPosition, tick++);
   115	        AssertAssemblyState(harness, BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction, completedWork: 2);
   116	
   117	        ExecuteDemoTick(harness, workPosition, tick++);
   118	        AssertAssemblyState(harness, BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete, completedWork: 3);
   119	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.PerformWork, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.Stage);
   120	        Assert.NotNull(harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId));
   121	
   122	        ExecuteDemoTick(harness, workPosition, tick);
   123	        BuildingSnapshot completed = Building(harness);
   124	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Completed, completed.Status);
   125	        Assert.Equal(completed.Definition.RequiredWork, completed.CompletedWork);
   126	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed, completed.BoxPlan!.CommitState);
   127	        Assert.Null(harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId));
   128	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.Status);
   129	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    private static void ExecuteDemoTick(
   133	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
   134	        CellId workerCell,
   135	        long tick)
   136	    {
   137	        for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 16; stepIndex++)
   138	        {
   139	            JobSnapshot? job = harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId);
   140	            if (job == null || job.IsTerminal)
   141	            {
   142	                return;
   143	            }
   144	
   145	            BuildingSnapshot building = Building(harness);
   146	            ItemStackSnapshot? box = harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId);
   147	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind step = Evaluate(
   148	                harness,
   149	                building,
   150	                box,
   151	                workerCell);
   152	            Assert.NotEqual(BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.None, step);
   153	            Execute(harness, step, workerCell, tick);
   154	
   155	            BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition disposition =
   156	                BuildingBoxAssemblyTickBoundaryPolicy.AfterSuccessfulStep(step);
   157	            if (disposition != BuildingBoxAssemblyTickDisposition.ContinueCurrentTick)
   158	            {
   159	                return;
   160	            }
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        Assert.Fail("BuildingBox assembly exceeded the immediate transition limit.");
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    private static void AssertAssemblyState(
   167	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
   168	        BuildingStatus expectedStatus,
   169	        int completedWork)
   170	    {
   171	        BuildingSnapshot building = Building(harness);
   172	        Assert.Equal(expectedStatus, building.Status);
   173	        Assert.Equal(completedWork, building.CompletedWork);
   174	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite, building.BoxPlan!.CommitState);
   175	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!.Status);
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    private static BuildingBoxHarness CreateAssigned(bool carriedByResident = false)
   179	    {
   180	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = new BuildingBoxHarness(carriedByResident);
   181	        Assert.True(harness.Confirm(
   182	            harness.BuildingId,
   183	            harness.JobId,
   184	            new CellId(3, 3)).IsSuccess);
   185	        JobSnapshot job = harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId)!;
   186	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Available)
   187	        {
   188	            InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates = new InMemoryJobCandidateProvider();
   189	            candidates.SetCandidates(harness.JobId, new[]
   190	            {
   191	                new JobCandidate(
   192	                    harness.WorkerId,
   193	                    5_000,
   194	                    distanceCost: 1,
   195	                    isAvailable: true),
   196	            });
   197	            JobAssignmentReport assigned = new AssignAvailableJobsHandler(
   198	                harness.JobRepository,
   199	                candidates,
   200	                harness.Journal).Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick: 400));
   201	            Assert.Single(assigned.Assignments);
   202	        }
   203	        else
   204	        {
   205	            Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Claimed, job.Status);
   206	            Assert.Equal(harness.WorkerId, job.AssignedAgentId);
   207	        }
   208	        return harness;
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    private static CellId ResolveWorkerCell(
   212	        JobSnapshot job,
   213	        BuildingSnapshot building,
   214	        ItemStackSnapshot? box)
   215	    {
   216	        bool acquiring = job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed
   217	            || job.Stage == JobStageKind.AcquireItem;
   218	        if (acquiring
   219	            && box?.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World
   220	            && box.Location.HasCell)
   221	        {
   222	            return box.Location.CellId;
   223	        }
   224	
   225	        return building.WorkPosition;
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    private static BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind Evaluate(
   229	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
   230	        BuildingSnapshot building,
   231	        ItemStackSnapshot? box,
   232	        CellId workerCell)
   233	    {
   234	        Result<BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind> result =
   235	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionPolicy.Evaluate(
   236	                harness.Jobs.Get(harness.JobId),
   237	                building,
   238	                box,
   239	                workerCell);
   240	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   241	        return result.Value;
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    private static void Execute(
   245	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
   246	        BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind step,
   247	        CellId workerCell,
   248	        long tick)
   249	    {
   250	        Result result = step switch
   251	        {
   252	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.StartJob => Advance(harness, tick),
   253	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AcquireBox =>
   254	                new AcquireBuildingBoxForAssemblyHandler(
   255	                    harness.BuildingsRepository,
   256	                    harness.InventoryRepository,
   257	                    harness.JobRepository,
   258	                    harness.Journal).Handle(new AcquireBuildingBoxForAssemblyCommand(
   259	                        harness.BuildingId,
   260	                        harness.JobId,
   261	                        workerCell,
   262	                        tick)),
   263	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage => Advance(harness, tick),
   264	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.CommitBoxToSite =>
   265	                new CommitBuildingBoxToSiteHandler(
   266	                    harness.BuildingsRepository,
   267	                    harness.InventoryRepository,
   268	                    harness.JobRepository,
   269	                    harness.Journal).Handle(new CommitBuildingBoxToSiteCommand(
   270	                        harness.BuildingId,
   271	                        harness.JobId,
   272	                        tick)),
   273	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.AddWork =>
   274	                new AddBuildingBoxAssemblyWorkHandler(
   275	                    harness.BuildingsRepository,
   276	                    harness.JobRepository,
   277	                    harness.Journal).Handle(new AddBuildingBoxAssemblyWorkCommand(
   278	                        harness.BuildingId,
   279	                        harness.JobId,
   280	                        workAmount: 1,
   281	                        tick: tick)),
   282	            BuildingBoxAssemblyExecutionStepKind.CompleteAssembly =>
   283	                new CompleteBuildingBoxAssemblyHandler(
   284	                    harness.BuildingsRepository,
   285	                    harness.InventoryRepository,
   286	                    harness.JobRepository,
   287	                    harness.Journal,
   288	                    AgentSkillGrantTestFactory.Create(
   289	                        harness.WorkerId,
   290	                        harness.Journal))
   291	                    .Handle(new CompleteBuildingBoxAssemblyCommand(
   292	                        harness.BuildingId,
   293	                        harness.JobId,
   294	                        tick)),
   295	            _ => Result.Failure(new DomainError("test.invalid_step", "Unexpected step.")),
   296	        };
   297	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    private static Result Advance(BuildingBoxHarness harness, long tick)
   301	    {
   302	        return new AdvanceJobHandler(
   303	            harness.JobRepository,
   304	            harness.Journal).Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(harness.JobId, tick));
   305	    }
   306	
   307	    private static BuildingSnapshot Building(BuildingBoxHarness harness)
   308	    {
   309	        return harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!;
   310	    }
   311	
   312	    private static ItemStackSnapshot Box(BuildingBoxHarness harness)
   313	    {
   314	        return harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId)!;
   315	    }
   316	}
   317	
   318	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingBoxPackingExecutionPolicyTests.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     2	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class BuildingBoxPackingExecutionPolicyTests
    14	{
    15	    [Fact]
    16	    public void Available_job_and_worker_away_from_target_produce_no_execution_step()
    17	    {
    18	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    19	        Assert.True(harness.Start().IsSuccess);
    20	        BuildingSnapshot building = harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!;
    21	
    22	        Assert.Equal(
    23	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None,
    24	            Evaluate(harness, building.Origin));
    25	
    26	        Assign(harness);
    27	        Assert.Equal(
    28	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None,
    29	            Evaluate(harness, building.Origin));
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    [Fact]
    33	    public void Logical_worker_position_drives_the_complete_execution_sequence()
    34	    {
    35	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    36	        Assert.True(harness.Start().IsSuccess);
    37	        Assign(harness);
    38	        CellId workPosition = harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition;
    39	
    40	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.StartJob, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    41	        Advance(harness, tick: 501);
    42	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    43	        Advance(harness, tick: 502);
    44	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AddWork, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    45	        Assert.True(harness.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    46	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AddWork, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    47	        Assert.True(harness.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    48	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    49	        Advance(harness, tick: 503);
    50	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.CompletePacking, Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    [Fact]
    54	    public void Policy_driven_execution_completes_once_and_creates_one_box()
    55	    {
    56	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    57	        Assert.True(harness.Start().IsSuccess);
    58	        Assign(harness);
    59	        CellId workPosition = harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition;
    60	
    61	        for (long tick = 600; tick < 610; tick++)
    62	        {
    63	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind step = Evaluate(harness, workPosition);
    64	            Result result = Execute(harness, step, tick);
    65	            Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    66	            if (harness.Jobs.Get(harness.PackingJobId)!.IsTerminal)
    67	            {
    68	                break;
    69	            }
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        Assert.Equal(
    73	            BuildingStatus.Removed,
    74	            harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!.Status);
    75	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(harness.PackingJobId)!.Status);
    76	        Assert.NotNull(harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.OutputStackId));
    77	        Assert.Equal(1, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(harness.BoxItemId));
    78	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
    79	        Assert.Equal(
    80	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None,
    81	            Evaluate(harness, workPosition));
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    [Fact]
    85	    public void Terminal_packing_job_produces_no_replay_step()
    86	    {
    87	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    88	        Assert.True(harness.Start().IsSuccess);
    89	        harness.AssignAndAdvanceToWork();
    90	        Assert.True(harness.AddWork(amount: 2).IsSuccess);
    91	        harness.AdvanceToFinalize();
    92	        Assert.True(harness.Complete().IsSuccess);
    93	        CellId origin = harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId)!.Origin;
    94	
    95	        Assert.Equal(
    96	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None,
    97	            Evaluate(harness, origin));
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    private static BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind Evaluate(
   101	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness,
   102	        CellId workerCell)
   103	    {
   104	        Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind> result =
   105	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionPolicy.Evaluate(
   106	                harness.Jobs.Get(harness.PackingJobId),
   107	                harness.Buildings.Get(harness.BuildingId),
   108	                workerCell);
   109	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   110	        return result.Value;
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    private static Result Execute(
   114	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness,
   115	        BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind step,
   116	        long tick)
   117	    {
   118	        return step switch
   119	        {
   120	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.StartJob => AdvanceResult(harness, tick),
   121	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage => AdvanceResult(harness, tick),
   122	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AddWork =>
   123	                new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkHandler(
   124	                    harness.Assembly.BuildingsRepository,
   125	                    harness.Assembly.JobRepository,
   126	                    harness.Assembly.Journal).Handle(new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkCommand(
   127	                        harness.BuildingId,
   128	                        harness.PackingJobId,
   129	                        workAmount: 1,
   130	                        tick: tick)),
   131	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.CompletePacking =>
   132	                new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingHandler(
   133	                    harness.Assembly.BuildingsRepository,
   134	                    harness.Assembly.InventoryRepository,
   135	                    harness.Assembly.JobRepository,
   136	                    harness.Assembly.Journal,
   137	                    AgentSkillGrantTestFactory.Create(
   138	                        harness.WorkerId,
   139	                        harness.Assembly.Journal))
   140	                    .Handle(new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingCommand(
   141	                        harness.BuildingId,
   142	                        harness.PackingJobId,
   143	                        tick)),
   144	            _ => Result.Success(),
   145	        };
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    private static void Assign(BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness)
   149	    {
   150	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates = new InMemoryJobCandidateProvider();
   151	        candidates.SetCandidates(harness.PackingJobId, new[]
   152	        {
   153	            new JobCandidate(
   154	                harness.WorkerId,
   155	                skillLevel: 5_000,
   156	                distanceCost: 1,
   157	                isAvailable: true),
   158	        });
   159	        JobAssignmentReport report = new AssignAvailableJobsHandler(
   160	            harness.Assembly.JobRepository,
   161	            candidates,
   162	            harness.Assembly.Journal).Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick: 500));
   163	        Assert.Single(report.Assignments);
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    private static void Advance(BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness, long tick)
   167	    {
   168	        Result result = AdvanceResult(harness, tick);
   169	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    private static Result AdvanceResult(BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness, long tick)
   173	    {
   174	        return new AdvanceJobHandler(
   175	            harness.Assembly.JobRepository,
   176	            harness.Assembly.Journal).Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(
   177	                harness.PackingJobId,
   178	                tick));
   179	    }
   180	}
   181	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingBoxPackingSaveTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    11	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    12	using Xunit;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Tests
    15	{
    16	
    17	public sealed class BuildingBoxPackingSaveTests
    18	{
    19	    [Fact]
    20	    public void Active_packing_round_trip_continues_to_one_output_box()
    21	    {
    22	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness original = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    23	        Assert.True(original.Start().IsSuccess);
    24	        original.AssignAndAdvanceToWork();
    25	        Assert.True(original.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    26	
    27	        SaveGameBuilder builder = new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry());
    28	        SaveGameDocument document = builder.Build(new SaveGameContext(
    29	            Metadata(),
    30	            original.Assembly.WorldRepository.Get(),
    31	            original.Inventory,
    32	            original.Jobs,
    33	            original.Buildings));
    34	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec codec = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    35	        SaveGameDocument decoded = codec.Deserialize(codec.Serialize(document));
    36	        SaveGameLoader loader = new SaveGameLoader(CreateMigrations(), CreateRegistry());
    37	
    38	        Result<LoadedGameState> result = loader.Load(
    39	            decoded,
    40	            CreateMaterials(),
    41	            original.Inventory.Catalog,
    42	            original.Assembly.Catalog);
    43	
    44	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    45	        LoadedGameState loaded = result.Value;
    46	        BuildingSnapshot restored = loaded.Buildings.Get(original.BuildingId)!;
    47	        JobSnapshot restoredJob = loaded.Jobs.Get(original.PackingJobId)!;
    48	        Assert.Equal(BuildingPackingCommitState.Active, restored.PackingPlan!.CommitState);
    49	        Assert.Equal(1, restored.PackingPlan.CompletedWork);
    50	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, restoredJob.Status);
    51	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.PerformWork, restoredJob.Stage);
    52	        Assert.Equal(original.WorkerId, restoredJob.AssignedAgentId);
    53	        Assert.Equal(new[]
    54	        {
    55	            ReservationKey.ForJob(original.PackingJobId),
    56	            ReservationKey.ForAgent(original.WorkerId),
    57	            ReservationKey.ForPosition(restored.WorkPosition),
    58	            ReservationKey.ForDestination(original.BuildingId),
    59	        }, loaded.Jobs.GetReservations().Select(item => item.Key).ToArray());
    60	        Assert.All(
    61	            loaded.Jobs.GetReservations(),
    62	            item => Assert.Equal(original.WorkerId, item.AgentId));
    63	        Assert.Null(loaded.Inventory.GetStack(original.OutputStackId));
    64	
    65	        CompleteLoadedPacking(loaded, original);
    66	        Assert.True(original.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    67	        original.AdvanceToFinalize();
    68	        Assert.True(original.Complete().IsSuccess);
    69	
    70	        BuildingSnapshot completed = loaded.Buildings.Get(original.BuildingId)!;
    71	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Removed, completed.Status);
    72	        Assert.Equal(BuildingPackingCommitState.Completed, completed.PackingPlan!.CommitState);
    73	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, loaded.Jobs.Get(original.PackingJobId)!.Status);
    74	        Assert.Empty(loaded.Jobs.GetReservations());
    75	        ItemStackSnapshot output = loaded.Inventory.GetStack(original.OutputStackId)!;
    76	        Assert.Equal(original.BoxItemId, output.ItemId);
    77	        Assert.Equal(1, output.Quantity);
    78	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(completed.Origin), output.Location);
    79	
    80	        Assert.Equal(
    81	            original.Buildings.Get(original.BuildingId)!.Status,
    82	            completed.Status);
    83	        Assert.Equal(
    84	            original.Buildings.Get(original.BuildingId)!.PackingPlan!.CommitState,
    85	            completed.PackingPlan.CommitState);
    86	        Assert.Equal(original.Inventory.GetTotal(original.BoxItemId),
    87	            loaded.Inventory.GetTotal(original.BoxItemId));
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    [Fact]
    91	    public void Active_packing_with_premature_output_stack_is_rejected()
    92	    {
    93	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness harness = new BuildingBoxPackingHarness();
    94	        Assert.True(harness.Start().IsSuccess);
    95	        SaveGameDocument document = new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry()).Build(
    96	            new SaveGameContext(
    97	                Metadata(),
    98	                harness.Assembly.WorldRepository.Get(),
    99	                harness.Inventory,
   100	                harness.Jobs,
   101	                harness.Buildings));
   102	        document.Inventory.Stacks.Add(new ItemStackSaveData
   103	        {
   104	            StackId = harness.OutputStackId.ToString(),
   105	            ItemId = harness.BoxItemId.ToString(),
   106	            Quantity = 1,
   107	            Location = new ItemLocationSaveData
   108	            {
   109	                Kind = (int)ItemLocationKind.World,
   110	                CellX = 3,
   111	                CellY = 3,
   112	            },
   113	        });
   114	
   115	        Result<LoadedGameState> result = new SaveGameLoader(
   116	            CreateMigrations(),
   117	            CreateRegistry()).Load(
   118	                document,
   119	                CreateMaterials(),
   120	                harness.Inventory.Catalog,
   121	                harness.Assembly.Catalog);
   122	
   123	        Assert.True(result.IsFailure);
   124	        Assert.Equal(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument, result.Error);
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    private static void CompleteLoadedPacking(
   128	        LoadedGameState loaded,
   129	        BuildingBoxPackingHarness source)
   130	    {
   131	        InMemoryBuildingsRepository buildings = new InMemoryBuildingsRepository(
   132	            loaded.Buildings);
   133	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventory = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(
   134	            loaded.Inventory);
   135	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs = new InMemoryJobRepository(loaded.Jobs);
   136	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
   137	        long tick = 500;
   138	
   139	        Assert.True(new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkHandler(
   140	            buildings,
   141	            jobs,
   142	            journal).Handle(new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkCommand(
   143	                source.BuildingId,
   144	                source.PackingJobId,
   145	                workAmount: 1,
   146	                tick: tick++)).IsSuccess);
   147	        Assert.True(new AdvanceJobHandler(jobs, journal).Handle(
   148	            new AdvanceJobCommand(source.PackingJobId, tick++)).IsSuccess);
   149	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.Finalize, loaded.Jobs.Get(source.PackingJobId)!.Stage);
   150	        Assert.True(new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingHandler(
   151	            buildings,
   152	            inventory,
   153	            jobs,
   154	            journal,
   155	            AgentSkillGrantTestFactory.Create(source.WorkerId, journal))
   156	            .Handle(new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingCommand(
   157	                source.BuildingId,
   158	                source.PackingJobId,
   159	                tick)).IsSuccess);
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry CreateRegistry()
   163	    {
   164	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   165	        {
   166	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec(),
   167	            new BuildingBoxPackingJobSaveCodec(),
   168	        });
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    private static SaveMigrationPipeline CreateMigrations()
   172	    {
   173	        return new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
   174	        {
   175	            new LegacySaveVersionZeroMigration(),
   176	            new SaveVersionOneBuildingsMigration(),
   177	            new SaveVersionTwoPackingMigration(),
   178	            new SaveVersionThreeAgentSkillsMigration(),
   179	            new SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration(),
   180	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
   181	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   182	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   183	        });
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   187	    {
   188	        return BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Materials();
   189	    }
   190	
   191	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata()
   192	    {
   193	        return new SaveMetadataData
   194	        {
   195	            SlotId = "packing-active",
   196	            DisplayName = "Packing active",
   197	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-15T22:40:00Z",
   198	            SimulationTick = 200,
   199	            WorldSeed = 1,
   200	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   201	        };
   202	    }

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingBoxPlacementPresenterTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class BuildingBoxPlacementPresenterTests
    14	{
    15	    private static readonly ItemId BoxItem = new ItemId("building_box.preview");
    16	    private static readonly EntityId StackId = Id(1);
    17	    private static readonly EntityId ReservationJobId = Id(2);
    18	    private readonly BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter _presenter =
    19	        new BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter(new BuildingPlacementValidator());
    20	
    21	    [Fact]
    22	    public void Upper_layer_preview_uses_rotated_footprint_and_work_position()
    23	    {
    24	        CellId origin = new CellId(2, 2, 1);
    25	
    26	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel north = Preview(
    27	            origin,
    28	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    29	            reachable: new[] { new CellId(2, 1, 1) });
    30	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel east = Preview(
    31	            origin,
    32	            BuildingOrientation.East,
    33	            reachable: new[] { new CellId(3, 2, 1) });
    34	
    35	        Assert.True(north.IsValid);
    36	        Assert.True(north.IsVisible);
    37	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding, north.PlacementKind);
    38	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxGhostStyle.Valid, north.Style);
    39	        Assert.Equal(
    40	            new[] { new CellId(2, 2, 1), new CellId(3, 2, 1) },
    41	            north.Footprint.ToArray());
    42	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(1, 2, 1), north.WorkPosition);
    43	        Assert.True(east.IsValid);
    44	        Assert.True(east.IsVisible);
    45	        Assert.Equal(
    46	            new[] { new CellId(2, 2, 1), new CellId(2, 3, 1) },
    47	            east.Footprint.ToArray());
    48	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(3, 2, 1), east.WorkPosition);
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    [Fact]
    52	    public void Z0_preview_is_single_cell_box_relocation()
    53	    {
    54	        CellId target = new CellId(4, 3, 0);
    55	
    56	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview = Preview(
    57	            target,
    58	            BuildingOrientation.West,
    59	            reachable: new[] { target });
    60	        Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft> drafted =
    61	            _presenter.CreateConfirmationDraft(preview);
    62	
    63	        Assert.True(preview.IsValid);
    64	        Assert.True(preview.IsVisible);
    65	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox, preview.PlacementKind);
    66	        Assert.Equal(new[] { target }, preview.Footprint);
    67	        Assert.Equal(target, preview.WorkPosition);
    68	        Assert.True(drafted.IsSuccess);
    69	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox, drafted.Value.PlacementKind);
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    [Fact]
    73	    public void Unsupported_air_hides_box_and_building_ghosts()
    74	    {
    75	        CellId boxTarget = new CellId(4, 3, 0);
    76	        CellId buildingOrigin = new CellId(2, 2, 1);
    77	        WorldSnapshot air = BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Empty();
    78	
    79	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel box = _presenter.Preview(
    80	            Stack(),
    81	            Item(),
    82	            Definition(),
    83	            boxTarget,
    84	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    85	            air,
    86	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
    87	            new[] { boxTarget });
    88	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel building = _presenter.Preview(
    89	            Stack(),
    90	            Item(),
    91	            Definition(),
    92	            buildingOrigin,
    93	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    94	            air,
    95	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
    96	            new[] { new CellId(2, 1, 1) });
    97	
    98	        Assert.False(box.IsValid);
    99	        Assert.False(box.IsVisible);
   100	        Assert.Equal(PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing.Code, box.ReasonCode);
   101	        Assert.True(_presenter.CreateConfirmationDraft(box).IsFailure);
   102	        Assert.False(building.IsValid);
   103	        Assert.False(building.IsVisible);
   104	        Assert.Equal(PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing.Code, building.ReasonCode);
   105	        Assert.True(_presenter.CreateConfirmationDraft(building).IsFailure);
   106	    }

   109	    public void Z0_relocation_ignores_non_building_occupants_by_contract()
   110	    {

   132	    [Fact]
   133	    public void Placement_mode_rotation_is_stable_and_reversible()
   134	    {
   135	        BuildingBoxPlacementModeState initial = new BuildingBoxPlacementModeState(
   136	            StackId,
   137	            Definition().Id);
   138	
   139	        BuildingBoxPlacementModeState clockwise = initial
   140	            .RotateClockwise()
   141	            .RotateClockwise()
   142	            .RotateClockwise()
   143	            .RotateClockwise();
   144	        BuildingBoxPlacementModeState counter = initial
   145	            .RotateCounterClockwise()
   146	            .RotateClockwise();
   147	
   148	        Assert.Equal(BuildingOrientation.North, clockwise.Orientation);
   149	        Assert.Equal(BuildingOrientation.North, counter.Orientation);
   150	        Assert.Equal(StackId, clockwise.SourceStackId);
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    [Fact]
   154	    public void Source_mismatch_and_reservation_have_typed_reasons()
   155	    {
   156	        ItemDefinition wrongItem = new ItemDefinition(
   157	            new ItemId("other"),
   158	            "Other",
   159	            maximumStackSize: 1,
   160	            isTool: false);
   161	        ItemStackSnapshot reserved = new ItemStackSnapshot(
   162	            StackId,
   163	            BoxItem,
   164	            quantity: 1,
   165	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1)),
   166	            new[] { new ItemQuantityReservationSnapshot(ReservationJobId, 1) });
   167	        CellId origin = new CellId(2, 2, 1);
   168	        CellId[] reachable = { new CellId(2, 1, 1) };
   169	        WorldSnapshot world = BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Supported(
   170	            Definition(),
   171	            origin,
   172	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   173	            reachable);
   174	
   175	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel mismatch = _presenter.Preview(
   176	            Stack(),
   177	            wrongItem,
   178	            Definition(),
   179	            origin,
   180	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   181	            world,
   182	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   183	            reachable);
   184	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel unavailable = _presenter.Preview(
   185	            reserved,
   186	            Item(),
   187	            Definition(),
   188	            origin,
   189	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   190	            world,
   191	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   192	            reachable);
   193	
   194	        Assert.False(mismatch.IsValid);
   195	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.ItemMismatch, mismatch.ReasonCode);
   196	        Assert.False(unavailable.IsValid);
   197	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.BoxUnavailable, unavailable.ReasonCode);
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    [Fact]
   201	    public void Authoritative_placement_reason_is_preserved_in_invalid_ghost()
   202	    {
   203	        CellId origin = new CellId(2, 2, 1);
   204	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel occupied = Preview(
   205	            origin,
   206	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   207	            occupied: new[] { origin },
   208	            reachable: new[] { new CellId(2, 1, 1) });
   209	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel unreachable = Preview(
   210	            origin,
   211	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   212	            reachable: Array.Empty<CellId>());
   213	
   214	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxGhostStyle.Invalid, occupied.Style);
   215	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.PlacementOccupied.Code, occupied.ReasonCode);
   216	        Assert.Equal(
   217	            BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition.Code,
   218	            unreachable.ReasonCode);
   219	    }
   220	
   221	    [Fact]
   222	    public void Confirmation_draft_exists_only_for_valid_preview()
   223	    {
   224	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel valid = Preview(
   225	            new CellId(2, 2, 1),
   226	            BuildingOrientation.East,
   227	            reachable: new[] { new CellId(3, 2, 1) });
   228	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel invalid = _presenter.Preview(
   229	            sourceStack: null,
   230	            sourceItem: null,
   231	            Definition(),
   232	            new CellId(2, 2, 1),
   233	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   234	            BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Empty(),
   235	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   236	            new[] { new CellId(2, 1, 1) });
   237	
   238	        Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft> draft =
   239	            _presenter.CreateConfirmationDraft(valid);
   240	        Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft> rejected =
   241	            _presenter.CreateConfirmationDraft(invalid);
   242	
   243	        Assert.True(draft.IsSuccess);
   244	        Assert.Equal(StackId, draft.Value.SourceStackId);
   245	        Assert.Equal(Definition().Id, draft.Value.DefinitionId);
   246	        Assert.Equal(BuildingOrientation.East, draft.Value.Orientation);
   247	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(3, 2, 1), draft.Value.WorkPosition);
   248	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding, draft.Value.PlacementKind);
   249	        Assert.True(rejected.IsFailure);
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    private BuildingBoxGhostViewModel Preview(
   253	        CellId origin,
   254	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   255	        CellId[]? occupied = null,
   256	        CellId[]? reachable = null)
   257	    {
   258	        CellId[] routes = reachable ?? Array.Empty<CellId>();
   259	        return _presenter.Preview(
   260	            Stack(),
   261	            Item(),
   262	            Definition(),
   263	            origin,
   264	            orientation,
   265	            BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Supported(
   266	                Definition(),
   267	                origin,
   268	                orientation,
   269	                routes),
   270	            occupied ?? Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   271	            routes);
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    private static BuildingDefinition Definition()
   275	    {
   276	        return new BuildingDefinition(
   277	            new BuildingDefinitionId("preview.workshop"),
   278	            "Preview Workshop",
   279	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0), new CellOffset(1, 0) },
   280	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, -1) },
   281	            Array.Empty<BuildingMaterialRequirement>(),
   282	            requiredWork: 3,
   283	            maximumDurability: 100,
   284	            boxPolicy: new BuildingBoxPolicy(BoxItem, packingWork: 2));
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    private static ItemDefinition Item()
   288	    {
   289	        return new ItemDefinition(
   290	            BoxItem,
   291	            "Preview Box",
   292	            maximumStackSize: 1,
   293	            isTool: false);
   294	    }
   295	
   296	    private static ItemStackSnapshot Stack()
   297	    {
   298	        return new ItemStackSnapshot(
   299	            StackId,
   300	            BoxItem,
   301	            quantity: 1,
   302	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1)),
   303	            Array.Empty<ItemQuantityReservationSnapshot>());
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    private static EntityId Id(int value)
   307	    {
   308	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
   309	    }
