===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingConstructionTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using System;
     3	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BuildingConstructionTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly ItemId Rock = new ItemId("resource.rock");
    17	    private static readonly EntityId BuildingId =
    18	        EntityId.Parse("72000000000000000000000000000001");
    19	    private static readonly EntityId SourceStackId =
    20	        EntityId.Parse("73000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerId =
    22	        EntityId.Parse("74000000000000000000000000000001");
    23	
    24	    [Fact]
    25	    public void Placement_delivery_and_construction_complete_exactly_once()
    26	    {
    27	        BuildingConstructionHarness harness = CreateHarness();
    28	        harness.Place();
    29	        harness.Deliver(
    30	            EntityId.Parse("75000000000000000000000000000001"),
    31	            quantity: 4,
    32	            EntityId.Parse("76000000000000000000000000000001"));
    33	        Assert.True(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsHandler(
    34	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
    35	            harness.InventoryRepository,
    36	            harness.Journal).Handle(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsCommand(BuildingId)).IsSuccess);
    37	        EntityId constructionJobId =
    38	            EntityId.Parse("77000000000000000000000000000001");
    39	        CreateConstructionJobHandler createConstruction = new CreateConstructionJobHandler(
    40	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
    41	            harness.JobRepository,
    42	            harness.Journal);
    43	        Assert.True(createConstruction.Handle(new CreateConstructionJobCommand(
    44	            constructionJobId,
    45	            BuildingId,
    46	            new[] { new CellId(2, 3) },
    47	            priority: 700,
    48	            tick: 10)).IsSuccess);
    49	        harness.AssignAndAdvanceToPerformWork(constructionJobId, tick: 11);
    50	        Assert.True(new AddConstructionWorkHandler(
    51	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
    52	            harness.JobRepository,
    53	            harness.Journal).Handle(new AddConstructionWorkCommand(
    54	                constructionJobId,
    55	                BuildingId,
    56	                workAmount: 10,
    57	                tick: 13)).IsSuccess);
    58	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.Finalize, harness.Jobs.Get(constructionJobId)!.Stage);
    59	        CompleteConstructionHandler complete = new CompleteConstructionHandler(
    60	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
    61	            harness.InventoryRepository,
    62	            harness.JobRepository,
    63	            harness.Journal,
    64	            AgentSkillGrantTestFactory.Create(WorkerId, harness.Journal));
    65	
    66	        Assert.True(complete.Handle(new CompleteConstructionCommand(
    67	            constructionJobId,
    68	            BuildingId,
    69	            tick: 14)).IsSuccess);
    70	
    71	        BuildingSnapshot building = harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!;
    72	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Completed, building.Status);
    73	        Assert.Equal(100, building.Durability);
    74	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(constructionJobId)!.Status);
    75	        Assert.Equal(6, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(Rock));
    76	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetQuantityAt(
    77	            Rock,
    78	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(BuildingId)));
    79	        Assert.Equal(
    80	            1,
    81	            harness.Journal.Events.OfType<BuildingConstructionProgressed>()
    82	                .Count(value => value.BuildingId == BuildingId
    83	                    && value.CompletedWork == value.RequiredWork));
    84	        Assert.Equal(
    85	            1,
    86	            harness.Journal.Events.OfType<BuildingCompleted>()
    87	                .Count(value => value.BuildingId == BuildingId));
    88	
    89	        Assert.True(complete.Handle(new CompleteConstructionCommand(
    90	            constructionJobId,
    91	            BuildingId,
    92	            tick: 15)).IsFailure);
    93	        Assert.Equal(
    94	            1,
    95	            harness.Journal.Events.OfType<BuildingCompleted>()
    96	                .Count(value => value.BuildingId == BuildingId));
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    [Fact]
   100	    public void Cancellation_releases_jobs_and_returns_delivered_materials()
   101	    {
   102	        BuildingConstructionHarness harness = CreateHarness();
   103	        harness.Place();
   104	        EntityId completedDelivery =
   105	            EntityId.Parse("75000000000000000000000000000002");
   106	        harness.Deliver(
   107	            completedDelivery,
   108	            quantity: 2,
   109	            EntityId.Parse("76000000000000000000000000000002"));
   110	        EntityId activeDelivery =
   111	            EntityId.Parse("75000000000000000000000000000003");
   112	        Assert.True(new CreateBuildingDeliveryHandler(
   113	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   114	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   115	            harness.JobRepository,
   116	            harness.Journal).Handle(new CreateBuildingDeliveryCommand(
   117	                activeDelivery,
   118	                BuildingId,
   119	                SourceStackId,
   120	                quantity: 2,
   121	                priority: 500,
   122	                tick: 8)).IsSuccess);
   123	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetStack(SourceStackId)!.ReservedQuantity);
   124	        CancelBuildingHandler cancel = new CancelBuildingHandler(
   125	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   126	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   127	            harness.JobRepository,
   128	            harness.Journal);
   129	
   130	        Assert.True(cancel.Handle(new CancelBuildingCommand(
   131	            BuildingId,
   132	            "Player cancelled the blueprint.",
   133	            tick: 9)).IsSuccess);
   134	
   135	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Cancelled, harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.Status);
   136	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Cancelled, harness.Jobs.Get(activeDelivery)!.Status);
   137	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetStack(SourceStackId)!.ReservedQuantity);
   138	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetQuantityAt(
   139	            Rock,
   140	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(BuildingId)));
   141	        Assert.Equal(10, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(Rock));
   142	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetQuantityAt(
   143	            Rock,
   144	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(3, 3))));
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    [Fact]
   148	    public void Construction_job_is_blocked_with_reachable_position_reason()
   149	    {
   150	        BuildingConstructionHarness harness = CreateHarness();
   151	        harness.Place();
   152	        harness.Deliver(
   153	            EntityId.Parse("75000000000000000000000000000004"),
   154	            quantity: 4,
   155	            EntityId.Parse("76000000000000000000000000000004"));
   156	        Assert.True(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsHandler(
   157	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   158	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   159	            harness.Journal).Handle(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsCommand(BuildingId)).IsSuccess);
   160	        CreateConstructionJobHandler create = new CreateConstructionJobHandler(
   161	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   162	            harness.JobRepository,
   163	            harness.Journal);
   164	
   165	        Result blocked = create.Handle(new CreateConstructionJobCommand(
   166	            EntityId.Parse("77000000000000000000000000000004"),
   167	            BuildingId,
   168	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   169	            priority: 500,
   170	            tick: 10));
   171	
   172	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition, blocked.Error);
   173	        Assert.Contains("unreachable", harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.DiagnosticReason!);
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    [Fact]
   177	    public void Completed_building_can_be_damaged_repaired_and_removed()
   178	    {
   179	        BuildingConstructionHarness harness = CreateCompletedHarness();
   180	        DamageBuildingHandler damage = new DamageBuildingHandler(
   181	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   182	            harness.Journal);
   183	        RepairBuildingHandler repair = new RepairBuildingHandler(harness.BuildingsRepository);
   184	        RemoveBuildingHandler remove = new RemoveBuildingHandler(
   185	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   186	            harness.Journal);
   187	
   188	        Assert.True(damage.Handle(new DamageBuildingCommand(
   189	            BuildingId,
   190	            amount: 30,
   191	            tick: 20)).IsSuccess);
   192	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Damaged, harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.Status);
   193	        Assert.True(repair.Handle(new RepairBuildingCommand(BuildingId, amount: 30)).IsSuccess);
   194	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Completed, harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.Status);
   195	        Assert.True(remove.Handle(new RemoveBuildingCommand(
   196	            BuildingId,
   197	            "Demolished by player.",
   198	            tick: 21)).IsSuccess);
   199	        Assert.Equal(BuildingStatus.Removed, harness.Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.Status);
   200	        Assert.Contains(
   201	            harness.Journal.Events,
   202	            value => value is BuildingRemoved removed && removed.BuildingId == BuildingId);
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    private static BuildingConstructionHarness CreateCompletedHarness()
   206	    {
   207	        BuildingConstructionHarness harness = CreateHarness();
   208	        harness.Place();
   209	        harness.Deliver(
   210	            EntityId.Parse("75000000000000000000000000000005"),
   211	            quantity: 4,
   212	            EntityId.Parse("76000000000000000000000000000005"));
   213	        Assert.True(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsHandler(
   214	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   215	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   216	            harness.Journal).Handle(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsCommand(BuildingId)).IsSuccess);
   217	        EntityId constructionJob =
   218	            EntityId.Parse("77000000000000000000000000000005");
   219	        Assert.True(new CreateConstructionJobHandler(
   220	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   221	            harness.JobRepository,
   222	            harness.Journal).Handle(new CreateConstructionJobCommand(
   223	                constructionJob,
   224	                BuildingId,
   225	                new[] { new CellId(2, 3) },
   226	                priority: 500,
   227	                tick: 10)).IsSuccess);
   228	        harness.AssignAndAdvanceToPerformWork(constructionJob, tick: 11);
   229	        Assert.True(new AddConstructionWorkHandler(
   230	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   231	            harness.JobRepository,
   232	            harness.Journal).Handle(new AddConstructionWorkCommand(
   233	                constructionJob,
   234	                BuildingId,
   235	                workAmount: 10,
   236	                tick: 13)).IsSuccess);
   237	        Assert.True(new CompleteConstructionHandler(
   238	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   239	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   240	            harness.JobRepository,
   241	            harness.Journal,
   242	            AgentSkillGrantTestFactory.Create(WorkerId, harness.Journal))
   243	            .Handle(new CompleteConstructionCommand(
   244	                constructionJob,
   245	                BuildingId,
   246	                tick: 14)).IsSuccess);
   247	        return harness;
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    private static BuildingConstructionHarness CreateHarness()
   251	    {
   252	        ItemCatalog items = new ItemCatalog(new[]
   253	        {
   254	            new ItemDefinition(Rock, "Rock", 100, isTool: false),
   255	        });
   256	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
   257	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
   258	            SourceStackId,
   259	            Rock,
   260	            quantity: 10,
   261	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1)),
   262	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   263	        return new BuildingConstructionHarness(
   264	            inventory,
   265	            BuildingId,
   266	            SourceStackId,
   267	            WorkerId);
   268	    }
   269	}
   270	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingPlacementTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	using Xunit;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Tests
     9	{
    10	
    11	public sealed class BuildingPlacementTests
    12	{
    13	    private static readonly EntityId FirstBuildingId =
    14	        EntityId.Parse("71000000000000000000000000000001");
    15	    private static readonly EntityId SecondBuildingId =
    16	        EntityId.Parse("71000000000000000000000000000002");
    17	
    18	    [Fact]
    19	    public void Placement_requires_empty_explored_unoccupied_footprint()
    20	    {
    21	        WorldState world = CreateEmptyWorld();
    22	        BuildingDefinition definition = CreateDefinition();
    23	        BuildingPlacementValidator validator = new BuildingPlacementValidator();
    24	        BuildingsState buildings = new BuildingsState();
    25	        CellId origin = new CellId(3, 3);
    26	        CellId workPosition = new CellId(2, 3);
    27	        BuildingPlacementResult valid = validator.Validate(
    28	            definition,
    29	            origin,
    30	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    31	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    32	            buildings.GetOccupiedCells(),
    33	            new[] { workPosition });
    34	
    35	        Assert.True(valid.Succeeded);
    36	        Assert.True(buildings.Place(
    37	            FirstBuildingId,
    38	            definition,
    39	            origin,
    40	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    41	            valid,
    42	            tick: 1).IsSuccess);
    43	
    44	        BuildingPlacementResult overlap = validator.Validate(
    45	            definition,
    46	            origin,
    47	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    48	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    49	            buildings.GetOccupiedCells(),
    50	            new[] { workPosition });
    51	
    52	        Assert.False(overlap.Succeeded);
    53	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.PlacementOccupied, overlap.Error);
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    [Fact]
    57	    public void Permanent_mushroom_site_blocks_building_but_not_world_items()
    58	    {
    59	        WorldState world = CreateEmptyWorld();
    60	        CellId origin = new CellId(3, 3);
    61	        BuildingPlacementResult placement = new BuildingPlacementValidator().Validate(
    62	            CreateDefinition(),
    63	            origin,
    64	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    65	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    66	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
    67	            new[] { new CellId(2, 3) },
    68	            ecologyBlockedCells: new[] { origin });
    69	
    70	        Assert.False(placement.Succeeded);
    71	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.PlacementEcologyBlocked, placement.Error);
    72	
    73	        ItemId cap = new ItemId("material.mushroom_cap");
    74	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[]
    75	        {
    76	            new ItemDefinition(cap, "Mushroom cap", 100, isTool: false),
    77	        }));
    78	        Assert.True(inventory.AddUnit(
    79	            EntityId.Parse("71000000000000000000000000000003"),
    80	            cap,
    81	            ItemLocation.InWorld(origin),
    82	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    [Fact]
    86	    public void Placement_reports_unreachable_work_position()
    87	    {
    88	        WorldState world = CreateEmptyWorld();
    89	        BuildingPlacementResult placement = new BuildingPlacementValidator().Validate(
    90	            CreateDefinition(),
    91	            new CellId(3, 3),
    92	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    93	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    94	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
    95	            Array.Empty<CellId>());
    96	
    97	        Assert.False(placement.Succeeded);
    98	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition, placement.Error);
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    [Fact]
   102	    public void Placement_reports_solid_and_out_of_bounds_footprints()
   103	    {
   104	        MaterialId rock = new MaterialId("rock");
   105	        MaterialId air = new MaterialId("air");
   106	        MaterialCatalog materials = new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   107	        {
   108	            new MaterialDefinition(rock, isSolid: true, hardness: 100),
   109	            new MaterialDefinition(air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   110	        });
   111	        WorldState solidWorld = Require(WorldState.CreateFilled(
   112	            new WorldSize(4, 4),
   113	            chunkSize: 2,
   114	            materials,
   115	            rock,
   116	            explored: true));
   117	        BuildingPlacementValidator validator = new BuildingPlacementValidator();
   118	        BuildingDefinition definition = CreateDefinition();
   119	
   120	        BuildingPlacementResult solid = validator.Validate(
   121	            definition,
   122	            new CellId(1, 1),
   123	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   124	            solidWorld.CreateSnapshot(),
   125	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   126	            new[] { new CellId(1, 0) });
   127	        BuildingPlacementResult outside = validator.Validate(
   128	            definition,
   129	            new CellId(3, 3),
   130	            BuildingOrientation.East,
   131	            solidWorld.CreateSnapshot(),
   132	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   133	            new[] { new CellId(3, 2) });
   134	
   135	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.PlacementSolid, solid.Error);
   136	        Assert.Equal(BuildingErrors.PlacementOutOfBounds, outside.Error);
   137	    }
   138	
   139	    internal static BuildingDefinition CreateDefinition()
   140	    {
   141	        return new BuildingDefinition(
   142	            new BuildingDefinitionId("workshop.basic"),
   143	            "Basic workshop",
   144	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0), new CellOffset(1, 0) },
   145	            new[] { new CellOffset(-1, 0), new CellOffset(2, 0) },
   146	            new[]
   147	            {
   148	                new BuildingMaterialRequirement(new ItemId("resource.rock"), 4),
   149	            },
   150	            requiredWork: 10,
   151	            maximumDurability: 100);
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    internal static WorldState CreateEmptyWorld()
   155	    {
   156	        MaterialId air = new MaterialId("air");
   157	        MaterialCatalog materials = new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   158	        {
   159	            new MaterialDefinition(air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   160	        });
   161	        return Require(WorldState.CreateFilled(
   162	            new WorldSize(8, 8),
   163	            chunkSize: 4,
   164	            materials,
   165	            air,
   166	            explored: true));
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    private static T Require<T>(Result<T> result)
   170	    {
   171	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   172	        return result.Value;
   173	    }
   174	}
   175	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingProductionPresenterTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Production;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.Production;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BuildingProductionPresenterTests
    15	{
    16	    [Fact]
    17	    public void Campfire_panel_shows_six_products_four_stock_toggles_and_shortage()
    18	    {
    19	        ItemCatalog items = CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems();
    20	        BuildingCatalog buildings = new BuildingCatalog(
    21	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateBuildings());
    22	        ProductionContentCatalog content = ProductionContentCatalog.ValidateAndCreate(
    23	            items,
    24	            buildings,
    25	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(1),
    26	            Array.Empty<TechnologyDefinition>(),
    27	            new[] { CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation() }).Catalog!;
    28	        EntityId buildingId = EntityId.Parse("a1000000000000000000000000000001");
    29	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    30	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    31	            EntityId.Parse("a2000000000000000000000000000001"),
    32	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    33	            1,
    34	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(buildingId),
    35	            0).IsSuccess);
    36	        BuildingSupplyState supply = new BuildingSupplyState();
    37	        supply.Register(buildingId, content.GetWorkstation(
    38	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.CampfireBuildingId), 0);
    39	        ProductionState production = new ProductionState();
    40	        EntityId orderId = EntityId.Parse("a3000000000000000000000000000001");
    41	        production.Enqueue(
    42	            orderId,
    43	            content.GetRecipe(CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId),
    44	            buildingId,
    45	            0);
    46	
    47	        BuildingProductionViewModel model = new BuildingProductionPresenter(
    48	            content,
    49	            items).Present(
    50	                buildingId,
    51	                production,
    52	                supply.Get(buildingId, inventory.CreateSnapshot())!);
    53	
    54	        Assert.Equal(6, model.Products.Count);
    55	        Assert.Equal(4, model.Stocks.Count);
    56	        ProductionIconViewModel grilled = model.Products.Single(value =>
    57	            value.RecipeId == CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId);
    58	        Assert.Equal(1, grilled.QueuedCount);
    59	        Assert.True(grilled.HasInputs);
    60	        ProductionIconViewModel tent = model.Products.Single(value =>
    61	            value.RecipeId == CampfireProductionContent.TentRecipeId);
    62	        Assert.True(tent.IsOrange);
    63	        Assert.Contains("Mushroom leg", tent.Tooltip, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    64	        Assert.All(model.Stocks, stock => Assert.True(stock.DeliveryEnabled));
    65	    }
    66	}
    67	
    68	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingProductionSaveRoundTripTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Production;
     7	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Content;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    12	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    13	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    14	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    15	using Dig.Domain.World;
    16	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    17	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    18	using Xunit;
    19	
    20	namespace Dig.Tests
    21	{
    22	
    23	public sealed class BuildingProductionSaveRoundTripTests
    24	{
    25	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("terrain.air");
    26	
    27	    [Fact]
    28	    public void Mid_step_round_trip_continues_without_reconsuming_finished_steps()
    29	    {
    30	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness(100);
    31	        EntityId orderId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(500);
    32	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(501);
    33	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId, 2, 510);
    34	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, 1, 511);
    35	        Assert.True(harness.Enqueue(orderId, CampfireProductionContent.TentRecipeId, 1).IsSuccess);
    36	        Assert.True(harness.Prepare(jobId, 2).IsSuccess);
    37	        harness.ClaimBeginAndReachWork(orderId, jobId, 3);
    38	        Assert.True(harness.Work(orderId, jobId, elapsedTicks: 120, tick: 6).IsSuccess);
    39	        Assert.True(harness.Supply.SetDeliveryEnabled(
    40	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
    41	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    42	            enabled: false,
    43	            tick: 7).IsSuccess);
    44	
    45	        SaveGameDocument document = Builder().Build(new SaveGameContext(
    46	            Metadata("mid-step"),
    47	            CreateWorld(),
    48	            harness.Inventory,
    49	            harness.Jobs,
    50	            harness.Buildings,
    51	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
    52	            production: harness.Production,
    53	            buildingSupply: harness.Supply));
    54	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec codec = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    55	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = Loader().Load(
    56	            codec.Deserialize(codec.Serialize(document)),
    57	            CreateMaterials(),
    58	            harness.Items,
    59	            harness.BuildingsCatalog,
    60	            terrainDepositCatalog: null,
    61	            mushroomCatalog: null,
    62	            harness.Content);
    63	
    64	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    65	        ProductionOrderSnapshot restored = loaded.Value.Production.Get(orderId)!;
    66	        Assert.Equal(ProductionOrderStatus.InProgress, restored.Status);
    67	        Assert.True(restored.MaterialSteps[0].Consumed);
    68	        Assert.Equal(20, restored.MaterialSteps[1].CompletedTicks);
    69	        Assert.Equal(1, loaded.Value.Inventory.GetTotal(
    70	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId));
    71	        Assert.False(loaded.Value.BuildingSupply.Get(
    72	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
    73	            loaded.Value.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!.Stocks.Single(value =>
    74	                value.ItemId == CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId)
    75	            .DeliveryEnabled);
    76	
    77	        InMemoryProductionRepository production = new InMemoryProductionRepository(
    78	            loaded.Value.Production);
    79	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventory = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(
    80	            loaded.Value.Inventory);
    81	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs = new InMemoryJobRepository(loaded.Value.Jobs);
    82	        InMemoryAgentRepository agents = new InMemoryAgentRepository();
    83	        Assert.True(agents.Add(AgentTestFactory.CreateAgent(
    84	            id: CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId)).IsSuccess);
    85	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
    86	        Assert.True(new ApplyProductionWorkHandler(
    87	            production,
    88	            inventory,
    89	            jobs,
    90	            agents,
    91	            journal).Handle(new ApplyProductionWorkCommand(
    92	                orderId,
    93	                jobId,
    94	                baseWork: 180,
    95	                conditionEfficiencyBasisPoints: 10_000,
    96	                tick: 8)).IsSuccess);
    97	
    98	        AgentSkillGrantService skills = new AgentSkillGrantService(agents, journal);
    99	        EntityId outputId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(512);
   100	        Assert.True(new CompleteProductionOrderHandler(
   101	            production,
   102	            inventory,
   103	            jobs,
   104	            journal,
   105	            skills).Handle(new CompleteProductionOrderCommand(
   106	                orderId,
   107	                jobId,
   108	                new[] { outputId },
   109	                tick: 9,
   110	                ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(4, 2, 0)))).IsSuccess);
   111	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.TentBoxItemId,
   112	            loaded.Value.Inventory.GetStack(outputId)!.ItemId);
   113	        Assert.Equal(1, loaded.Value.Inventory.GetTotal(
   114	            CampfireProductionContent.TentBoxItemId));
   115	        Assert.Equal(200, agents.Get(CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId)!
   116	            .CreateSkillProgressionSnapshot()
   117	            .GetLevel(AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking));
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    [Fact]
   121	    public void Active_mixed_supply_and_job_definition_round_trip()
   122	    {
   123	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness(1);
   124	        EntityId supplyJobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(520);
   125	        EntityId sourceId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(521);
   126	        EntityId transitId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(522);
   127	        EntityId depositId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(523);
   128	        Assert.True(harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   129	            sourceId,
   130	            CampfireProductionContent.StoneItemId,
   131	            2,
   132	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1, 0)),
   133	            1).IsSuccess);
   134	        Assert.True(harness.Inventory.ReserveQuantity(sourceId, supplyJobId, 2, 2).IsSuccess);
   135	        ItemReservationAllocation allocation = new ItemReservationAllocation(
   136	            sourceId,
   137	            CampfireProductionContent.StoneItemId,
   138	            2);
   139	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition definition = new BuildingSupplyJobDefinition(
   140	            supplyJobId,
   141	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   142	            harness.Buildings.Get(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition,
   143	            new[] { allocation },
   144	            new[] { transitId },
   145	            new[] { depositId },
   146	            priority: 400,
   147	            createdTick: 2,
   148	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   149	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
   150	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.MakeAvailable(supplyJobId, 2).IsSuccess);
   151	        Assert.True(harness.Supply.ReserveIncoming(
   152	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   153	            supplyJobId,
   154	            new[] { new ItemConsumptionRequest(CampfireProductionContent.StoneItemId, 2) },
   155	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation().StockRules.ToDictionary(
   156	                value => value.ItemId,
   157	                _ => 0),
   158	            2).IsSuccess);
   159	
   160	        SaveGameDocument document = Builder().Build(new SaveGameContext(
   161	            Metadata("supply"),
   162	            CreateWorld(),
   163	            harness.Inventory,
   164	            harness.Jobs,
   165	            harness.Buildings,
   166	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
   167	            production: harness.Production,
   168	            buildingSupply: harness.Supply));
   169	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = Loader().Load(
   170	            document,
   171	            CreateMaterials(),
   172	            harness.Items,
   173	            harness.BuildingsCatalog,
   174	            terrainDepositCatalog: null,
   175	            mushroomCatalog: null,
   176	            harness.Content);
   177	
   178	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
   179	        BuildingSupplySnapshot supply = loaded.Value.BuildingSupply.Get(
   180	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   181	            loaded.Value.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
   182	        Assert.Equal(supplyJobId, supply.ActiveSupplyJobId);
   183	        Assert.Equal(2, supply.Stocks.Single(value =>
   184	            value.ItemId == CampfireProductionContent.StoneItemId).Incoming);
   185	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition restored = Assert.IsType<BuildingSupplyJobDefinition>(
   186	            loaded.Value.Jobs.Get(supplyJobId)!.Definition);
   187	        Assert.Equal(allocation, Assert.Single(restored.Allocations));
   188	        Assert.Equal(transitId, Assert.Single(restored.TransitStackIds));
   189	        Assert.Equal(depositId, Assert.Single(restored.DepositStackIds));
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    private static SaveGameBuilder Builder()
   193	    {
   194	        return new SaveGameBuilder(Registry());
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    private static SaveGameLoader Loader()
   198	    {
   199	        return new SaveGameLoader(
   200	            new SaveMigrationPipeline(Array.Empty<ISaveMigration>()),
   201	            Registry());
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry Registry()
   205	    {
   206	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   207	        {
   208	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec(),
   209	            new ProductionWorkJobSaveCodec(),
   210	            new BuildingSupplyJobSaveCodec(),
   211	        });
   212	    }
   213	
   214	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   215	    {
   216	        return new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   217	        {
   218	            new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   219	        });
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    private static WorldState CreateWorld()
   223	    {
   224	        return WorldState.CreateFilled(
   225	            new WorldSize(8, 8),
   226	            chunkSize: 4,
   227	            CreateMaterials(),
   228	            Air,
   229	            explored: true).Value;
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata(string slot)
   233	    {
   234	        return new SaveMetadataData
   235	        {
   236	            SlotId = slot,
   237	            DisplayName = slot,
   238	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-27T18:30:00Z",
   239	            SimulationTick = 10,
   240	            WorldSeed = 7,
   241	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   242	        };
   243	    }
   244	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingSupplyApplicationTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     3	using Dig.Application.Production;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Production;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BuildingSupplyApplicationTests
    15	{
    16	    [Fact]
    17	    public void Resident_collects_mixed_partial_load_and_deposits_separate_stacks()
    18	    {
    19	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness();
    20	        CellId capCell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    21	        CellId legCell = new CellId(2, 1, 0);
    22	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
    23	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(200),
    24	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    25	            4,
    26	            ItemLocation.InWorld(capCell),
    27	            0);
    28	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
    29	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(201),
    30	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId,
    31	            4,
    32	            ItemLocation.InWorld(legCell),
    33	            0);
    34	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(202);
    35	        EntityId capDeposit = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(220);
    36	        EntityId legDeposit = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(221);
    37	        CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler create = new CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler(
    38	            harness.Content,
    39	            harness.SupplyRepository,
    40	            harness.ProductionRepository,
    41	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
    42	            harness.InventoryRepository,
    43	            harness.JobsRepository,
    44	            harness.Journal);
    45	
    46	        Assert.True(create.Handle(new CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand(
    47	            jobId,
    48	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
    49	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId,
    50	            new[] { capCell, legCell },
    51	            new[] { capCell, legCell, harness.Buildings.Get(
    52	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition },
    53	            Enumerable.Range(210, 6).Select(CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id).ToArray(),
    54	            new[] { capDeposit, legDeposit },
    55	            priority: 500,
    56	            tick: 1)).IsSuccess);
    57	        Assert.True(new AcquireBuildingSupplyHandler(
    58	            harness.InventoryRepository,
    59	            harness.JobsRepository,
    60	            harness.Journal).Handle(new AcquireBuildingSupplyCommand(jobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
    61	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.TravelToDestination, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
    62	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.AdvanceStage(jobId, 3).IsSuccess);
    63	        Assert.True(new DepositBuildingSupplyHandler(
    64	            harness.SupplyRepository,
    65	            harness.InventoryRepository,
    66	            harness.JobsRepository,
    67	            harness.Journal).Handle(new DepositBuildingSupplyCommand(jobId, 4)).IsSuccess);
    68	
    69	        Assert.Equal(4, harness.Inventory.GetAvailableQuantityAt(
    70	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    71	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)));
    72	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetAvailableQuantityAt(
    73	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId,
    74	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)));
    75	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetStack(
    76	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(201))!.AvailableQuantity);
    77	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId),
    78	            harness.Inventory.GetStack(capDeposit)!.Location);
    79	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId),
    80	            harness.Inventory.GetStack(legDeposit)!.Location);
    81	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Status);
    82	        Assert.False(harness.Supply.Get(
    83	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
    84	            harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!.HasActiveSupply);
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    [Fact]
    88	    public void Resident_acquires_mixed_supply_at_each_world_source_before_travel()
    89	    {
    90	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness();
    91	        CellId capCell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    92	        CellId legCell = new CellId(2, 1, 0);
    93	        EntityId capSource = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(240);
    94	        EntityId legSource = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(241);
    95	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(242);
    96	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
    97	            capSource,
    98	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    99	            4,
   100	            ItemLocation.InWorld(capCell),
   101	            0);
   102	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   103	            legSource,
   104	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId,
   105	            4,
   106	            ItemLocation.InWorld(legCell),
   107	            0);
   108	        CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler create = new CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler(
   109	            harness.Content,
   110	            harness.SupplyRepository,
   111	            harness.ProductionRepository,
   112	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   113	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   114	            harness.JobsRepository,
   115	            harness.Journal);
   116	        Assert.True(create.Handle(new CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand(
   117	            jobId,
   118	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   119	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId,
   120	            new[] { capCell, legCell },
   121	            new[] { capCell, legCell, harness.Buildings.Get(
   122	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition },
   123	            Enumerable.Range(250, 6).Select(CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id).ToArray(),
   124	            new[]
   125	            {
   126	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(260),
   127	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(261),
   128	            },
   129	            priority: 500,
   130	            tick: 1)).IsSuccess);
   131	        AcquireBuildingSupplySourceHandler acquire = new AcquireBuildingSupplySourceHandler(
   132	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   133	            harness.JobsRepository,
   134	            harness.Journal);
   135	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Claimed, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Status);
   136	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Start(jobId, 2).IsSuccess);
   137	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.TravelToTarget, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
   138	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.AdvanceStage(jobId, 2).IsSuccess);
   139	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.AcquireItem, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
   140	
   141	        Result firstAcquire = acquire.Handle(new AcquireBuildingSupplySourceCommand(
   142	            jobId,
   143	            capSource,
   144	            2));
   145	        Assert.True(firstAcquire.IsSuccess, firstAcquire.Error?.ToString());
   146	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Status);
   147	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.AcquireItem, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
   148	        Assert.Null(harness.Inventory.GetStack(capSource));
   149	        Assert.NotNull(harness.Inventory.GetStack(legSource));
   150	        Assert.NotEmpty(harness.Inventory.GetResidentSlotClaims(jobId));
   151	
   152	        Result secondAcquire = acquire.Handle(new AcquireBuildingSupplySourceCommand(
   153	            jobId,
   154	            legSource,
   155	            3));
   156	        Assert.True(secondAcquire.IsSuccess, secondAcquire.Error?.ToString());
   157	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.TravelToDestination, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
   158	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.GetResidentSlotClaims(jobId));
   159	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetStack(legSource)!.AvailableQuantity);
   160	    }
   161	
   162	
   163	    [Fact]
   164	    public void Direct_internal_pickup_creates_replacement_supply_demand()
   165	    {
   166	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness();
   167	        EntityId internalStack = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(300);
   168	        EntityId pickupJob = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(301);
   169	        EntityId carriedStack = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(302);
   170	        ItemId cap = Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId;
   171	        Assert.True(harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   172	            internalStack,
   173	            cap,
   174	            4,
   175	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId),
   176	            0).IsSuccess);
   177	        CellId work = harness.Buildings.Get(
   178	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition;
   179	        CreateWorldItemPickupHandler createPickup = new CreateWorldItemPickupHandler(
   180	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   181	            harness.JobsRepository,
   182	            harness.Journal);
   183	        Assert.True(createPickup.Handle(new CreateWorldItemPickupCommand(
   184	            pickupJob,
   185	            internalStack,
   186	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId,
   187	            work,
   188	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId),
   189	            quantity: 1,
   190	            destinationStackId: carriedStack,
   191	            priority: 675,
   192	            tick: 1)).IsSuccess);
   193	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Start(pickupJob, 2).IsSuccess);
   194	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.AdvanceStage(pickupJob, 3).IsSuccess);
   195	        Assert.True(new CompleteWorldItemPickupHandler(
   196	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   197	            harness.JobsRepository,
   198	            harness.Journal).Handle(new CompleteWorldItemPickupCommand(
   199	                pickupJob,
   200	                4)).IsSuccess);
   201	
   202	        BuildingSupplySnapshot supply = harness.Supply.Get(
   203	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   204	            harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
   205	        Assert.Equal(1, supply.Stocks.Single(value => value.ItemId == cap).Missing);
   206	
   207	        CellId sourceCell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
   208	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   209	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(303),
   210	            cap,
   211	            1,
   212	            ItemLocation.InWorld(sourceCell),
   213	            5);
   214	        Result replacement = new CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler(
   215	            harness.Content,
   216	            harness.SupplyRepository,
   217	            harness.ProductionRepository,
   218	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   219	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   220	            harness.JobsRepository,
   221	            harness.Journal).Handle(new CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand(
   222	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(304),
   223	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   224	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId,
   225	                new[] { sourceCell },
   226	                new[] { sourceCell, work },
   227	                new[] { CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(305) },
   228	                new[] { CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(306) },
   229	                priority: 500,
   230	                tick: 6));
   231	        Assert.True(replacement.IsSuccess, replacement.Error?.ToString());
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    [Fact]
   235	    public void Cancelling_uncollected_supply_releases_all_reservations_and_incoming()
   236	    {
   237	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness();
   238	        CellId cell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
   239	        EntityId source = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(230);
   240	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(231);
   241	        harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   242	            source,
   243	            Dig.Domain.Content.CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
   244	            4,
   245	            ItemLocation.InWorld(cell),
   246	            0);
   247	        CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler create = new CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler(
   248	            harness.Content,
   249	            harness.SupplyRepository,
   250	            harness.ProductionRepository,
   251	            harness.BuildingsRepository,
   252	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   253	            harness.JobsRepository,
   254	            harness.Journal);
   255	        Assert.True(create.Handle(new CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand(
   256	            jobId,
   257	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   258	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId,
   259	            new[] { cell },
   260	            new[] { cell, harness.Buildings.Get(
   261	                CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId)!.WorkPosition },
   262	            Enumerable.Range(232, 4)
   263	                .Select(CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id)
   264	                .ToArray(),
   265	            new[] { CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(236) },
   266	            500,
   267	            1)).IsSuccess);
   268	
   269	        Assert.True(new CancelBuildingSupplyHandler(
   270	            harness.SupplyRepository,
   271	            harness.InventoryRepository,
   272	            harness.JobsRepository,
   273	            harness.Journal).Handle(new CancelBuildingSupplyCommand(
   274	                jobId,
   275	                "player_cancelled",
   276	                2)).IsSuccess);
   277	
   278	        Assert.Equal(4, harness.Inventory.GetStack(source)!.AvailableQuantity);
   279	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.GetResidentSlotClaims(jobId));
   280	        Assert.False(harness.Supply.Get(
   281	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   282	            harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!.HasActiveSupply);
   283	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Cancelled, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Status);
   284	    }
   285	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingSupplyStateTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Production;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class BuildingSupplyStateTests
    14	{
    15	    private static readonly EntityId BuildingId = Id(1);
    16	
    17	    [Fact]
    18	    public void Planner_builds_deterministic_mixed_partial_load()
    19	    {
    20	        ItemCatalog items = CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems();
    21	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    22	        CellId capCell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    23	        CellId legCell = new CellId(2, 1, 0);
    24	        CellId stoneCell = new CellId(3, 1, 0);
    25	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    26	            Id(10),
    27	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    28	            4,
    29	            ItemLocation.InWorld(capCell),
    30	            0).IsSuccess);
    31	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    32	            Id(11),
    33	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId,
    34	            4,
    35	            ItemLocation.InWorld(legCell),
    36	            0).IsSuccess);
    37	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    38	            Id(12),
    39	            CampfireProductionContent.StoneItemId,
    40	            4,
    41	            ItemLocation.InWorld(stoneCell),
    42	            0).IsSuccess);
    43	        BuildingSupplyState state = new BuildingSupplyState();
    44	        Assert.True(state.Register(
    45	            BuildingId,
    46	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation(),
    47	            0).IsSuccess);
    48	        BuildingSupplySnapshot supply = state.Get(
    49	            BuildingId,
    50	            inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
    51	
    52	        BuildingSupplyPlan plan = BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
    53	            supply,
    54	            inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
    55	            new[] { capCell, legCell, stoneCell },
    56	            new[] { capCell, legCell, stoneCell },
    57	            new CellId(4, 1, 0),
    58	            freeSlotCount: 6,
    59	            productionActive: false);
    60	
    61	        Assert.Equal(2, plan.Allocations.Count);
    62	        Assert.Equal(6, plan.SlotCount);
    63	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, plan.Allocations[0].ItemId);
    64	        Assert.Equal(4, plan.Allocations[0].Quantity);
    65	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId, plan.Allocations[1].ItemId);
    66	        Assert.Equal(2, plan.Allocations[1].Quantity);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    [Fact]
    70	    public void Hidden_disabled_or_active_production_suppresses_supply()
    71	    {
    72	        ItemCatalog items = CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems();
    73	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    74	        CellId cell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    75	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    76	            Id(20),
    77	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    78	            4,
    79	            ItemLocation.InWorld(cell),
    80	            0).IsSuccess);
    81	        BuildingSupplyState state = new BuildingSupplyState();
    82	        state.Register(BuildingId, CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation(), 0);
    83	        state.SetDeliveryEnabled(
    84	            BuildingId,
    85	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    86	            enabled: false,
    87	            tick: 1);
    88	        BuildingSupplySnapshot supply = state.Get(BuildingId, inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
    89	
    90	        Assert.Empty(BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
    91	            supply,
    92	            inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
    93	            new[] { cell },
    94	            new[] { cell },
    95	            cell,
    96	            4,
    97	            false).Allocations);
    98	
    99	        state.SetDeliveryEnabled(
   100	            BuildingId,
   101	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
   102	            enabled: true,
   103	            tick: 2);
   104	        supply = state.Get(BuildingId, inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
   105	        Assert.Empty(BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
   106	            supply,
   107	            inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
   108	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
   109	            new[] { cell },
   110	            cell,
   111	            4,
   112	            false).Allocations);
   113	        Assert.Empty(BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
   114	            supply,
   115	            inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
   116	            new[] { cell },
   117	            new[] { cell },
   118	            cell,
   119	            4,
   120	            productionActive: true).Allocations);
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    [Fact]
   124	    public void Registered_workstation_inventory_is_protected_automatic_source()
   125	    {
   126	        BuildingSupplyState state = new BuildingSupplyState();
   127	        state.Register(BuildingId, CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation(), 0);
   128	
   129	        Assert.True(state.IsProtectedAutomaticSource(ItemLocation.InBuilding(BuildingId)));
   130	        Assert.False(state.IsProtectedAutomaticSource(ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(0, 0))));
   131	    }

   133	    private static EntityId Id(int value)
   134	    {
   135	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BuildingWorldPresenterTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BuildingWorldPresenterTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly ItemId BoxItemId = new ItemId("building_box.world_presenter");
    17	
    18	    [Fact]
    19	    public void Active_buildings_are_sorted_and_removed_buildings_are_not_rendered()
    20	    {
    21	        BuildingWorldPresenter presenter = new BuildingWorldPresenter(
    22	            new BuildingFunctionsPresenter());
    23	        BuildingSnapshot later = Snapshot(Id(2), BuildingStatus.Completed);
    24	        BuildingSnapshot removed = Snapshot(Id(3), BuildingStatus.Removed);
    25	        BuildingSnapshot first = Snapshot(Id(1), BuildingStatus.Completed);
    26	
    27	        var models = presenter.Load(new[] { later, removed, first });
    28	
    29	        Assert.Equal(2, models.Count);
    30	        Assert.Equal(Id(1).ToString(), models[0].Id);
    31	        Assert.Equal(Id(2).ToString(), models[1].Id);
    32	        Assert.All(models, model => Assert.True(model.IsSelectable));
    33	        Assert.Equal("Box Workshop", models[0].Name);
    34	        Assert.Single(models[0].Footprint);
    35	        Assert.True(models[0].Functions.Actions[0].IsEnabled);
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    [Fact]
    39	    public void Projection_preserves_orientation_work_position_and_assembly_progress()
    40	    {
    41	        BuildingWorldPresenter presenter = new BuildingWorldPresenter(
    42	            new BuildingFunctionsPresenter());
    43	        BuildingSnapshot source = Snapshot(
    44	            Id(1),
    45	            BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction,
    46	            orientation: BuildingOrientation.East,
    47	            completedWork: 1);
    48	
    49	        BuildingWorldViewModel model = Assert.Single(presenter.Load(new[] { source }));
    50	
    51	        Assert.Equal(BuildingOrientation.East, model.Orientation);
    52	        Assert.Equal(source.WorkPosition.X, model.WorkPositionX);
    53	        Assert.Equal(source.WorkPosition.Y, model.WorkPositionY);
    54	        Assert.Equal(1d / 3d, model.AssemblyProgress, precision: 6);
    55	        Assert.Equal(BuildingVisualState.Assembly, model.VisualState);
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    [Fact]
    59	    public void Pending_box_plan_projects_source_stack_job_and_transformation_identity()
    60	    {

    62	            new BuildingFunctionsPresenter());

    73	        Assert.Equal(BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved, model.BuildingBoxCommitState);
    74	        Assert.True(model.HasBuildingBoxLifecycle);
    75	        Assert.True(model.IsPendingBuildingBoxLifecycle);
    76	    }

    78	    [Fact]
    79	    public void Packing_state_overrides_completed_visual_state()
    80	    {
    81	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot active = new BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot(

   117	    [Fact]
   118	    public void Enabled_pack_action_dispatches_exactly_one_typed_command()
   119	    {
   120	        RecordingPackingHandler handler = new RecordingPackingHandler();
   121	        BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter adapter = new BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter(
   122	            new BuildingFunctionsPresenter(),
   123	            handler);
   124	        EntityId jobId = Id(10);
   125	        EntityId outputId = Id(11);
   126	
   127	        Result result = adapter.StartPacking(
   128	            Snapshot(Id(1), BuildingStatus.Completed),
   129	            jobId,
   130	            outputId,
   131	            priority: 640,
   132	            tick: 77);
   133	
   134	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
   135	        Assert.Equal(1, handler.CallCount);
   136	        Assert.NotNull(handler.LastCommand);
   137	        Assert.Equal(Id(1), handler.LastCommand!.BuildingId);
   138	        Assert.Equal(jobId, handler.LastCommand.JobId);
   139	        Assert.Equal(outputId, handler.LastCommand.OutputStackId);
   140	        Assert.Equal(640, handler.LastCommand.Priority);
   141	        Assert.Equal(77, handler.LastCommand.Tick);
   142	    }

   144	    [Fact]
   145	    public void Disabled_pack_action_emits_no_application_command()
   146	    {
   147	        RecordingPackingHandler handler = new RecordingPackingHandler();
   148	        BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter adapter = new BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter(
   149	            new BuildingFunctionsPresenter(),
   150	            handler);
   151	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot active = new BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot(
   152	            Id(20),
   153	            Id(21),
   154	            completedWork: 0,
   155	            commitState: BuildingPackingCommitState.Active);
   156	
   157	        Result result = adapter.StartPacking(
   158	            Snapshot(Id(1), BuildingStatus.Completed, active),
   159	            Id(22),
   160	            Id(23),
   161	            priority: 500,
   162	            tick: 8);
   163	
   164	        Assert.True(result.IsFailure);
   165	        Assert.Equal(BuildingFunctionsCommandErrors.ActionUnavailable, result.Error);
   166	        Assert.Equal(0, handler.CallCount);
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    private static BuildingSnapshot Snapshot(
   170	        EntityId id,
   171	        BuildingStatus status,
   172	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? packingPlan = null,
   173	        BuildingOrientation orientation = BuildingOrientation.North,
   174	        int? completedWork = null,
   175	        BuildingBoxCommitState boxCommitState = BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed)
   176	    {
   177	        BuildingDefinition definition = new BuildingDefinition(
   178	            new BuildingDefinitionId("box.world_presenter"),
   179	            "Box Workshop",
   180	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0) },
   181	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, -1) },
   182	            Array.Empty<BuildingMaterialRequirement>(),
   183	            requiredWork: 3,
   184	            maximumDurability: 100,
   185	            boxPolicy: new BuildingBoxPolicy(BoxItemId, packingWork: 2));
   186	        CellId origin = new CellId(id == Id(1) ? 2 : 5, 4);
   187	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   188	            id,
   189	            definition,
   190	            origin,
   191	            orientation,
   192	            definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, orientation),
   193	            definition.ResolveWorkPositions(origin, orientation)[0],
   194	            status,
   195	            completedWork ?? definition.RequiredWork,
   196	            durability: definition.MaximumDurability,
   197	            version: 1,
   198	            diagnosticReason: null,
   199	            boxPlan: new BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot(
   200	                Id(30 + origin.X),
   201	                Id(40 + origin.X),
   202	                boxCommitState),
   203	            packingPlan: packingPlan);
   204	    }
   205	
   206	    private static EntityId Id(int value)
   207	    {
   208	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    private sealed class RecordingPackingHandler
   212	        : ICommandHandler<StartBuildingBoxPackingCommand, Result>
   213	    {
   214	        public int CallCount { get; private set; }
   215	
   216	        public StartBuildingBoxPackingCommand? LastCommand { get; private set; }
   217	
   218	        public Result Handle(StartBuildingBoxPackingCommand command)
   219	        {
   220	            CallCount++;
   221	            LastCommand = command;
   222	            return Result.Success();
   223	        }
   224	    }
   225	}
   226	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/CampfireFoodSaveTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     3	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    10	using Dig.Domain.World;
    11	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    12	using Xunit;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Tests
    15	{
    16	
    17	public sealed class CampfireFoodSaveTests
    18	{
    19	    private static readonly MaterialId Rock = new MaterialId("terrain.rock");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId ResidentId = Id(1);
    21	    private static readonly EntityId StackId = Id(2);
    22	    private static readonly EntityId JobId = Id(3);
    23	
    24	    [Fact]
    25	    public void Builder_loader_round_trip_preserves_active_meal_and_applied_bite()
    26	    {
    27	        MaterialCatalog materials = CreateMaterials();
    28	        ItemCatalog items = CreateItems();
    29	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    30	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    31	            StackId,
    32	            CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomItemId,
    33	            1,
    34	            ItemLocation.InAgent(ResidentId),
    35	            tick: 1).IsSuccess);
    36	        InMemoryAgentRepository agents = new InMemoryAgentRepository();
    37	        AgentState resident = CreateResident(nutrition: 1_000);
    38	        Assert.True(agents.Add(resident).IsSuccess);
    39	        Assert.True(new StartResidentFoodMealHandler(
    40	            agents,
    41	            new InMemoryInventoryRepository(inventory),
    42	            new InMemoryExecutionJournal()).Handle(
    43	                new StartResidentFoodMealCommand(ResidentId, StackId, tick: 10)).IsSuccess);
    44	        Assert.False(resident.AdvanceFoodMealBite(11).Value);
    45	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
    46	            new WorldSize(4, 4),
    47	            chunkSize: 2,
    48	            materials,
    49	            Rock,
    50	            explored: true).Value;
    51	        JobDefinitionSaveRegistry registry = new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(
    52	            new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[] { new WorldItemPickupJobSaveCodec() });
    53	        SaveGameDocument document = new SaveGameBuilder(registry).Build(
    54	            new SaveGameContext(
    55	                Metadata(),
    56	                world,
    57	                inventory,
    58	                new JobSystem(),
    59	                new BuildingsState(),
    60	                new[] { resident }));
    61	
    62	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = new SaveGameLoader(
    63	            new SaveMigrationPipeline(Array.Empty<ISaveMigration>()),
    64	            registry).Load(document, materials, items);
    65	
    66	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    67	        AgentRuntimeSnapshot runtime = loaded.Value.AgentRuntime[ResidentId];
    68	        Assert.Equal(1_500, runtime.Needs.Nutrition.Points);
    69	        Assert.Equal(10, runtime.MealStartedTick);
    70	        Assert.Equal(1, runtime.ActiveMeal!.CompletedBites);
    71	        Assert.Equal(2, runtime.ActiveMeal.RemainingBites);
    72	        Assert.Empty(loaded.Value.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    [Fact]
    76	    public void Pickup_codec_round_trips_use_action_and_old_payload_defaults_to_none()
    77	    {
    78	        WorldItemPickupJobSaveCodec codec = new WorldItemPickupJobSaveCodec();
    79	        WorldItemPickupJobDefinition definition = new WorldItemPickupJobDefinition(
    80	            JobId,
    81	            StackId,
    82	            quantity: 1,
    83	            new CellId(2, 2, 0),
    84	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(2, 2, 0)),
    85	            destinationStackId: default,
    86	            priority: 700,
    87	            createdTick: 5,
    88	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default,
    89	            completionAction: WorldItemPickupCompletionAction.UseConsumable);
    90	
    91	        JobDefinitionSaveData encoded = codec.Encode(definition);
    92	        WorldItemPickupJobDefinition restored =
    93	            (WorldItemPickupJobDefinition)codec.Decode(encoded);
    94	        Assert.Equal(
    95	            WorldItemPickupCompletionAction.UseConsumable,
    96	            restored.CompletionAction);
    97	
    98	        encoded.Properties.RemoveAll(value => value.Key == "completion_action");
    99	        WorldItemPickupJobDefinition legacy =
   100	            (WorldItemPickupJobDefinition)codec.Decode(encoded);
   101	        Assert.Equal(WorldItemPickupCompletionAction.None, legacy.CompletionAction);
   102	    }
   103

   112	                new NeedValue(10_000),
   113	                new NeedValue(10_000)),
   114	            new DailySchedule(
   115	                ticksPerDay: 24,
   116	                new[] { new ScheduleSegment(0, 24, ScheduleActivity.Work) }));
   117	    }

   127	            WorldSeed = 7,
   128	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   129	        };
   130	    }

   100	        private static string Read(string root, string file)
   101	        {
   102	            return File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root, file));
   103	        }
   104	
   105	        private static string RuntimeRoot()
   106	        {
   107	            return Path.Combine(
   108	                FindRepositoryRoot(),
   109	                "unity",
   110	                "Dig.Unity",
   111	                "Assets",
   112	                "Dig.Unity",
   113	                "Runtime");
   114	        }
   115	
   116	        private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   117	        {
   118	            DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   119	            while (current != null)
   120	            {
   121	                if (Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "src"))
   122	                    && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "unity")))
   123	                {
   124	                    return current.FullName;
   125	                }
   126	
   127	                current = current.Parent;
   128	            }
   129	
   130	            throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   131	        }
   132	    }

===== tests/Dig.Tests/CampfireProductionContentTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class CampfireProductionContentTests
    14	{
    15	    [Fact]
    16	    public void Campfire_workstation_is_fully_data_driven()
    17	    {
    18	        ItemCatalog items = CreateItems();
    19	        BuildingCatalog buildings = new BuildingCatalog(
    20	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateBuildings());
    21	        ContentValidationResult result = ProductionContentCatalog.ValidateAndCreate(
    22	            items,
    23	            buildings,
    24	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(
    25	                CampfireProductionContent.TestProductionMaterialTicks),
    26	            Array.Empty<TechnologyDefinition>(),
    27	            new[] { CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation() });
    28	
    29	        Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("\n", result.Issues));
    30	        ProductionContentCatalog catalog = result.Catalog!;
    31	        ProductionWorkstationDefinition workstation = catalog.GetWorkstation(
    32	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.CampfireBuildingId);
    33	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.AnimationProfileId, workstation.AnimationProfileId);
    34	        Assert.Equal(6, workstation.RecipeIds.Count);
    35	        Assert.Equal(new[] { 4, 4, 4, 2 }, workstation.StockRules
    36	            .OrderByDescending(value => value.Priority)
    37	            .Select(value => value.Capacity));
    38	        Assert.All(workstation.StockRules, value => Assert.True(value.DefaultDeliveryEnabled));
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    [Theory]
    42	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.building.tent", "building_box.tent", 1, 3)]
    43	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.building.stone_mason", "building_box.stone_mason", 1, 6)]
    44	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.building.wood_workshop", "building_box.wood_workshop", 1, 5)]
    45	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.building.campfire", "building_box.campfire", 1, 4)]
    46	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.food.grilled_mushroom", "food.grilled_mushroom", 2, 1)]
    47	    [InlineData("recipe.campfire.food.roasted_hamster", "food.roasted_hamster", 2, 1)]
    48	    public void Campfire_recipe_outputs_and_steps_match_content(
    49	        string recipeId,
    50	        string outputItemId,
    51	        int outputQuantity,
    52	        int stepCount)
    53	    {
    54	        RecipeDefinition recipe = CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(1)
    55	            .Single(value => value.Id == new RecipeId(recipeId));
    56	
    57	        Assert.Equal(new ItemId(outputItemId), Assert.Single(recipe.Outputs).ItemId);
    58	        Assert.Equal(outputQuantity, Assert.Single(recipe.Outputs).Quantity);
    59	        Assert.Equal(stepCount, recipe.MaterialSteps.Count);
    60	        Assert.Equal(ProductionSkillGrantScale.PerOrder, recipe.SkillGrantScale);
    61	    }

    63	    [Fact]
    64	    public void Grilled_mushroom_consumes_one_cap_creates_two_food_units()
    65	    {
    66	        RecipeDefinition recipe = CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(
    67	                CampfireProductionContent.ProductionMaterialTicks)
    68	            .Single(value => value.Id == CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId);
    69	        ContentItemQuantity input = Assert.Single(recipe.Inputs);
    70	        ContentItemQuantity output = Assert.Single(recipe.Outputs);
    71	        ItemDefinition item = CreateItems().Get(output.ItemId);
    72	
    73	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, input.ItemId);
    74	        Assert.Equal(1, input.Quantity);
    75	        Assert.Equal(CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomItemId, output.ItemId);
    76	        Assert.Equal(2, output.Quantity);
    77	        Assert.Contains(CampfireProductionContent.FoodCategoryId, item.Categories);
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    [Fact]
    81	    public void Material_skills_and_order_grants_match_balance()
    82	    {
    83	        IReadOnlyList<RecipeDefinition> recipes =
    84	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(100);
    85	        RecipeDefinition stoneMason = recipes.Single(value =>
    86	            value.Id == CampfireProductionContent.StoneMasonRecipeId);
    87	        Assert.Equal(2, stoneMason.MaterialSteps.Count(value =>
    88	            value.SkillId == AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking));
    89	        Assert.Equal(4, stoneMason.MaterialSteps.Count(value =>
    90	            value.SkillId == AgentSkillCatalog.Stonework));
    91	        Assert.Equal(100, stoneMason.SkillGrantProfile!.PerUnit.Single(value =>
    92	            value.SkillId == AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking).RequestedUnits);
    93	        Assert.Equal(300, stoneMason.SkillGrantProfile.PerUnit.Single(value =>
    94	            value.SkillId == AgentSkillCatalog.Stonework).RequestedUnits);
    95	
    96	        RecipeDefinition grilled = recipes.Single(value =>
    97	            value.Id == CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId);
    98	        Assert.Equal(AgentSkillCatalog.Cooking, Assert.Single(grilled.MaterialSteps).SkillId);
    99	        Assert.Equal(120, Assert.Single(grilled.SkillGrantProfile!.PerUnit).RequestedUnits);
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    [Theory]
   103	    [InlineData(0, 900)]
   104	    [InlineData(25, 675)]
   105	    [InlineData(50, 450)]
   106	    [InlineData(100, 1)]
   107	    public void Grilled_mushroom_duration_is_fifteen_minutes_minus_cooking_percent(
   108	        int skillPoints,
   109	        long expectedTicks)
   110	    {
   111	        Assert.Equal(
   112	            expectedTicks,
   113	            ProductionStepTiming.ResolveDurationTicks(
   114	                CampfireProductionContent.ProductionMaterialTicks,
   115	                skillPoints * AgentSkillCatalog.UnitsPerPoint));
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    internal static ItemCatalog CreateItems()
   119	    {
   120	        List<ItemDefinition> values = CampfireProductionContent.CreateItems().ToList();
   121	        values.Add(CampfireBuildingBoxContent.Definition.BoxItem);
   122	        return new ItemCatalog(values);
   123	    }
   124	}
   125	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/CampfireProductionTestHarness.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Application.Production;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Content;

    13	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;

    24	internal sealed class CampfireProductionTestHarness
    25	{
    26	    public static readonly EntityId BuildingId = Id(1);
    27	    public static readonly EntityId WorkerId = Id(2);
    28	
    29	    public CampfireProductionTestHarness(long materialTicks = 100)
    30	    {
    31	        Items = CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems();
    32	        BuildingsCatalog = new BuildingCatalog(CampfireProductionContent.CreateBuildings());
    33	        Content = ProductionContentCatalog.ValidateAndCreate(
    34	            Items,
    35	            BuildingsCatalog,
    36	            CampfireProductionContent.CreateRecipes(materialTicks),
    37	            Array.Empty<TechnologyDefinition>(),
    38	            new[] { CampfireProductionContent.CreateWorkstation() }).Catalog!;
    39	        Inventory = new InventoryState(Items);
    40	        Jobs = new JobSystem();
    41	        Buildings = CreateCompletedCampfire();
    42	        Production = new ProductionState();
    43	        Supply = new BuildingSupplyState();
    44	        Supply.Register(BuildingId, Content.GetWorkstation(
    45	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.CampfireBuildingId), 0);
    46	        Agents = new InMemoryAgentRepository();
    47	        Assert.True(Agents.Add(AgentTestFactory.CreateAgent(id: WorkerId)).IsSuccess);
    48	        Journal = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
    49	        ProductionRepository = new InMemoryProductionRepository(Production);
    50	        SupplyRepository = new InMemoryBuildingSupplyRepository(Supply);
    51	        InventoryRepository = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(Inventory);
    52	        BuildingsRepository = new InMemoryBuildingsRepository(Buildings);
    53	        JobsRepository = new InMemoryJobRepository(Jobs);
    54	        TechnologyRepository = new InMemoryTechnologyRepository(new TechnologyState());
    55	        SkillGrants = new AgentSkillGrantService(Agents, Journal);
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    public ItemCatalog Items { get; }
    59	    public BuildingCatalog BuildingsCatalog { get; }
    60	    public ProductionContentCatalog Content { get; }
    61	    public InventoryState Inventory { get; }
    62	    public BuildingsState Buildings { get; }
    63	    public ProductionState Production { get; }
    64	    public BuildingSupplyState Supply { get; }
    65	    public JobSystem Jobs { get; }
    66	    public InMemoryAgentRepository Agents { get; }
