    69	    public InMemoryBuildingSupplyRepository SupplyRepository { get; }
    70	    public InMemoryInventoryRepository InventoryRepository { get; }
    71	    public InMemoryBuildingsRepository BuildingsRepository { get; }
    72	    public InMemoryJobRepository JobsRepository { get; }
    73	    public InMemoryTechnologyRepository TechnologyRepository { get; }
    74	    public AgentSkillGrantService SkillGrants { get; }

    76	    public void AddBuildingStock(ItemId itemId, int quantity, int id)
    77	    {
    78	        Assert.True(Inventory.AddStack(
    79	            Id(id),
    80	            itemId,
    81	            quantity,
    82	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(BuildingId),
    83	            0).IsSuccess);
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    public void GrantSkill(AgentSkillId skillId, int points, long tick = 0)
    87	    {
    88	        Result result = Agents.Get(WorkerId)!.ApplySkillGrant(new SkillGrantBundle(
    89	            WorkerId,
    90	            SkillGrantSourceKind.TrainingCompleted,
    91	            $"test:{skillId}:{points}",
    92	            tick,
    93	            new[] { new SkillGrant(skillId, points * AgentSkillCatalog.UnitsPerPoint) }));
    94	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    public Result Enqueue(EntityId orderId, RecipeId recipeId, long tick)
    98	    {

   100	            new EnqueueProductionOrderCommand(orderId, recipeId, BuildingId, tick));
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    public Result Prepare(EntityId jobId, long tick)
   104	    {

   106	            ProductionRepository,
   107	            TechnologyRepository,
   108	            BuildingsRepository,
   109	            InventoryRepository,
   110	            JobsRepository,
   111	            new FixedEnergyAvailability(true),
   112	            Journal).Handle(new PrepareProductionOrderCommand(
   113	                jobId,
   114	                BuildingId,
   115	                new[] { Buildings.Get(BuildingId)!.WorkPosition },
   116	                priority: 500,
   117	                tick));
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    public void ClaimBeginAndReachWork(EntityId orderId, EntityId jobId, long tick)
   121	    {
   122	        Assert.True(Jobs.Claim(jobId, WorkerId, tick).IsSuccess);
   123	        Assert.True(new BeginProductionWorkHandler(
   124	            ProductionRepository,
   125	            JobsRepository,
   126	            Agents,
   127	            Journal).Handle(new BeginProductionWorkCommand(
   128	                orderId,
   129	                jobId,
   130	                tick + 1)).IsSuccess);
   131	        Assert.True(Jobs.AdvanceStage(jobId, tick + 2).IsSuccess);
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    public Result Work(EntityId orderId, EntityId jobId, int elapsedTicks, long tick)
   135	    {
   136	        return new ApplyProductionWorkHandler(
   137	            ProductionRepository,
   138	            InventoryRepository,
   139	            JobsRepository,
   140	            Agents,
   141	            Journal).Handle(new ApplyProductionWorkCommand(
   142	                orderId,
   143	                jobId,
   144	                elapsedTicks,
   145	                conditionEfficiencyBasisPoints: 10_000,
   146	                tick));
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    public Result Complete(
   150	        EntityId orderId,
   151	        EntityId jobId,
   152	        EntityId outputId,
   153	        CellId outputCell,
   154	        long tick)
   155	    {
   156	        return new CompleteProductionOrderHandler(
   157	            ProductionRepository,
   158	            InventoryRepository,
   159	            JobsRepository,
   160	            Journal,
   161	            SkillGrants).Handle(new CompleteProductionOrderCommand(
   162	                orderId,
   163	                jobId,
   164	                new[] { outputId },
   165	                tick,
   166	                ItemLocation.InWorld(outputCell)));
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    public static EntityId Id(int value)
   170	    {
   171	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    private BuildingsState CreateCompletedCampfire()
   175	    {
   176	        BuildingDefinition definition = BuildingsCatalog.Get(
   177	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.CampfireBuildingId);
   178	        CellId origin = new CellId(4, 4, 0);
   179	        CellId work = new CellId(4, 3, 0);
   180	        EntityId sourceBoxId = Id(900);
   181	        EntityId assemblyJobId = Id(901);
   182	        BuildingsState buildings = new BuildingsState();
   183	        BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition jobDefinition =
   184	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition(
   185	                assemblyJobId,
   186	                BuildingId,
   187	                sourceBoxId,
   188	                origin,
   189	                work,
   190	                priority: 500,
   191	                createdTick: 0,
   192	                retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   193	        Assert.True(Jobs.Add(jobDefinition).IsSuccess);
   194	        Assert.True(Jobs.MakeAvailable(assemblyJobId, 0).IsSuccess);
   195	        Assert.True(Jobs.Claim(assemblyJobId, WorkerId, 0).IsSuccess);
   196	        Assert.True(Jobs.Start(assemblyJobId, 0).IsSuccess);
   197	
   198	        Result placed = buildings.PlaceBoxPlan(
   199	            BuildingId,
   200	            sourceBoxId,
   201	            assemblyJobId,
   202	            definition,
   203	            origin,
   204	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   205	            BuildingPlacementResult.Success(new[] { origin }, work),
   206	            0);
   207	        Assert.True(placed.IsSuccess, placed.Error?.ToString());
   208	        Assert.True(Jobs.AdvanceStage(assemblyJobId, 1).IsSuccess);
   209	        Assert.True(Jobs.AdvanceStage(assemblyJobId, 2).IsSuccess);
   210	        Result atSite = buildings.MarkBoxAtSite(BuildingId, 2);
   211	        Assert.True(atSite.IsSuccess, atSite.Error?.ToString());
   212	        Assert.True(Jobs.AdvanceStage(assemblyJobId, 3).IsSuccess);
   213	        Result started = buildings.StartConstruction(BuildingId);
   214	        Assert.True(started.IsSuccess, started.Error?.ToString());
   215	        Result progressed = buildings.AddConstructionWork(
   216	            BuildingId,
   217	            definition.RequiredWork,
   218	            4);
   219	        Assert.True(progressed.IsSuccess, progressed.Error?.ToString());
   220	        Assert.True(Jobs.AdvanceStage(assemblyJobId, 5).IsSuccess);
   221	        Result completed = buildings.CompleteBoxConstruction(BuildingId, 5);
   222	        Assert.True(completed.IsSuccess, completed.Error?.ToString());
   223	        Assert.True(Jobs.Complete(assemblyJobId, 5).IsSuccess);
   224	        buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents();
   225	        Jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents();
   226	        return buildings;
   227	    }
   228	}
   229	
   230	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/ContextInputRouterPriorityTests.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     2	using Dig.Domain.World;
     3	using Dig.Presentation.Input;
     4	using Xunit;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Tests
     7	{
     8	
     9	public sealed class ContextInputRouterPriorityTests
    10	{
    11	    private static readonly EntityId Resident = Id(1);
    12	    private static readonly EntityId Stack = Id(2);
    13	    private static readonly EntityId Target = Id(3);
    14	    private static readonly CellId Cell = new CellId(5, 6);
    15	    private readonly ContextInputRouter _router = new ContextInputRouter();
    16	
    17	    [Fact]
    18	    public void Valid_building_placement_has_highest_world_priority()
    19	    {
    20	        ContextInputDecision decision = WorldLeft(
    21	            new ContextInputState(
    22	                selectedResidentId: Resident,
    23	                selectedInventoryStackId: Stack,
    24	                buildingPlacementActive: true,
    25	                buildingPlacementValid: true,
    26	                excavationTool: ExcavationToolKind.Tunnel),
    27	            Ground(reachable: true));
    28	
    29	        AssertCommand(decision, ApplicationInputCommandKind.ConfirmBuildingPlacement);
    30	        Assert.Equal(Resident, decision.ActorId);
    31	        Assert.Equal(Stack, decision.TargetEntityId);
    32	        Assert.Equal(Cell, decision.TargetCell);
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    [Fact]
    36	    public void Invalid_building_placement_keeps_preview_and_emits_no_command()
    37	    {
    38	        ContextInputDecision decision = WorldLeft(
    39	            new ContextInputState(
    40	                selectedResidentId: Resident,
    41	                buildingPlacementActive: true,
    42	                buildingPlacementValid: false,
    43	                buildingPlacementReasonCode: "placement.cell_blocked"),
    44	            Ground(reachable: true));
    45	
    46	        Assert.False(decision.HasApplicationCommand);
    47	        Assert.True(decision.ConsumesPointer);
    48	        Assert.True(decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.KeepBuildingPreview));
    49	        Assert.True(decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.ShowReason));
    50	        Assert.Equal("placement.cell_blocked", decision.ReasonCode);
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    [Fact]
    54	    public void Alt_inventory_use_precedes_other_inventory_behavior()
    55	    {
    56	        ContextInputDecision decision = _router.Route(
    57	            new ContextPointerEvent(
    58	                PointerInputSurface.ResidentInventory,
    59	                PointerButtonKind.Left,
    60	                altPressed: true),
    61	            new ContextInputState(
    62	                selectedResidentId: Resident,
    63	                selectedInventoryStackId: Stack,
    64	                selectedInventoryItemUsable: true,
    65	                selectedInventoryItemIsBuildingBox: true,
    66	                canUseSelectedInventoryItem: true),
    67	            new ContextPointerTarget(ContextWorldTargetKind.GenericItem, Stack));
    68	
    69	        AssertCommand(decision, ApplicationInputCommandKind.UseInventoryItem);
    70	        Assert.Equal(Stack, decision.TargetEntityId);
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    [Fact]
    74	    public void Targeted_drop_precedes_resident_move()
    75	    {
    76	        ContextInputDecision decision = WorldLeft(
    77	            new ContextInputState(
    78	                selectedResidentId: Resident,
    79	                selectedInventoryStackId: Stack,
    80	                excavationTool: ExcavationToolKind.Room),
    81	            Ground(reachable: true));
    82	
    83	        AssertCommand(decision, ApplicationInputCommandKind.DropInventoryStack);
    84	        Assert.Equal(Stack, decision.TargetEntityId);
    85	        Assert.Equal(Cell, decision.TargetCell);
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    [Fact]
    89	    public void Supported_alt_building_box_creates_pickup_command()
    90	    {
    91	        ContextInputDecision decision = _router.Route(
    92	            new ContextPointerEvent(
    93	                PointerInputSurface.World,
    94	                PointerButtonKind.Left,
    95	                altPressed: true),
    96	            new ContextInputState(selectedResidentId: Resident),
    97	            new ContextPointerTarget(
    98	                ContextWorldTargetKind.BuildingBox,
    99	                Target,
   100	                Cell,
   101	                reachable: true,
   102	                supportsAltInteraction: true));

   105	        Assert.Equal(Target, decision.TargetEntityId);
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    [Fact]

   122	                supportsAltInteraction: false));

   128	    }
   129

   179	
   180	    [Fact]

   227	            new ContextPointerEvent(

   255	    private ContextInputDecision WorldLeft(
   256	        ContextInputState state,
   257	        ContextPointerTarget target)
   258	    {
   259	        return _router.Route(
   260	            new ContextPointerEvent(
   261	                PointerInputSurface.World,
   262	                PointerButtonKind.Left),
   263	            state,
   264	            target);
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    private static ContextPointerTarget Ground(bool reachable)
   268	    {
   269	        return new ContextPointerTarget(
   270	            ContextWorldTargetKind.Ground,
   271	            cell: Cell,
   272	            reachable: reachable);
   273	    }
   274	
   275	    private static void AssertCommand(
   276	        ContextInputDecision decision,
   277	        ApplicationInputCommandKind expected)
   278	    {
   279	        Assert.True(decision.ConsumesPointer);
   280	        Assert.True(decision.HasApplicationCommand);
   281	        Assert.Equal(expected, decision.CommandKind);
   282	        Assert.Equal(PresentationInputEffect.None, decision.Effects);
   283	    }

   286	    {
   287	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
   288	    }

===== tests/Dig.Tests/DeepGameplayInteractionRegressionTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Input;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class DeepGameplayInteractionRegressionTests
    14	{
    15	    private static readonly EntityId Resident = EntityId.Parse(
    16	        "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa");
    17	    private static readonly EntityId Target = EntityId.Parse(
    18	        "bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb");
    19	    private static readonly CellId Cell = new CellId(3, 3);
    20	
    21	    [Fact]
    22	    public void Loose_items_use_plain_left_click_while_building_boxes_keep_alt_pickup()
    23	    {
    24	        ContextInputRouter router = new ContextInputRouter();
    25	        ContextInputState state = new ContextInputState(selectedResidentId: Resident);
    26	        ContextInputDecision loose = router.Route(
    27	            new ContextPointerEvent(
    28	                PointerInputSurface.World,
    29	                PointerButtonKind.Left),
    30	            state,
    31	            new ContextPointerTarget(
    32	                ContextWorldTargetKind.GenericItem,
    33	                Target,
    34	                Cell,
    35	                reachable: true,
    36	                supportsAltInteraction: true));
    37	        ContextInputDecision boxSelection = router.Route(
    38	            new ContextPointerEvent(
    39	                PointerInputSurface.World,
    40	                PointerButtonKind.Left),
    41	            state,
    42	            new ContextPointerTarget(
    43	                ContextWorldTargetKind.BuildingBox,
    44	                Target,
    45	                Cell,
    46	                reachable: true,
    47	                supportsAltInteraction: true));
    48	        ContextInputDecision boxPickup = router.Route(
    49	            new ContextPointerEvent(
    50	                PointerInputSurface.World,
    51	                PointerButtonKind.Left,
    52	                altPressed: true),
    53	            state,
    54	            new ContextPointerTarget(
    55	                ContextWorldTargetKind.BuildingBox,
    56	                Target,
    57	                Cell,
    58	                reachable: true,
    59	                supportsAltInteraction: true));
    60	
    61	        Assert.Equal(ApplicationInputCommandKind.PickupWorldItem, loose.CommandKind);
    62	        Assert.True(boxSelection.Effects.HasFlag(
    63	            PresentationInputEffect.SelectBuildingBox));
    64	        Assert.Equal(ApplicationInputCommandKind.None, boxSelection.CommandKind);
    65	        Assert.Equal(ApplicationInputCommandKind.PickupBuildingBox, boxPickup.CommandKind);
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    [Fact]
    69	    public void Side_view_placement_prefers_a_horizontal_work_position()
    70	    {
    71	        WorldState world = CreateEmptyWorld();
    72	        BuildingDefinition definition = new BuildingDefinition(
    73	            new BuildingDefinitionId("campfire.side_view"),
    74	            "Campfire",
    75	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0) },
    76	            new[]
    77	            {
    78	                new CellOffset(0, -1),
    79	                new CellOffset(-1, 0),
    80	                new CellOffset(1, 0),
    81	                new CellOffset(0, 1),
    82	            },
    83	            new[]
    84	            {
    85	                new BuildingMaterialRequirement(new ItemId("stone"), 1),
    86	            },
    87	            requiredWork: 3,
    88	            maximumDurability: 100);
    89	        CellId origin = new CellId(3, 3);
    90	
    91	        BuildingPlacementResult placement = new BuildingPlacementValidator().Validate(
    92	            definition,
    93	            origin,
    94	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    95	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    96	            Array.Empty<CellId>(),
    97	            new[]
    98	            {
    99	                new CellId(3, 2),
   100	                new CellId(2, 3),
   101	                new CellId(4, 3),
   102	                new CellId(3, 4),
   103	            });
   104	
   105	        Assert.True(placement.Succeeded);
   106	        Assert.Equal(origin.Y, placement.WorkPosition.Y);
   107	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(2, 3), placement.WorkPosition);
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    [Fact]
   111	    public void Carried_source_and_loose_item_on_target_do_not_block_unpacking()
   112	    {
   113	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = new BuildingBoxHarness(carriedByResident: true);
   114	        CellId origin = new CellId(3, 3);

   116	        Result added = harness.Inventory.AddStack(
   117	            looseStack,
   118	            harness.BoxItemId,
   119	            quantity: 1,
   120	            ItemLocation.InWorld(origin),
   121	            tick: 0);
   122	
   123	        Result confirmed = harness.Confirm(
   124	            harness.BuildingId,
   125	            harness.JobId,
   126	            origin);
   127	
   128	        Assert.True(added.IsSuccess, added.Error?.ToString());
   129	        Assert.True(confirmed.IsSuccess, confirmed.Error?.ToString());
   130	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InResidentSlot(
   131	                harness.WorkerId,
   132	                ResidentInventoryCompartment.Main,
   133	                0),
   134	            harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.SourceStackId)!.Location);
   135	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(origin),
   136	            harness.Inventory.GetStack(looseStack)!.Location);
   137	    }

   194	    private static WorldState CreateEmptyWorld()
   195	    {
   196	        MaterialId air = new MaterialId("air");
   197	        MaterialCatalog materials = new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   198	        {
   199	            new MaterialDefinition(air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   200	        });
   201	        return WorldState.CreateFilled(
   202	            new WorldSize(8, 8),
   203	            chunkSize: 4,
   204	            materials,
   205	            air,
   206	            explored: true).Value;
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    private static string Read(string runtime, string file)
   210	    {
   211	        return Normalize(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(runtime, file)));
   212	    }
   213	
   214	    private static string RuntimeRoot()
   215	    {
   216	        return Path.Combine(
   217	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
   218	            "unity",
   219	            "Dig.Unity",
   220	            "Assets",
   221	            "Dig.Unity",
   222	            "Runtime");
   223	    }
   224	
   225	    private static string Normalize(string source)
   226	    {
   227	        return source
   228	            .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   229	            .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   230	            .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   231	            .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   235	    {
   236	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   237	        while (current != null)
   238	        {
   239	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   240	            {
   241	                return current.FullName;
   242	            }
   243	
   244	            current = current.Parent;
   245	        }
   246	
   247	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   248	    }
   249	}

     1	using System;

    15	    public void Small_room_even_row_uses_centered_half_cell_boundaries()
    16	    {
    17	        CaveRoomPlan plan = Plan(CaveRoomPresetKind.Small);
    18	        CellId[][] rows = FrontRows(plan);
    19	
    20	        Assert.Equal(new[] { 5, 5, 3 }, rows.Select(row => row.Length));
    21	        Assert.Equal(Enumerable.Range(8, 5), rows[0].Select(cell => cell.X));
    22	        Assert.Equal(Enumerable.Range(8, 5), rows[1].Select(cell => cell.X));
    23	        Assert.Equal(Enumerable.Range(9, 3), rows[2].Select(cell => cell.X));
    24	        Assert.Equal(8, CaveRoomPlanner.ResolveRowMinX(plan.Preset, 10, level: 1));
    25	        Assert.True(plan.TryGetExcavationTarget(
    26	            new CellId(8, 8, 0),
    27	            out CaveRoomExcavationTarget left));
    28	        Assert.True(plan.TryGetExcavationTarget(
    29	            new CellId(12, 8, 0),
    30	            out CaveRoomExcavationTarget right));
    31	        Assert.Equal(
    32	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperRight | ExcavationQuarter.LowerRight,
    33	            left.RequiredQuarters);
    34	        Assert.Equal(
    35	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperLeft | ExcavationQuarter.LowerLeft,
    36	            right.RequiredQuarters);
    37	    }

    99	        Assert.DoesNotContain("targetRemovedSupport", renderer);

   102	        int movementWait = visual.IndexOf("if (_duration > 0f)", StringComparison.Ordinal);

   106	    [Fact]
   107	    public void Assigned_job_movement_wins_before_unsupported_idle_recovery()
   108	    {
   109	        string navigation = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
   110	            RuntimeRoot(),
   111	            "DigTerrainWorkNavigation.cs"));
   112	
   113	        int assigned = navigation.IndexOf(
   114	            "HashSet<string> assignedAgentIds",
   115	            StringComparison.Ordinal);
   116	        int jobLoop = navigation.IndexOf(
   117	            "foreach (JobSnapshot job in activeJobs)",
   118	            StringComparison.Ordinal);
   119	        int recoveryLoop = navigation.IndexOf(
   120	            "foreach (AgentViewModel agent in agents.OrderBy",
   121	            StringComparison.Ordinal);
   122	        Assert.True(assigned >= 0 && jobLoop > assigned && recoveryLoop > jobLoop);
   123	        Assert.Contains(
   124	            "assignedAgentIds.Contains(agent.Id) || movement.ContainsKey(agent.Id)",
   125	            navigation);
   126	        Assert.Contains("_supportRecoveryPlanner.Plan(", navigation);
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    private static CaveRoomPlan Plan(CaveRoomPresetKind kind)
   130	    {
   131	        CaveRoomPlanResult result = new CaveRoomPlanner().Plan(
   132	            CreateWorld(horizontalTunnelY: 9),
   133	            new ExcavationBoundaryPolicy(20, 14, 2),
   134	            kind,
   135	            new CellId(10, 9));
   136	        Assert.True(result.Succeeded, result.Detail);
   137	        return result.Plan!;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    private static CellId[][] FrontRows(CaveRoomPlan plan)
   141	    {
   142	        return plan.VolumeCells
   143	            .Where(cell => cell.Z == 0)
   144	            .GroupBy(cell => cell.Y)
   145	            .OrderByDescending(group => group.Key)
   146	            .Select(group => group.OrderBy(cell => cell.X).ToArray())
   147	            .ToArray();
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    private static WorldSnapshot CreateWorld(int horizontalTunnelY)
   151	    {
   152	        MaterialId rock = new MaterialId("test.rock");
   153	        MaterialId air = new MaterialId("test.air");
   154	        MaterialCatalog materials = new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   155	        {
   156	            new MaterialDefinition(rock, isSolid: true, hardness: 100),
   157	            new MaterialDefinition(air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   158	        });
   159	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
   160	            new WorldSize(20, 14),
   161	            chunkSize: 5,
   162	            materials,
   163	            rock,
   164	            explored: true).Value;
   165	        CellState empty = new CellState(
   166	            air,
   167	            CellDesignation.None,
   168	            isExplored: true,
   169	            damage: 0,
   170	            temperature: 20);
   171	        List<TerrainChange> changes = Enumerable.Range(1, 18)
   172	            .Select(x => new TerrainChange(
   173	                new CellId(x, horizontalTunnelY),
   174	                empty))
   175	            .ToList();
   176	        world.ApplyTerrainChanges(changes, tick: 1);
   177	        return world.CreateSnapshot();
   178	    }
   179	
   180	    private static string RuntimeRoot()
   181	    {
   182	        return Path.Combine(
   183	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
   184	            "unity",
   185	            "Dig.Unity",
   186	            "Assets",
   187	            "Dig.Unity",
   188	            "Runtime");
   189	    }
   190	
   191	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   192	    {
   193	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   194	        while (current != null)
   195	        {
   196	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   197	            {
   198	                return current.FullName;
   199	            }
   200	
   201	            current = current.Parent;
   202	        }
   203	
   204	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   205	    }
   206	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/JobOverlayPresentationTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Collections.Generic;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     9	using Dig.Presentation.Jobs;
    10	using Xunit;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Tests
    13	{
    14	
    15	public sealed class JobOverlayPresentationTests
    16	{
    17	    [Fact]
    18	    public void Presenter_maps_dig_target_worker_stage_and_reservations()
    19	    {
    20	        EntityId jobId = EntityId.Parse("20000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	        EntityId agentId = EntityId.Parse("30000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
    23	        DigJobDefinition definition = new DigJobDefinition(
    24	            jobId,
    25	            new DigJobTarget(new CellId(8, 5)),
    26	            priority: 700,
    27	            createdTick: 2,
    28	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    29	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
    30	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(jobId, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
    31	        Assert.True(jobs.Claim(jobId, agentId, tick: 3).IsSuccess);
    32	        Assert.True(jobs.Start(jobId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    33	        InMemoryJobRepository repository = new InMemoryJobRepository(jobs);
    34	        JobOverlayPresenter presenter = new JobOverlayPresenter(
    35	            new GetJobsHandler(repository),
    36	            new GetJobReservationsHandler(repository));
    37	
    38	        JobOverlayViewModel model = Assert.Single(presenter.Load());
    39	
    40	        Assert.Equal(jobId.ToString(), model.Id);
    41	        Assert.Equal("InProgress", model.Status);
    42	        Assert.Equal("TravelToTarget", model.Stage);
    43	        Assert.Equal(agentId.ToString(), model.AssignedAgentId);
    44	        Assert.Equal(8, model.TargetX);
    45	        Assert.Equal(5, model.TargetY);
    46	        Assert.Equal(JobToolKind.Mining, model.PreferredToolKind);
    47	        Assert.Null(model.AssignmentDiagnostic);
    48	        Assert.Equal(4, model.Reservations.Count);
    49	        Assert.Equal(
    50	            new[] { "Agent", "Designation", "Job", "Position" },
    51	            model.Reservations.Select(item => item.Kind));
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    [Fact]
    55	    public void Presenter_marks_mushroom_work_and_maps_xyz_target()
    56	    {
    57	        EntityId jobId = EntityId.Parse("20000000000000000000000000000005");
    58	        EntityId siteId = EntityId.Parse("21000000000000000000000000000005");
    59	        EntityId agentId = EntityId.Parse("30000000000000000000000000000005");
    60	        CellId target = new CellId(7, 6, 2);
    61	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
    62	        MushroomChopJobDefinition definition = new MushroomChopJobDefinition(
    63	            jobId,
    64	            siteId,
    65	            target,
    66	            new CellId(6, 6, 2),
    67	            growthGeneration: 1,
    68	            requiredSwings: 3,
    69	            priority: 900,
    70	            createdTick: 4,
    71	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    72	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
    73	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(jobId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    74	        Assert.True(jobs.Claim(jobId, agentId, tick: 5).IsSuccess);
    75	        Assert.True(jobs.Start(jobId, tick: 6).IsSuccess);
    76	        Assert.True(jobs.AdvanceStage(jobId, tick: 7).IsSuccess);
    77	        InMemoryJobRepository repository = new InMemoryJobRepository(jobs);
    78	
    79	        JobOverlayViewModel model = Assert.Single(new JobOverlayPresenter(
    80	            new GetJobsHandler(repository),
    81	            new GetJobReservationsHandler(repository)).Load());
    82	
    83	        Assert.True(model.IsMushroomChop);
    84	        Assert.Equal(target.X, model.TargetX);
    85	        Assert.Equal(target.Y, model.TargetY);
    86	        Assert.Equal(target.Z, model.TargetZ);
    87	        Assert.Equal("PerformWork", model.Stage);
    88	        Assert.Equal(agentId.ToString(), model.AssignedAgentId);
    89	    }

    94	        EntityId jobId = EntityId.Parse("20000000000000000000000000000003");
    95	        EntityId agentId = EntityId.Parse("30000000000000000000000000000003");

    97	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();

    99	            jobId,
   100	            new DigJobTarget(new CellId(9, 4)),
   101	            priority: 650,
   102	            createdTick: 1,
   103	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   104	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
   105	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(jobId, tick: 1).IsSuccess);
   106	        Assert.True(jobs.Claim(jobId, agentId, toolStackId, tick: 2).IsSuccess);

   108	            tick: 2,
   109	            assignments: new[]
   110	            {
   111	                new JobAssignment(
   112	                    jobId,
   113	                    agentId,
   114	                    score: 5_650_499_999L,
   115	                    toolPreparation: JobToolPreparationOutcome.Switched,
   116	                    toolStackId: toolStackId),
   117	            },
   118	            failures: System.Array.Empty<JobAssignmentFailure>());

   120	        JobOverlayPresenter presenter = new JobOverlayPresenter(

   127	            Assert.IsType<JobAssignmentDiagnosticViewModel>(model.AssignmentDiagnostic);
   128	        Assert.False(diagnostic.IsFailure);

   130	        Assert.Equal(5_650_499_999L, diagnostic.Score);
   131	        Assert.Equal(JobToolPreparationOutcome.Switched, diagnostic.ToolPreparation);
   132	        Assert.Equal(toolStackId.ToString(), diagnostic.ToolStackId);
   133	        Assert.Contains(model.Reservations, value => value.Kind == "Tool");
   134	    }

     1	using System;

    22	    private static readonly ItemId Leg = new ItemId("material.mushroom_leg");

    24	    private static readonly EntityId FirstJobId = Id("b2000000000000000000000000000001");

    31	    [Fact]
    32	    public void Direct_workflow_creates_exact_units_and_grants_woodworking_once()
    33	    {
    34	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(
    35	            MushroomStage.Large,
    36	            firstRequiredSwings: 2,
    37	            firstWoodworkingUnits: 4_100);
    38	        Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> started = harness.Start.Handle(
    39	            new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
    40	                FirstJobId,
    41	                SiteId,
    42	                FirstWorkerId,
    43	                Work,
    44	                priority: 900,
    45	                tick: 1));
    46	        Assert.True(started.IsSuccess, started.Error?.ToString());
    47	        Assert.Equal(2, started.Value.RequiredSwings);
    48	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtMushroomCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
    49	        Assert.False(harness.Swing.Handle(new CompleteMushroomSwingCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).Value);
    50	        Assert.True(harness.Swing.Handle(new CompleteMushroomSwingCommand(FirstJobId, 4)).Value);
    51	
    52	        Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult> completed = harness.Complete.Handle(
    53	            new CompleteMushroomChopCommand(
    54	                FirstJobId,
    55	                Id("b4000000000000000000000000000001"),
    56	                tick: 5));
    57	
    58	        Assert.True(completed.IsSuccess, completed.Error?.ToString());
    59	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Large, completed.Value.ChoppedStage);
    60	        Assert.Equal(2, completed.Value.CapUnitIds.Count);
    61	        Assert.Single(completed.Value.LegUnitIds);
    62	        Assert.All(completed.Value.CapUnitIds, id =>
    63	        {
    64	            ItemStackSnapshot unit = harness.Inventory.GetStack(id)!;
    65	            Assert.Equal(Cap, unit.ItemId);
    66	            Assert.Equal(1, unit.Quantity);
    67	            Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(Target), unit.Location);
    68	        });
    69	        Assert.Equal(Leg, harness.Inventory.GetStack(completed.Value.LegUnitIds.Single())!.ItemId);
    70	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId)!.Status);
    71	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
    72	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.AbsentRegrowing, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
    73	        Assert.Equal(4_100 + MushroomDefinition.WoodworkingGrantUnits, harness.Agents
    74	            .Get(FirstWorkerId)!
    75	            .CreateSkillProgressionSnapshot()
    76	            .GetLevel(AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking));
    77	
    78	        Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult> duplicate = harness.Complete.Handle(
    79	            new CompleteMushroomChopCommand(
    80	                FirstJobId,
    81	                Id("b4000000000000000000000000000011"),
    82	                tick: 6));
    83	        Assert.True(duplicate.IsFailure);
    84	        Assert.Equal(3, harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks.Count);
    85	        Assert.Equal(4_100 + MushroomDefinition.WoodworkingGrantUnits, harness.Agents
    86	            .Get(FirstWorkerId)!
    87	            .CreateSkillProgressionSnapshot()
    88	            .GetLevel(AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking));
    89	    }

    91	    [Fact]
    92	    public void Second_direct_order_cancels_first_job_and_resets_progress()
    93	    {
    94	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(
    95	            MushroomStage.Medium,
    96	            firstRequiredSwings: 5,
    97	            secondRequiredSwings: 3,
    98	            firstWoodworkingUnits: 2_100,
    99	            secondWoodworkingUnits: 4_100);
   100	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
   101	            FirstJobId,
   102	            SiteId,
   103	            FirstWorkerId,
   104	            Work,
   105	            900,
   106	            1)).IsSuccess);
   107	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtMushroomCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
   108	        Assert.False(harness.Swing.Handle(new CompleteMushroomSwingCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).Value);
   109	        Assert.Equal(1, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.CompletedSwings);
   110	
   111	        Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> replacement = harness.Start.Handle(
   112	            new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
   113	                SecondJobId,
   114	                SiteId,
   115	                SecondWorkerId,
   116	                new CellId(6, 6, 0),
   117	                900,
   118	                4));
   119	
   120	        Assert.True(replacement.IsSuccess, replacement.Error?.ToString());
   121	        Assert.Equal(FirstJobId, replacement.Value.ReplacedJobId);
   122	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Cancelled, harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId)!.Status);
   123	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, harness.Jobs.Get(SecondJobId)!.Status);
   124	        MushroomSiteSnapshot site = harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!;
   125	        Assert.Equal(0, site.CompletedSwings);
   126	        Assert.Equal(3, site.RequiredSwings);
   127	        Assert.Equal(SecondWorkerId, site.ActiveWorkerId);
   128	        Assert.DoesNotContain(
   129	            harness.Jobs.GetReservations(),
   130	            reservation => reservation.JobId == FirstJobId);
   131	    }

   168	    [Fact]
   169	    public void Cancel_releases_target_and_resumes_remaining_growth_duration()
   170	    {
   171	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(MushroomStage.Tiny, firstRequiredSwings: 6);
   172	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
   173	            FirstJobId,
   174	            SiteId,
   175	            FirstWorkerId,
   176	            Work,
   177	            900,
   178	            4)).IsSuccess);
   179	        Assert.True(harness.Grow.Handle(new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommand(100)).IsSuccess);
   180	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Tiny, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
   181	
   182	        Assert.True(harness.Cancel.Handle(new CancelMushroomChopCommand(
   183	            FirstJobId,
   184	            "player_cancelled",
   185	            tick: 20)).IsSuccess);
   186	
   187	        Assert.Equal(26, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.NextStageTick);
   188	        Assert.True(harness.Grow.Handle(new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommand(25)).IsSuccess);
   189	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Tiny, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
   190	        Assert.True(harness.Grow.Handle(new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommand(26)).IsSuccess);
   191	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Small, harness.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    private static Harness CreateHarness(
   195	        MushroomStage stage,
   196	        int firstRequiredSwings,
   197	        int? secondRequiredSwings = null,
   198	        int firstWoodworkingUnits = 0,
   199	        int secondWoodworkingUnits = 0)
   200	    {
   201	        MushroomState mushrooms = new MushroomState(new MushroomCatalog(new[]
   202	        {
   203	            new MushroomDefinition(DefinitionId, 10, Cap, Leg),
   204	        }));
   205	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(SiteId, DefinitionId, Target, stage, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   206	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
   207	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[]
   208	        {
   209	            new ItemDefinition(Cap, "Mushroom cap", 100, isTool: false),
   210	            new ItemDefinition(Leg, "Mushroom leg", 100, isTool: false),
   211	        }));
   212	        InMemoryExecutionJournal events = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
   213	        InMemoryAgentRepository agents = new InMemoryAgentRepository();
   214	        AgentState firstWorker = AgentTestFactory.CreateAgent(id: FirstWorkerId);
   215	        AgentState secondWorker = AgentTestFactory.CreateAgent(id: SecondWorkerId);
   216	        Assert.True(firstWorker.SetSkillLevel(
   217	            AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking,
   218	            firstWoodworkingUnits).IsSuccess);
   219	        Assert.True(secondWorker.SetSkillLevel(
   220	            AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking,
   221	            secondWoodworkingUnits).IsSuccess);
   222	        Assert.True(agents.Add(firstWorker).IsSuccess);
   223	        Assert.True(agents.Add(secondWorker).IsSuccess);
   224	        AgentSkillGrantService skills = new AgentSkillGrantService(agents, events);
   225	        InMemoryMushroomRepository mushroomRepository = new InMemoryMushroomRepository(mushrooms);
   226	        InMemoryJobRepository jobRepository = new InMemoryJobRepository(jobs);
   227	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(inventory);
   228	        SequenceSwingRandom random = new SequenceSwingRandom(
   229	            firstRequiredSwings,
   230	            secondRequiredSwings ?? firstRequiredSwings);
   231	        return new Harness(
   232	            mushrooms,
   233	            jobs,
   234	            inventory,
   235	            agents,
   236	            new StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler(
   237	                mushroomRepository,
   238	                jobRepository,
   239	                skills,
   240	                random,
   241	                events),
   242	            new ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler(jobRepository, events),
   243	            new CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler(mushroomRepository, jobRepository, events),
   244	            new CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler(
   245	                mushroomRepository,
   246	                jobRepository,
   247	                inventoryRepository,
   248	                skills,
   249	                events),
   250	            new CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler(mushroomRepository, jobRepository, events),
   251	            new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommandHandler(mushroomRepository, events));
   252	    }
   253	
   254	    private static EntityId Id(string value) => EntityId.Parse(value);
   255	
   256	    private sealed class SequenceSwingRandom : IMushroomSwingRandom
   257	    {
   258	        private readonly int[] _values;
   259	        private int _index;
   260	
   261	        public SequenceSwingRandom(params int[] values)
   262	        {
   263	            _values = values;
   264	        }
   265	
   266	        public int SelectRequiredSwings(
   267	            EntityId siteId,
   268	            EntityId workerId,
   269	            int minimum,
   270	            int maximum)
   271	        {
   272	            int value = _values[Math.Min(_index, _values.Length - 1)];
   273	            _index++;
   274	            Assert.InRange(value, minimum, maximum);
   275	            return value;
   276	        }
   277	    }
   278	
   279	    private sealed class Harness
   280	    {
   281	        public Harness(
   282	            MushroomState mushrooms,
   283	            JobSystem jobs,
   284	            InventoryState inventory,
   285	            InMemoryAgentRepository agents,
   286	            StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler start,
   287	            ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler arrive,
   288	            CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler swing,
   289	            CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler complete,
   290	            CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler cancel,
   291	            AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommandHandler grow)
   292	        {
   293	            Mushrooms = mushrooms;
   294	            Jobs = jobs;
   295	            Inventory = inventory;
   296	            Agents = agents;
   297	            Start = start;
   298	            Arrive = arrive;
   299	            Swing = swing;
   300	            Complete = complete;
   301	            Cancel = cancel;
   302	            Grow = grow;
   303	        }
   304	
   305	        public MushroomState Mushrooms { get; }
   306	        public JobSystem Jobs { get; }
   307	        public InventoryState Inventory { get; }
   308	        public InMemoryAgentRepository Agents { get; }
   309	        public StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler Start { get; }
   310	        public ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler Arrive { get; }
   311	        public CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler Swing { get; }
   312	        public CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler Complete { get; }
   313	        public CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler Cancel { get; }
   314	        public AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommandHandler Grow { get; }
   315	    }
   316	}
   317

===== tests/Dig.Tests/MushroomMovementPlannerSourceContractTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Xunit;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Tests
     6	{
     7	public sealed class MushroomMovementPlannerSourceContractTests
     8	{
     9	    [Fact]
    10	    public void Partial_class_has_one_mushroom_movement_planner_owner()
    11	    {
    12	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    13	        string mushrooms = Read(runtime, "DigTerrainWorkSession.Mushrooms.cs");
    14	        string navigation = Read(runtime, "DigTerrainWorkSession.MushroomNavigation.cs");
    15	        int declarations = 0;
    16	
    17	        foreach (string path in Directory.GetFiles(runtime, "DigTerrainWorkSession*.cs"))
    18	        {
    19	            declarations += Count(
    20	                Normalize(File.ReadAllText(path)),
    21	                "boolTryPlanMushroomMovement(");
    22	        }
    23	
    24	        Assert.Equal(1, declarations);
    25	        Assert.DoesNotContain("TryPlanMushroomMovement(", mushrooms);
    26	        Assert.Contains("privateboolTryPlanMushroomMovement(", navigation);
    27	        Assert.Contains("_routePlans[job.Id]=newTerrainWorkRoutePlan", navigation);
    28	    }
    29	
    30	    private static string Read(string root, string file) => Normalize(
    31	        File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root, file)));
    32	
    33	    private static int Count(string source, string value)
    34	    {
    35	        int count = 0;
    36	        int index = 0;
    37	        while ((index = source.IndexOf(value, index, StringComparison.Ordinal)) >= 0)
    38	        {
    39	            count++;
    40	            index += value.Length;
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        return count;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    private static string RuntimeRoot() => Path.Combine(
    47	        FindRepositoryRoot(),
    48	        "unity",
    49	        "Dig.Unity",
    50	        "Assets",
    51	        "Dig.Unity",
    52	        "Runtime");
    53	
    54	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
    55	    {
    56	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
    57	        while (current != null)
    58	        {
    59	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
    60	            {
    61	                return current.FullName;
    62	            }
    63	
    64	            current = current.Parent;
    65	        }
    66	
    67	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    private static string Normalize(string source) => source
    71	        .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
    72	        .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
    73	        .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
    74	        .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    75	}
    76	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/MushroomResidentPresentationTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     8	using Xunit;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Tests
    11	{
    12	public sealed class MushroomResidentPresentationTests
    13	{
    14	    [Fact]
    15	    public void Assigned_mushroom_job_reports_gather_status_and_target()
    16	    {
    17	        EntityId agentId = Id(71);
    18	        EntityId jobId = Id(72);
    19	        EntityId siteId = Id(73);
    20	        CellId target = new CellId(6, 5, 1);
    21	        CellId work = new CellId(5, 5, 1);
    22	        AgentSnapshot agent = Agent(agentId);
    23	        JobSnapshot job = new JobSnapshot(
    24	            new MushroomChopJobDefinition(
    25	                jobId,
    26	                siteId,
    27	                target,
    28	                work,
    29	                growthGeneration: 0,
    30	                requiredSwings: 4,
    31	                priority: 900,
    32	                createdTick: 1,
    33	                JobRetryPolicy.Default),
    34	            JobStatus.InProgress,
    35	            JobStageKind.PerformWork,
    36	            agentId,
    37	            retryCount: 0,
    38	            nextRetryTick: 0,
    39	            version: 2,
    40	            reason: null);
    41	
    42	        ResidentActivityDescriptor activity = Assert.Single(
    43	            new ResidentRosterPresenter().Present(
    44	                new[] { agent },
    45	                society: null,
    46	                jobs: new[] { job },
    47	                selectedResidentId: agentId).Rows).Activity;
    48	
    49	        Assert.Equal(ResidentActivityKind.GatherMushroom, activity.Kind);
    50	        Assert.Equal("Добывает гриб", activity.LocalizationKey);
    51	        Assert.Equal(siteId.ToString(), activity.SubjectId);
    52	        Assert.Equal(target, activity.Destination);
    53	        Assert.Equal(jobId.ToString(), activity.SourceJobId);
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    private static AgentSnapshot Agent(EntityId id)
    57	    {
    58	        return new AgentSnapshot(
    59	            id,
    60	            "Gatherer",
    61	            version: 1,
    62	            isAlive: true,
    63	            needs: new AgentNeedsSnapshot(
    64	                new NeedValue(6000),
    65	                new NeedValue(6000),
    66	                new NeedValue(6000),
    67	                new NeedValue(6000)),
    68	            scheduledActivity: ScheduleActivity.Work,
    69	            activeAction: null,
    70	            playerOrder: null,
    71	            lastActionSwitchTick: -1,
    72	            lastDecision: null,
    73	            skills: Array.Empty<AgentSkillValue>(),
    74	            traits: Array.Empty<AgentTraitId>(),
    75	            position: new CellId(1, 1));
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    private static EntityId Id(int value)
    79	    {
    80	        return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
    81	    }
    82	}
    83	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/MushroomSaveRoundTripTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    11	using Xunit;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Tests
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class MushroomSaveRoundTripTests
    17	{
    18	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("terrain.air");
    19	    private static readonly MushroomDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    20	        new MushroomDefinitionId("ecology.mushroom.common");
    21	    private static readonly ItemId Cap = new ItemId("material.mushroom_cap");
    22	    private static readonly ItemId Leg = new ItemId("material.mushroom_leg");
    23	    private static readonly EntityId SiteId = Id("c1000000000000000000000000000001");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId JobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000001");
    25	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerId = Id("c3000000000000000000000000000001");
    26	    private static readonly CellId SiteCell = new CellId(3, 3, 1);
    27	    private static readonly CellId WorkCell = new CellId(2, 3, 1);
    28	
    29	    [Fact]
    30	    public void Mid_chop_round_trip_preserves_paused_growth_progress_and_reservations()
    31	    {
    32	        MaterialCatalog materials = CreateMaterials();
    33	        ItemCatalog items = CreateItems();
    34	        MushroomCatalog mushroomCatalog = CreateMushroomCatalog();
    35	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
    36	            new WorldSize(8, 8),
    37	            chunkSize: 4,
    38	            materials,
    39	            Air,
    40	            explored: true).Value;
    41	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
    42	        MushroomState mushrooms = new MushroomState(mushroomCatalog);
    43	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(
    44	            SiteId,
    45	            DefinitionId,
    46	            SiteCell,
    47	            MushroomStage.Medium,
    48	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    49	        Assert.True(mushrooms.BeginChop(
    50	            SiteId,
    51	            JobId,
    52	            WorkerId,
    53	            requiredSwings: 5,
    54	            tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    55	        Assert.False(mushrooms.CompleteSwing(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 5).Value);
    56	        Assert.False(mushrooms.CompleteSwing(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 6).Value);
    57	
    58	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
    59	        MushroomChopJobDefinition definition = new MushroomChopJobDefinition(
    60	            JobId,
    61	            SiteId,
    62	            SiteCell,
    63	            WorkCell,
    64	            growthGeneration: 0,
    65	            requiredSwings: 5,
    66	            priority: 900,
    67	            createdTick: 4,
    68	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    69	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(definition).IsSuccess);
    70	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(JobId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    71	        Assert.True(jobs.Claim(JobId, WorkerId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    72	        Assert.True(jobs.Start(JobId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    73	        Assert.True(jobs.AdvanceStage(JobId, tick: 5).IsSuccess);
    74	
    75	        SaveGameDocument document = CreateBuilder().Build(new SaveGameContext(
    76	            Metadata(),
    77	            world,
    78	            inventory,
    79	            jobs,
    80	            new BuildingsState(),
    81	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
    82	            mushrooms: mushrooms));
    83	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec json = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    84	        SaveGameDocument decoded = json.Deserialize(json.Serialize(document));
    85	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = CreateLoader().Load(
    86	            decoded,
    87	            materials,
    88	            items,
    89	            buildingCatalog: null,
    90	            terrainDepositCatalog: null,
    91	            mushroomCatalog);
    92	
    93	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    94	        MushroomSiteSnapshot restored = loaded.Value.Mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!;
    95	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Medium, restored.Stage);
    96	        Assert.Equal(2, restored.CompletedSwings);
    97	        Assert.Equal(5, restored.RequiredSwings);
    98	        Assert.Equal(4, restored.GrowthPausedAtTick);
    99	        Assert.Equal(JobId, restored.ActiveChopJobId);
   100	        Assert.Equal(WorkerId, restored.ActiveWorkerId);
   101	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.PerformWork, loaded.Value.Jobs.Get(JobId)!.Stage);
   102	        Assert.Contains(
   103	            loaded.Value.Jobs.GetReservations(),
   104	            reservation => reservation.Key == ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(SiteId));
   105	        Assert.Equal(SiteCell, Assert.Single(loaded.Value.Mushrooms.GetBuildingBlockedCells()));
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    [Fact]
   109	    public void Version_five_migration_adds_empty_mushroom_section()
   110	    {
   111	        SaveGameDocument document = new SaveGameDocument
   112	        {
   113	            FormatVersion = 5,
   114	            Mushrooms = null!,
   115	        };
   116	        SaveMigrationPipeline pipeline = new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
   117	        {
   118	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
   119	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   120	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   121	            new SaveVersionEightAgentRuntimeMigration(),
   122	            new SaveVersionNineCombatSpatialMigration(),
   123	        });
   124	
   125	        Result<SaveMigrationReport> migrated = pipeline.Apply(document);
   126	
   127	        Assert.True(migrated.IsSuccess, migrated.Error?.ToString());
   128	        Assert.Equal(SaveFormat.CurrentVersion, document.FormatVersion);
   129	        Assert.NotNull(document.Mushrooms);
   130	        Assert.Empty(document.Mushrooms.Sites);
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    private static SaveGameBuilder CreateBuilder()
   134	    {
   135	        return new SaveGameBuilder(new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   136	        {
   137	            new MushroomChopJobSaveCodec(),
   138	        }));
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    private static SaveGameLoader CreateLoader()
   142	    {
   143	        return new SaveGameLoader(
   144	            new SaveMigrationPipeline(Array.Empty<ISaveMigration>()),
   145	            new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   146	            {
   147	                new MushroomChopJobSaveCodec(),
   148	            }));
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata()
   152	    {
   153	        return new SaveMetadataData
   154	        {
   155	            SlotId = "mushroom-mid-chop",
   156	            DisplayName = "Mushroom checkpoint",
   157	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-27T10:00:00Z",
   158	            SimulationTick = 6,
   159	            WorldSeed = 42,
   160	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   161	        };
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   165	    {
   166	        return new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   167	        {
   168	            new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   169	        });
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    private static ItemCatalog CreateItems()
   173	    {
   174	        return new ItemCatalog(new[]
   175	        {
   176	            new ItemDefinition(Cap, "Mushroom cap", 100, isTool: false),
   177	            new ItemDefinition(Leg, "Mushroom leg", 100, isTool: false),
   178	        });
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    private static MushroomCatalog CreateMushroomCatalog()
   182	    {
   183	        return new MushroomCatalog(new[]
   184	        {
   185	            new MushroomDefinition(
   186	                DefinitionId,
   187	                stageDurationTicks: 10,
   188	                capItemId: Cap,
   189	                legItemId: Leg),
   190	        });
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    private static EntityId Id(string value) => EntityId.Parse(value);
   194	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/MushroomStateTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class MushroomStateTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly MushroomDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    17	        new MushroomDefinitionId("ecology.mushroom.common");
    18	    private static readonly ItemId Cap = new ItemId("material.mushroom_cap");
    19	    private static readonly ItemId Leg = new ItemId("material.mushroom_leg");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId SiteId =
    21	        EntityId.Parse("a1000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	    private static readonly EntityId JobId =
    23	        EntityId.Parse("a2000000000000000000000000000001");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerId =
    25	        EntityId.Parse("a3000000000000000000000000000001");
    26	
    27	    [Fact]
    28	    public void Growth_reaches_large_and_stops_there()
    29	    {
    30	        MushroomState mushrooms = CreateState(stageDurationTicks: 10);
    31	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(
    32	            SiteId,
    33	            DefinitionId,
    34	            new CellId(4, 5, 0),
    35	            MushroomStage.Tiny,
    36	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    37	
    38	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AdvanceGrowth(tick: 30).IsSuccess);
    39	        MushroomSiteSnapshot site = mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!;
    40	
    41	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Large, site.Stage);
    42	        Assert.Null(site.NextStageTick);
    43	        Assert.Equal(30, site.StageStartedTick);
    44	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AdvanceGrowth(tick: 1000).IsSuccess);
    45	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Large, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    [Fact]
    49	    public void Active_chop_pauses_growth_and_release_preserves_remaining_duration()
    50	    {
    51	        MushroomState mushrooms = CreateState(stageDurationTicks: 10);
    52	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(
    53	            SiteId,
    54	            DefinitionId,
    55	            new CellId(1, 2, 0),
    56	            MushroomStage.Tiny,
    57	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    58	        Assert.True(mushrooms.BeginChop(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, 3, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    59	
    60	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AdvanceGrowth(tick: 100).IsSuccess);
    61	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Tiny, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
    62	
    63	        Assert.True(mushrooms.ReleaseChop(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 20).IsSuccess);
    64	        MushroomSiteSnapshot released = mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!;
    65	        Assert.Equal(26, released.NextStageTick);
    66	        Assert.Equal(0, released.CompletedSwings);
    67	        Assert.Null(released.ActiveChopJobId);
    68	
    69	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AdvanceGrowth(tick: 25).IsSuccess);
    70	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Tiny, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
    71	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AdvanceGrowth(tick: 26).IsSuccess);
    72	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.Small, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    [Fact]
    76	    public void Release_resets_swings_and_new_worker_gets_full_attempt()
    77	    {
    78	        MushroomState mushrooms = CreateState(stageDurationTicks: 10);
    79	        EntityId newJob = EntityId.Parse("a2000000000000000000000000000002");
    80	        EntityId newWorker = EntityId.Parse("a3000000000000000000000000000002");
    81	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(
    82	            SiteId,
    83	            DefinitionId,
    84	            new CellId(2, 2, 0),
    85	            MushroomStage.Medium,
    86	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    87	        Assert.True(mushrooms.BeginChop(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, 5, tick: 1).IsSuccess);
    88	        Assert.False(mushrooms.CompleteSwing(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 2).Value);
    89	        Assert.False(mushrooms.CompleteSwing(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 3).Value);
    90	
    91	        Assert.True(mushrooms.ReleaseChop(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    92	        Assert.True(mushrooms.BeginChop(SiteId, newJob, newWorker, 3, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
    93	        MushroomSiteSnapshot replacement = mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!;
    94	
    95	        Assert.Equal(0, replacement.CompletedSwings);
    96	        Assert.Equal(3, replacement.RequiredSwings);
    97	        Assert.Equal(newWorker, replacement.ActiveWorkerId);
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    [Theory]
   101	    [InlineData(MushroomStage.Tiny, 1, 0)]
   102	    [InlineData(MushroomStage.Small, 1, 0)]
   103	    [InlineData(MushroomStage.Medium, 2, 0)]
   104	    [InlineData(MushroomStage.Large, 2, 1)]
   105	    public void Completion_uses_exact_stage_drop_table(
   106	        MushroomStage stage,
   107	        int expectedCaps,
   108	        int expectedLegs)
   109	    {
   110	        MushroomState mushrooms = CreateState(stageDurationTicks: 10);
   111	        Assert.True(mushrooms.AddSite(
   112	            SiteId,
   113	            DefinitionId,
   114	            new CellId(3, 4, 1),
   115	            stage,
   116	            tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   117	        Assert.True(mushrooms.BeginChop(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, 1, tick: 1).IsSuccess);
   118	        Assert.True(mushrooms.CompleteSwing(SiteId, JobId, WorkerId, tick: 2).Value);
   119	
   120	        Result<MushroomChopCommit> result = mushrooms.CommitChop(
   121	            SiteId,
   122	            JobId,
   123	            WorkerId,
   124	            tick: 3);
   125	
   126	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   127	        Assert.Equal(expectedCaps, result.Value.Drops.CapCount);
   128	        Assert.Equal(expectedLegs, result.Value.Drops.LegCount);
   129	        Assert.Equal(MushroomStage.AbsentRegrowing, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.Stage);
   130	        Assert.Equal(1, mushrooms.Get(SiteId)!.GrowthGeneration);
   131	        Assert.Equal($"mushroom:{SiteId}:1", result.Value.SkillSourceId);
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    [Fact]
   135	    public void Absent_stage_is_not_choppable_and_regrows_to_tiny()
   136	    {

   193	    {
   194	        CellId target = new CellId(4, 4, 0);
   195	        CellId work = new CellId(3, 4, 0);
   196	        MushroomChopJobDefinition definition = new MushroomChopJobDefinition(
   197	            JobId,
   198	            SiteId,
   199	            target,
   200	            work,
   201	            growthGeneration: 0,
   202	            requiredSwings: 6,
   203	            priority: 900,
   204	            createdTick: 0,
   205	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   206	
   207	        ReservationKey[] reservations = definition.CreateReservationKeys().ToArray();
   208	
   209	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(SiteId), reservations);
   210	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForPosition(work), reservations);
   211	        Assert.Equal(new[]
   212	        {

   214	            JobStageKind.PerformWork,
   215	            JobStageKind.Finalize,
   216	        }, definition.Stages);
   217	    }

===== tests/Dig.Tests/MushroomUnityRuntimeContractTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Xunit;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Tests
     6	{
     7	public sealed class MushroomUnityRuntimeContractTests
     8	{
     9	    [Fact]
    10	    public void Hover_cursor_and_click_share_mushroom_priority_over_buildings()
    11	    {
    12	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    13	        string priority = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.ResidentCommandPriority.cs");
    14	        string decisions = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.Decisions.cs");
    15	        string cursor = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.DirectCommandCursor.cs");
    16	        string pointerHits = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.PointerHits.cs");
    17	
    18	        Assert.True(
    19	            priority.IndexOf("TryResolveMushroomHit", StringComparison.Ordinal)
    20	            < priority.IndexOf("TryResolveCompletedBuildingHit", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    21	        Assert.True(
    22	            priority.IndexOf("TryResolveMushroomHit", StringComparison.Ordinal)
    23	            < priority.IndexOf("_excavationMode!=DigExcavationDrawingMode.None", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    24	        Assert.Contains("ContextWorldTargetKind.Mushroom", priority);
    25	        Assert.Contains("ApplicationInputCommandKind.ChopMushroom", decisions);
    26	        Assert.Contains("DirectCommandCursorKind.Axe", cursor);
    27	        Assert.Contains("TryResolveReachableMushroomHit(hits,out_)", cursor);
    28	        Assert.Contains("TryResolveReachableMushroomHit(hits,outDigMushroomVisualmushroom)", pointerHits);
    29	        Assert.DoesNotContain("?.SetHovered", pointerHits);
    30	        Assert.Contains("if(_hoveredMushroom!=null)", pointerHits);
    31	        Assert.Contains("_hoveredMushroom.SetHovered(false)", pointerHits);
    32	        Assert.Contains("_hoveredMushroom.SetHovered(true)", pointerHits);
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    [Fact]
    36	    public void Mushroom_visual_is_vertical_small_urp_lit_and_highlightable()
    37	    {
    38	        string visual = Read(RuntimeRoot(), "DigMushroomVisual.cs");
    39	        string renderer = Read(RuntimeRoot(), "DigMushroomRenderer.cs");
    40	
    41	        Assert.Contains("Shader.Find(\"UniversalRenderPipeline/Lit\")", visual);
    42	        Assert.Contains("MushroomStage.Large=>(0.84f,0.62f)", visual);
    43	        Assert.Contains("_collider!.center=newVector3(0f,height*0.5f,0f)", visual);
    44	        Assert.Contains("_collider.size=newVector3", visual);
    45	        Assert.Contains("transform.localRotation=Quaternion.identity", visual);
    46	        Assert.Contains("internalvoidSetHovered(boolhovered)", visual);
    47	        Assert.Contains("Color.Lerp(_baseColors[index],Color.white,HoverBlend)", visual);
    48	        Assert.Equal(2, Count(visual, "if(renderer==null)"));
    49	        Assert.DoesNotContain("Shader.Find(\"Standard\")", visual);
    50	        Assert.DoesNotContain("MushroomStage.Large=>(1.34f", visual);
    51	        Assert.Contains("DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink", renderer);
    52	        Assert.Contains("site.Cell.Z)+newVector3(0f,DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink,0f)", renderer);
    53	        Assert.DoesNotContain("FrontOffset", renderer);
    54	        Assert.Contains("SetParent(transform,worldPositionStays:true)", renderer);
    55	        Assert.DoesNotContain("SetParent(transform,worldPositionStays:false)", renderer);
    56	    }
    57	
    58	
    59	    [Fact]
    60	    public void Foreground_mushroom_material_blocks_axes_and_uses_pickup_targeting()
    61	    {
    62	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    63	        string priority = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.ResidentCommandPriority.cs");
    64	        string pointerHits = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.PointerHits.cs");
    65	
    66	        int itemBlock = priority.IndexOf(
    67	            "_itemRenderer.TryGetItem(hits[index],out_)",
    68	            StringComparison.Ordinal);
    69	        int mushroomResolution = priority.IndexOf(
    70	            "_mushroomRenderer.TryGetMushroom(hits[index],outmushroom)",
    71	            StringComparison.Ordinal);
    72	        Assert.True(itemBlock >= 0 && itemBlock < mushroomResolution);
    73	        Assert.Contains("Aphysicaldropinfrontofaregrownsite", priority);
    74	        Assert.Contains("TryResolveWorldItemHit(hits,outDigWorldItemVisualcandidate)", pointerHits);
    75	        Assert.Contains("candidate.Model.CanPickup", pointerHits);
    76	        Assert.Contains("_itemRenderer.TryGetItem(hits[index],out_)", priority);
    77	        Assert.Contains("building=null!;returnfalse", priority);
    78	    }
