   181	
   182	    private static int Count(string source, string value)
   183	    {
   184	        int count = 0;
   185	        int index = 0;
   186	        while ((index = source.IndexOf(value, index, StringComparison.Ordinal)) >= 0)
   187	        {
   188	            count++;
   189	            index += value.Length;
   190	        }
   191	
   192	        return count;
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    private static string Read(string root, string file) => Normalize(
   196	        File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root, file)));
   197	
   198	    private static string RuntimeRoot() => Path.Combine(
   199	        FindRepositoryRoot(),
   200	        "unity",
   201	        "Dig.Unity",
   202	        "Assets",
   203	        "Dig.Unity",
   204	        "Runtime");
   205	
   206	    private static string PresentationAgentsRoot() => Path.Combine(
   207	        FindRepositoryRoot(),
   208	        "src",
   209	        "Dig.Presentation.Abstractions",
   210	        "Agents");
   211	
   212	    private static string PlayModeRoot() => Path.Combine(
   213	        FindRepositoryRoot(),
   214	        "unity",
   215	        "Dig.Unity",
   216	        "Assets",
   217	        "Dig.Unity",
   218	        "Tests",
   219	        "PlayMode");
   220	
   221	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   222	    {
   223	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   224	        while (current != null)
   225	        {
   226	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   227	            {
   228	                return current.FullName;
   229	            }
   230	
   231	            current = current.Parent;
   232	        }
   233	
   234	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    private static string Normalize(string source) => source
   238	        .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   239	        .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   240	        .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   241	        .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   242	}
   243	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/PackableBuildingExecutionLoadRecoveryTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    11	using Xunit;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Tests
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class PackableBuildingExecutionLoadRecoveryTests
    17	{
    18	    private static readonly EntityId StaleWorker =
    19	        EntityId.Parse("87000000000000000000000000000001");
    20	
    21	    [Fact]
    22	    public void Load_rebinds_active_execution_to_authoritative_job_worker()
    23	    {
    24	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateActiveAssembly();
    25	        PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry executions = CreateExecution(
    26	            harness,
    27	            StaleWorker,
    28	            beginClock: true);
    29	        SaveGameDocument document = BuildDocument(harness, executions);
    30	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec codec = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    31	
    32	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = new SaveGameLoader(
    33	            CreateMigrations(),
    34	            CreateRegistry()).Load(
    35	                codec.Deserialize(codec.Serialize(document)),
    36	                CreateMaterials(),
    37	                harness.Inventory.Catalog,
    38	                harness.Catalog);
    39	
    40	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    41	        PackableBuildingExecutionSnapshot restored = Assert.Single(
    42	            loaded.Value.PackableBuildingExecutions.CreateSnapshot());
    43	        Assert.Equal(PackableBuildingExecutionStatus.Active, restored.Status);
    44	        Assert.Equal(harness.WorkerId, restored.ActiveWorkerId);
    45	        Assert.Equal(1, restored.CompletedIterations);
    46	        Assert.Equal(new[] { StaleWorker }, restored.CompletedByWorkers);
    47	        Assert.Null(restored.IterationClock);
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    [Fact]
    51	    public void Autosave_keeps_packable_execution_section()
    52	    {
    53	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = CreateActiveAssembly();
    54	        PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry executions = CreateExecution(
    55	            harness,
    56	            harness.WorkerId,
    57	            beginClock: true);
    58	        RecordingStore store = new RecordingStore();
    59	        SaveGameService service = new SaveGameService(
    60	            new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry()),
    61	            new SaveGameLoader(CreateMigrations(), CreateRegistry()),
    62	            store);
    63	
    64	        SaveGameDocument saved = service.Autosave(new SaveGameContext(
    65	            Metadata(),
    66	            harness.WorldRepository.Get(),
    67	            harness.Inventory,
    68	            harness.Jobs,
    69	            harness.Buildings,
    70	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
    71	            Array.Empty<TerrainDepositInstance>(),
    72	            executions));
    73	
    74	        Assert.Equal(SaveSlotNames.Autosave, saved.Metadata.SlotId);
    75	        Assert.Single(saved.PackableBuildingExecutions.Executions);
    76	        Assert.Same(saved, store.Saved);
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    private static BuildingBoxHarness CreateActiveAssembly()
    80	    {
    81	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = new BuildingBoxHarness();
    82	        Assert.True(harness.Confirm(
    83	            harness.BuildingId,
    84	            harness.JobId,
    85	            new CellId(3, 3)).IsSuccess);
    86	        harness.AssignAndAdvanceToDeposit();
    87	        Assert.True(harness.CommitToSite().IsSuccess);
    88	        harness.AdvanceToPerformWork();
    89	        Assert.True(harness.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    90	        return harness;
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    private static PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry CreateExecution(
    94	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
    95	        EntityId worker,
    96	        bool beginClock)
    97	    {
    98	        PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry executions =
    99	            new PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry();
   100	        Assert.True(executions.GetOrCreate(
   101	            harness.JobId,
   102	            harness.BuildingId,
   103	            new BuildingDefinitionId("workshop.box"),
   104	            PackableBuildingOperationKind.Unpack,
   105	            totalIterations: 3).IsSuccess);
   106	        Assert.True(executions.StartOrResume(harness.JobId, worker).IsSuccess);
   107	        Assert.True(executions.CompleteIteration(harness.JobId, worker).IsSuccess);
   108	        Assert.True(executions.Interrupt(harness.JobId).IsSuccess);
   109	        Assert.True(executions.StartOrResume(harness.JobId, worker).IsSuccess);
   110	        if (beginClock)
   111	        {
   112	            Assert.True(executions.BeginIteration(
   113	                harness.JobId,
   114	                worker,
   115	                startTick: 100,
   116	                durationSeconds: 500).IsSuccess);
   117	        }
   118	
   119	        return executions;
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    private static SaveGameDocument BuildDocument(
   123	        BuildingBoxHarness harness,
   124	        PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry executions)
   125	    {
   126	        return new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry()).Build(new SaveGameContext(
   127	            Metadata(),
   128	            harness.WorldRepository.Get(),
   129	            harness.Inventory,
   130	            harness.Jobs,
   131	            harness.Buildings,
   132	            Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
   133	            Array.Empty<TerrainDepositInstance>(),
   134	            executions));
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry CreateRegistry()
   138	    {
   139	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
   140	        {
   141	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec(),
   142	        });
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    private static SaveMigrationPipeline CreateMigrations()
   146	    {
   147	        return new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
   148	        {
   149	            new LegacySaveVersionZeroMigration(),
   150	            new SaveVersionOneBuildingsMigration(),
   151	            new SaveVersionTwoPackingMigration(),
   152	            new SaveVersionThreeAgentSkillsMigration(),
   153	            new SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration(),
   154	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
   155	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   156	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   157	        });
   158	    }
   159	
   160	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   161	    {
   162	        return BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Materials();
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata()
   166	    {
   167	        return new SaveMetadataData
   168	        {
   169	            SlotId = "recovery",
   170	            DisplayName = "Recovery",
   171	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-24T17:30:00Z",
   172	            SimulationTick = 150,
   173	            WorldSeed = 1,
   174	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   175	        };
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    private sealed class RecordingStore : ISaveSlotStore
   179	    {
   180	        public SaveGameDocument? Saved { get; private set; }
   181	
   182	        public void Save(string slotId, SaveGameDocument document)
   183	        {
   184	            Saved = document;
   185	        }
   186	
   187	        public SaveGameDocument Load(string slotId)
   188	        {
   189	            return Saved ?? throw new InvalidOperationException();
   190	        }
   191	
   192	        public IReadOnlyList<SaveSlotInfo> List()
   193	        {
   194	            return Array.Empty<SaveSlotInfo>();
   195	        }
   196	    }
   197	}
   198	
   199	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/PackableBuildingExecutionSaveIntegrationTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
    10	using Xunit;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Tests
    13	{
    14	
    15	public sealed class PackableBuildingExecutionSaveIntegrationTests
    16	{
    17	    [Fact]
    18	    public void In_flight_unpack_iteration_survives_full_save_load()
    19	    {
    20	        BuildingBoxHarness harness = new BuildingBoxHarness();
    21	        Assert.True(harness.Confirm(
    22	            harness.BuildingId,
    23	            harness.JobId,
    24	            new CellId(3, 3)).IsSuccess);
    25	        harness.AssignAndAdvanceToDeposit();
    26	        Assert.True(harness.CommitToSite().IsSuccess);
    27	        harness.AdvanceToPerformWork();
    28	        Assert.True(harness.AddWork(amount: 1).IsSuccess);
    29	
    30	        PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry executions =
    31	            new PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry();
    32	        Assert.True(executions.GetOrCreate(
    33	            harness.JobId,
    34	            harness.BuildingId,
    35	            new BuildingDefinitionId("workshop.box"),
    36	            PackableBuildingOperationKind.Unpack,
    37	            totalIterations: 3).IsSuccess);
    38	        Assert.True(executions.StartOrResume(harness.JobId, harness.WorkerId).IsSuccess);
    39	        Assert.True(executions.CompleteIteration(harness.JobId, harness.WorkerId).IsSuccess);
    40	        Assert.True(executions.BeginIteration(
    41	            harness.JobId,
    42	            harness.WorkerId,
    43	            startTick: 100,
    44	            durationSeconds: 500).IsSuccess);
    45	
    46	        SaveGameDocument document = new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry()).Build(
    47	            new SaveGameContext(
    48	                Metadata(),
    49	                harness.WorldRepository.Get(),
    50	                harness.Inventory,
    51	                harness.Jobs,
    52	                harness.Buildings,
    53	                Array.Empty<AgentState>(),
    54	                Array.Empty<TerrainDepositInstance>(),
    55	                executions));
    56	        DataContractJsonSaveCodec codec = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec();
    57	        SaveGameDocument decoded = codec.Deserialize(codec.Serialize(document));
    58	
    59	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = new SaveGameLoader(
    60	            CreateMigrations(),
    61	            CreateRegistry()).Load(
    62	                decoded,
    63	                CreateMaterials(),
    64	                harness.Inventory.Catalog,
    65	                harness.Catalog);
    66	
    67	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess, loaded.Error?.ToString());
    68	        PackableBuildingExecutionSnapshot restored = Assert.Single(
    69	            loaded.Value.PackableBuildingExecutions.CreateSnapshot());
    70	        Assert.Equal(harness.JobId, restored.OperationId);
    71	        Assert.Equal(harness.BuildingId, restored.PackageId);
    72	        Assert.Equal(1, restored.CompletedIterations);
    73	        Assert.Equal(new[] { harness.WorkerId }, restored.CompletedByWorkers);
    74	        Assert.NotNull(restored.IterationClock);
    75	        Assert.Equal(100, restored.IterationClock!.StartTick);
    76	        Assert.Equal(500, restored.IterationClock.DurationSeconds);
    77	        Assert.False(loaded.Value.PackableBuildingExecutions.IsIterationReady(
    78	            harness.JobId,
    79	            harness.WorkerId,
    80	            tick: 599).Value);
    81	        Assert.True(loaded.Value.PackableBuildingExecutions.IsIterationReady(
    82	            harness.JobId,
    83	            harness.WorkerId,
    84	            tick: 600).Value);
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry CreateRegistry()
    88	    {
    89	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new IJobDefinitionSaveCodec[]
    90	        {
    91	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec(),
    92	        });
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private static SaveMigrationPipeline CreateMigrations()
    96	    {
    97	        return new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
    98	        {
    99	            new LegacySaveVersionZeroMigration(),
   100	            new SaveVersionOneBuildingsMigration(),
   101	            new SaveVersionTwoPackingMigration(),
   102	            new SaveVersionThreeAgentSkillsMigration(),
   103	            new SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration(),
   104	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
   105	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   106	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   107	        });
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   111	    {
   112	        return BuildingBoxPlacementTestWorld.Materials();
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata()
   116	    {
   117	        return new SaveMetadataData
   118	        {
   119	            SlotId = "packable-execution",
   120	            DisplayName = "Packable execution",
   121	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-24T17:20:00Z",
   122	            SimulationTick = 150,
   123	            WorldSeed = 1,
   124	            GeneratorVersion = 1,

===== tests/Dig.Tests/PlayModeRuntimeVisibilityContractTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
     4	using Xunit;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Tests
     7	{
     8	public sealed class PlayModeRuntimeVisibilityContractTests
     9	{
    10	    [Fact]
    11	    public void Play_mode_fixtures_do_not_directly_access_internal_runtime_members()
    12	    {
    13	        string playMode = Path.Combine(
    14	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
    15	            "unity",
    16	            "Dig.Unity",
    17	            "Assets",
    18	            "Dig.Unity",
    19	            "Tests",
    20	            "PlayMode");
    21	        string barrel = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    22	            playMode,
    23	            "BarrelDestructionPlayModeTests.cs"));
    24	        string mushroom = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    25	            playMode,
    26	            "MushroomDepthProjectionPlayModeTests.cs"));
    27	        string caveRoom = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    28	            playMode,
    29	            "CaveRoomRuntimeRecoveryPlayModeTests.cs"));
    30	
    31	        Assert.Contains("using System;", barrel);
    32	        Assert.DoesNotContain("DigTunnelProjection.", barrel);
    33	        Assert.Contains("GetProjectionConstant(\"DepthOrigin\")", barrel);
    34	        Assert.Contains("GetProjectionConstant(\"DepthSpacing\")", barrel);
    35	        Assert.DoesNotContain("value.Model", mushroom);
    36	        Assert.DoesNotContain("visual.Model", mushroom);
    37	        Assert.Contains("GetProperty(visual, \"Model\")", mushroom);
    38	        Assert.Empty(Regex.Matches(
    39	            barrel,
    40	            @"(?<!UnityEngine\.)\bObject\.Destroy\("));
    41	        Assert.Equal(3, Regex.Matches(
    42	            barrel,
    43	            @"UnityEngine\.Object\.Destroy\(").Count);
    44	
    45	        Assert.DoesNotContain(
    46	            "DigCaveTemplateTrimRenderer renderer",
    47	            caveRoom);
    48	        Assert.DoesNotContain(
    49	            "AddComponent<DigCaveTemplateTrimRenderer>()",
    50	            caveRoom);
    51	        Assert.Contains(
    52	            "\"Dig.Unity.DigCaveTemplateTrimRenderer\"",
    53	            caveRoom);
    54	        Assert.Contains("root.AddComponent(rendererType)", caveRoom);
    55	        Assert.Contains(
    56	            "GetProperty<int>(renderer, \"InstanceCount\")",
    57	            caveRoom);
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
    61	    {
    62	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
    63	        while (current != null)
    64	        {
    65	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
    66	            {
    67	                return current.FullName;
    68	            }
    69	
    70	            current = current.Parent;
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
    74	    }

   139	
   140	    private static string Read(string root, string file)
   141	    {
   142	        return File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root, file));
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    private static string RuntimeRoot()
   146	    {
   147	        return Path.Combine(
   148	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
   149	            "unity",
   150	            "Dig.Unity",
   151	            "Assets",
   152	            "Dig.Unity",
   153	            "Runtime");
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   157	    {
   158	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   159	        while (current != null)
   160	        {
   161	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   162	            {
   163	                return current.FullName;
   164	            }
   165	
   166	            current = current.Parent;
   167	        }
   168	
   169	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    private static string Normalize(string value)
   173	    {
   174	        return value
   175	            .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   176	            .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   177	            .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   178	            .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   179	    }
   180	}
   181	
   182	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/ProductionOutputPlacementTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Production;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class ProductionOutputPlacementTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("terrain.air");
    17	    private static readonly MaterialId Rock = new MaterialId("terrain.rock");
    18	
    19	    [Fact]
    20	    public void Output_uses_next_free_cell_in_right_side_zone()
    21	    {
    22	        WorldState world = CreateWorld();
    23	        BuildingSnapshot building = CreateBuilding(BuildingOrientation.North);
    24	        CellId first = ProductionOutputPlacement.CreateCandidates(building, 2)[0];
    25	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(
    26	            CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems());
    27	        Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    28	            EntityId.Parse("b1000000000000000000000000000001"),
    29	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    30	            1,
    31	            ItemLocation.InWorld(first),
    32	            0).IsSuccess);
    33	
    34	        Result<CellId> result = ProductionOutputPlacement.Resolve(
    35	            building,
    36	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    37	            building.Footprint,
    38	            inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks,
    39	            2);
    40	
    41	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    42	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(first.X + 1, first.Y, first.Z), result.Value);
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    [Fact]
    46	    public void Unsupported_right_candidate_is_skipped()
    47	    {
    48	        WorldState world = CreateWorld();
    49	        BuildingSnapshot building = CreateBuilding(BuildingOrientation.North);
    50	        CellId first = ProductionOutputPlacement.CreateCandidates(building, 1)[0];
    51	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(
    52	            new CellId(first.X, first.Y + 1, first.Z),
    53	            Air,
    54	            tick: 3).IsSuccess);
    55	
    56	        Result<CellId> result = ProductionOutputPlacement.Resolve(
    57	            building,
    58	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    59	            building.Footprint,
    60	            Array.Empty<ItemStackSnapshot>(),
    61	            maximumLateralDistance: 1);
    62	
    63	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    64	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(first.X + 1, first.Y, first.Z), result.Value);
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    [Fact]
    68	    public void Occupied_right_zone_does_not_fall_back_to_other_sides()
    69	    {
    70	        WorldState world = CreateWorld();
    71	        BuildingSnapshot building = CreateBuilding(BuildingOrientation.North);
    72	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(
    73	            CampfireProductionContentTests.CreateItems());
    74	        CellId[] zone = ProductionOutputPlacement.CreateCandidates(building, 1)
    75	            .ToArray();
    76	        for (int index = 0; index < zone.Length; index++)
    77	        {
    78	            Assert.True(inventory.AddStack(
    79	                EntityId.Parse((index + 20).ToString("x32")),
    80	                CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    81	                1,
    82	                ItemLocation.InWorld(zone[index]),
    83	                0).IsSuccess);
    84	        }
    85	
    86	        Result<CellId> result = ProductionOutputPlacement.Resolve(
    87	            building,
    88	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
    89	            building.Footprint,
    90	            inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks,
    91	            maximumLateralDistance: 1);
    92	
    93	        Assert.True(result.IsFailure);
    94	        Assert.Equal(ProductionErrors.OutputSpaceUnavailable, result.Error);
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    [Fact]
    98	    public void Every_orientation_uses_same_screen_right_zone()
    99	    {
   100	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(5, 4, 0), First(BuildingOrientation.North));
   101	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(5, 4, 0), First(BuildingOrientation.East));
   102	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(5, 4, 0), First(BuildingOrientation.South));
   103	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(5, 4, 0), First(BuildingOrientation.West));
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    private static CellId First(BuildingOrientation orientation)
   107	    {
   108	        return ProductionOutputPlacement.CreateCandidates(
   109	            CreateBuilding(orientation),
   110	            0).First();
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    private static BuildingSnapshot CreateBuilding(BuildingOrientation orientation)
   114	    {
   115	        BuildingDefinition definition = CampfireBuildingBoxContent.Definition.Building;
   116	        CellId origin = new CellId(4, 4, 0);
   117	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   118	            EntityId.Parse("b2000000000000000000000000000001"),
   119	            definition,
   120	            origin,
   121	            orientation,
   122	            definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, orientation),
   123	            definition.ResolveWorkPositions(origin, orientation).First(),
   124	            BuildingStatus.Completed,
   125	            definition.RequiredWork,
   126	            definition.MaximumDurability,
   127	            1,
   128	            null);
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    private static WorldState CreateWorld()
   132	    {
   133	        MaterialCatalog materials = new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   134	        {
   135	            new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   136	            new MaterialDefinition(Rock, isSolid: true, hardness: 100),
   137	        });
   138	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
   139	            new WorldSize(10, 10),
   140	            5,
   141	            materials,
   142	            Rock,
   143	            explored: true).Value;
   144	        long tick = 1;
   145	        for (int y = 0; y < 5; y++)
   146	        {
   147	            for (int x = 0; x < 10; x++)
   148	            {
   149	                Result<WorldMutationResult> excavated = world.Excavate(
   150	                    new CellId(x, y, 0),
   151	                    Air,
   152	                    tick++);
   153	                Assert.True(excavated.IsSuccess, excavated.Error?.ToString());
   154	            }
   155	        }
   156	
   157	        world.DequeueUncommittedEvents();
   158	        return world;
   159	    }
   160	}
   161	
   162	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/ProgressiveBuildingProductionTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Production;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class ProgressiveBuildingProductionTests
    15	{
    16	    [Fact]
    17	    public void Each_material_step_consumes_one_unit_at_skill_resolved_duration()
    18	    {
    19	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness(100);
    20	        EntityId orderId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(100);
    21	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(101);
    22	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId, 2, 110);
    23	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, 1, 111);
    24	        harness.GrantSkill(AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking, 25);
    25	        Assert.True(harness.Enqueue(
    26	            orderId,
    27	            CampfireProductionContent.TentRecipeId,
    28	            1).IsSuccess);
    29	        Assert.True(harness.Prepare(jobId, 2).IsSuccess);
    30	        harness.ClaimBeginAndReachWork(orderId, jobId, 3);
    31	
    32	        ProductionOrderSnapshot started = harness.Production.Get(orderId)!;
    33	        Assert.All(started.MaterialSteps, step => Assert.Equal(75, step.RequiredTicks));
    34	        Assert.True(harness.Work(orderId, jobId, elapsedTicks: 74, tick: 6).IsSuccess);
    35	        Assert.Equal(2, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId));
    36	        Assert.True(harness.Work(orderId, jobId, elapsedTicks: 1, tick: 7).IsSuccess);
    37	        Assert.Equal(1, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId));
    38	        Assert.Equal(ProductionOrderStatus.InProgress, harness.Production.Get(orderId)!.Status);
    39	
    40	        Assert.True(harness.Work(orderId, jobId, elapsedTicks: 150, tick: 8).IsSuccess);
    41	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomLegItemId));
    42	        Assert.Equal(0, harness.Inventory.GetTotal(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId));
    43	        Assert.Equal(ProductionOrderStatus.ReadyToComplete, harness.Production.Get(orderId)!.Status);
    44	        Assert.Equal(JobStageKind.Finalize, harness.Jobs.Get(jobId)!.Stage);
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    [Fact]
    48	    public void Cooking_output_and_skill_grant_are_per_order_not_per_output_unit()
    49	    {
    50	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness(1);
    51	        EntityId orderId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(120);
    52	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(121);
    53	        EntityId outputId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(122);
    54	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, 1, 123);
    55	        Assert.True(harness.Enqueue(
    56	            orderId,
    57	            CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId,
    58	            1).IsSuccess);
    59	        Assert.True(harness.Prepare(jobId, 2).IsSuccess);
    60	        harness.ClaimBeginAndReachWork(orderId, jobId, 3);
    61	        Assert.True(harness.Work(orderId, jobId, 1, 6).IsSuccess);
    62	        CellId outputCell = new CellId(4, 2, 0);
    63	
    64	        Assert.True(harness.Complete(
    65	            orderId,
    66	            jobId,
    67	            outputId,
    68	            outputCell,
    69	            7).IsSuccess);
    70	
    71	        ItemStackSnapshot output = harness.Inventory.GetStack(outputId)!;
    72	        Assert.Equal(2, output.Quantity);
    73	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(outputCell), output.Location);
    74	        Assert.Equal(120, harness.Agents.Get(CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId)!
    75	            .CreateSnapshot(7)
    76	            .GetSkillLevel(AgentSkillCatalog.Cooking));
    77	        Assert.True(harness.Complete(
    78	            orderId,
    79	            jobId,
    80	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(124),
    81	            outputCell,
    82	            8).IsFailure);
    83	        Assert.Equal(120, harness.Agents.Get(CampfireProductionTestHarness.WorkerId)!
    84	            .CreateSnapshot(8)
    85	            .GetSkillLevel(AgentSkillCatalog.Cooking));
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    [Fact]
    89	    public void Active_order_suppresses_replenishment_until_completion()
    90	    {
    91	        CampfireProductionTestHarness harness = new CampfireProductionTestHarness(1);
    92	        EntityId orderId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(130);
    93	        EntityId jobId = CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(131);
    94	        CellId sourceCell = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    95	        harness.AddBuildingStock(CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId, 1, 132);
    96	        Assert.True(harness.Inventory.AddStack(
    97	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(133),
    98	            CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId,
    99	            3,
   100	            ItemLocation.InWorld(sourceCell),
   101	            0).IsSuccess);
   102	        harness.Enqueue(orderId, CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId, 1);
   103	        harness.Prepare(jobId, 2);
   104	        BuildingSupplySnapshot supply = harness.Supply.Get(
   105	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   106	            harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
   107	
   108	        Assert.Empty(BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
   109	            supply,
   110	            harness.Inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
   111	            new[] { sourceCell },
   112	            new[] { sourceCell },
   113	            new CellId(4, 3, 0),
   114	            4,
   115	            harness.Production.HasActiveOrder(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId))
   116	            .Allocations);
   117	
   118	        harness.ClaimBeginAndReachWork(orderId, jobId, 3);
   119	        harness.Work(orderId, jobId, 1, 6);
   120	        harness.Complete(
   121	            orderId,
   122	            jobId,
   123	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.Id(134),
   124	            new CellId(4, 2, 0),
   125	            7);
   126	        supply = harness.Supply.Get(
   127	            CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId,
   128	            harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot())!;
   129	        Assert.NotEmpty(BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
   130	            supply,
   131	            harness.Inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
   132	            new[] { sourceCell },
   133	            new[] { sourceCell },
   134	            new CellId(4, 3, 0),
   135	            4,
   136	            harness.Production.HasActiveOrder(CampfireProductionTestHarness.BuildingId))
   137	            .Allocations);
   138	    }
   139	}

    47	
    48	        Assert.Contains(
    49	            "AgentRenderer.SynchronizeWorkFacing(",
    50	            equipment);
    51	        Assert.Contains("AgentSession.TunnelVolume", equipment);
    52	        Assert.Contains("WorldSession.LoadSnapshot()", equipment);
    53	        Assert.Contains("JobStageKind.PerformWork", facing);

   173	        Assert.Contains("WorldItemFrontDepthOffset", itemPolicy);

   175	        Assert.DoesNotContain("private void OnGUI()", boxHud);
   176	        Assert.DoesNotContain("Concat(_buildingInventoryPresenter.Load())", itemProjection);
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    private static string RuntimeRoot()
   180	    {
   181	        return Path.Combine(
   182	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
   183	            "unity",
   184	            "Dig.Unity",
   185	            "Assets",
   186	            "Dig.Unity",
   187	            "Runtime");
   188	    }
   189	
   190	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   191	    {
   192	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   193	        while (current != null)
   194	        {
   195	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   196	            {
   197	                return current.FullName;
   198	            }
   199	
   200	            current = current.Parent;
   201	        }
   202	
   203	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   204	    }
   205	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/ResidentFoodMealTests.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     7	using Xunit;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Tests
    10	{
    11	    public sealed class ResidentFoodMealTests
    12	    {
    13	        [Fact]
    14	        public void Starting_meal_consumes_one_portion_and_applies_three_exact_bites()
    15	        {
    16	            Harness harness = new Harness(foodQuantity: 2, nutrition: 1_000);
    17	
    18	            Result started = harness.Start(tick: 10);
    19	
    20	            Assert.True(started.IsSuccess, started.Error?.ToString());
    21	            Assert.Equal(1, harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.StackId)!.Quantity);
    22	            FoodMealSnapshot active = Assert.IsType<FoodMealSnapshot>(
    23	                harness.Agent.CreateFoodMealSnapshot());
    24	            Assert.Equal(3, active.BiteCount);
    25	            Assert.Equal(0, active.CompletedBites);
    26	
    27	            Assert.False(harness.Advance(11));
    28	            Assert.Equal(1_500, harness.Nutrition(11));
    29	            Assert.False(harness.Advance(12));
    30	            Assert.Equal(2_000, harness.Nutrition(12));
    31	            Assert.True(harness.Advance(13));
    32	            Assert.Equal(2_500, harness.Nutrition(13));
    33	            Assert.False(harness.Agent.HasActiveFoodMeal);
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        [Fact]
    37	        public void Runtime_snapshot_restores_completed_bites_without_replaying_nutrition()
    38	        {
    39	            Harness harness = new Harness(foodQuantity: 1, nutrition: 1_000);
    40	            Assert.True(harness.Start(10).IsSuccess);
    41	            Assert.False(harness.Advance(11));
    42	            AgentRuntimeSnapshot saved = harness.Agent.CreateRuntimeSnapshot();
    43	            AgentState restored = new AgentState(
    44	                harness.ResidentId,
    45	                "Restored cook",
    46	                new AgentNeedsSnapshot(
    47	                    new NeedValue(9_000),
    48	                    new NeedValue(9_000),
    49	                    new NeedValue(9_000),
    50	                    new NeedValue(10_000)),
    51	                new DailySchedule(
    52	                    ticksPerDay: 24,
    53	                    new[] { new ScheduleSegment(0, 24, ScheduleActivity.Work) }));
    54	
    55	            Result result = restored.RestoreRuntime(saved);
    56	
    57	            Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
    58	            Assert.Equal(1_500, restored.CreateSnapshot(11).Needs.Nutrition.Points);
    59	            Assert.Equal(1, restored.CreateFoodMealSnapshot()!.CompletedBites);
    60	            Assert.Equal(AgentIntentKind.Eat, restored.CreateSnapshot(11).ActiveAction!.Value.IntentKind);
    61	            Assert.False(restored.AdvanceFoodMealBite(12).Value);
    62	            Assert.True(restored.AdvanceFoodMealBite(13).Value);
    63	            Assert.Equal(2_500, restored.CreateSnapshot(13).Needs.Nutrition.Points);
    64	            Assert.False(restored.HasActiveFoodMeal);
    65	        }
    66	
    67	        [Fact]
    68	        public void Interrupted_meal_keeps_completed_bites_and_loses_consumed_remainder()
    69	        {
    70	            Harness harness = new Harness(foodQuantity: 1, nutrition: 1_000);
    71	            Assert.True(harness.Start(10).IsSuccess);
    72	            Assert.False(harness.Advance(11));
    73	
    74	            Result interrupted = harness.Agent.InterruptFoodMeal(
    75	                "direct_command_replaced",
    76	                tick: 12);
    77	
    78	            Assert.True(interrupted.IsSuccess, interrupted.Error?.ToString());
    79	            Assert.Null(harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.StackId));
    80	            Assert.Equal(1_500, harness.Nutrition(12));
    81	            Assert.False(harness.Agent.HasActiveFoodMeal);
    82	            Result<bool> next = harness.Agent.AdvanceFoodMealBite(13);
    83	            Assert.True(next.IsFailure);
    84	            Assert.Equal(FoodMealErrors.NotActive, next.Error);
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        [Fact]
    88	        public void Unsupported_carried_item_is_not_consumed()
    89	        {
    90	            Harness harness = new Harness(foodQuantity: 1, nutrition: 1_000, useFood: false);
    91	
    92	            Result result = harness.Start(10);
    93	
    94	            Assert.True(result.IsFailure);
    95	            Assert.Equal(ResidentFoodMealErrors.UnsupportedFood, result.Error);
    96	            Assert.Equal(1, harness.Inventory.GetStack(harness.StackId)!.Quantity);
    97	            Assert.False(harness.Agent.HasActiveFoodMeal);
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        private sealed class Harness
   101	        {
   102	            private static readonly ItemId Rock = new ItemId("material.rock");
   103	
   104	            internal Harness(int foodQuantity, int nutrition, bool useFood = true)
   105	            {
   106	                ResidentId = Id(1);
   107	                StackId = Id(2);
   108	                ItemCatalog catalog = new ItemCatalog(new[]
   109	                {
   110	                    new ItemDefinition(
   111	                        CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomItemId,
   112	                        "Grilled mushroom",
   113	                        100,
   114	                        false,
   115	                        new[] { CampfireProductionContent.FoodCategoryId }),
   116	                    new ItemDefinition(Rock, "Rock", 100, false),
   117	                });
   118	                Inventory = new InventoryState(catalog);
   119	                Assert.True(Inventory.AddStack(
   120	                    StackId,
   121	                    useFood ? CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomItemId : Rock,
   122	                    foodQuantity,
   123	                    ItemLocation.InAgent(ResidentId),
   124	                    tick: 1).IsSuccess);
   125	                InventoryRepository = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(Inventory);
   126	                Agents = new InMemoryAgentRepository();
   127	                Agent = new AgentState(
   128	                    ResidentId,
   129	                    "Cook",
   130	                    new AgentNeedsSnapshot(
   131	                        new NeedValue(nutrition),
   132	                        new NeedValue(10_000),
   133	                        new NeedValue(10_000),
   134	                        new NeedValue(10_000)),
   135	                    new DailySchedule(
   136	                        ticksPerDay: 24,
   137	                        new[] { new ScheduleSegment(0, 24, ScheduleActivity.Work) }));
   138	                Assert.True(Agents.Add(Agent).IsSuccess);
   139	                Journal = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
   140	                Handler = new StartResidentFoodMealHandler(
   141	                    Agents,
   142	                    InventoryRepository,
   143	                    Journal);
   144	            }
   145	
   146	            internal EntityId ResidentId { get; }
   147	            internal EntityId StackId { get; }
   148	            internal InventoryState Inventory { get; }
   149	            internal InMemoryInventoryRepository InventoryRepository { get; }
   150	            internal InMemoryAgentRepository Agents { get; }
   151	            internal InMemoryExecutionJournal Journal { get; }
   152	            internal AgentState Agent { get; }
   153	            internal StartResidentFoodMealHandler Handler { get; }
   154	
   155	            internal Result Start(long tick)
   156	            {
   157	                return Handler.Handle(new StartResidentFoodMealCommand(
   158	                    ResidentId,
   159	                    StackId,
   160	                    tick));
   161	            }
   162	
   163	            internal bool Advance(long tick)
   164	            {
   165	                Result<bool> result = Agent.AdvanceFoodMealBite(tick);
   166	                Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, result.Error?.ToString());
   167	                return result.Value;
   168	            }
   169	
   170	            internal int Nutrition(long tick)
   171	            {
   172	                return Agent.CreateSnapshot(tick).Needs.Nutrition.Points;
   173	            }
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        private static EntityId Id(int value)
   177	        {
   178	            return EntityId.Parse(value.ToString("x32"));
   179	        }
   180	    }
   181	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/ResidentMovementModeResolverTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     7	using Xunit;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Tests
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class ResidentMovementModeResolverTests
    13	{
    14	    private static readonly EntityId ResidentId =
    15	        EntityId.Parse("40000000000000000000000000000042");
    16	
    17	    [Fact]
    18	    public void Vertical_traversal_overrides_carrying_and_fast_modes()
    19	    {
    20	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
    21	            traversal: TunnelTraversalKind.VerticalClimb,
    22	            repeated: true,
    23	            carriesBox: true,
    24	            hoverboard: true,
    25	            intent: AgentIntentKind.Flee));
    26	
    27	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Climbing, resolution.Mode);
    28	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementModeReason.VerticalTraversal, resolution.Reason);
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    [Fact]
    32	    public void Building_box_disables_fast_and_personal_mobility()
    33	    {
    34	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver(
    35	            automaticMobilityMinimumRemainingSteps: 2).Resolve(Request(
    36	                repeated: true,
    37	                remainingSteps: 10,
    38	                carriesBox: true,
    39	                hoverboard: true));
    40	
    41	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Carrying, resolution.Mode);
    42	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMobilityKind.Hoverboard, resolution.Mobility);
    43	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementModeReason.BuildingBoxCarried, resolution.Reason);
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    [Fact]
    47	    public void Hoverboard_has_priority_and_repeat_activates_personal_mobility()
    48	    {
    49	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
    50	            repeated: true,
    51	            rideHamster: true,
    52	            hoverboard: true));
    53	
    54	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Mobility, resolution.Mode);
    55	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMobilityKind.Hoverboard, resolution.Mobility);
    56	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementModeReason.HoverboardAvailable, resolution.Reason);
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    [Fact]
    60	    public void Fleeing_wins_over_forced_fast_without_personal_mobility()
    61	    {
    62	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
    63	            repeated: true,
    64	            intent: AgentIntentKind.Flee));
    65	
    66	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Fleeing, resolution.Mode);
    67	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementModeReason.FleeIntent, resolution.Reason);
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    [Fact]
    71	    public void Repeated_manual_destination_uses_forced_fast_mode()
    72	    {
    73	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
    74	            repeated: true));
    75	
    76	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.ForcedFast, resolution.Mode);
    77	        Assert.Equal(
    78	            ResidentMovementModeReason.RepeatedManualCommand,
    79	            resolution.Reason);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    [Fact]
    83	    public void Critical_alertness_uses_tired_mode()
    84	    {
    85	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
    86	            alertness: 2_000));
    87	
    88	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Tired, resolution.Mode);
    89	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementModeReason.CriticalAlertness, resolution.Reason);
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    [Fact]
    93	    public void Custom_data_definition_controls_cadence_and_visual_duration()
    94	    {
    95	        ResidentMovementModeCatalog catalog = new ResidentMovementModeCatalog(
    96	            Enum.GetValues(typeof(ResidentMovementMode))
    97	                .Cast<ResidentMovementMode>()
    98	                .Select(mode => new ResidentMovementModeDefinition(
    99	                    mode,
   100	                    speedMultiplier: mode == ResidentMovementMode.Tired ? 0.5d : 1d,
   101	                    transitionDurationMultiplier:
   102	                        mode == ResidentMovementMode.Tired ? 1.75d : 1d))
   103	                .ToArray());
   104	        ResidentMovementModeResolver resolver = new ResidentMovementModeResolver(
   105	            new ResidentMovementModePolicy(2_000, null, catalog));
   106	
   107	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = resolver.Resolve(Request(
   108	            alertness: 1_000,
   109	            inventorySpeed: 0.8d));
   110	
   111	        Assert.Equal(0.4d, resolution.EffectiveSpeedMultiplier, 8);
   112	        Assert.Equal(0.4d, resolution.AuthoritativeCadenceMultiplier, 8);
   113	        Assert.Equal(1.75d, resolution.TransitionDurationMultiplier, 8);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    [Fact]
   117	    public void Default_policy_does_not_invent_legacy_autodistance_threshold()
   118	    {
   119	        ResidentMovementModeResolution resolution = Resolver().Resolve(Request(
   120	            remainingSteps: 100,
   121	            hoverboard: true));
   122	
   123	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMobilityKind.Hoverboard, resolution.Mobility);
   124	        Assert.Equal(ResidentMovementMode.Normal, resolution.Mode);
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    private static ResidentMovementModeResolver Resolver(
   128	        int? automaticMobilityMinimumRemainingSteps = null)
   129	    {
   130	        return new ResidentMovementModeResolver(new ResidentMovementModePolicy(
   131	            2_000,
   132	            automaticMobilityMinimumRemainingSteps,
   133	            ResidentMovementModeCatalog.CreateNeutralDefaults()));
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    private static ResidentMovementModeRequest Request(
   137	        int alertness = 8_000,
   138	        AgentIntentKind intent = AgentIntentKind.Work,
   139	        TunnelTraversalKind traversal = TunnelTraversalKind.SupportedWalk,
   140	        bool repeated = false,
   141	        int remainingSteps = 1,
   142	        double inventorySpeed = 1d,
   143	        bool carriesBox = false,
   144	        bool rideHamster = false,
   145	        bool hoverboard = false)
   146	    {
   147	        return new ResidentMovementModeRequest(
   148	            ResidentId,
   149	            alertness,
   150	            intent,
   151	            ResidentMovementCommandSource.Manual,
   152	            traversal,
   153	            repeated,
   154	            remainingSteps,
   155	            inventorySpeed,
   156	            carriesBox,
   157	            rideHamster,
   158	            hoverboard);
   159	    }
   160	}

   162	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/RuntimeInteractionRootCauseContractTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.World;
     5	using Xunit;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Tests
     8	{
     9	    public sealed class RuntimeInteractionRootCauseContractTests
    10	    {
    11	        [Fact]
    12	        public void Excavation_cadence_is_external_to_quarter_reservation()
    13	        {
    14	            ExcavationWorkCoordinator coordinator = new ExcavationWorkCoordinator();
    15	            ExcavationWorkTarget target = new ExcavationWorkTarget(
    16	                new CellId(4, 4, 0),
    17	                0);
    18	            EntityId worker = EntityId.Parse(
    19	                "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000041");
    20	            ExcavationWorkerAssignment assignment = coordinator.Assign(
    21	                worker,
    22	                target,
    23	                ExcavationApproachSide.Left,
    24	                miningSkill: 0);
    25	            ExcavationQuarter reserved = assignment.ReservedQuarters;
    26	
    27	            ExcavationQuarterCompletion completion = Assert.Single(
    28	                coordinator.ApplyWork(worker));
    29	
    30	            Assert.Equal(reserved, completion.Quarter);
    31	            Assert.Equal(reserved, coordinator.GetState(target).Completed);
    32	            Assert.Contains(coordinator.GetProgress(), value =>
    33	                value.Target.Equals(target)
    34	                && value.Completed == reserved);
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        [Fact]
    38	        public void Runtime_routes_objects_before_movement_and_uses_explicit_placement_modes()
    39	        {
    40	            string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    41	            string priority = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.ResidentCommandPriority.cs");
    42	            string interaction = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.cs");
    43	            string boxes = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.BuildingBoxes.cs");
    44	            string boxGhost = Read(runtime, "DigBuildingBoxGhostRenderer.Representatives.cs");
    45	            string itemPlacement = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.InventoryItemPlacement.cs");
    46	            string itemGhost = Read(runtime, "DigInventoryItemGhostRenderer.cs");
    47	            string itemVisual = Read(runtime, "DigWorldItemVisual.cs");
    48	            string itemSelection = Read(runtime, "DigWorldItemVisual.Selection.cs");
    49	
    50	            Assert.True(
    51	                priority.IndexOf("TryResolveCompletedBuildingHit", StringComparison.Ordinal)
    52	                < priority.IndexOf("TryApplyTunnelMove", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    53	            Assert.Contains("TryHandleBuildingPlacementClick()", interaction);
    54	            Assert.Contains("TryHandleInventoryItemPlacementClick()", interaction);
    55	            Assert.Contains("ConfirmBuildingPlacement();", boxes);
    56	            Assert.Contains("BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox", boxGhost);
    57	            Assert.Contains("BuildingVisualState.BuildingBox", boxGhost);
    58	            Assert.Contains("BuildingVisualState.Completed", boxGhost);
    59	            Assert.Contains("ValidateResidentInventoryPlacement", itemPlacement);
    60	            Assert.Contains("CreateResidentInventoryPlacement", itemPlacement);
    61	            Assert.Contains("DigTransparentVisualSurface", itemGhost);
    62	            Assert.Contains("_interactionCollider.isTrigger=true", itemVisual);
    63	            Assert.Contains("SetInteractionHighlighted", itemSelection);
    64	        }
    65	
    66	        [Fact]
    67	        public void Runtime_gates_job_finalization_on_visible_quarter_progress()
    68	        {
    69	            string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    70	            string terrain = Read(runtime, "DigTerrainWorkSession.cs");
    71	            string spatial = Read(runtime, "DigTerrainSpatialExcavation.cs");

   194	            string stroke = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.Excavation.cs");

   196	                runtime,

   199	                runtime,

   218	            Assert.Contains("renderer.enabled=!excavated", marker);
   219	            Assert.DoesNotContain("ExcavatedColor", marker);

   221	            Assert.Contains("SetExcavationQuarterProgress", cursor);
   222	            Assert.Contains("OrderBy(value=>value!.TargetDistance)", nearest);
   223	            Assert.Contains("AssignedAgentId.GetValueOrDefault()", nearest);
   224	            Assert.DoesNotContain("AssignedAgentId.Value", nearest);
   225	            Assert.Contains("StageExcavationCell(target,active)", stroke);
   226	            Assert.Contains("CommitPendingExcavationStroke()", stroke);
   227	            Assert.Contains("usingDig.Application.Jobs;", strokeBatch);
   228	            Assert.Contains("StageExcavationDesignation", strokeBatch);
   229	            Assert.Contains("CommitExcavationDesignationBatch", strokeBatch);
   230	            Assert.Contains("StageExcavationDesignation", driver);
   231	            Assert.Contains("CommitExcavationDesignationBatch", driver);
   232	            Assert.Contains("AssignNearestAutomaticDigJobs(agents,cells,tick)", designations);
   233	            Assert.Contains("AssignNearestAutomaticSpatialJobs(agents,tick)", spatial);
   234	            Assert.Equal(
   235	                2,
   236	                CountOccurrences(roomPreview, "_overlays!.ConfigureLineRenderer("));
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        private static int CountOccurrences(string source, string value)
   240	        {
   241	            int count = 0;
   242	            int startIndex = 0;
   243	            while (true)
   244	            {
   245	                int index = source.IndexOf(value, startIndex, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   246	                if (index < 0)
   247	                {
   248	                    return count;
   249	                }
   250	
   251	                count++;
   252	                startIndex = index + value.Length;
   253	            }
   254	        }
   255	
   256	        private static string Read(string runtime, string file)
   257	        {
   258	            return Normalize(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(runtime, file)));
   259	        }
   260	
   261	        private static string RuntimeRoot()
   262	        {
   263	            return Path.Combine(
   264	                FindRepositoryRoot(),
   265	                "unity",
   266	                "Dig.Unity",
   267	                "Assets",
   268	                "Dig.Unity",
   269	                "Runtime");
   270	        }
   271	
   272	        private static string Normalize(string source)
   273	        {
   274	            return source
   275	                .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   276	                .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   277	                .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   278	                .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   279	        }
   280	
   281	        private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   282	        {
   283	            DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   284	            while (current != null)
   285	            {
   286	                if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   287	                {
   288	                    return current.FullName;
   289	                }
   290	
   291	                current = current.Parent;
   292	            }
   293	
   294	            throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   295	        }
   296	    }
   297	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/SaveMigrationAndCorruptionTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Infrastructure.Saving;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	public sealed class SaveMigrationAndCorruptionTests
    14	{
    15	    private static readonly MaterialId Rock = new MaterialId("terrain.rock");
    16	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("ore.test");
    17	    private static readonly EntityId StackId = Id("fa000000000000000000000000000001");
    18	    private static readonly EntityId JobId = Id("fb000000000000000000000000000002");
    19	
    20	    [Fact]
    21	    public void Version_zero_fixture_migrates_in_order_and_is_idempotent()
    22	    {
    23	        string fixturePath = Path.Combine(
    24	            AppContext.BaseDirectory,
    25	            "Fixtures",
    26	            "save-v0.json");
    27	        SaveGameDocument document = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec().Deserialize(
    28	            File.ReadAllBytes(fixturePath));
    29	        SaveMigrationPipeline pipeline = CreateMigrations();
    30	
    31	        Result<SaveMigrationReport> first = pipeline.Apply(document);
    32	        Result<SaveMigrationReport> replay = pipeline.Apply(document);
    33	
    34	        Assert.True(first.IsSuccess);
    35	        Assert.Equal(new[]
    36	        {
    37	            "save.v0_to_v1.metadata",
    38	            "save.v1_to_v2.buildings",
    39	            "save.v2_to_v3.packing",
    40	            "save.v3_to_v4.agent_skills",
    41	            "save.v4_to_v5.authoritative_xyz",
    42	            "save.v5_to_v6.mushrooms",
    43	            "save.v6_to_v7.building_production",
    44	            "save.v7_to_v8.world_excavation_progress",
    45	            "save.v8_to_v9.agent_runtime",
    46	            "save.v9_to_v10.combat_spatial",
    47	        }, first.Value.AppliedSteps);
    48	        Assert.Equal(SaveFormat.CurrentVersion, document.FormatVersion);
    49	        Assert.Equal(1, document.Metadata.GeneratorVersion);
    50	        Assert.Equal("legacy", document.Metadata.DisplayName);
    51	        Assert.NotNull(document.Buildings);
    52	        Assert.Empty(document.Buildings.Buildings);
    53	        Assert.Equal(4, document.World.Depth);
    54	        Assert.NotNull(document.AgentPositions);
    55	        Assert.Empty(document.AgentPositions.Agents);
    56	        Assert.NotNull(document.AgentRuntime);
    57	        Assert.Empty(document.AgentRuntime.Agents);
    58	        Assert.True(replay.IsSuccess);
    59	        Assert.Empty(replay.Value.AppliedSteps);
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    [Fact]
    63	    public void Version_three_fixture_adds_empty_agent_skills_once()
    64	    {
    65	        string fixturePath = Path.Combine(
    66	            AppContext.BaseDirectory,
    67	            "Fixtures",
    68	            "save-v3.json");
    69	        SaveGameDocument document = new DataContractJsonSaveCodec().Deserialize(

    84	                "save.v7_to_v8.world_excavation_progress",
    85	                "save.v8_to_v9.agent_runtime",
    86	                "save.v9_to_v10.combat_spatial",
    87	            },
    88	            first.Value.AppliedSteps);
    89	        Assert.Equal(SaveFormat.CurrentVersion, document.FormatVersion);
    90	        Assert.NotNull(document.AgentSkills);
    91	        Assert.Empty(document.AgentSkills.Agents);
    92	        Assert.Equal(4, document.World.Depth);
    93	        Assert.NotNull(document.AgentPositions);
    94	        Assert.Empty(document.AgentPositions.Agents);
    95	        Assert.NotNull(document.AgentRuntime);
    96	        Assert.Empty(document.AgentRuntime.Agents);
    97	        Assert.True(replay.IsSuccess);
    98	        Assert.Empty(replay.Value.AppliedSteps);
    99	    }
   100	    [Fact]

   102	    {

   115	    {

   117	        ItemCatalog items = CreateItems();
   118	        SaveGameDocument unknownJob = CreateValidDocument(materials, items);
   119	        unknownJob.Jobs.Jobs[0].Definition.TypeId = "job.unknown.v99";
   120	        SaveGameDocument unknownItem = CreateValidDocument(materials, items);
   121	        unknownItem.Inventory.Stacks[0].ItemId = "item.missing";
   122	        SaveGameLoader loader = CreateLoader();
   123	
   124	        Result<LoadedGameState> jobResult = loader.Load(unknownJob, materials, items);
   125	        Result<LoadedGameState> itemResult = loader.Load(unknownItem, materials, items);
   126

   150	
   151	    [Fact]
   152	    public void Corrupted_slot_is_visible_and_load_fails_with_controlled_reason()
   153	    {
   154	        string directory = CreateTempDirectory();
   155	        try
   156	        {
   157	            FileSaveSlotStore store = new FileSaveSlotStore(
   158	                directory,
   159	                new DataContractJsonSaveCodec());
   160	            store.Save("broken", MinimalDocument("broken"));
   161	            File.WriteAllText(
   162	                Path.Combine(directory, "broken.digsave"),
   163	                "{not valid json");
   164	
   165	            SaveSlotInfo slot = Assert.Single(store.List());
   166	            SaveStorageException exception = Assert.Throws<SaveStorageException>(
   167	                () => store.Load("broken"));
   168	
   169	            Assert.True(slot.IsCorrupted);
   170	            Assert.Null(slot.Metadata);
   171	            Assert.Equal(SaveStorageFailureKind.Corrupted, exception.Kind);
   172	        }
   173	        finally
   174	        {
   175	            Directory.Delete(directory, recursive: true);
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    [Fact]
   180	    public void Interrupted_atomic_replacement_recovers_previous_slot()
   181	    {
   182	        string directory = CreateTempDirectory();
   183	        try
   184	        {
   185	            FileSaveSlotStore store = new FileSaveSlotStore(
   186	                directory,
   187	                new DataContractJsonSaveCodec());
   188	            SaveGameDocument original = MinimalDocument("checkpoint");
   189	            original.Metadata.DisplayName = "Previous complete save";
   190	            store.Save("checkpoint", original);
   191	            string target = Path.Combine(directory, "checkpoint.digsave");
   192	            File.Move(target, target + ".bak");
   193	            File.WriteAllText(target + ".tmp", "partial write");
   194	
   195	            SaveGameDocument recovered = store.Load("checkpoint");
   196	
   197	            Assert.Equal("Previous complete save", recovered.Metadata.DisplayName);
   198	            Assert.True(File.Exists(target));
   199	            Assert.False(File.Exists(target + ".tmp"));
   200	            Assert.False(File.Exists(target + ".bak"));
   201	        }
   202	        finally
   203	        {
   204	            Directory.Delete(directory, recursive: true);
   205	        }
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    [Fact]
   209	    public void Overwrite_commits_new_document_without_temp_or_backup_files()
   210	    {
   211	        string directory = CreateTempDirectory();
   212	        try
   213	        {
   214	            FileSaveSlotStore store = new FileSaveSlotStore(
   215	                directory,
   216	                new DataContractJsonSaveCodec());
   217	            SaveGameDocument first = MinimalDocument("slot-1");
   218	            first.Metadata.DisplayName = "First";
   219	            SaveGameDocument second = MinimalDocument("slot-1");
   220	            second.Metadata.DisplayName = "Second";
   221	
   222	            store.Save("slot-1", first);
   223	            store.Save("slot-1", second);
   224	            SaveGameDocument loaded = store.Load("slot-1");
   225	
   226	            Assert.Equal("Second", loaded.Metadata.DisplayName);
   227	            Assert.False(File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory, "slot-1.digsave.tmp")));
   228	            Assert.False(File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory, "slot-1.digsave.bak")));
   229	        }
   230	        finally
   231	        {
   232	            Directory.Delete(directory, recursive: true);
   233	        }
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    private static SaveGameDocument CreateValidDocument(
   237	        MaterialCatalog materials,
   238	        ItemCatalog items)
   239	    {
   240	        WorldState world = WorldState.CreateFilled(
   241	            new WorldSize(4, 4),
   242	            chunkSize: 2,
   243	            materials,
   244	            Rock,
   245	            explored: true).Value;
   246	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(items);
   247	        inventory.AddStack(
   248	            StackId,
   249	            Ore,
   250	            quantity: 5,
   251	            ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1)),
   252	            tick: 0);
   253	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
   254	        jobs.Add(new DigJobDefinition(
   255	            JobId,
   256	            new DigJobTarget(new CellId(2, 2)),
   257	            priority: 1,
   258	            createdTick: 0,
   259	            JobRetryPolicy.Default));
   260	        return new SaveGameBuilder(CreateRegistry()).Build(new SaveGameContext(
   261	            Metadata("valid"),
   262	            world,
   263	            inventory,
   264	            jobs));
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    private static SaveGameDocument MinimalDocument(string slotId)
   268	    {
   269	        return new SaveGameDocument
   270	        {
   271	            FormatVersion = SaveFormat.CurrentVersion,
   272	            Metadata = Metadata(slotId),
   273	            World = new WorldSaveData(),
   274	            Inventory = new InventorySaveData(),
   275	            Jobs = new JobsSaveData(),
   276	        };
   277	    }
   278	
   279	    private static SaveMetadataData Metadata(string slotId)
   280	    {
   281	        return new SaveMetadataData
   282	        {
   283	            SlotId = slotId,
   284	            DisplayName = slotId,
   285	            SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-15T15:00:00Z",
   286	            SimulationTick = 0,
   287	            WorldSeed = 1,
   288	            GeneratorVersion = 1,
   289	        };
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    private static SaveGameLoader CreateLoader()
   293	    {
   294	        return new SaveGameLoader(CreateMigrations(), CreateRegistry());
   295	    }

   298	    {

   303	            new SaveVersionTwoPackingMigration(),
   304	            new SaveVersionThreeAgentSkillsMigration(),
   305	            new SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration(),
   306	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
   307	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
   308	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
   309	            new SaveVersionEightAgentRuntimeMigration(),
   310	            new SaveVersionNineCombatSpatialMigration(),
   311	        });
   312	    }
   313	
   314	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry CreateRegistry()
   315	    {
   316	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(new[]
   317	        {
   318	            new DigJobDefinitionSaveCodec(),
   319	        });
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    private static MaterialCatalog CreateMaterials()
   323	    {

   325	        {

   327	        });
   328	    }
   329

   331	    {
   332	        return new ItemCatalog(new[]
   333	        {

   335	        });
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    private static string CreateTempDirectory()
   339	    {

   341	            Path.GetTempPath(),

===== tests/Dig.Tests/SettlementItemSummaryPresenterTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Presentation.Management;
    10	using Xunit;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Tests
    13	{
    14	
    15	public sealed class SettlementItemSummaryPresenterTests
    16	{
    17	    private static readonly EntityId BuildingId = Id('b');
    18	    private static readonly EntityId StorageId = Id('c');
    19	
    20	    [Fact]
    21	    public void Summary_groups_items_and_breaks_container_quantities_out()
    22	    {
    23	        ItemDefinition stone = Item("material.stone", "Stone", "material.raw");
    24	        ItemDefinition ironOre = Item("ore.iron", "Iron ore", "material.raw");
    25	        ItemDefinition sword = Item("weapon.sword", "Sword", "weapon");
    26	        ItemDefinition meal = Item("food.stew", "Stew", "food");
    27	        ItemDefinition potion = Item("potion.health", "Health potion", "potion");
    28	        ItemDefinition pickaxe = new ItemDefinition(
    29	            new ItemId("tool.pickaxe"),
    30	            "Pickaxe",
    31	            maximumStackSize: 1,
    32	            isTool: true);
    33	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[]
    34	        {
    35	            stone,
    36	            ironOre,
    37	            sword,
    38	            meal,
    39	            potion,
    40	            pickaxe,
    41	        }));
    42	        Add(inventory, Id('1'), stone.Id, 4, ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(1, 1)));
    43	        Add(inventory, Id('2'), stone.Id, 6, ItemLocation.InBuilding(BuildingId));
    44	        Add(inventory, Id('3'), ironOre.Id, 3, ItemLocation.InStorage(StorageId));
    45	        Add(inventory, Id('4'), sword.Id, 1, ItemLocation.InAgent(Id('a')));
    46	        Add(inventory, Id('5'), meal.Id, 2, ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(2, 1)));
    47	        Add(inventory, Id('6'), potion.Id, 1, ItemLocation.InWorld(new CellId(3, 1)));
    48	        Add(inventory, Id('7'), pickaxe.Id, 1, ItemLocation.EquippedBy(Id('a')));
    49	
    50	        SettlementItemSummaryViewModel result = new SettlementItemSummaryPresenter()
    51	            .Present(
    52	                inventory.CreateSnapshot(),
    53	                inventory.Catalog,
    54	                new[] { Building(BuildingId, "Stone Warehouse") });
    55	
    56	        SettlementItemLocationViewModel warehouse = Assert.Single(
    57	            result.Locations,
    58	            value => value.Label == "Stone Warehouse");
    59	        Assert.Contains(result.Locations, value => value.Label.StartsWith(
    60	            "Storage ",
    61	            StringComparison.Ordinal));
    62	        SettlementItemSummaryRowViewModel stones = Assert.Single(
    63	            result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Materials),
    64	            value => value.Id == "material.stone");
    65	        Assert.Equal(10, stones.Total);
    66	        Assert.Equal(6, stones.GetQuantity(warehouse.Id));
    67	        Assert.Equal(
    68	            3,
    69	            Assert.Single(
    70	                result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Materials),
    71	                value => value.Id == "ore.iron").Total);
    72	        Assert.Equal(
    73	            "Sword",
    74	            Assert.Single(result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Weapons)).Label);
    75	        Assert.Equal(
    76	            "Stew",
    77	            Assert.Single(result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Food)).Label);
    78	        Assert.Equal(
    79	            "Health potion",
    80	            Assert.Single(result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Potions)).Label);
    81	        Assert.Contains(
    82	            result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Tools),
    83	            value => value.Label == "Pickaxe");
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    [Fact]
    87	    public void Materials_keep_the_requested_rows_when_inventory_is_empty()
    88	    {
    89	        ItemDefinition unrelated = Item("misc.test", "Other", "misc");
    90	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[] { unrelated }));
    91	
    92	        SettlementItemSummaryViewModel result = new SettlementItemSummaryPresenter()
    93	            .Present(
    94	                inventory.CreateSnapshot(),
    95	                inventory.Catalog,
    96	                Array.Empty<BuildingWorldViewModel>());
    97	
    98	        string[] ids = result.GetRows(SettlementItemGroup.Materials)
    99	            .Select(value => value.Id)
   100	            .ToArray();
   101	        Assert.Contains("material.stone", ids);
   102	        Assert.Contains("material.mushroom_stem", ids);
   103	        Assert.Contains("material.mushroom_cap", ids);
   104	        Assert.Contains("ore.gold", ids);
   105	        Assert.Contains("ore.iron", ids);
   106	        Assert.Contains("material.coal", ids);
   107	        Assert.Contains("ore.crystal", ids);
   108	        Assert.Contains("material.gold", ids);
   109	        Assert.Contains("material.iron", ids);
   110	        Assert.Contains("material.crystal", ids);
   111	        Assert.Contains("creature.hamster", ids);
   112	        Assert.Contains("creature.grub", ids);
   113	    }
