===== tests/Dig.Tests/UnityInputParserMergeRegressionTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Dig.Presentation.Input;
     4	using Xunit;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Tests
     7	{
     8	public sealed class UnityInputParserMergeRegressionTests
     9	{
    10	    [Fact]
    11	    public void Cursor_and_pointer_partials_keep_compile_safe_method_boundaries()
    12	    {
    13	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    14	        string cursor = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.DirectCommandCursor.cs");
    15	        string itemCursor = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.ItemInteractionCursor.cs");
    16	        string pointerHits = Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.PointerHits.cs");
    17	        string normalizedCursor = Normalize(cursor);
    18	        string normalizedItemCursor = Normalize(itemCursor);
    19	        string normalizedPointerHits = Normalize(pointerHits);
    20	
    21	        Assert.Equal(Count(cursor, '{'), Count(cursor, '}'));
    22	        Assert.Equal(Count(itemCursor, '{'), Count(itemCursor, '}'));
    23	        Assert.Equal(Count(pointerHits, '{'), Count(pointerHits, '}'));
    24	        Assert.Contains("TryResolveFoodItemHoverTarget", normalizedCursor);
    25	        Assert.Contains(
    26	            "privateboolTryResolveFoodItemHoverTarget",
    27	            normalizedItemCursor);
    28	        Assert.Contains(
    29	            "privatevoidSetInteractionHighlightedItem",
    30	            normalizedItemCursor);
    31	        Assert.Contains(
    32	            "best=candidate;}}agent=best!;returnbest!=null;}privateboolTryProjectResidentBounds",
    33	            normalizedPointerHits);
    34	        Assert.Contains("Eat=6", normalizedCursor);
    35	        Assert.Contains("Sword=5", normalizedCursor);
    36	        Assert.Contains("Drop=7", normalizedCursor);
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    [Fact]
    40	    public void Food_and_barrel_input_identities_are_distinct()
    41	    {
    42	        Assert.NotEqual(
    43	            (int)ContextWorldTargetKind.FoodItem,
    44	            (int)ContextWorldTargetKind.Barrel);
    45	        Assert.NotEqual(
    46	            (int)ApplicationInputCommandKind.EatWorldItem,
    47	            (int)ApplicationInputCommandKind.AttackBarrel);
    48	        Assert.Equal(8, (int)ContextWorldTargetKind.Barrel);
    49	        Assert.Equal(10, (int)ApplicationInputCommandKind.AttackBarrel);
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    [Fact]
    53	    public void Bootstrap_binds_each_world_interaction_dependency_once()
    54	    {
    55	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    56	        string bootstrap = Normalize(Read(runtime, "DigUnityBootstrap.cs"));
    57	        string interaction = Normalize(Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.cs"));
    58	        const string expectedCall =
    59	            "interaction.Initialize(targetCamera,cameraController,worldSession,worldRenderer,"
    60	            + "agentRenderer,creatureRenderer,mushroomRenderer,barrelRenderer,jobRenderer,"
    61	            + "buildingRenderer,buildingInternalStockRenderer,itemRenderer,ghostRenderer,"
    62	            + "terrainSession,stockpileRenderer,agentSession,simulation,hud);";
    63	
    64	        Assert.Equal(1, Count(bootstrap, "interaction.Initialize("));
    65	        Assert.Contains(expectedCall, bootstrap);
    66	        Assert.Contains("DigBarrelRendererbarrelRenderer", interaction);
    67	        Assert.Contains("_barrelRenderer=barrelRenderer;", interaction);
    68	        Assert.Contains("&&_barrelRenderer!=null", interaction);
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    [Fact]
    72	    public void World_consumable_commands_use_the_overlay_HUD_contract()
    73	    {
    74	        string runtime = RuntimeRoot();
    75	        string interaction = Normalize(Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.cs"));
    76	        string worldFood = Normalize(Read(runtime, "DigWorldInteraction.WorldFood.cs"));
    77	
    78	        Assert.Contains("privateDigHudOverlay?_hud;", interaction);
    79	        Assert.Contains("DigHudOverlayhud=_hud!;", worldFood);
    80	        Assert.DoesNotContain("DigGameHudCanvashud=_hud!;", worldFood);
    81	        Assert.Contains("hud.SetCommandResult(effectOwner);", worldFood);
    82	        Assert.Contains("hud.SetJobs(jobs);", worldFood);
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    private static int Count(string source, char value)
    86	    {
    87	        int count = 0;
    88	        for (int index = 0; index < source.Length; index++)
    89	        {
    90	            if (source[index] == value)
    91	            {
    92	                count++;
    93	            }
    94	        }
    95	
    96	        return count;
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    private static int Count(string source, string value)
   100	    {
   101	        int count = 0;
   102	        int start = 0;
   103	        while ((start = source.IndexOf(value, start, StringComparison.Ordinal)) >= 0)
   104	        {
   105	            count++;
   106	            start += value.Length;
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        return count;
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    private static string Normalize(string source) => source
   113	        .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   114	        .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   115	        .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   116	        .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   117	
   118	    private static string Read(string root, string file)
   119	    {
   120	        return File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root, file));
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    private static string RuntimeRoot()
   124	    {
   125	        return Path.Combine(
   126	            FindRepositoryRoot(),
   127	            "unity",
   128	            "Dig.Unity",
   129	            "Assets",
   130	            "Dig.Unity",
   131	            "Runtime");
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
   135	    {
   136	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   137	        while (current != null)
   138	        {
   139	            if (Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "src"))
   140	                && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "unity")))
   141	            {
   142	                return current.FullName;
   143	            }
   144	
   145	            current = current.Parent;
   146	        }
   147	
   148	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
   149	    }
   150	}
   151	}

   133	    {
   134	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
   135	        while (current != null)
   136	        {
   137	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
   138	            {
   139	                return current.FullName;

   142	            current = current.Parent;

   146	    }

   149	        .Replace(" ", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   150	        .Replace("\t", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   151	        .Replace("\r", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal)
   152	        .Replace("\n", string.Empty, StringComparison.Ordinal);
   153	}
   154	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/WorldOwnedExcavationNavigationTests.cs =====
     1	using System;

    18	    [Fact]
    19	    public void Tunnel_path_prefers_depth_detour_over_horizontal_shaft_gap()
    20	    {
    21	        CellId start = new CellId(0, 0, 0);
    22	        CellId shaft = new CellId(1, 0, 0);
    23	        CellId goal = new CellId(2, 0, 0);
    24	        CellId[] open =
    25	        {
    26	            start,
    27	            shaft,
    28	            goal,
    29	            new CellId(0, 0, 1),
    30	            new CellId(1, 0, 1),
    31	            new CellId(2, 0, 1),
    32	        };
    33	        TunnelNavigationVolume volume = new TunnelNavigationVolume(
    34	            width: 3,
    35	            height: 1,
    36	            depth: 2,
    37	            open,
    38	            verticalCells: new[] { shaft },
    39	            supportedCells: open.Where(cell => cell != shaft).ToArray());
    40	
    41	        TunnelPathResult route = volume.FindPath(start, goal);
    42	
    43	        Assert.True(route.Succeeded);
    44	        Assert.DoesNotContain(
    45	            TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse,
    46	            route.Path!.TraversalKinds);
    47	        Assert.Contains(TunnelTraversalKind.DepthTraverse, route.Path.TraversalKinds);
    48	        Assert.Contains(new CellId(1, 0, 1), route.Path.Cells);
    49	    }

    52	    public void Direct_horizontal_shaft_crossing_is_typed_as_climbing_gap()
    53	    {

    55	        CellId shaft = new CellId(1, 0, 0);

    57	        TunnelNavigationVolume volume = new TunnelNavigationVolume(
    58	            width: 3,
    59	            height: 1,
    60	            depth: 1,
    61	            openCells: new[] { start, shaft, goal },
    62	            verticalCells: new[] { shaft },
    63	            supportedCells: new[] { start, goal });

    73	    [Fact]
    74	    public void Navigation_pathfinder_prefers_depth_detour_over_excavated_shaft_gap()
    75	    {
    76	        WorldState world = CreateFilledWorld(new WorldSize(3, 2, 4));
    77	        long tick = 1;
    78	        CellId start = new CellId(0, 0, 0);
    79	        CellId crossing = new CellId(1, 0, 0);
    80	        CellId goal = new CellId(2, 0, 0);
    81	        CellId[] openRouteCells =
    82	        {
    83	            start,
    84	            crossing,
    85	            goal,
    86	            new CellId(0, 0, 1),
    87	            new CellId(1, 0, 1),
    88	            new CellId(2, 0, 1),
    89	        };
    90	        for (int index = 0; index < openRouteCells.Length; index++)
    91	        {
    92	            Assert.True(world.Excavate(openRouteCells[index], Air, tick++).IsSuccess);
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        CellId shaftBelow = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
    96	        OpenVerticalCell(world, shaftBelow, ref tick);
    97	        NavigationMap map = new NavigationMap(TraversalProfile.CreateFreeMover());
    98	        Assert.True(map.Rebuild(
    99	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
   100	            Array.Empty<TraversalLink>()).IsSuccess);
   101	        NavigationSnapshot snapshot = map.GetSnapshot().Value;
   102	        PathResult result = new NavigationPathfinder().FindPath(
   103	            snapshot,
   104	            new PathRequest(start, goal, snapshot.NavigationVersion));
   105	
   106	        Assert.True(result.Succeeded);
   107	        Assert.Contains(new CellId(1, 0, 1), result.Path!.Cells);
   108	        Assert.DoesNotContain(crossing, result.Path.Cells.Skip(1).SkipLast(1));
   109	    }

   164	    [Fact]
   165	    public void Unsupported_idle_resident_recovers_to_nearest_supported_cell()
   166	    {
   167	        WorldState world = CreateFilledWorld(new WorldSize(3, 3, 4));
   168	        CellId left = new CellId(0, 0, 0);
   169	        CellId start = new CellId(1, 0, 0);
   170	        CellId right = new CellId(2, 0, 0);
   171	        long tick = 1;
   172	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(left, Air, tick++).IsSuccess);
   173	        OpenVerticalCell(world, start, ref tick);
   174	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(right, Air, tick++).IsSuccess);
   175	        CellId partialSupport = new CellId(1, 1, 0);
   176	        Assert.True(world.SetDigDesignation(
   177	            partialSupport,
   178	            designated: true,
   179	            tick: tick++).IsSuccess);
   180	        Assert.True(world.CommitExcavationQuarter(
   181	            partialSupport,
   182	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperLeft,
   183	            ExcavationCutPattern.HorizontalRows,
   184	            Air,
   185	            tick: tick++).IsSuccess);
   186	        NavigationMap map = new NavigationMap(
   187	            TraversalProfile.CreateGroundedDwarf());
   188	        Assert.True(map.Rebuild(
   189	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
   190	            Array.Empty<TraversalLink>()).IsSuccess);
   191	        NavigationSnapshot navigation = map.GetSnapshot().Value;
   192	
   193	        UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan? plan =
   194	            new UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner(
   195	                new NavigationPathfinder()).Plan(
   196	                    start,
   197	                    navigation,
   198	                    world.CreateSnapshot());
   199	
   200	        Assert.NotNull(plan);
   201	        Assert.Equal(left, plan!.Destination);
   202	        Assert.Equal(new[] { start, left }, plan.Path.Cells);
   203	        Assert.Equal(1, plan.ShaftGapCount);
   204	        Assert.Equal(
   205	            TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse,
   206	            navigation.GetTransitions(start)
   207	                .Single(transition => transition.Target == left)
   208	                .TraversalKind);

   233	    [Fact]
   234	    public void Reachable_depth_work_cell_is_preferred_over_hanging_above_target()
   235	    {
   236	        WorldState world = CreateFilledWorld(new WorldSize(1, 2, 4));
   237	        CellId current = new CellId(0, 0, 0);
   238	        CellId target = new CellId(0, 1, 0);
   239	        CellId depthBridge = new CellId(0, 0, 1);
   240	        CellId depthWorkCell = new CellId(0, 1, 1);
   241	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(current, Air, tick: 1).IsSuccess);
   242	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(depthBridge, Air, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
   243	        Assert.True(world.Excavate(depthWorkCell, Air, tick: 3).IsSuccess);
   244	        Assert.True(world.SetDigDesignation(target, designated: true, tick: 4).IsSuccess);
   245	        Assert.True(world.CommitExcavationQuarter(
   246	            target,
   247	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperLeft,
   248	            ExcavationCutPattern.HorizontalRows,
   249	            Air,
   250	            tick: 5).IsSuccess);
   251	        NavigationSnapshot navigation = BuildNavigation(world);
   252	
   253	        Result<TerrainWorkRoutePlan> result = new TerrainWorkRoutePlanner(
   254	            new NavigationPathfinder()).Plan(
   255	                CreateDigJob(target),
   256	                current,
   257	                navigation,
   258	                world.CreateSnapshot());
   259	
   260	        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
   261	        Assert.True(result.Value.Succeeded);
   262	        Assert.Equal(depthWorkCell, result.Value.WorkCell);
   263	        Assert.Equal(TerrainWorkPosture.DepthBraced, result.Value.Posture);
   264	    }
   265	
   266	    private static WorldState CreateFilledWorld(WorldSize size)
   267	    {
   268	        return WorldState.CreateFilled(
   269	            size,
   270	            chunkSize: 1,
   271	            new MaterialCatalog(new[]
   272	            {
   273	                new MaterialDefinition(Rock, isSolid: true, hardness: 100),
   274	                new MaterialDefinition(Air, isSolid: false, hardness: 0),
   275	            }),
   276	            Rock,
   277	            explored: true).Value;
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    private static void OpenVerticalCell(
   281	        WorldState world,
   282	        CellId cell,
   283	        ref long tick)
   284	    {
   285	        Assert.True(world.SetDigDesignation(cell, designated: true, tick++).IsSuccess);
   286	        foreach (ExcavationQuarter quarter in new[]
   287	        {
   288	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperLeft,
   289	            ExcavationQuarter.UpperRight,
   290	            ExcavationQuarter.LowerLeft,
   291	            ExcavationQuarter.LowerRight,
   292	        })
   293	        {
   294	            Assert.True(world.CommitExcavationQuarter(
   295	                cell,
   296	                quarter,
   297	                ExcavationCutPattern.HorizontalRows,
   298	                Air,
   299	                tick++).IsSuccess);
   300	        }
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    private static NavigationSnapshot BuildNavigation(WorldState world)
   304	    {
   305	        NavigationMap map = new NavigationMap(TraversalProfile.CreateFreeMover());
   306	        Assert.True(map.Rebuild(
   307	            world.CreateSnapshot(),
   308	            Array.Empty<TraversalLink>()).IsSuccess);
   309	        return map.GetSnapshot().Value;
   310	    }
   311	
   312	    private static JobSnapshot CreateDigJob(CellId target)
   313	    {
   314	        EntityId jobId = EntityId.Parse("40000000000000000000000000000009");
   315	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
   316	        Assert.True(jobs.Add(new DigJobDefinition(
   317	            jobId,
   318	            new DigJobTarget(target),
   319	            priority: 750,
   320	            createdTick: 1,
   321	            JobRetryPolicy.Default)).IsSuccess);
   322	        Assert.True(jobs.MakeAvailable(jobId, tick: 1).IsSuccess);
   323	        return jobs.Get(jobId)!;
   324	    }
   325	}
   326	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigAgentRenderer.WorkFacing.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Jobs;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Unity
    10	{
    11	    public sealed partial class DigAgentRenderer
    12	    {
    13	        internal void SynchronizeWorkFacing(
    14	            IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> jobs,
    15	            TunnelNavigationVolume tunnelVolume,
    16	            WorldSnapshot world)
    17	        {
    18	            if (jobs == null)
    19	            {
    20	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
    21	            }
    22	
    23	            if (tunnelVolume == null)
    24	            {
    25	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(tunnelVolume));
    26	            }
    27	
    28	            if (world == null)
    29	            {
    30	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(world));
    31	            }
    32	
    33	            Dictionary<string, CellId> workTargets =
    34	                new Dictionary<string, CellId>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    35	            HashSet<string> nonClimbingWorkers = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    36	            HashSet<string> barrelWorkers = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    37	            for (int index = 0; index < jobs.Count; index++)
    38	            {
    39	                JobOverlayViewModel job = jobs[index];
    40	                if (!IsActiveToolWork(job)
    41	                    || workTargets.ContainsKey(job.AssignedAgentId!))
    42	                {
    43	                    continue;
    44	                }
    45	
    46	                workTargets.Add(
    47	                    job.AssignedAgentId!,
    48	                    new CellId(
    49	                        job.TargetX!.Value,
    50	                        job.TargetY!.Value,
    51	                        job.TargetZ!.Value));
    52	                if (job.IsMushroomChop || job.IsBarrelAttack)
    53	                {
    54	                    nonClimbingWorkers.Add(job.AssignedAgentId!);
    55	                }
    56	
    57	                if (job.IsBarrelAttack)
    58	                {
    59	                    barrelWorkers.Add(job.AssignedAgentId!);
    60	                }
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            Dictionary<CellId, CellSnapshot> worldCells = world.Chunks
    64	                .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    65	                .ToDictionary(cell => cell.Id);
    66	            foreach (KeyValuePair<string, DigAgentVisual> pair in _agents)
    67	            {
    68	                bool hasToolWork = workTargets.TryGetValue(pair.Key, out CellId target);
    69	                CellId current = new CellId(
    70	                    pair.Value.Model.CellX,
    71	                    pair.Value.Model.CellY,
    72	                    pair.Value.Model.CellZ);
    73	                CellId below = new CellId(current.X, current.Y + 1, current.Z);
    74	                bool hasFullSupport = worldCells.TryGetValue(
    75	                        below,
    76	                        out CellSnapshot support)
    77	                    && support.IsSolid
    78	                    && support.State.CompletedExcavationQuarters == ExcavationQuarter.None;
    79	                bool climbingWork = RequiresClimbingWorkPose(
    80	                    nonClimbingWorkers.Contains(pair.Key),
    81	                    hasFullSupport,
    82	                    tunnelVolume.Contains(current));
    83	                bool barrelAttack = hasToolWork && barrelWorkers.Contains(pair.Key);
    84	                CellId? poseTarget = hasToolWork
    85	                    ? target
    86	                    : climbingWork ? current : (CellId?)null;
    87	                pair.Value.SetWorkTarget(
    88	                    poseTarget,
    89	                    climbingWork,
    90	                    animateToolWork: hasToolWork && !barrelAttack,
    91	                    animateAttackWork: barrelAttack);
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        internal static bool RequiresClimbingWorkPose(
    96	            bool isNonClimbingWork,
    97	            bool hasFullSupport,
    98	            bool isOpenTunnelCell)
    99	        {
   100	            // World support remains authoritative after a job becomes terminal. An
   101	            // unsupported resident in an open tunnel keeps the stationary climbing
   102	            // posture until another route starts or a supported landing is reached.
   103	            return !isNonClimbingWork && !hasFullSupport && isOpenTunnelCell;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        private static bool IsActiveToolWork(JobOverlayViewModel job)
   107	        {
   108	            bool supportedTool = job.PreferredToolKind == JobToolKind.Mining
   109	                || job.IsMushroomChop
   110	                || job.IsBarrelAttack;
   111	            return job.AssignedAgentId != null
   112	                && job.HasTarget
   113	                && supportedTool
   114	                && string.Equals(
   115	                    job.Status,
   116	                    JobStatus.InProgress.ToString(),
   117	                    StringComparison.Ordinal)
   118	                && string.Equals(
   119	                    job.Stage,
   120	                    JobStageKind.PerformWork.ToString(),
   121	                    StringComparison.Ordinal);
   122	        }
   123	    }
   124	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigAgentSimulationDriverBase.Loop.cs =====
     1	using System.Collections.Generic;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     3	using Dig.Domain.World;
     4	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Presentation.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Presentation.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Navigation;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.World;
     9	using UnityEngine;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Unity
    12	{
    13	    public abstract partial class DigAgentSimulationDriverBase
    14	    {
    15	        protected virtual void Update()
    16	        {
    17	            if (!IsInitialized())
    18	            {
    19	                return;
    20	            }
    21	
    22	            HandlePlaybackInput();
    23	            int dueTicks = Playback.ConsumeDueTicks(
    24	                Time.unscaledDeltaTime,
    25	                TickIntervalSeconds,
    26	                MaximumTicksPerFrame);
    27	            for (int index = 0; index < dueTicks && enabled; index++)
    28	            {
    29	                AdvanceOneTick();
    30	            }
    31	        }
    32	
    33	        private void HandlePlaybackInput()
    34	        {
    35	            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Space))
    36	            {
    37	                TogglePause();
    38	            }
    39	
    40	            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Period)
    41	                || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadPeriod))
    42	            {
    43	                StepOnce();
    44	            }
    45	
    46	            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Minus)
    47	                || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadMinus))
    48	            {
    49	                Playback.SlowDown();
    50	            }
    51	            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Equals)
    52	                || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadPlus))
    53	            {
    54	                Playback.SpeedUp();
    55	            }
    56	        }
    57	
    58	        private void AdvanceOneTick()
    59	        {
    60	            IReadOnlyList<string> selectedAgentIds = AgentRenderer!.SelectedAgentIds;
    61	            string? primarySelectedAgentId = AgentRenderer!.SelectedAgentId;
    62	            string? selectedJobId = JobRenderer!.SelectedJobId;
    63	            string? selectedBuildingId = BuildingRenderer!.SelectedBuildingId;
    64	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> before = AgentSession!.LoadView();
    65	            long nextTick = checked(AgentSession.Tick + 1);
    66	            IReadOnlyList<string> manualMovementIds =
    67	                AgentSession.ActiveManualTunnelResidentIds;
    68	            Result result = TerrainSession!.InterruptForManualMovement(
    69	                manualMovementIds,
    70	                nextTick);
    71	            if (result.IsSuccess)
    72	            {
    73	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeDesignations(nextTick, before);
    74	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeSpatialExcavations(nextTick, before);
    75	                result = TerrainSession.SynchronizeResidentInventoryPlacement(nextTick);
    76	            }
    77	
    78	            if (result.IsSuccess)
    79	            {
    80	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeBuildingBoxRelocation(nextTick, before);
    81	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeBuildingBoxAssembly(nextTick, before);
    82	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeBuildingPacking(nextTick, before);
    83	                TerrainSession.SynchronizeBuildingProduction(nextTick, before);
    84	                result = TerrainSession.InterruptForManualMovement(
    85	                    AgentSession.ActiveManualTunnelResidentIds,
    86	                    nextTick);
    87	            }
    88	
    89	            if (result.IsSuccess)
    90	            {
    91	                IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CellId> movement =
    92	                    TerrainSession.PlanMovement(before, nextTick);
    93	                IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CellId> spatialMovement =
    94	                    TerrainSession.PlanSpatialExcavationMovement(before);
    95	                AgentSession.SetSpatialWorkMovementTargets(spatialMovement);
    96	                result = AgentSession.Advance(movement);
    97	            }
    98	
    99	            DomainError? movementWarning =
   100	                AgentSession.ConsumeManualTunnelMovementWarning();
   101	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents = AgentSession.LoadView();
   102	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   103	            {
   104	                TerrainSession.AdvanceReadyManualQuarterExcavations(
   105	                    AgentSession.Tick,
   106	                    agents);
   107	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceSpatialExcavationWork(
   108	                    AgentSession.Tick,
   109	                    agents);
   110	            }
   111	
   112	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   113	            {
   114	                IReadOnlyList<SpatialExcavationCommit> commits =
   115	                    TerrainSession.LoadSpatialExcavationsToFinalize();
   116	                for (int index = 0; index < commits.Count && result.IsSuccess; index++)
   117	                {
   118	                    result = CompleteSpatialExcavation(commits[index]);
   119	                }
   120	            }
   121	
   122	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   123	            {
   124	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceBarrels(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   125	            }
   126	
   127	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   128	            {
   129	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceMushrooms(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   130	            }
   131	
   132	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   133	            {
   134	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceBuildingProduction(
   135	                    AgentSession.Tick,
   136	                    agents);
   137	            }
   138	
   139	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   140	            {
   141	                result = AdvanceTerrainForAgents(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   142	            }
   143	
   144	            result = ReconcileCommittedTerrainRuntime(result, AgentSession.Tick);
   145	
   146	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   147	            {
   148	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceResidentInventoryPlacement(
   149	                    AgentSession.Tick,
   150	                    agents);
   151	            }
   152	
   153	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   154	            {
   155	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceBuildingBoxPickup(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   156	            }
   157	
   158	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   159	            {
   160	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceWorldItemPickup(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   161	            }
   162	
   163	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   164	            {
   165	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceBuildingBoxAssembly(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   166	            }
   167	
   168	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   169	            {
   170	                result = TerrainSession.AdvanceBuildingPacking(AgentSession.Tick, agents);
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   174	            {
   175	                result = TerrainSession.SettleUnsupportedBarrels(AgentSession.Tick);
   176	            }
   177	
   178	            if (result.IsSuccess)
   179	            {
   180	                result = TerrainSession.SettleWorldItems(AgentSession.Tick);
   181	            }
   182	
   183	            DomainError? tickWarning = null;
   184	            if (result.IsFailure)
   185	            {
   186	                tickWarning = result.Error;
   187	                Hud!.SetCommandResult(result);
   188	            }
   189	
   190	            IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> jobs = TerrainSession.LoadJobs();
   191	            IReadOnlyList<WorldItemViewModel> items = TerrainSession.LoadAllWorldItems();
   192	            IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> routes = TerrainSession.LoadRoutes();
   193	            IReadOnlyList<Dig.Presentation.Buildings.BuildingWorldViewModel> buildings =
   194	                TerrainSession.LoadBuildings();
   195	            DigStorageStatus storage = TerrainSession.GetStorageStatus();
   196	            if (TerrainSession.ConsumeWorldChanged())
   197	            {
   198	                WorldViewModel world = WorldSession!.LoadView();
   199	                WorldRenderer!.Render(world);
   200	                WorldRenderer.SetProtectedCells(WorldSession.ProtectedCells);
   201	                WorldRenderer.SetTerrainDeposits(WorldSession.LoadTerrainDeposits());
   202	                WorldOverlayRenderer!.RenderWorld(
   203	                    world,
   204	                    WorldSession.LoadTerrainDeposits());
   205	                Hud!.SetWorld(world);
   206	            }
   207	
   208	            float movementDuration = TickIntervalSeconds * 0.82f;
   209	            if (!Playback.IsPaused)
   210	            {
   211	                movementDuration /= Playback.SpeedMultiplier;
   212	            }
   213	
   214	            AgentRenderer.RenderWithMovementModes(
   215	                agents,
   216	                movementDuration,
   217	                AgentSession.LoadMovementModes());
   218	            AgentRenderer.SynchronizeProductionWaitOffsets(
   219	                TerrainSession.LoadProductionWaitOffsets());
   220	            RefreshEquipmentVisuals();
   221	            MushroomRenderer!.Render(TerrainSession!.LoadMushrooms());
   222	            BarrelRenderer!.Render(TerrainSession.LoadBarrels());
   223	            JobRenderer.Render(jobs);
   224	            BuildingRenderer.Render(buildings);
   225	            BuildingInternalStockRenderer!.Render(
   226	                TerrainSession.LoadAllBuildingProduction(),
   227	                buildings);
   228	            ItemRenderer!.Render(items);
   229	            StockpileRenderer!.Render(storage);
   230	            RouteRenderer!.Render(routes);
   231	            WorldOverlayRenderer!.RenderDynamic(buildings, storage, routes);
   232	            EffectRuntime!.Flush(AgentSession.Tick);
   233	            Hud!.SetAgents(agents, AgentSession.Tick);
   234	            Hud.SetJobs(jobs);
   235	            Hud.SetStorageStatus(storage);
   236	            RestoreSelection(
   237	                selectedAgentIds,
   238	                primarySelectedAgentId,
   239	                selectedJobId,
   240	                selectedBuildingId);
   241	            if (movementWarning != null)
   242	            {
   243	                Hud.SetStatus($"Manual movement cancelled: {movementWarning}");
   244	            }
   245	            else if (tickWarning != null)
   246	            {
   247	                Hud.SetStatus($"Work command deferred: {tickWarning}");
   248	            }
   249	        }
   250	    }
   251	}

   100	            }

   117	
   118	        internal Result MoveResident(string residentId, CellId destination)
   119	        {
   120	            if (AgentSession == null || AgentRenderer == null || Hud == null)
   121	            {
   122	                return Result.Failure(NotInitialized);
   123	            }
   124	
   125	            Result result = AgentSession.MoveResident(residentId, destination);
   126	            if (result.IsFailure)
   127	            {
   128	                return result;
   129	            }
   130	
   131	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents = AgentSession.LoadView();
   132	            AgentRenderer.RenderWithMovementModes(
   133	                agents,
   134	                movementDuration: 0.25f,
   135	                AgentSession.LoadMovementModes());
   136	            RefreshEquipmentVisuals();
   137	            Hud.SetAgents(agents, AgentSession.Tick);
   138	            Hud.SetAgentSelection(
   139	                AgentRenderer.SelectedModel,
   140	                AgentRenderer.SelectedCount);
   141	            return Result.Success();
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        protected bool IsInitialized()
   145	        {
   146	            return WorldSession != null
   147	                && WorldRenderer != null
   148	                && AgentSession != null
   149	                && AgentRenderer != null
   150	                && TerrainSession != null
   151	                && MushroomRenderer != null
   152	                && BarrelRenderer != null
   153	                && JobRenderer != null
   154	                && BuildingRenderer != null
   155	                && BuildingInternalStockRenderer != null
   156	                && ItemRenderer != null
   157	                && StockpileRenderer != null
   158	                && RouteRenderer != null
   159	                && WorldOverlayRenderer != null
   160	                && EffectRuntime != null
   161	                && Hud != null;
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        protected virtual void OnValidate()
   165	        {
   166	            tickIntervalSeconds = Mathf.Max(0.1f, tickIntervalSeconds);
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigAgentVisual.Movement.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     2	using Dig.Domain.World;
     3	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     4	using UnityEngine;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Unity
     7	{
     8	public sealed partial class DigAgentVisual
     9	{
    10	    private const float ClimbWallDepthOffset = 0.16f;
    11	    private bool _isClimbing;
    12	    private bool _climbingAscending;
    13	    private TunnelNavigationVolume? _tunnelVolume;
    14	    private TunnelTraversalKind _activeTraversalKind;
    15	
    16	    internal void SetTunnelNavigationVolume(TunnelNavigationVolume? volume)
    17	    {
    18	        _tunnelVolume = volume;
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    private void PrepareTraversalKind()
    22	    {
    23	        CellId from = new CellId(_previousX, _previousY, _previousZ);
    24	        CellId to = new CellId(_currentX, _currentY, _currentZ);
    25	        _activeTraversalKind = _tunnelVolume?.ClassifyTraversal(from, to)
    26	            ?? TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
    27	        if (_activeTraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid
    28	            && _previousX == _currentX
    29	            && _previousZ == _currentZ
    30	            && _previousY != _currentY)
    31	        {
    32	            _activeTraversalKind = TunnelTraversalKind.VerticalClimb;
    33	        }
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    private void Update()
    37	    {
    38	        if (_duration <= 0f)
    39	        {
    40	            if (_productionWaitPose)
    41	            {
    42	                FaceTowardMainCamera();
    43	            }
    44	
    45	            ApplyToolWorkAnimation();
    46	            return;
    47	        }
    48	        _elapsed = Mathf.Min(_duration, _elapsed + Time.deltaTime);
    49	        double progress = _elapsed / _duration;
    50	        AgentInterpolatedSpatialPosition position = AgentSpatialPositionInterpolator.Interpolate(
    51	            _previousVisualX, _previousY, _previousZ,
    52	            _currentVisualX, _currentY, _currentZ, progress);
    53	        Vector3 world = ToWorld((float)position.X, (float)position.Y, (float)position.Z);
    54	        if (_isClimbing)
    55	        {
    56	            float wallBlend = Mathf.Sin(Mathf.PI * (float)progress);
    57	            world += Vector3.back * (ClimbWallDepthOffset * wallBlend);
    58	            FaceAwayFromMainCamera();
    59	            _rig?.ApplyClimbPose((float)progress, _climbingAscending);
    60	        }
    61	
    62	        transform.position = world;
    63	        if (_elapsed >= _duration)
    64	        {
    65	            _duration = 0f;
    66	            _isClimbing = false;
    67	            _activeTraversalKind = TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
    68	            transform.position = ToWorld(_currentVisualX, _currentY, _currentZ);
    69	            ApplyAction(isMoving: false);
    70	            ApplyWorkFacingIfIdle();
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    private void Face(Vector3 direction)
    75	    {
    76	        _isClimbing = _activeTraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.VerticalClimb
    77	            || _activeTraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse;
    78	        if (_isClimbing)
    79	        {
    80	            _climbingAscending = _currentY < _previousY;
    81	            FaceAwayFromMainCamera();
    82	            _rig?.ApplyClimbPose(0f, _climbingAscending);
    83	            return;
    84	        }
    85	
    86	        Vector3 planar = new Vector3(direction.x, 0f, direction.z);
    87	        if (planar.sqrMagnitude > 0.001f)
    88	            transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(planar.normalized, Vector3.up);
    89	    }
    90	
    91	    private void FaceAwayFromMainCamera()
    92	    {
    93	        Camera? mainCamera = Camera.main;
    94	        if (mainCamera == null)
    95	        {
    96	            transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(Vector3.back, Vector3.up);
    97	            return;
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        Vector3 away = transform.position - mainCamera.transform.position;
   101	        away.y = 0f;
   102	        if (away.sqrMagnitude <= 0.001f)
   103	        {
   104	            away = Vector3.back;
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(away.normalized, Vector3.up);
   108	    }
   109	}
   110	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingBoxAssemblyCandidates.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Unity
    11	{
    12	    internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    13	    {
    14	        private static CellId ResolveBuildingBoxAssemblyTarget(
    15	            JobSnapshot job,
    16	            BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition assembly,
    17	            ItemStackSnapshot? box)
    18	        {
    19	            bool acquiring = job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed
    20	                || job.Stage == JobStageKind.AcquireItem;
    21	            if (acquiring
    22	                && box?.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World
    23	                && box.Location.HasCell)
    24	            {
    25	                return box.Location.CellId;
    26	            }
    27	
    28	            return assembly.WorkPosition;
    29	        }
    30	
    31	        private static IReadOnlyList<JobCandidate> CreateBuildingBoxAssemblyCandidates(
    32	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
    33	            ItemStackSnapshot box)
    34	        {
    35	            if (box.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.AgentInventory && box.Location.HasOwner)
    36	            {
    37	                return agents
    38	                    .Where(agent => string.Equals(
    39	                        agent.Id,
    40	                        box.Location.OwnerId.ToString(),
    41	                        StringComparison.Ordinal))
    42	                    .Select(agent => new JobCandidate(
    43	                        EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
    44	                        skillLevel: 5_000,
    45	                        distanceCost: 0,
    46	                        isAvailable: agent.IsAvailableForAutomaticPlanning))
    47	                    .ToArray();
    48	            }
    49	
    50	            if (box.Location.Kind != ItemLocationKind.World || !box.Location.HasCell)
    51	            {
    52	                return Array.Empty<JobCandidate>();
    53	            }
    54	
    55	            CellId source = box.Location.CellId;
    56	            return agents.Select((agent, index) => new JobCandidate(
    57	                EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
    58	                skillLevel: 4_800 - (index * 150),
    59	                distanceCost: Math.Abs(agent.CellX - source.X) + Math.Abs(agent.CellY - source.Y),
    60	                isAvailable: agent.IsAvailableForAutomaticPlanning)).ToArray();
    61	        }
    62	    }
    63	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingBoxGhostRenderer.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Domain.World;
     4	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Presentation.World;
     6	using UnityEngine;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Unity
     9	{
    10	    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    11	    public sealed partial class DigBuildingBoxGhostRenderer : MonoBehaviour
    12	    {
    13	        private const string CatalogResourcePath =
    14	            "Dig/VisualCatalogs/Buildings";
    15	
    16	        [SerializeField]
    17	        private DigBuildingVisualCatalog? visualCatalog;
    18	
    19	        private Transform? _root;
    20	        private Transform? _previewContainer;
    21	        private GameObject? _previewInstance;
    22	        private DigVisualTintTarget? _previewTint;
    23	        private string _assetKey = string.Empty;
    24	        private Material? _markerMaterial;
    25	        private GameObject? _workMarker;
    26	
    27	        private void Awake()
    28	        {
    29	            InitializeRepresentatives();
    30	            InitializeItemVisuals();
    31	            if (visualCatalog == null)
    32	            {
    33	                visualCatalog = Resources.Load<DigBuildingVisualCatalog>(
    34	                    CatalogResourcePath);
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	
    38	        public void SetVisualCatalog(DigBuildingVisualCatalog? catalog)
    39	        {
    40	            visualCatalog = catalog;
    41	            _assetKey = string.Empty;
    42	        }
    43	
    44	        private DigBuildingVisualResolution ResolveCatalogBuildingBox(string stableId)
    45	        {
    46	            if (visualCatalog == null)
    47	            {
    48	                throw new InvalidOperationException("Building visual catalog is unavailable.");
    49	            }
    50	
    51	            return visualCatalog.ResolveBuilding(
    52	                stableId,
    53	                BuildingVisualState.BuildingBox);
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        public void Render(BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview)
    57	        {
    58	            if (preview == null)
    59	            {
    60	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(preview));
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            if (!preview.IsVisible)
    64	            {
    65	                Clear();
    66	                return;
    67	            }
    68	
    69	            EnsureResources();
    70	            if (preview.PlacementKind == BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox)
    71	            {
    72	                RenderBuildingBoxItemPreview(preview);
    73	                return;
    74	            }
    75	
    76	            DigBuildingVisualResolution resolution = Resolve(preview);
    77	            EnsurePreviewInstance(preview, resolution);
    78	            ApplyPreviewTransform(preview, resolution);
    79	            RenderWorkMarker(preview);
    80	        }
    81	
    82	        public void Clear()
    83	        {
    84	            if (_previewContainer != null)
    85	            {
    86	                _previewContainer.gameObject.SetActive(false);
    87	            }
    88	
    89	            if (_workMarker != null)
    90	            {
    91	                _workMarker.SetActive(false);
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        private void EnsurePreviewInstance(
    96	            BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview,
    97	            DigBuildingVisualResolution resolution)
    98	        {
    99	            string key = $"building:{resolution.Asset.StableId}";
   100	            if (_previewInstance != null
   101	                && _assetKey == key)
   102	            {
   103	                _previewContainer!.gameObject.SetActive(true);
   104	                return;
   105	            }
   106	
   107	            if (_previewInstance != null)
   108	            {
   109	                _previewInstance.SetActive(false);
   110	                Destroy(_previewInstance);
   111	            }
   112	
   113	            _previewInstance = DigVisualPrefabFactory.Create(
   114	                resolution.Asset,
   115	                _previewContainer!,
   116	                $"Building ghost {preview.DefinitionId}",
   117	                PrimitiveType.Cube);
   118	            _assetKey = key;
   119	            _previewTint = _previewInstance.GetComponent<DigVisualTintTarget>();
   120	            DigBuildingDetailGroup[] groups =
   121	                _previewInstance.GetComponentsInChildren<DigBuildingDetailGroup>(
   122	                    includeInactive: true);
   123	            for (int index = 0; index < groups.Length; index++)
   124	            {
   125	                groups[index].SetDetailLevel(TerrainVisualDetailLevel.Reduced);
   126	            }
   127	
   128	            SetLayerRecursively(_previewInstance, layer: 2);
   129	            DisableColliders(_previewInstance);
   130	            _previewContainer!.gameObject.SetActive(true);
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        private void ApplyPreviewTransform(
   134	            BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview,
   135	            DigBuildingVisualResolution resolution)
   136	        {
   137	            _previewContainer!.localPosition =
   138	                DigTunnelProjection.ResidentWorldPosition(
   139	                    preview.Origin.X,
   140	                    preview.Origin.Y,
   141	                    preview.Origin.Z)
   142	                + (Vector3.up * (DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink + 0.03f));
   143	            _previewContainer.localRotation = ResolveOrientation(preview.Orientation)
   144	                * (preview.IsValid
   145	                    ? Quaternion.identity
   146	                    : Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 7f));
   147	            _previewContainer.localScale = preview.IsValid
   148	                ? Vector3.one
   149	                : new Vector3(0.92f, 1.18f, 0.92f);
   150	
   151	            if (resolution.Asset.IsFallback || resolution.Asset.Prefab == null)
   152	            {
   153	                ResolveLocalBounds(preview, out Vector2 center, out Vector2 size);
   154	                _previewInstance!.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(
   155	                    center.x,
   156	                    0.12f,
   157	                    center.y);
   158	                _previewInstance.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
   159	                _previewInstance.transform.localScale = new Vector3(
   160	                    Mathf.Max(0.50f, size.x * 0.86f),
   161	                    0.24f,
   162	                    Mathf.Max(0.50f, size.y * 0.86f));
   163	            }
   164	            else
   165	            {
   166	                _previewInstance!.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(
   167	                    -resolution.PivotCell.x,
   168	                    0f,
   169	                    -resolution.PivotCell.y);
   170	                _previewInstance.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
   171	                _previewInstance.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
   172	            }
   173	
   174	            _previewTint?.SetTint(preview.IsValid
   175	                ? new Color(0.25f, 0.82f, 0.56f, 0.72f)
   176	                : new Color(0.92f, 0.32f, 0.28f, 0.82f));
   177	        }
   178	
   179	        private void RenderWorkMarker(BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview)
   180	        {
   181	            if (!preview.WorkPosition.HasValue)
   182	            {
   183	                _workMarker!.SetActive(false);
   184	                return;
   185	            }
   186	
   187	            CellId cell = preview.WorkPosition.Value;
   188	            _workMarker!.SetActive(true);
   189	            _workMarker.name = $"Building work position {cell}";
   190	            _workMarker.transform.localPosition =
   191	                DigTunnelProjection.ResidentWorldPosition(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z)
   192	                + (Vector3.up * (DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink + 0.24f));
   193	            _workMarker.transform.localRotation = preview.IsValid
   194	                ? Quaternion.identity
   195	                : Quaternion.Euler(0f, 45f, 0f);
   196	            _workMarker.transform.localScale = new Vector3(0.24f, 0.48f, 0.24f);
   197	            Renderer renderer = _workMarker.GetComponent<Renderer>();
   198	            renderer.sharedMaterial = _markerMaterial;
   199	            renderer.GetComponent<DigVisualTintTarget>()?.SetTint(preview.IsValid
   200	                ? new Color(0.25f, 0.82f, 0.56f, 0.82f)
   201	                : new Color(0.92f, 0.32f, 0.28f, 0.92f));
   202	        }
   203	
   204	        private void EnsureResources()
   205	        {
   206	            if (_root == null)
   207	            {
   208	                _root = new GameObject("Building Placement Ghost").transform;
   209	                // Preview coordinates come from DigTunnelProjection and are already
   210	                // world-space. Keep the ghost root unrotated so the preview stays
   211	                // under the pointer instead of being rotated outside the play area.
   212	                _root.SetParent(transform, worldPositionStays: true);
   213	                _previewContainer = new GameObject("Preview").transform;
   214	                _previewContainer.SetParent(_root, worldPositionStays: false);
   215	            }
   216	
   217	            if (_workMarker != null)
   218	            {
   219	                return;
   220	            }
   221	
   222	            Shader shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit");
   223	            if (shader == null)
   224	            {
   225	                shader = Shader.Find("Standard");
   226	            }
   227	
   228	            if (shader == null)
   229	            {
   230	                throw new InvalidOperationException("No supported ghost shader was found.");
   231	            }
   232	
   233	            _markerMaterial = new Material(shader)
   234	            {
   235	                name = "Dig Building Ghost Marker",
   236	            };
   237	            _workMarker = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
   238	            _workMarker.layer = 2;
   239	            _workMarker.transform.SetParent(_root, worldPositionStays: false);
   240	            Collider? collider = _workMarker.GetComponent<Collider>();
   241	            if (collider != null)
   242	            {
   243	                collider.enabled = false;
   244	            }
   245	
   246	            if (_workMarker.GetComponent<DigVisualPrefabRoot>() == null)
   247	            {
   248	                _workMarker.AddComponent<DigVisualPrefabRoot>();
   249	            }
   250	
   251	            _workMarker.AddComponent<DigVisualTintTarget>();
   252	            _workMarker.SetActive(false);
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        private static void ResolveLocalBounds(
   256	            BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview,
   257	            out Vector2 center,
   258	            out Vector2 size)
   259	        {
   260	            int minX = int.MaxValue;
   261	            int maxX = int.MinValue;
   262	            int minY = int.MaxValue;
   263	            int maxY = int.MinValue;
   264	            for (int index = 0; index < preview.Footprint.Count; index++)
   265	            {
   266	                CellId cell = preview.Footprint[index];
   267	                ResolveLocalOffset(
   268	                    cell.X - preview.Origin.X,
   269	                    cell.Y - preview.Origin.Y,
   270	                    preview.Orientation,
   271	                    out int x,
   272	                    out int y);
   273	                minX = Math.Min(minX, x);
   274	                maxX = Math.Max(maxX, x);
   275	                minY = Math.Min(minY, y);
   276	                maxY = Math.Max(maxY, y);
   277	            }
   278	
   279	            center = new Vector2((minX + maxX) * 0.5f, (minY + maxY) * 0.5f);
   280	            size = new Vector2((maxX - minX) + 1, (maxY - minY) + 1);
   281	        }
   282	
   283	        private static void ResolveLocalOffset(
   284	            int x,
   285	            int y,
   286	            BuildingOrientation orientation,
   287	            out int localX,
   288	            out int localY)
   289	        {
   290	            switch (orientation)
   291	            {
   292	                case BuildingOrientation.East:
   293	                    localX = y;
   294	                    localY = -x;
   295	                    break;
   296	                case BuildingOrientation.South:
   297	                    localX = -x;
   298	                    localY = -y;
   299	                    break;
   300	                case BuildingOrientation.West:
   301	                    localX = -y;
   302	                    localY = x;
   303	                    break;
   304	                default:
   305	                    localX = x;
   306	                    localY = y;
   307	                    break;
   308	            }
   309	        }
   310	
   311	        private static Quaternion ResolveOrientation(BuildingOrientation orientation)
   312	        {
   313	            float yaw = orientation switch
   314	            {
   315	                BuildingOrientation.North => 0f,
   316	                BuildingOrientation.East => -90f,
   317	                BuildingOrientation.South => 180f,
   318	                BuildingOrientation.West => 90f,
   319	                _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(orientation)),
   320	            };
   321	            return Quaternion.Euler(0f, yaw, 0f);
   322	        }
   323	        private static void DisableColliders(GameObject root)
   324	        {
   325	            Collider[] colliders = root.GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>(includeInactive: true);
   326	            for (int index = 0; index < colliders.Length; index++)
   327	            {
   328	                colliders[index].enabled = false;
   329	            }
   330	        }
   331	
   332	        private static void SetLayerRecursively(GameObject root, int layer)
   333	        {
   334	            Transform[] transforms = root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(includeInactive: true);
   335	            for (int index = 0; index < transforms.Length; index++)
   336	            {
   337	                transforms[index].gameObject.layer = layer;
   338	            }
   339	        }
   340	        private void OnDestroy()
   341	        {
   342	            DisposeRepresentatives();
   343	            DisposeItemVisuals();
   344	            if (_markerMaterial != null)
   345	            {
   346	                Destroy(_markerMaterial);
   347	            }
   348	        }
   349	    }
   350	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingInternalStockRenderer.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Presentation.Production;
     6	using UnityEngine;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Unity
     9	{
    10	
    11	[DisallowMultipleComponent]
    12	public sealed partial class DigBuildingInternalStockRenderer : MonoBehaviour
    13	{
    14	    private const float VisibleDepthOffset = 0.12f;
    15	    private readonly Dictionary<string, GameObject> _units =
    16	        new Dictionary<string, GameObject>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    17	    private readonly Dictionary<string, Material> _materials =
    18	        new Dictionary<string, Material>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    19	    private readonly Dictionary<string, DigBuildingInternalStockBayVisual> _bays =
    20	        new Dictionary<string, DigBuildingInternalStockBayVisual>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    21	    private Transform? _root;
    22	
    23	    internal int ActiveUnitCount => _units.Values.Count(value => value.activeSelf);
    24	    internal int ActiveBayCount => _bays.Values.Count(value => value.gameObject.activeSelf);
    25	
    26	    internal void Render(
    27	        IReadOnlyList<BuildingProductionViewModel> production,
    28	        IReadOnlyList<BuildingWorldViewModel> buildings)
    29	    {
    30	        if (production == null)
    31	        {
    32	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(production));
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        if (buildings == null)
    36	        {
    37	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(buildings));
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        EnsureRoot();
    41	        Dictionary<string, BuildingWorldViewModel> buildingById = buildings
    42	            .ToDictionary(value => value.Id, StringComparer.Ordinal);
    43	        HashSet<string> visible = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    44	        HashSet<string> visibleBays = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    45	        for (int productionIndex = 0;
    46	            productionIndex < production.Count;
    47	            productionIndex++)
    48	        {
    49	            BuildingProductionViewModel model = production[productionIndex];
    50	            if (!buildingById.TryGetValue(
    51	                model.BuildingId.ToString(),
    52	                out BuildingWorldViewModel? building))
    53	            {
    54	                continue;
    55	            }
    56	
    57	            RenderZones(building, visibleBays);
    58	            for (int stockIndex = 0; stockIndex < model.Stocks.Count; stockIndex++)
    59	            {
    60	                BuildingStockIconViewModel stock = model.Stocks[stockIndex];
    61	                RenderStock(building, stock, stockIndex, visible);
    62	            }
    63	        }
    64	
    65	        RemoveMissing(visible, visibleBays);
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    private void RenderStock(
    69	        BuildingWorldViewModel building,
    70	        BuildingStockIconViewModel stock,
    71	        int stockIndex,
    72	        ISet<string> visible)
    73	    {
    74	        for (int unitIndex = 0; unitIndex < stock.Current; unitIndex++)
    75	        {
    76	            string key = building.Id + ":" + stock.ItemId + ":" + unitIndex;
    77	            visible.Add(key);
    78	            if (!_units.TryGetValue(key, out GameObject? unit))
    79	            {
    80	                unit = CreateUnit(
    81	                    building.Id,
    82	                    stock.ItemId.ToString(),
    83	                    key);
    84	                _units.Add(key, unit);
    85	            }
    86	
    87	            unit.SetActive(true);
    88	            ApplyUnitTransform(unit, building, stockIndex, unitIndex);
    89	        }
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    internal bool TryGetStock(
    93	        RaycastHit hit,
    94	        out DigBuildingInternalStockVisual visual)
    95	    {
    96	        visual = hit.collider == null
    97	            ? null!
    98	            : hit.collider.GetComponentInParent<DigBuildingInternalStockVisual>();
    99	        return visual != null;
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    private GameObject CreateUnit(string buildingId, string itemId, string key)
   103	    {
   104	        PrimitiveType primitive = ResolvePrimitive(itemId);
   105	        GameObject unit = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(primitive);
   106	        unit.name = "Internal Stock " + key;
   107	        unit.transform.SetParent(_root, worldPositionStays: true);
   108	        Renderer renderer = unit.GetComponent<Renderer>();
   109	        renderer.sharedMaterial = ResolveMaterial(itemId);
   110	        DigBuildingInternalStockVisual visual =
   111	            unit.AddComponent<DigBuildingInternalStockVisual>();
   112	        visual.Initialize(buildingId, itemId);
   113	        return unit;
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    private static void ApplyUnitTransform(
   117	        GameObject unit,
   118	        BuildingWorldViewModel building,
   119	        int stockIndex,
   120	        int unitIndex)
   121	    {
   122	        int column = unitIndex % 2;
   123	        int layer = unitIndex / 2;
   124	        float pileX = -0.24f + (stockIndex * 0.16f);
   125	        BuildingFootprintCellViewModel anchor = ResolveInternalZoneCell(building);
   126	        Vector3 basePosition = DigTunnelProjection.ResidentWorldPosition(
   127	            anchor.X,
   128	            anchor.Y,
   129	            anchor.Z) + (Vector3.up * DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink);
   130	        unit.transform.position = basePosition + new Vector3(
   131	            pileX + (column * 0.07f),
   132	            0.12f + (layer * 0.16f),
   133	            VisibleDepthOffset + (stockIndex * 0.008f));
   134	        unit.transform.localScale = ResolveScale(unit.name);
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    private Material ResolveMaterial(string itemId)
   138	    {
   139	        string family = ResolveFamily(itemId);
   140	        if (_materials.TryGetValue(family, out Material? material))
   141	        {
   142	            return material;
   143	        }
   144	
   145	        Shader shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit")
   146	            ?? Shader.Find("Standard")
   147	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(
   148	                "No supported building-zone shader was found.");
   149	        material = new Material(shader)
   150	        {
   151	            name = "Dig Building Zone " + family,
   152	        };
   153	        DigMaterialColorUtility.SetColor(material, ResolveColor(family));
   154	        _materials.Add(family, material);
   155	        return material;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    private static PrimitiveType ResolvePrimitive(string itemId)
   159	    {
   160	        string family = ResolveFamily(itemId);
   161	        if (family == "leg")
   162	        {
   163	            return PrimitiveType.Cylinder;
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        if (family == "cap" || family == "hamster")
   167	        {
   168	            return PrimitiveType.Sphere;
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        return PrimitiveType.Cube;
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    private static Vector3 ResolveScale(string objectName)
   175	    {
   176	        if (objectName.IndexOf("mushroom_leg", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   177	        {
   178	            return new Vector3(0.11f, 0.15f, 0.11f);
   179	        }
   180	
   181	        if (objectName.IndexOf("mushroom_cap", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   182	        {
   183	            return new Vector3(0.18f, 0.10f, 0.15f);
   184	        }
   185	
   186	        if (objectName.IndexOf("hamster", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   187	        {
   188	            return new Vector3(0.18f, 0.13f, 0.14f);
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        return new Vector3(0.16f, 0.12f, 0.15f);
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    private static string ResolveFamily(string itemId)
   195	    {
   196	        if (string.Equals(itemId, "internal.stock.zone", StringComparison.Ordinal))
   197	        {
   198	            return "internal-zone";
   199	        }
   200	
   201	        if (string.Equals(itemId, "finished.output.zone", StringComparison.Ordinal))
   202	        {
   203	            return "output-zone";
   204	        }
   205	
   206	        if (itemId.IndexOf("mushroom_cap", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   207	        {
   208	            return "cap";
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        if (itemId.IndexOf("mushroom_leg", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   212	        {
   213	            return "leg";
   214	        }
   215	
   216	        if (itemId.IndexOf("hamster", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
   217	        {
   218	            return "hamster";
   219	        }
   220	
   221	        return itemId.IndexOf("stone", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0
   222	            ? "stone"
   223	            : "generic";
   224	    }
   225	
   226	    private static Color ResolveColor(string family)
   227	    {
   228	        if (family == "internal-zone")
   229	        {
   230	            return new Color(0.24f, 0.42f, 0.56f, 1f);
   231	        }
   232	
   233	        if (family == "output-zone")
   234	        {
   235	            return new Color(0.62f, 0.52f, 0.20f, 1f);
   236	        }
   237	
   238	        if (family == "cap")
   239	        {
   240	            return new Color(0.75f, 0.18f, 0.14f, 1f);
   241	        }
   242	
   243	        if (family == "leg")
   244	        {
   245	            return new Color(0.82f, 0.70f, 0.46f, 1f);
   246	        }
   247	
   248	        if (family == "hamster")
   249	        {
   250	            return new Color(0.56f, 0.32f, 0.16f, 1f);
   251	        }
   252	
   253	        return family == "stone"
   254	            ? new Color(0.42f, 0.48f, 0.56f, 1f)
   255	            : new Color(0.70f, 0.70f, 0.70f, 1f);
   256	    }
   257	
   258	    private void EnsureRoot()
   259	    {
   260	        if (_root != null)
   261	        {
   262	            return;
   263	        }
   264	
   265	        GameObject root = new GameObject("Building Input And Output Zones");
   266	        root.transform.SetParent(transform, worldPositionStays: true);
   267	        _root = root.transform;
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    private void RemoveMissing(
   271	        ISet<string> visible,
   272	        ISet<string> visibleBays)
   273	    {
   274	        string[] removed = _units.Keys
   275	            .Where(value => !visible.Contains(value))
   276	            .ToArray();
   277	        for (int index = 0; index < removed.Length; index++)
   278	        {
   279	            string key = removed[index];
   280	            Destroy(_units[key]);
   281	            _units.Remove(key);
   282	        }
   283	
   284	        string[] removedBays = _bays.Keys
   285	            .Where(value => !visibleBays.Contains(value))
   286	            .ToArray();
   287	        for (int index = 0; index < removedBays.Length; index++)
   288	        {
   289	            string key = removedBays[index];
   290	            Destroy(_bays[key].gameObject);
   291	            _bays.Remove(key);
   292	        }
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    private void OnDestroy()
   296	    {
   297	        foreach (Material material in _materials.Values)
   298	        {
   299	            if (material != null)
   300	            {
   301	                Destroy(material);
   302	            }
   303	        }
   304	
   305	        _materials.Clear();
   306	    }
   307	}
