===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingPackingExecution.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
    11	using Dig.Domain.World;
    12	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    13	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    14	using Dig.Presentation.Inventory;
    15	using Dig.Presentation.Navigation;
    16	
    17	namespace Dig.Unity
    18	{
    19	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    20	{
    21	    private readonly Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingPackingRoutePlan> _buildingPackingRoutes =
    22	        new Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingPackingRoutePlan>();
    23	    private InMemoryJobCandidateProvider? _buildingPackingCandidates;
    24	    private AssignAvailableJobsHandler? _buildingPackingAssignment;
    25	    private AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkHandler? _buildingPackingWork;
    26	    private CompleteBuildingBoxPackingHandler? _buildingPackingCompletion;
    27	    private InventoryWorldPresenter? _buildingInventoryPresenter;
    28	    private NavigationPathfinder? _buildingPackingPathfinder;
    29	
    30	    private void InitializeBuildingPackingExecution(InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
    31	    {
    32	        if (_buildingsRepository == null || _buildingInventoryRepository == null)
    33	        {
    34	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Building demo state must be initialized first.");
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        _packableBuildingExecutions ??= new PackableBuildingExecutionRegistry();
    38	        InitializePackableBuildingIterationProgression();
    39	        _buildingPackingCandidates = new InMemoryJobCandidateProvider();
    40	        _buildingPackingAssignment = new AssignAvailableJobsHandler(
    41	            _jobRepository,
    42	            _buildingPackingCandidates,
    43	            journal);
    44	        _buildingPackingWork = new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkHandler(
    45	            _buildingsRepository,
    46	            _jobRepository,
    47	            journal);
    48	        _buildingPackingCompletion = new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingHandler(
    49	            _buildingsRepository,
    50	            _buildingInventoryRepository,
    51	            _jobRepository,
    52	            journal,
    53	            _skillGrants);
    54	        _buildingInventoryPresenter = new InventoryWorldPresenter(
    55	            new GetInventorySnapshotQueryHandler(_buildingInventoryRepository));
    56	        _buildingPackingPathfinder = new NavigationPathfinder();
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    public void SynchronizeBuildingPacking(
    60	        long tick,
    61	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
    62	    {
    63	        if (agents == null)
    64	        {
    65	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(agents));
    66	        }
    67	
    68	        EnsureBuildingPackingExecutionInitialized();
    69	        bool hasAvailablePacking = false;
    70	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot building in _buildingsRepository!.Get().GetAll())
    71	        {
    72	            BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? packing = building.PackingPlan;
    73	            if (packing?.CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
    74	            {
    75	                continue;
    76	            }
    77	
    78	            JobSnapshot? job = _jobRepository.Get().Get(packing.JobId);
    79	            if (job?.Status != JobStatus.Available
    80	                || job.Definition is not BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition definition)
    81	            {
    82	                continue;
    83	            }
    84	
    85	            _buildingPackingCandidates!.SetCandidates(
    86	                job.Id,
    87	                CreateBuildingPackingCandidates(agents, definition.WorkPosition));
    88	            hasAvailablePacking = true;
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        if (hasAvailablePacking)
    92	        {
    93	            _buildingPackingAssignment!.Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick));
    94	        }
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    public Result AdvanceBuildingPacking(
    98	        long tick,
    99	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   100	    {
   101	        if (agents == null)
   102	        {
   103	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(agents));
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
   107	            agent => agent.Id,
   108	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
   109	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
   110	        {
   111	            if (!IsActive(job)
   112	                || job.Definition is not BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition
   113	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   114	                || !agentsById.TryGetValue(
   115	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   116	                    out AgentViewModel? agent))
   117	            {
   118	                continue;
   119	            }
   120	
   121	            TryAdvanceBuildingPacking(job, agent, tick, out Result result);
   122	            if (result.IsFailure)
   123	            {
   124	                return result;
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	
   128	        return Result.Success();
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    internal bool TryPlanBuildingPackingMovement(
   132	        JobSnapshot job,
   133	        AgentViewModel agent,
   134	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
   135	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
   136	    {
   137	        if (job.Definition is not BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition packing)
   138	        {
   139	            return false;
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        EnsureBuildingPackingExecutionInitialized();
   143	        CellId start = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
   144	        PathResult path = _buildingPackingPathfinder!.FindPath(
   145	            navigation,
   146	            new PathRequest(start, packing.WorkPosition, navigation.NavigationVersion));
   147	        _buildingPackingRoutes[job.Id] = new BuildingPackingRoutePlan(
   148	            packing.WorkPosition,
   149	            path);
   150	        if (path.Succeeded)
   151	        {
   152	            movement[agent.Id] = path.Path!.Cells.Count > 1
   153	                ? path.Path.Cells[1]
   154	                : packing.WorkPosition;
   155	        }
   156	
   157	        return true;
   158	    }
   159	
   160	    internal bool TryAdvanceBuildingPacking(
   161	        JobSnapshot job,
   162	        AgentViewModel agent,
   163	        long tick,
   164	        out Result result)
   165	    {
   166	        if (job.Definition is not BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition packing)
   167	        {
   168	            result = Result.Success();
   169	            return false;
   170	        }
   171	
   172	        EnsureBuildingPackingExecutionInitialized();
   173	        BuildingSnapshot? building = _buildingsRepository!.Get().Get(packing.BuildingId);
   174	        Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind> evaluated =
   175	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionPolicy.Evaluate(
   176	                job,
   177	                building,
   178	                new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ));
   179	        if (evaluated.IsFailure)
   180	        {
   181	            result = Result.Failure(evaluated.Error!);
   182	            return true;
   183	        }
   184	
   185	        result = ExecuteBuildingPackingStep(
   186	            evaluated.Value,
   187	            building!,
   188	            job.AssignedAgentId!.Value,
   189	            packing.BuildingId,
   190	            job.Id,
   191	            tick);
   192	        if (result.IsSuccess
   193	            && evaluated.Value == BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.CompletePacking)
   194	        {
   195	            _buildingPackingRoutes.Remove(job.Id);
   196	        }
   197	
   198	        return true;
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    internal IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> LoadBuildingPackingRoutes()
   202	    {
   203	        List<RouteViewModel> routes = new List<RouteViewModel>();
   204	        foreach (KeyValuePair<EntityId, BuildingPackingRoutePlan> pair
   205	            in _buildingPackingRoutes.OrderBy(
   206	                value => value.Key.ToString(),
   207	                StringComparer.Ordinal))
   208	        {
   209	            JobSnapshot? job = _jobRepository.Get().Get(pair.Key);
   210	            if (job == null || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   211	            {
   212	                continue;
   213	            }
   214	
   215	            PathResult path = pair.Value.Path;
   216	            RouteCellViewModel[] cells = path.Path == null
   217	                ? Array.Empty<RouteCellViewModel>()
   218	                : path.Path.Cells
   219	                    .Select(cell => new RouteCellViewModel(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z))
   220	                    .ToArray();
   221	            routes.Add(new RouteViewModel(
   222	                pair.Key.ToString(),
   223	                job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   224	                pair.Value.Target.X,
   225	                pair.Value.Target.Y,
   226	                path.Succeeded,
   227	                "Building packing: " + path.Diagnostics.Detail,
   228	                path.Path?.TotalCost ?? 0,
   229	                path.Diagnostics.SnapshotVersion,
   230	                cells));
   231	        }
   232	
   233	        return routes;
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    internal IReadOnlyList<WorldItemViewModel> LoadAllWorldItems()
   237	    {
   238	        // The demo uses one authoritative inventory repository for terrain output,
   239	        // ordinary world items, and BuildingBoxes. Project it exactly once; concatenating
   240	        // a second presenter produced duplicate stack ids with conflicting interaction kinds.
   241	        return _inventoryPresenter.Load();
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    private Result ExecuteBuildingPackingStep(
   245	        BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind step,
   246	        BuildingSnapshot building,
   247	        EntityId workerId,
   248	        EntityId buildingId,
   249	        EntityId jobId,
   250	        long tick)
   251	    {
   252	        if (step == BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None)
   253	        {
   254	            return Result.Success();
   255	        }
   256	
   257	        Result<PackableBuildingExecutionState> execution =
   258	            GetOrCreatePackableBuildingExecution(
   259	                jobId,
   260	                buildingId,
   261	                building.Definition.Id,
   262	                PackableBuildingOperationKind.Pack,
   263	                building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.PackingWork);
   264	        if (execution.IsFailure)
   265	        {
   266	            return Result.Failure(execution.Error!);
   267	        }
   268	
   269	        if (step == BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.StartJob)
   270	        {
   271	            Result started = _packableBuildingExecutions!.StartOrResume(jobId, workerId);
   272	            return started.IsFailure
   273	                ? started
   274	                : _advanceHandler.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(jobId, tick));
   275	        }
   276	
   277	        if (step == BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AddWork)
   278	        {
   279	            return ExecutePackableBuildingIteration(
   280	                jobId,
   281	                workerId,
   282	                tick,
   283	                () => _buildingPackingWork!.Handle(new AddBuildingBoxPackingWorkCommand(
   284	                    buildingId,
   285	                    jobId,
   286	                    workAmount: 1,
   287	                    tick: tick)));
   288	        }
   289	
   290	        return step switch
   291	        {
   292	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage =>
   293	                _advanceHandler.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(jobId, tick)),
   294	            BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.CompletePacking =>
   295	                _buildingPackingCompletion!.Handle(new CompleteBuildingBoxPackingCommand(
   296	                    buildingId,
   297	                    jobId,
   298	                    tick)),
   299	            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(step)),
   300	        };
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    private static IReadOnlyList<JobCandidate> CreateBuildingPackingCandidates(
   304	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
   305	        CellId target)
   306	    {
   307	        JobCandidate[] candidates = new JobCandidate[agents.Count];
   308	        for (int index = 0; index < agents.Count; index++)
   309	        {
   310	            AgentViewModel agent = agents[index];
   311	            int distance = Math.Abs(agent.CellX - target.X)
   312	                + Math.Abs(agent.CellY - target.Y);
   313	            candidates[index] = new JobCandidate(
   314	                EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
   315	                skillLevel: 4_500 - (index * 200),
   316	                distanceCost: distance,
   317	                isAvailable: agent.IsAvailableForAutomaticPlanning);
   318	        }
   319	
   320	        return candidates;
   321	    }
   322	
   323	    private void EnsureBuildingPackingExecutionInitialized()
   324	    {
   325	        if (_buildingsRepository == null
   326	            || _buildingInventoryRepository == null
   327	            || _buildingPackingCandidates == null
   328	            || _buildingPackingAssignment == null
   329	            || _buildingPackingWork == null
   330	            || _buildingPackingCompletion == null
   331	            || _buildingInventoryPresenter == null
   332	            || _buildingPackingPathfinder == null
   333	            || _packableBuildingExecutions == null
   334	            || _campfireIterationProgression == null)
   335	        {
   336	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   337	                "Building packing execution is not initialized.");
   338	        }
   339	    }
   340	}
   341	
   342	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingProductionFoodDependencies.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Ecology;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    10	using Dig.Domain.World;
    11	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Unity
    14	{
    15	    internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    16	    {
    17	        private const int ProductionFoodDependencyPriority = 625;
    18	
    19	        private void CreateEligibleFoodDependencyJobs(
    20	            long tick,
    21	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
    22	        {
    23	            if (_mushroomRepository == null
    24	                || _startMushroomChop == null
    25	                || _buildingSupplyRepository == null
    26	                || _productionRepository == null
    27	                || _buildingInventoryRepository == null)
    28	            {
    29	                return;
    30	            }
    31	
    32	            HashSet<CellId> revealed = GetProductionRevealedCells().ToHashSet();
    33	            HashSet<CellId> reachable = GetProductionReachableCells().ToHashSet();
    34	            InventorySnapshot inventory = _buildingInventoryRepository.Get().CreateSnapshot();
    35	            BuildingSupplyState supplies = _buildingSupplyRepository.Get();
    36	
    37	            foreach (BuildingSupplySnapshot supply in supplies.GetAll(inventory))
    38	            {
    39	                ProductionOrderSnapshot? order = _productionRepository.Get()
    40	                    .GetNextQueued(supply.BuildingId);
    41	                if (order == null
    42	                    || order.Recipe.Id != CampfireProductionContent.GrilledMushroomRecipeId
    43	                    || supply.HasActiveSupply
    44	                    || _productionRepository.Get().HasActiveOrder(supply.BuildingId))
    45	                {
    46	                    continue;
    47	                }
    48	
    49	                BuildingStockSnapshot capStock = supply.Stocks.First(
    50	                    value => value.ItemId == CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId);
    51	                if (capStock.Current > 0 || capStock.Incoming > 0)
    52	                {
    53	                    continue;
    54	                }
    55	
    56	                bool eligibleWorldCap = inventory.Stacks.Any(stack =>
    57	                    stack.ItemId == CampfireProductionContent.MushroomCapItemId
    58	                    && stack.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World
    59	                    && stack.Location.HasCell
    60	                    && stack.AvailableQuantity > 0
    61	                    && revealed.Contains(stack.Location.CellId)
    62	                    && reachable.Contains(stack.Location.CellId));
    63	                if (eligibleWorldCap)
    64	                {
    65	                    // The ordinary BuildingSupply pipeline owns an existing cap. Do not
    66	                    // create extra biological work merely because no worker is free yet.
    67	                    continue;
    68	                }
    69	
    70	                FoodDependencyCandidate? candidate = ResolveFoodDependencyCandidate(
    71	                    agents,
    72	                    revealed,
    73	                    reachable);
    74	                if (!candidate.HasValue)
    75	                {
    76	                    continue;
    77	                }
    78	
    79	                EntityId jobId = DemoId('9', checked(++_nextMushroomJobSequence));
    80	                Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> started = _startMushroomChop.Handle(
    81	                    new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
    82	                        jobId,
    83	                        candidate.Value.Site.SiteId,
    84	                        candidate.Value.ResidentId,
    85	                        candidate.Value.WorkPosition,
    86	                        ProductionFoodDependencyPriority,
    87	                        tick));
    88	                if (started.IsSuccess)
    89	                {
    90	                    // One new biological dependency per synchronization tick is enough;
    91	                    // its world drops are reconciled by the normal supply chain.
    92	                    return;
    93	                }
    94	            }
    95	        }
    96	
    97	        private FoodDependencyCandidate? ResolveFoodDependencyCandidate(
    98	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
    99	            HashSet<CellId> revealed,
   100	            HashSet<CellId> reachable)
   101	        {
   102	            FoodDependencyCandidate? best = null;
   103	            foreach (MushroomSiteSnapshot site in _mushroomRepository!.Get().GetAll()
   104	                .Where(value => value.Stage == MushroomStage.Large
   105	                    && !value.IsChopActive
   106	                    && revealed.Contains(value.Cell))
   107	                .OrderBy(value => value.Cell.Y)
   108	                .ThenBy(value => value.Cell.X)
   109	                .ThenBy(value => value.Cell.Z)
   110	                .ThenBy(value => value.SiteId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   111	            {
   112	                foreach (AgentViewModel agent in agents
   113	                    .Where(IsAvailableForAutomaticWork)
   114	                    .OrderBy(value => value.Id, StringComparer.Ordinal))
   115	                {
   116	                    CellId workerCell = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
   117	                    if (!TryResolveMushroomWorkPosition(
   118	                            site.Cell,
   119	                            workerCell,
   120	                            out CellId workPosition)
   121	                        || !reachable.Contains(workPosition))
   122	                    {
   123	                        continue;
   124	                    }
   125	
   126	                    FoodDependencyCandidate current = new FoodDependencyCandidate(
   127	                        site,
   128	                        EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
   129	                        workPosition,
   130	                        Distance(agent, workPosition));
   131	                    if (!best.HasValue || current.CompareTo(best.Value) < 0)
   132	                    {
   133	                        best = current;
   134	                    }
   135	                }
   136	            }
   137	
   138	            return best;
   139	        }
   140	
   141	        private readonly struct FoodDependencyCandidate
   142	            : IComparable<FoodDependencyCandidate>
   143	        {
   144	            internal FoodDependencyCandidate(
   145	                MushroomSiteSnapshot site,
   146	                EntityId residentId,
   147	                CellId workPosition,
   148	                int distance)
   149	            {
   150	                Site = site;
   151	                ResidentId = residentId;
   152	                WorkPosition = workPosition;
   153	                DistanceCost = distance;
   154	            }
   155	
   156	            internal MushroomSiteSnapshot Site { get; }
   157	            internal EntityId ResidentId { get; }
   158	            internal CellId WorkPosition { get; }
   159	            internal int DistanceCost { get; }
   160	
   161	            public int CompareTo(FoodDependencyCandidate other)
   162	            {
   163	                int distance = DistanceCost.CompareTo(other.DistanceCost);
   164	                if (distance != 0)
   165	                {
   166	                    return distance;
   167	                }
   168	
   169	                int site = string.Compare(
   170	                    Site.SiteId.ToString(),
   171	                    other.Site.SiteId.ToString(),
   172	                    StringComparison.Ordinal);
   173	                return site != 0
   174	                    ? site
   175	                    : string.Compare(
   176	                        ResidentId.ToString(),
   177	                        other.ResidentId.ToString(),
   178	                        StringComparison.Ordinal);
   179	            }
   180	        }
   181	    }
   182	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingProductionRuntime.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Application.Production;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
    10	using Dig.Domain.World;
    11	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    12	using Dig.Presentation.Navigation;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Unity
    15	{
    16	
    17	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    18	{
    19	    private Result AdvanceSupplyJob(
    20	        JobSnapshot job,
    21	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition supply,
    22	        AgentViewModel worker,
    23	        long tick)
    24	    {
    25	        Result checkedStock = AdvanceSupplyWorkstationCheck(
    26	            job,
    27	            supply,
    28	            worker,
    29	            tick,
    30	            out JobSnapshot currentJob);
    31	        if (checkedStock.IsFailure)
    32	        {
    33	            return checkedStock;
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        job = currentJob;
    37	
    38	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.AcquireItem)
    39	        {
    40	            ItemReservationAllocation? pending = FindPendingSupplyAllocation(job.Id, supply);
    41	            if (!pending.HasValue)
    42	            {
    43	                return Result.Success();
    44	            }
    45	
    46	            ItemStackSnapshot? source = _buildingInventoryRepository!.Get().GetStack(
    47	                pending.Value.StackId);
    48	            if (source?.Location.Kind != ItemLocationKind.World
    49	                || !source.Location.HasCell)
    50	            {
    51	                return _cancelBuildingSupply!.Handle(
    52	                    new CancelBuildingSupplyCommand(
    53	                        job.Id,
    54	                        "source_unavailable",
    55	                        tick));
    56	            }
    57	
    58	            if (!At(worker, source.Location.CellId))
    59	            {
    60	                return Result.Success();
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            return _acquireBuildingSupplySource!.Handle(
    64	                new AcquireBuildingSupplySourceCommand(
    65	                    job.Id,
    66	                    source.StackId,
    67	                    tick));
    68	        }
    69	
    70	        if (!At(worker, supply.WorkPosition))
    71	        {
    72	            return Result.Success();
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToDestination)
    76	        {
    77	            Result advanced = _advanceHandler.Handle(
    78	                new Dig.Application.Jobs.AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
    79	            if (advanced.IsFailure)
    80	            {
    81	                return advanced;
    82	            }
    83	        }
    84	
    85	        JobSnapshot? current = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id);
    86	        if (current?.Stage == JobStageKind.DepositItem)
    87	        {
    88	            Result deposited = _depositBuildingSupply!.Handle(
    89	                new DepositBuildingSupplyCommand(job.Id, tick));
    90	            if (deposited.IsSuccess)
    91	            {
    92	                _buildingSupplyRoutes.Remove(job.Id);
    93	            }
    94	
    95	            return deposited;
    96	        }
    97	
    98	        return Result.Success();
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    private bool PlanBuildingProductionRoute(
   102	        IDictionary<EntityId, BuildingProductionRoutePlan> routes,
   103	        JobSnapshot job,
   104	        AgentViewModel agent,
   105	        CellId target,
   106	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
   107	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
   108	    {
   109	        CellId start = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
   110	        PathResult path = _productionPathfinder!.FindPath(
   111	            navigation,
   112	            new PathRequest(start, target, navigation.NavigationVersion));
   113	        routes[job.Id] = new BuildingProductionRoutePlan(target, path);
   114	        if (path.Succeeded)
   115	        {
   116	            movement[agent.Id] = path.Path!.Cells.Count > 1
   117	                ? path.Path.Cells[1]
   118	                : target;
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        return true;
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    private IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> PresentBuildingProductionRoutes(
   125	        IReadOnlyDictionary<EntityId, BuildingProductionRoutePlan> routes,
   126	        string label)
   127	    {
   128	        List<RouteViewModel> values = new List<RouteViewModel>();
   129	        foreach (KeyValuePair<EntityId, BuildingProductionRoutePlan> pair in routes
   130	            .OrderBy(value => value.Key.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   131	        {
   132	            JobSnapshot? job = _jobRepository.Get().Get(pair.Key);
   133	            if (job == null || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   134	            {
   135	                continue;
   136	            }
   137	
   138	            PathResult path = pair.Value.Path;
   139	            RouteCellViewModel[] cells = path.Path == null
   140	                ? Array.Empty<RouteCellViewModel>()
   141	                : path.Path.Cells
   142	                    .Select(cell => new RouteCellViewModel(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z))
   143	                    .ToArray();
   144	            values.Add(new RouteViewModel(
   145	                pair.Key.ToString(),
   146	                job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   147	                pair.Value.Target.X,
   148	                pair.Value.Target.Y,
   149	                path.Succeeded,
   150	                label + ": " + path.Diagnostics.Detail,
   151	                path.Path?.TotalCost ?? 0,
   152	                path.Diagnostics.SnapshotVersion,
   153	                cells));
   154	        }
   155	
   156	        return values;
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    private CellId[] GetProductionRevealedCells()
   160	    {
   161	        return _worldSession.LoadSnapshot().Chunks
   162	            .SelectMany(value => value.Cells)
   163	            .Where(value => value.State.IsExplored)
   164	            .Select(value => value.Id)
   165	            .OrderBy(value => value)
   166	            .ToArray();
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    private IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> GetProductionReachableCells()
   170	    {
   171	        return _worldSession.LoadSnapshot().Chunks
   172	            .SelectMany(value => value.Cells)
   173	            .Where(value => value.State.IsExplored && !value.IsSolid)
   174	            .Select(value => value.Id)
   175	            .OrderBy(value => value)
   176	            .ToArray();
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    private static int Distance(AgentViewModel agent, CellId target)
   180	    {
   181	        return Math.Abs(agent.CellX - target.X)
   182	            + Math.Abs(agent.CellY - target.Y)
   183	            + Math.Abs(agent.CellZ - target.Z);
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    private static bool At(AgentViewModel agent, CellId cell)
   187	    {
   188	        return agent.CellX == cell.X
   189	            && agent.CellY == cell.Y
   190	            && agent.CellZ == cell.Z;
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    private static EntityId NextProductionEntityId(char prefix, ref long sequence)
   194	    {
   195	        sequence = checked(sequence + 1);
   196	        return DemoId(prefix, checked(10_000_000L + sequence));
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private void EnsureBuildingProductionInitialized()
   200	    {
   201	        if (_productionContent == null
   202	            || _productionRepository == null
   203	            || _buildingSupplyRepository == null
   204	            || _buildingProductionPresenter == null
   205	            || _enqueueProduction == null
   206	            || _prepareProduction == null
   207	            || _beginProduction == null
   208	            || _applyProductionWork == null
   209	            || _completeProduction == null
   210	            || _cancelProduction == null
   211	            || _createBuildingSupply == null
   212	            || _acquireBuildingSupplySource == null
   213	            || _depositBuildingSupply == null
   214	            || _setBuildingStockDelivery == null
   215	            || _productionCandidates == null
   216	            || _productionAssignment == null
   217	            || _productionPathfinder == null)
   218	        {
   219	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   220	                "Building production execution is not initialized.");
   221	        }
   222	    }

   224	    private sealed class BuildingProductionRoutePlan
   225	    {
   226	        internal BuildingProductionRoutePlan(CellId target, PathResult path)
   227	        {
   228	            Target = target;
   229	            Path = path;
   230	        }
   231

   233	        internal PathResult Path { get; }
   234	    }

===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingProductionSupplyCheck.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Unity
    10	{
    11	
    12	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    13	{
    14	    private Result AdvanceSupplyWorkstationCheck(
    15	        JobSnapshot job,
    16	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition supply,
    17	        AgentViewModel worker,
    18	        long tick,
    19	        out JobSnapshot current)
    20	    {
    21	        current = job;
    22	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed)
    23	        {
    24	            if (!At(worker, supply.WorkPosition))
    25	            {
    26	                return Result.Success();
    27	            }
    28	
    29	            Result started = _advanceHandler.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
    30	            if (started.IsFailure)
    31	            {
    32	                return started;
    33	            }
    34	
    35	            current = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id)
    36	                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Started supply job disappeared.");
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        if (current.Status == JobStatus.InProgress
    40	            && current.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
    41	        {
    42	            if (!At(worker, supply.WorkPosition))
    43	            {
    44	                return Result.Success();
    45	            }
    46	
    47	            Result checkedStock = _advanceHandler.Handle(
    48	                new AdvanceJobCommand(current.Id, tick));
    49	            if (checkedStock.IsFailure)
    50	            {
    51	                return checkedStock;
    52	            }
    53	
    54	            current = _jobRepository.Get().Get(current.Id)
    55	                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Checked supply job disappeared.");
    56	        }
    57	
    58	        return Result.Success();
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    private ItemReservationAllocation? FindPendingSupplyAllocation(
    62	        EntityId jobId,
    63	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition supply)
    64	    {
    65	        InventoryState inventory = _buildingInventoryRepository!.Get();
    66	        return supply.Allocations
    67	            .Where(value =>
    68	            {
    69	                ItemStackSnapshot? stack = inventory.GetStack(value.StackId);
    70	                return stack?.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World
    71	                    && stack.Reservations.Any(reservation =>
    72	                        reservation.JobId == jobId
    73	                        && reservation.Quantity > 0);
    74	            })
    75	            .OrderBy(value => value.StackId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
    76	            .Select(value => (ItemReservationAllocation?)value)
    77	            .FirstOrDefault();
    78	    }
    79	}
    80	
    81	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingProductionSynchronization.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Production;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Content;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    12	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    13	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
    14	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    15	using Dig.Domain.Technology;
    16	using Dig.Domain.World;
    17	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    18	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    19	using Dig.Presentation.Production;
    20	using Dig.Presentation.Navigation;
    21	
    22	namespace Dig.Unity
    23	{
    24	
    25	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    26	{
    27	    internal void SynchronizeBuildingProduction(
    28	        long tick,
    29	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
    30	    {
    31	        EnsureBuildingProductionInitialized();
    32	        SynchronizeProductionWorkstationRegistrations(tick);
    33	        PrepareEligibleProductionOrders(tick);
    34	        AssignProductionJobs(tick, agents);
    35	        CreateEligibleSupplyJobs(tick, agents);
    36	        CreateEligibleFoodDependencyJobs(tick, agents);
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    internal Result AdvanceBuildingProduction(
    40	        long tick,
    41	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
    42	    {
    43	        EnsureBuildingProductionInitialized();
    44	        Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
    45	            value => value.Id,
    46	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
    47	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
    48	        {
    49	            if (!IsActive(job)
    50	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
    51	                || !agentsById.TryGetValue(
    52	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
    53	                    out AgentViewModel? worker))
    54	            {
    55	                continue;
    56	            }
    57	
    58	            Result result = Result.Success();
    59	            if (job.Definition is ProductionWorkJobDefinition production)
    60	            {
    61	                result = AdvanceProductionJob(job, production, worker, tick);
    62	            }
    63	            else if (job.Definition is BuildingSupplyJobDefinition supply)
    64	            {
    65	                result = AdvanceSupplyJob(job, supply, worker, tick);
    66	            }
    67	
    68	            if (result.IsFailure)
    69	            {
    70	                return result;
    71	            }
    72	        }
    73	
    74	        return Result.Success();
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    internal bool TryPlanBuildingSupplyMovement(
    78	        JobSnapshot job,
    79	        AgentViewModel agent,
    80	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
    81	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
    82	    {
    83	        if (job.Definition is not BuildingSupplyJobDefinition supply)
    84	        {
    85	            return false;
    86	        }
    87	
    88	        EnsureBuildingProductionInitialized();
    89	        CellId target = supply.WorkPosition;
    90	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.AcquireItem)
    91	        {
    92	            ItemReservationAllocation? pending = FindPendingSupplyAllocation(
    93	                job.Id,
    94	                supply);
    95	            if (pending.HasValue)
    96	            {
    97	                ItemStackSnapshot? source = _buildingInventoryRepository!.Get()
    98	                    .GetStack(pending.Value.StackId);
    99	                if (source?.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World
   100	                    && source.Location.HasCell)
   101	                {
   102	                    target = source.Location.CellId;
   103	                }
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        return PlanBuildingProductionRoute(
   108	            _buildingSupplyRoutes,
   109	            job,
   110	            agent,
   111	            target,
   112	            navigation,
   113	            movement);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    internal IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> LoadBuildingProductionRoutes()
   117	    {
   118	        return PresentBuildingProductionRoutes(
   119	                _buildingProductionRoutes,
   120	                "Production")
   121	            .Concat(PresentBuildingProductionRoutes(
   122	                _buildingSupplyRoutes,
   123	                "Building supply"))
   124	            .ToArray();
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    private void SynchronizeProductionWorkstationRegistrations(long tick)
   128	    {
   129	        BuildingSupplyState supply = _buildingSupplyRepository!.Get();
   130	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot building in _buildingsRepository!.Get().GetAll())
   131	        {
   132	            if (building.Status == BuildingStatus.Completed
   133	                && _productionContent!.ContainsWorkstation(building.Definition.Id))
   134	            {
   135	                Result registered = supply.Register(
   136	                    building.Id,
   137	                    _productionContent.GetWorkstation(building.Definition.Id),
   138	                    tick);
   139	                if (registered.IsFailure)
   140	                {
   141	                    throw new InvalidOperationException(registered.Error!.ToString());
   142	                }
   143	            }
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        _buildingSupplyRepository.Save(supply);
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    private void PrepareEligibleProductionOrders(long tick)
   150	    {
   151	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> reachable = GetProductionReachableCells();
   152	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot building in _buildingsRepository!.Get().GetAll())
   153	        {
   154	            if (building.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   155	                || !_productionContent!.ContainsWorkstation(building.Definition.Id)
   156	                || _productionRepository!.Get().HasActiveOrder(building.Id)
   157	                || _productionRepository.Get().GetNextQueued(building.Id) == null)
   158	            {
   159	                continue;
   160	            }
   161	
   162	            EntityId jobId = NextProductionEntityId(
   163	                'f',
   164	                ref _nextProductionJobSequence);
   165	            Result prepared = _prepareProduction!.Handle(
   166	                new PrepareProductionOrderCommand(
   167	                    jobId,
   168	                    building.Id,
   169	                    reachable,
   170	                    priority: 700,
   171	                    tick));
   172	            if (prepared.IsFailure
   173	                && prepared.Error != InventoryErrors.InsufficientAvailableQuantity
   174	                && prepared.Error != ProductionErrors.QueueBlocked)
   175	            {
   176	                continue;
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    private void AssignProductionJobs(
   182	        long tick,
   183	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   184	    {
   185	        bool available = false;
   186	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
   187	        {
   188	            if (job.Status != JobStatus.Available
   189	                || job.Definition is not ProductionWorkJobDefinition production)
   190	            {
   191	                continue;
   192	            }
   193	
   194	            RecipeDefinition recipe = _productionContent!.GetRecipe(production.RecipeId);
   195	            AgentSkillId skill = recipe.MaterialSteps.Count == 0
   196	                ? AgentSkillCatalog.Logistics
   197	                : recipe.MaterialSteps[0].SkillId;
   198	            JobCandidate[] candidates = agents
   199	                .Select(agent => new JobCandidate(
   200	                    EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
   201	                    _productionAgents!.Get(EntityId.Parse(agent.Id))?
   202	                        .CreateSnapshot(tick)
   203	                        .GetSkillLevel(skill) ?? 0,
   204	                    Distance(agent, production.WorkPosition),
   205	                    IsAvailableForAutomaticWork(agent)))
   206	                .ToArray();
   207	            _productionCandidates!.SetCandidates(job.Id, candidates);
   208	            available = true;
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        if (available)
   212	        {
   213	            _productionAssignment!.Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick));
   214	        }
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    private void CreateEligibleSupplyJobs(
   218	        long tick,
   219	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   220	    {
   221	        CellId[] revealed = GetProductionRevealedCells();
   222	        CellId[] reachable = GetProductionReachableCells().ToArray();
   223	        BuildingSupplyState supply = _buildingSupplyRepository!.Get();
   224	        InventorySnapshot inventory = _buildingInventoryRepository!.Get().CreateSnapshot();
   225	        foreach (BuildingSupplySnapshot snapshot in supply.GetAll(inventory))
   226	        {
   227	            BuildingSnapshot? building = _buildingsRepository!.Get().Get(
   228	                snapshot.BuildingId);
   229	            if (building == null
   230	                || building.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   231	                || snapshot.HasActiveSupply
   232	                || _productionRepository!.Get().HasActiveOrder(snapshot.BuildingId)
   233	                || snapshot.Stocks.All(value =>
   234	                    !value.DeliveryEnabled || value.Missing == 0))
   235	            {
   236	                continue;
   237	            }
   238	
   239	            AgentViewModel[] candidates = agents
   240	                .Where(IsAvailableForAutomaticWork)
   241	                .OrderBy(value => Distance(value, building.WorkPosition))
   242	                .ThenBy(value => value.Id, StringComparer.Ordinal)
   243	                .ToArray();
   244	            foreach (AgentViewModel agent in candidates)
   245	            {
   246	                EntityId jobId = NextProductionEntityId(
   247	                    'd',
   248	                    ref _nextSupplyJobSequence);
   249	                EntityId[] transit = Enumerable.Range(0, 12)
   250	                    .Select(_ => NextProductionEntityId(
   251	                        'c',
   252	                        ref _nextSupplyTransitSequence))
   253	                    .ToArray();
   254	                EntityId[] deposits = Enumerable.Range(0, snapshot.Stocks.Count)
   255	                    .Select(_ => NextProductionEntityId(
   256	                        'b',
   257	                        ref _nextSupplyDepositSequence))
   258	                    .ToArray();
   259	                Result created = _createBuildingSupply!.Handle(
   260	                    new CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand(
   261	                        jobId,
   262	                        snapshot.BuildingId,
   263	                        EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
   264	                        revealed,
   265	                        reachable,
   266	                        transit,
   267	                        deposits,
   268	                        priority: 650,
   269	                        tick));
   270	                if (created.IsSuccess)
   271	                {
   272	                    break;
   273	                }
   274	            }
   275	        }
   276	    }
   277	}
   278	
   279	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigBuildingProductionZones.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Production;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    11	using Dig.Domain.World;
    12	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Unity
    15	{
    16	
    17	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    18	{
    19	    private const float ProductionWaitOffset = 0.28f;
    20	    private readonly Dictionary<string, float> _productionWaitOffsets =
    21	        new Dictionary<string, float>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    22	
    23	    private Result AdvanceProductionJob(
    24	        JobSnapshot job,
    25	        ProductionWorkJobDefinition production,
    26	        AgentViewModel worker,
    27	        long tick)
    28	    {
    29	        bool atWorkstation = At(worker, production.WorkPosition);
    30	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed)
    31	        {
    32	            if (!atWorkstation)
    33	            {
    34	                return Result.Success();
    35	            }
    36	
    37	            Result begun = _beginProduction!.Handle(
    38	                new BeginProductionWorkCommand(production.OrderId, job.Id, tick));
    39	            if (begun.IsFailure)
    40	            {
    41	                return begun;
    42	            }
    43	
    44	            Result travelled = _advanceHandler.Handle(
    45	                new Dig.Application.Jobs.AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
    46	            if (travelled.IsFailure)
    47	            {
    48	                return travelled;
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	        else if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
    52	        {
    53	            if (!atWorkstation)
    54	            {
    55	                return Result.Success();
    56	            }
    57	
    58	            Result travelled = _advanceHandler.Handle(
    59	                new Dig.Application.Jobs.AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
    60	            if (travelled.IsFailure)
    61	            {
    62	                return travelled;
    63	            }
    64	        }
    65	
    66	        JobSnapshot? current = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id);
    67	        if (current?.Stage == JobStageKind.PerformWork)
    68	        {
    69	            if (!atWorkstation || tick % 2 != 0)
    70	            {
    71	                return Result.Success();
    72	            }
    73	
    74	            Result worked = _applyProductionWork!.Handle(
    75	                new ApplyProductionWorkCommand(
    76	                    production.OrderId,
    77	                    job.Id,
    78	                    baseWork: 1,
    79	                    conditionEfficiencyBasisPoints: 10_000,
    80	                    tick));
    81	            if (worked.IsFailure)
    82	            {
    83	                return worked;
    84	            }
    85	
    86	            current = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id);
    87	        }
    88	
    89	        if (current?.Stage != JobStageKind.Finalize)
    90	        {
    91	            return Result.Success();
    92	        }
    93	
    94	        BuildingSnapshot? building = _buildingsRepository!.Get().Get(
    95	            production.BuildingId);
    96	        if (building == null)
    97	        {
    98	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.WorkstationMismatch);
    99	        }
   100	
   101	        Result<CellId> outputCell = ResolveProductionOutputCell(building);
   102	        if (outputCell.IsFailure || !At(worker, outputCell.Value))
   103	        {
   104	            return Result.Success();
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        ProductionOrderSnapshot? order = _productionRepository!.Get().Get(
   108	            production.OrderId);
   109	        if (order == null)
   110	        {
   111	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.OrderNotFound);
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        EntityId[] outputs = order.Recipe.Outputs
   115	            .Select(_ => NextProductionEntityId(
   116	                'a',
   117	                ref _nextProductionOutputSequence))
   118	            .ToArray();
   119	        Result completed = _completeProduction!.Handle(
   120	            new CompleteProductionOrderCommand(
   121	                production.OrderId,
   122	                job.Id,
   123	                outputs,
   124	                tick,
   125	                ItemLocation.InWorld(outputCell.Value)));
   126	        if (completed.IsSuccess)
   127	        {
   128	            _buildingProductionRoutes.Remove(job.Id);
   129	            _productionWaitOffsets[worker.Id] = ProductionWaitOffset;
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        return completed;
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    internal bool TryPlanBuildingProductionMovement(
   136	        JobSnapshot job,
   137	        AgentViewModel agent,
   138	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
   139	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
   140	    {
   141	        if (job.Definition is not ProductionWorkJobDefinition production)
   142	        {
   143	            return false;
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        EnsureBuildingProductionInitialized();
   147	        CellId target = production.WorkPosition;
   148	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.Finalize)
   149	        {
   150	            BuildingSnapshot? building = _buildingsRepository!.Get().Get(
   151	                production.BuildingId);
   152	            if (building == null)
   153	            {
   154	                return true;
   155	            }
   156	
   157	            Result<CellId> outputCell = ResolveProductionOutputCell(building);
   158	            if (outputCell.IsFailure)
   159	            {
   160	                return true;
   161	            }
   162	
   163	            target = outputCell.Value;
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        return PlanBuildingProductionRoute(
   167	            _buildingProductionRoutes,
   168	            job,
   169	            agent,
   170	            target,
   171	            navigation,
   172	            movement);
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    internal IReadOnlyDictionary<string, float> LoadProductionWaitOffsets()
   176	    {
   177	        HashSet<string> active = _jobRepository.Get().GetAll()
   178	            .Where(IsActive)
   179	            .Where(value => value.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   180	            .Select(value => value.AssignedAgentId!.Value.ToString())
   181	            .ToHashSet(StringComparer.Ordinal);
   182	        string[] cleared = _productionWaitOffsets.Keys
   183	            .Where(value => active.Contains(value)
   184	                || (_isManualMovementActive?.Invoke(value) ?? false))
   185	            .ToArray();
   186	        for (int index = 0; index < cleared.Length; index++)
   187	        {
   188	            _productionWaitOffsets.Remove(cleared[index]);
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        return _productionWaitOffsets.ToDictionary(
   192	            value => value.Key,
   193	            value => value.Value,
   194	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    private Result<CellId> ResolveProductionOutputCell(BuildingSnapshot building)
   198	    {
   199	        return ProductionOutputPlacement.Resolve(
   200	            building,
   201	            _worldSession.LoadSnapshot(),
   202	            _buildingsRepository!.Get().GetOccupiedCells(),
   203	            _buildingInventoryRepository!.Get().CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   204	    }
   205	}
   206	
   207	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigMushroomRenderer.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     4	using UnityEngine;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Unity
     7	{
     8	[DisallowMultipleComponent]
     9	public sealed class DigMushroomRenderer : MonoBehaviour
    10	{
    11	    private readonly Dictionary<string, DigMushroomVisual> _visuals =
    12	        new Dictionary<string, DigMushroomVisual>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    13	    private Transform? _root;
    14	
    15	    internal int ActiveCount => _visuals.Count;
    16	
    17	    internal void Render(IReadOnlyList<MushroomSiteSnapshot> sites)
    18	    {
    19	        if (sites == null)
    20	        {
    21	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(sites));
    22	        }
    23	
    24	        EnsureRoot();
    25	        HashSet<string> visible = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    26	        for (int index = 0; index < sites.Count; index++)
    27	        {
    28	            MushroomSiteSnapshot site = sites[index];
    29	            if (!site.IsVisible)
    30	            {
    31	                continue;
    32	            }
    33	
    34	            string id = site.SiteId.ToString();
    35	            visible.Add(id);
    36	            if (!_visuals.TryGetValue(id, out DigMushroomVisual? visual))
    37	            {
    38	                GameObject root = new GameObject("Mushroom");
    39	                root.transform.SetParent(_root, worldPositionStays: false);
    40	                visual = root.AddComponent<DigMushroomVisual>();
    41	                _visuals.Add(id, visual);
    42	            }
    43	
    44	            visual.Configure(site);
    45	            visual.transform.position = DigTunnelProjection.ResidentWorldPosition(
    46	                site.Cell.X,
    47	                site.Cell.Y,
    48	                site.Cell.Z) + new Vector3(
    49	                    0f,
    50	                    DigTunnelProjection.ResidentFootSink,
    51	                    0f);
    52	        }
    53	
    54	        RemoveMissing(visible);
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    internal bool TryGetMushroom(RaycastHit hit, out DigMushroomVisual visual)
    58	    {
    59	        DigMushroomVisual? candidate = hit.collider == null
    60	            ? null
    61	            : hit.collider.GetComponentInParent<DigMushroomVisual>();
    62	        if (candidate != null
    63	            && candidate.Model.IsVisible
    64	            && _visuals.TryGetValue(candidate.Model.SiteId.ToString(), out DigMushroomVisual? tracked)
    65	            && ReferenceEquals(candidate, tracked))
    66	        {
    67	            visual = candidate;
    68	            return true;
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        visual = null!;
    72	        return false;
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    private void EnsureRoot()
    76	    {
    77	        if (_root != null)
    78	        {
    79	            return;
    80	        }
    81	
    82	        GameObject root = new GameObject("Mushrooms");
    83	        _root = root.transform;
    84	        // Mushroom roots use world-space tunnel projection and must not inherit the
    85	        // side-view bootstrap rotation. The stem/cap Y axis must remain world-up.
    86	        _root.SetParent(transform, worldPositionStays: true);
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    private void RemoveMissing(HashSet<string> visible)
    90	    {
    91	        List<string> removed = new List<string>();
    92	        foreach (KeyValuePair<string, DigMushroomVisual> pair in _visuals)
    93	        {
    94	            if (!visible.Contains(pair.Key))
    95	            {
    96	                removed.Add(pair.Key);
    97	            }
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        for (int index = 0; index < removed.Count; index++)
   101	        {
   102	            string id = removed[index];
   103	            Destroy(_visuals[id].gameObject);
   104	            _visuals.Remove(id);
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	}
   108	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigMushroomVisual.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     3	using UnityEngine;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Unity
     6	{
     7	[DisallowMultipleComponent]
     8	[RequireComponent(typeof(BoxCollider))]
     9	public sealed class DigMushroomVisual : MonoBehaviour
    10	{
    11	    private const float HoverBlend = 0.48f;
    12	    private static readonly int BaseColorId = Shader.PropertyToID("_BaseColor");
    13	    private static readonly int ColorId = Shader.PropertyToID("_Color");
    14	
    15	    private Transform? _stem;
    16	    private Transform? _cap;
    17	    private BoxCollider? _collider;
    18	    private Material? _stemMaterial;
    19	    private Material? _capMaterial;
    20	    private Renderer[] _renderers = Array.Empty<Renderer>();
    21	    private Color[] _baseColors = Array.Empty<Color>();
    22	    private MaterialPropertyBlock? _properties;
    23	    private bool _hovered;
    24	
    25	    internal MushroomSiteSnapshot Model { get; private set; } = null!;
    26	
    27	    internal void Configure(MushroomSiteSnapshot model)
    28	    {
    29	        Model = model ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(model));
    30	        EnsureGeometry();
    31	        (float height, float width) = ResolveSize(model.Stage);
    32	        transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
    33	        transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
    34	        _stem!.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, height * 0.41f, 0f);
    35	        _stem.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
    36	        _stem.localScale = new Vector3(width * 0.30f, height * 0.82f, width * 0.30f);
    37	        _cap!.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, height * 0.84f, 0f);
    38	        _cap.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
    39	        _cap.localScale = new Vector3(width, height * 0.30f, width * 0.82f);
    40	        _collider!.center = new Vector3(0f, height * 0.5f, 0f);
    41	        _collider.size = new Vector3(
    42	            Mathf.Max(0.12f, width),
    43	            height,
    44	            Mathf.Max(0.10f, width * 0.72f));
    45	        name = $"Mushroom {model.Stage} {model.SiteId}";
    46	        if (_hovered)
    47	        {
    48	            ApplyHover();
    49	        }
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    internal void SetHovered(bool hovered)
    53	    {
    54	        if (_hovered == hovered)
    55	        {
    56	            return;
    57	        }
    58	
    59	        _hovered = hovered;
    60	        if (_hovered)
    61	        {
    62	            ApplyHover();
    63	        }
    64	        else
    65	        {
    66	            RestoreColors();
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    private void EnsureGeometry()
    71	    {
    72	        if (_collider != null)
    73	        {
    74	            return;
    75	        }
    76	
    77	        _collider = GetComponent<BoxCollider>();
    78	        Shader shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit")
    79	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The URP/Lit mushroom shader was not found.");
    80	        _stemMaterial = CreateMaterial(
    81	            shader,
    82	            "Dig Mushroom Stem",
    83	            new Color(0.78f, 0.67f, 0.49f, 1f));
    84	        _capMaterial = CreateMaterial(
    85	            shader,
    86	            "Dig Mushroom Cap",
    87	            new Color(0.76f, 0.18f, 0.20f, 1f));
    88	        _stem = CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, "Stem", _stemMaterial);
    89	        _cap = CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Sphere, "Cap", _capMaterial);
    90	        _renderers = new[]
    91	        {
    92	            _stem.GetComponent<Renderer>(),
    93	            _cap.GetComponent<Renderer>(),
    94	        };
    95	        _baseColors = new[] { _stemMaterial.color, _capMaterial.color };
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    private Transform CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType type, string childName, Material material)
    99	    {
   100	        GameObject child = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(type);
   101	        child.name = childName;
   102	        child.layer = gameObject.layer;
   103	        child.transform.SetParent(transform, worldPositionStays: false);
   104	        Collider? generated = child.GetComponent<Collider>();
   105	        if (generated != null)
   106	        {
   107	            generated.enabled = false;
   108	            Destroy(generated);
   109	        }
   110	
   111	        child.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = material;
   112	        return child.transform;
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    private static Material CreateMaterial(Shader shader, string name, Color color)
   116	    {
   117	        return new Material(shader)
   118	        {
   119	            name = name,
   120	            color = color,
   121	            enableInstancing = true,
   122	        };
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    private void ApplyHover()
   126	    {
   127	        MaterialPropertyBlock properties = ResolveProperties();
   128	        for (int index = 0; index < _renderers.Length; index++)
   129	        {
   130	            Renderer renderer = _renderers[index];
   131	            if (renderer == null)
   132	            {
   133	                continue;
   134	            }
   135	
   136	            Color highlighted = Color.Lerp(_baseColors[index], Color.white, HoverBlend);
   137	            highlighted.a = _baseColors[index].a;
   138	            properties.Clear();
   139	            renderer.GetPropertyBlock(properties);
   140	            properties.SetColor(BaseColorId, highlighted);
   141	            properties.SetColor(ColorId, highlighted);
   142	            renderer.SetPropertyBlock(properties);
   143	        }
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    private void RestoreColors()
   147	    {
   148	        MaterialPropertyBlock properties = ResolveProperties();
   149	        for (int index = 0; index < _renderers.Length; index++)
   150	        {
   151	            Renderer renderer = _renderers[index];
   152	            if (renderer == null)
   153	            {
   154	                continue;
   155	            }
   156	
   157	            properties.Clear();
   158	            renderer.GetPropertyBlock(properties);
   159	            properties.SetColor(BaseColorId, _baseColors[index]);
   160	            properties.SetColor(ColorId, _baseColors[index]);
   161	            renderer.SetPropertyBlock(properties);
   162	        }
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    private MaterialPropertyBlock ResolveProperties()
   166	    {
   167	        return _properties ??= new MaterialPropertyBlock();
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    private static (float Height, float Width) ResolveSize(MushroomStage stage)
   171	    {
   172	        return stage switch
   173	        {
   174	            MushroomStage.Tiny => (0.18f, 0.16f),
   175	            MushroomStage.Small => (0.34f, 0.28f),
   176	            MushroomStage.Medium => (0.56f, 0.44f),
   177	            MushroomStage.Large => (0.84f, 0.62f),
   178	            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(stage)),
   179	        };
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    private void OnDestroy()
   183	    {
   184	        if (_stemMaterial != null)
   185	        {
   186	            Destroy(_stemMaterial);
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        if (_capMaterial != null)
   190	        {
   191	            Destroy(_capMaterial);
   192	        }
   193	    }
   194	}
   195	}

    41	internal static class DigProductionIconGlyph
    42	{
    43	    internal static string Resolve(string itemId)
    44	    {
    45	        if (itemId.IndexOf("tent", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    46	        {
    47	            return "⌂";
    48	        }
    49	
    50	        if (itemId.IndexOf("stone_mason", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    51	        {
    52	            return "▦";
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        if (itemId.IndexOf("wood_workshop", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    56	        {
    57	            return "▥";
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        if (itemId.IndexOf("campfire", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    61	        {
    62	            return "♨";
    63	        }
    64	
    65	        if (itemId.IndexOf("mushroom_cap", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0
    66	            || itemId.IndexOf("grilled_mushroom", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    67	        {
    68	            return "●";
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        if (itemId.IndexOf("mushroom_leg", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    72	        {
    73	            return "│";
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        if (itemId.IndexOf("stone", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    77	        {
    78	            return "◆";
    79	        }
    80	
    81	        if (itemId.IndexOf("hamster", StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
    82	        {
    83	            return "◉";
    84	        }
    85	
    86	        if (itemId.StartsWith("building_box.", StringComparison.Ordinal))
    87	        {
    88	            return "□";
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        return itemId.StartsWith("food.", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? "○" : "•";
    92	    }
    93	}
