===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigResidentInventory.Consumables.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     3	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Unity
    10	{
    11	    internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    12	    {
    13	        private static readonly ItemCategoryId PotionCategoryId =
    14	            new ItemCategoryId("potion");
    15	        private static readonly ItemCategoryId DrinkCategoryId =
    16	            new ItemCategoryId("drink");
    17	        private static readonly ItemCategoryId BeverageCategoryId =
    18	            new ItemCategoryId("beverage");
    19	
    20	        internal Result UseResidentInventoryActionWithSlotGuard(
    21	            string residentId,
    22	            string stackId,
    23	            long tick)
    24	        {
    25	            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(residentId)
    26	                || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stackId))
    27	            {
    28	                throw new ArgumentException("Resident and stack ids are required.");
    29	            }
    30	
    31	            EntityId resident = EntityId.Parse(residentId);
    32	            EntityId stack = EntityId.Parse(stackId);
    33	            InMemoryInventoryRepository? repository = ResolveWorldItemRepository(stack);
    34	            ItemStackSnapshot? snapshot = repository?.Get().GetStack(stack);
    35	            if (repository == null || snapshot == null)
    36	            {
    37	                return Result.Failure(InventoryErrors.StackNotFound);
    38	            }
    39	
    40	            ItemDefinition definition = repository.Get().Catalog.Get(snapshot.ItemId);
    41	            if (definition.HasCategory(CampfireProductionContent.FoodCategoryId))
    42	            {
    43	                if (_productionAgents == null)
    44	                {
    45	                    return Result.Failure(ResidentFoodMealErrors.ResidentNotFound);
    46	                }
    47	
    48	                return new StartResidentFoodMealHandler(
    49	                    _productionAgents,
    50	                    repository,
    51	                    _worldSession.Journal).Handle(
    52	                        new StartResidentFoodMealCommand(
    53	                            resident,
    54	                            stack,
    55	                            tick));
    56	            }
    57	
    58	            if (IsPotionOrDrink(definition))
    59	            {
    60	                return Result.Failure(
    61	                    ResidentInventoryConsumableErrors.EffectOwnerUnavailable);
    62	            }
    63	
    64	            return UseResidentInventoryItemWithSlotGuard(
    65	                residentId,
    66	                stackId,
    67	                tick);
    68	        }
    69	
    70	        internal Result ValidateWorldConsumableAction(string stackId)
    71	        {
    72	            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stackId))
    73	            {
    74	                throw new ArgumentException("Stack id is required.", nameof(stackId));
    75	            }
    76	
    77	            EntityId stack = EntityId.Parse(stackId);
    78	            InMemoryInventoryRepository? repository = ResolveWorldItemRepository(stack);
    79	            ItemStackSnapshot? snapshot = repository?.Get().GetStack(stack);
    80	            if (repository == null || snapshot == null)
    81	            {
    82	                return Result.Failure(InventoryErrors.StackNotFound);
    83	            }
    84	
    85	            ItemDefinition definition = repository.Get().Catalog.Get(snapshot.ItemId);
    86	            if (definition.HasCategory(CampfireProductionContent.FoodCategoryId))
    87	            {
    88	                return Result.Success();
    89	            }
    90	
    91	            return IsPotionOrDrink(definition)
    92	                ? Result.Failure(
    93	                    ResidentInventoryConsumableErrors.EffectOwnerUnavailable)
    94	                : Result.Failure(
    95	                    ResidentInventoryConsumableErrors.EffectOwnerUnavailable);
    96	        }
    97	
    98	        private static bool IsPotionOrDrink(ItemDefinition definition)
    99	        {
   100	            return definition.HasCategory(PotionCategoryId)
   101	                || definition.HasCategory(DrinkCategoryId)
   102	                || definition.HasCategory(BeverageCategoryId);
   103	        }
   104	    }
   105	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkNavigation.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Application.Navigation;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    11	using Dig.Presentation.Navigation;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Unity
    14	{
    15	    internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    16	    {
    17	        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CellId> PlanMovement(
    18	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
    19	            long tick)
    20	        {
    21	            if (agents == null)
    22	            {
    23	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(agents));
    24	            }
    25	
    26	            if (tick < 0)
    27	            {
    28	                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(tick));
    29	            }
    30	
    31	            Result refresh = RefreshNavigation();
    32	            if (refresh.IsFailure)
    33	            {
    34	                throw new InvalidOperationException(refresh.Error!.ToString());
    35	            }
    36	
    37	            NavigationMap map = _navigationRepository.Get(_profile.Id)
    38	                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Navigation map not available.");
    39	            Result<NavigationSnapshot> snapshotResult = map.GetSnapshot();
    40	            if (snapshotResult.IsFailure)
    41	            {
    42	                throw new InvalidOperationException(snapshotResult.Error!.ToString());
    43	            }
    44	
    45	            NavigationSnapshot navigation = snapshotResult.Value;
    46	            WorldSnapshot world = _worldSession.LoadSnapshot();
    47	            Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
    48	                agent => agent.Id,
    49	                StringComparer.Ordinal);
    50	            Dictionary<string, CellId> movement =
    51	                new Dictionary<string, CellId>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    52	            _routePlans.Clear();
    53	            _haulingRoutes.Clear();
    54	            _buildingPackingRoutes.Clear();
    55	            _buildingBoxPickupRoutes.Clear();
    56	            _worldItemPickupRoutes.Clear();
    57	            _residentInventoryPlacementRoutes.Clear();
    58	            _buildingBoxAssemblyRoutes.Clear();
    59	            _buildingProductionRoutes.Clear();
    60	            _buildingSupplyRoutes.Clear();
    61	            JobSnapshot[] activeJobs = _jobRepository.Get().GetAll()
    62	                .Where(job => IsActive(job) && job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
    63	                .ToArray();
    64	            HashSet<string> assignedAgentIds = new HashSet<string>(
    65	                activeJobs.Select(job => job.AssignedAgentId!.Value.ToString()),
    66	                StringComparer.Ordinal);
    67	            foreach (JobSnapshot job in activeJobs)
    68	            {
    69	                if (!IsActive(job) || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
    70	                {
    71	                    continue;
    72	                }
    73	
    74	                if (job.Definition is SpatialDigJobDefinition)
    75	                {
    76	                    continue;
    77	                }
    78	
    79	                string agentId = job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString();
    80	                if (!agentsById.TryGetValue(agentId, out AgentViewModel? agent))
    81	                {
    82	                    continue;
    83	                }
    84	
    85	                if (TryPlanResidentInventoryPlacementMovement(
    86	                    job,
    87	                    agent,
    88	                    navigation,
    89	                    movement))
    90	                {
    91	                    continue;
    92	                }
    93	
    94	                if (TryPlanBarrelMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
    95	                {
    96	                    continue;
    97	                }
    98	
    99	                if (TryPlanMushroomMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   100	                {
   101	                    continue;
   102	                }
   103	
   104	                if (TryPlanBuildingBoxPickupMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   105	                {
   106	                    continue;
   107	                }
   108	
   109	                if (TryPlanWorldItemPickupMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   110	                {
   111	                    continue;
   112	                }
   113	
   114	                if (TryPlanBuildingBoxAssemblyMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   115	                {
   116	                    continue;
   117	                }
   118	
   119	                if (TryPlanBuildingSupplyMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   120	                {
   121	                    continue;
   122	                }
   123	
   124	                if (TryPlanBuildingProductionMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   125	                {
   126	                    continue;
   127	                }
   128	
   129	                if (TryPlanHaulingMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   130	                {
   131	                    continue;
   132	                }
   133	
   134	                if (TryPlanBuildingPackingMovement(job, agent, navigation, movement))
   135	                {
   136	                    continue;
   137	                }
   138	
   139	                Result<TerrainWorkRoutePlan> planned = _routePlanner.Plan(
   140	                    job,
   141	                    new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ),
   142	                    navigation,
   143	                    world);
   144	                if (planned.IsFailure)
   145	                {
   146	                    ReleaseUnroutableExcavationAssignment(job, tick);
   147	                    continue;
   148	                }
   149	
   150	                TerrainWorkRoutePlan route = planned.Value;
   151	                if (!route.Succeeded || !route.WorkCell.HasValue)
   152	                {
   153	                    ReleaseUnroutableExcavationAssignment(job, tick);
   154	                    continue;
   155	                }
   156	
   157	                _routePlans[job.Id] = route;
   158	
   159	                NavigationPath path = route.PathResult.Path!;
   160	                movement[agentId] = path.Cells.Count > 1
   161	                    ? path.Cells[1]
   162	                    : route.WorkCell.Value;
   163	            }
   164	
   165	            foreach (AgentViewModel agent in agents.OrderBy(value => value.Id, StringComparer.Ordinal))
   166	            {
   167	                if (assignedAgentIds.Contains(agent.Id) || movement.ContainsKey(agent.Id))
   168	                {
   169	                    continue;
   170	                }
   171	
   172	                CellId start = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
   173	                UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan? recovery = _supportRecoveryPlanner.Plan(
   174	                    start,
   175	                    navigation,
   176	                    world);
   177	                if (recovery == null)
   178	                {
   179	                    continue;
   180	                }
   181	
   182	                movement[agent.Id] = recovery.Path.Cells.Count > 1
   183	                    ? recovery.Path.Cells[1]
   184	                    : recovery.Destination;
   185	            }
   186	
   187	            return movement;
   188	        }
   189	
   190	
   191	        private void ReleaseUnroutableExcavationAssignment(
   192	            JobSnapshot job,
   193	            long tick)
   194	        {
   195	            if (job.Definition is not DigJobDefinition
   196	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   197	                || _releaseAssignment == null)
   198	            {
   199	                return;
   200	            }
   201	
   202	            Result released = _releaseAssignment.Handle(
   203	                new ReleaseJobAssignmentCommand(job.Id, tick));
   204	            if (released.IsFailure)
   205	            {
   206	                return;
   207	            }
   208	
   209	            _excavationQuarterWork.Cancel(job.AssignedAgentId.Value);
   210	            _routePlans.Remove(job.Id);
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        public IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> LoadRoutes()
   214	        {
   215	            List<RouteViewModel> routes = new List<RouteViewModel>();
   216	            foreach (KeyValuePair<EntityId, TerrainWorkRoutePlan> pair in _routePlans
   217	                .OrderBy(item => item.Key.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   218	            {
   219	                JobSnapshot? job = _jobRepository.Get().Get(pair.Key);
   220	                if (job is null || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   221	                {
   222	                    continue;
   223	                }
   224	
   225	                routes.Add(_routePresenter.Present(
   226	                    pair.Value,
   227	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value));
   228	            }
   229	
   230	            routes.AddRange(LoadHaulingRoutes());
   231	            routes.AddRange(LoadBuildingPackingRoutes());
   232	            routes.AddRange(LoadBuildingBoxPickupRoutes());
   233	            routes.AddRange(LoadWorldItemPickupRoutes());
   234	            routes.AddRange(LoadBuildingBoxAssemblyRoutes());
   235	            routes.AddRange(LoadBuildingProductionRoutes());
   236	            return routes;
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        internal Result RefreshCommittedTerrainNavigation()
   240	        {
   241	            return RefreshNavigation();
   242	        }
   243	
   244	        private Result RefreshNavigation()
   245	        {
   246	            IReadOnlyList<ChunkId> dirty = _worldSession.PeekDirtyChunks();
   247	            if (dirty.Count == 0)
   248	            {
   249	                return Result.Success();
   250	            }
   251	
   252	            Result<NavigationUpdateDiagnostics> refreshed =
   253	                new RefreshNavigationCommandHandler(_navigationRepository).Handle(
   254	                    new RefreshNavigationCommand(
   255	                        _profile.Id,
   256	                        _worldSession.LoadSnapshot(),
   257	                        dirty,
   258	                        Array.Empty<TraversalLink>()));
   259	            if (refreshed.IsFailure)
   260	            {
   261	                return Result.Failure(refreshed.Error!);
   262	            }
   263	
   264	            _worldSession.DrainDirtyChunks();
   265	            return Result.Success();
   266	        }
   267	    }
   268	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.BarrelNavigation.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
     9	using Dig.Presentation.World;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Unity
    12	{
    13	
    14	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    15	{
    16	    private bool TryPlanBarrelMovement(
    17	        JobSnapshot job,
    18	        AgentViewModel agent,
    19	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
    20	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
    21	    {
    22	        if (job.Definition is not BarrelAttackJobDefinition definition)
    23	        {
    24	            return false;
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        CellId start = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
    28	        PathResult path = new NavigationPathfinder().FindPath(
    29	            navigation,
    30	            new PathRequest(start, definition.WorkPosition, navigation.NavigationVersion));
    31	        if (!path.Succeeded
    32	            || path.Path == null
    33	            || !IsSupportedBarrelAttackPath(navigation, path.Path))
    34	        {
    35	            return true;
    36	        }
    37	
    38	        _routePlans[job.Id] = new TerrainWorkRoutePlan(
    39	            job.Id,
    40	            definition.TargetCell,
    41	            definition.WorkPosition,
    42	            path,
    43	            candidateCount: 1);
    44	        movement[agent.Id] = path.Path.Cells.Count > 1
    45	            ? path.Path.Cells[1]
    46	            : definition.WorkPosition;
    47	        return true;
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    private bool TryResolveBarrelWorkPosition(
    51	        CellId target,
    52	        CellId workerCell,
    53	        out CellId workPosition)
    54	    {
    55	        workPosition = default;
    56	        if (RefreshNavigation().IsFailure)
    57	        {
    58	            return false;
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        NavigationMap? map = _navigationRepository.Get(_profile.Id);
    62	        if (map == null)
    63	        {
    64	            return false;
    65	        }
    66	
    67	        Dig.Domain.Core.Result<NavigationSnapshot> snapshotResult = map.GetSnapshot();
    68	        if (snapshotResult.IsFailure)
    69	        {
    70	            return false;
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        NavigationSnapshot navigation = snapshotResult.Value;
    74	        HashSet<CellId> reserved = _jobRepository.Get().GetReservations()
    75	            .Where(value => value.Key.Kind == ReservationKind.Position)
    76	            .Select(value => ParsePositionReservation(value.Key.Value))
    77	            .Where(value => value.HasValue)
    78	            .Select(value => value!.Value)
    79	            .ToHashSet();
    80	        CellId[] candidates =
    81	        {
    82	            new CellId(target.X - 1, target.Y, target.Z),
    83	            new CellId(target.X + 1, target.Y, target.Z),
    84	            target.Z > CellId.MinimumDepth
    85	                ? new CellId(target.X, target.Y, target.Z - 1)
    86	                : target,
    87	            target.Z < CellId.MaximumDepth
    88	                ? new CellId(target.X, target.Y, target.Z + 1)
    89	                : target,
    90	        };
    91	        PathResult? selectedPath = null;
    92	        CellId selectedCell = default;
    93	        foreach (CellId candidate in candidates
    94	            .Where(navigation.IsWalkable)
    95	            .Where(HasFullStandingSupport)
    96	            .Where(value => !reserved.Contains(value))
    97	            .Distinct()
    98	            .OrderBy(value => value))
    99	        {
   100	            PathResult path = new NavigationPathfinder().FindPath(
   101	                navigation,
   102	                new PathRequest(workerCell, candidate, navigation.NavigationVersion));
   103	            if (!path.Succeeded
   104	                || path.Path == null
   105	                || !IsSupportedBarrelAttackPath(navigation, path.Path))
   106	            {
   107	                continue;
   108	            }
   109	
   110	            if (selectedPath == null
   111	                || path.Path.TotalCost < selectedPath.Path!.TotalCost)
   112	            {
   113	                selectedPath = path;
   114	                selectedCell = candidate;
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        if (selectedPath == null)
   119	        {
   120	            return false;
   121	        }
   122	
   123	        workPosition = selectedCell;
   124	        return true;
   125	    }
   126	
   127	
   128	    private bool IsSupportedBarrelAttackPath(
   129	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
   130	        NavigationPath path)
   131	    {
   132	        if (path.Cells.Any(cell => !HasFullStandingSupport(cell)))
   133	        {
   134	            return false;
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        for (int index = 0; index + 1 < path.Cells.Count; index++)
   138	        {
   139	            CellId from = path.Cells[index];
   140	            CellId to = path.Cells[index + 1];
   141	            bool supportedSurfaceTransition = navigation.GetTransitions(from).Any(
   142	                transition => transition.Target == to
   143	                    && (transition.TraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.SupportedWalk
   144	                        || transition.TraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.DepthTraverse));
   145	            if (!supportedSurfaceTransition)
   146	            {
   147	                return false;
   148	            }
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        return true;
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    private bool HasFullStandingSupport(CellId cell)
   155	    {
   156	        CellId support = new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y + 1, cell.Z);
   157	        return _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
   158	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
   159	            .Any(value => value.X == support.X
   160	                && value.Y == support.Y
   161	                && value.Z == support.Z
   162	                && value.HasFullActorSupport);
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    private CellId[] FindBarrelDemoCells(
   166	        bool surface,
   167	        int count,
   168	        HashSet<CellId>? excluded)
   169	    {
   170	        WorldCellViewModel[] cells = _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
   171	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
   172	            .ToArray();
   173	        Dictionary<CellId, WorldCellViewModel> byId = cells.ToDictionary(
   174	            value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z));
   175	        HashSet<CellId> occupied = excluded ?? new HashSet<CellId>();
   176	        occupied.UnionWith(MushroomBuildingBlockedCells);
   177	        if (_buildingsRepository != null)
   178	        {
   179	            occupied.UnionWith(_buildingsRepository.Get().GetOccupiedCells());
   180	        }
   181	
   182	        foreach (Dig.Domain.Inventory.ItemStackSnapshot stack
   183	            in _inventoryRepository.Get().CreateSnapshot().Stacks)
   184	        {
   185	            if (stack.Location.Kind == Dig.Domain.Inventory.ItemLocationKind.World
   186	                && stack.Location.HasCell)
   187	            {
   188	                occupied.Add(stack.Location.CellId);
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	
   192	        IEnumerable<CellId> candidates = cells
   193	            .Where(value => !value.IsSolid)
   194	            .Select(value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z))
   195	            .Where(value => !occupied.Contains(value))
   196	            .Where(value => byId.TryGetValue(
   197	                new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z),
   198	                out WorldCellViewModel below)
   199	                && below.IsSolid);
   200	        candidates = surface
   201	            ? candidates.Where(value => value.Z == 0)
   202	                .OrderBy(value => value.Y)
   203	                .ThenBy(value => value.X)
   204	            : candidates.Where(value => value.Z > 0)
   205	                .OrderByDescending(value => value.Z)
   206	                .ThenByDescending(value => value.Y)
   207	                .ThenByDescending(value => value.X);
   208	        CellId[] selected = candidates.Take(count).ToArray();
   209	        if (selected.Length != count)
   210	        {
   211	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   212	                surface
   213	                    ? "The demo has insufficient supported surface cells for barrels."
   214	                    : "The demo has insufficient lower-cave cells for barrels.");
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        return selected;
   218	    }
   219	
   220	    private bool HasSolidSupport(CellId cell) => HasFullStandingSupport(cell);
   221	
   222	    private bool TryResolveBarrelLanding(CellId source, out CellId landing)
   223	    {
   224	        landing = default;
   225	        HashSet<CellId> occupied = LoadBarrels()
   226	            .Where(value => value.Lifecycle
   227	                == Dig.Domain.WorldObjects.BarrelLifecycle.Supported)
   228	            .Select(value => value.Cell)
   229	            .ToHashSet();
   230	        for (int y = source.Y + 1; ; y++)
   231	        {
   232	            CellId candidate = new CellId(source.X, y, source.Z);
   233	            CellId below = new CellId(source.X, y + 1, source.Z);
   234	            if (!TryGetWorldCell(candidate, out bool candidateSolid)
   235	                || !TryGetWorldCell(below, out bool belowSolid))
   236	            {
   237	                return false;
   238	            }
   239	
   240	            if (!candidateSolid && belowSolid && !occupied.Contains(candidate))
   241	            {
   242	                landing = candidate;
   243	                return true;
   244	            }
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	
   248	    private bool TryGetWorldCell(CellId cell, out bool isSolid)
   249	    {
   250	        foreach (WorldCellViewModel model in _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
   251	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells))
   252	        {
   253	            if (model.X == cell.X && model.Y == cell.Y && model.Z == cell.Z)
   254	            {
   255	                isSolid = model.IsSolid;
   256	                return true;
   257	            }
   258	        }
   259	
   260	        isSolid = false;
   261	        return false;
   262	    }
   263	
   264	    private static CellId? ParsePositionReservation(string value)
   265	    {
   266	        string[] parts = value.Split(',');
   267	        if (parts.Length != 3
   268	            || !int.TryParse(parts[0], out int x)
   269	            || !int.TryParse(parts[1], out int y)
   270	            || !int.TryParse(parts[2], out int z))
   271	        {
   272	            return null;
   273	        }
   274	
   275	        return new CellId(x, y, z);
   276	    }
   277	}
   278	
   279	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.Barrels.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.WorldObjects;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Runtime;
     9	using Dig.Domain.World;
    10	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
    11	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    12	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Unity
    15	{
    16	
    17	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    18	{
    19	    private const int BarrelDirectPriority = 900;
    20	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DemoBarrelDefinitionId =
    21	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    22	    private static readonly ItemId BarrelStoneItemId = new ItemId("material.stone");
    23	    private static readonly ItemId BarrelOreItemId = new ItemId("ore.iron");
    24	
    25	    private InMemoryBarrelRepository? _barrelRepository;
    26	    private StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler? _startBarrelAttack;
    27	    private ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler? _arriveAtBarrel;
    28	    private CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler? _completeBarrelHit;
    29	    private CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler? _completeBarrelDestruction;
    30	    private CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler? _cancelBarrelAttack;
    31	    private SettleBarrelAfterSupportLossCommandHandler? _settleBarrel;
    32	    private long _nextBarrelJobSequence;
    33	    private long _nextBarrelOutputSequence;
    34	
    35	    internal void InitializeBarrelDemo(long tick)
    36	    {
    37	        if (_barrelRepository != null)
    38	        {
    39	            return;
    40	        }
    41	
    42	        BarrelDefinition definition = new BarrelDefinition(
    43	            DemoBarrelDefinitionId,
    44	            new[] { BarrelStoneItemId, BarrelOreItemId });
    45	        BarrelState state = new BarrelState(new BarrelCatalog(new[] { definition }));
    46	        CellId[] surface = FindBarrelDemoCells(surface: true, count: 2, excluded: null);
    47	        CellId[] cave = FindBarrelDemoCells(
    48	            surface: false,
    49	            count: 2,
    50	            excluded: new HashSet<CellId>(surface));
    51	        CellId[] cells = surface.Concat(cave).ToArray();
    52	        for (int index = 0; index < cells.Length; index++)
    53	        {
    54	            EntityId barrelId = DemoId('b', index + 1);
    55	            Require(state.Add(
    56	                barrelId,
    57	                definition.Id,
    58	                cells[index],
    59	                SelectBarrelContents(barrelId),
    60	                tick));
    61	        }
    62	
    63	        _barrelRepository = new InMemoryBarrelRepository(state);
    64	        _startBarrelAttack = new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler(
    65	            _barrelRepository,
    66	            _jobRepository,
    67	            _journal);
    68	        _arriveAtBarrel = new ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler(_jobRepository, _journal);
    69	        _completeBarrelHit = new CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler(
    70	            _jobRepository,
    71	            _journal);
    72	        _completeBarrelDestruction = new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler(
    73	            _barrelRepository,
    74	            _jobRepository,
    75	            _inventoryRepository,
    76	            _journal);
    77	        _cancelBarrelAttack = new CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler(
    78	            _jobRepository,
    79	            _journal);
    80	        _settleBarrel = new SettleBarrelAfterSupportLossCommandHandler(
    81	            _barrelRepository,
    82	            _journal);
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    internal IReadOnlyList<BarrelSnapshot> LoadBarrels() =>
    86	        _barrelRepository?.Get().GetAll() ?? Array.Empty<BarrelSnapshot>();
    87	
    88	    internal IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> BarrelBuildingBlockedCells =>
    89	        _barrelRepository?.Get().GetBuildingBlockedCells() ?? Array.Empty<CellId>();
    90	
    91	    internal IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> BuildingPlacementBlockedCells =>
    92	        MushroomBuildingBlockedCells
    93	            .Concat(BarrelBuildingBlockedCells)
    94	            .Distinct()
    95	            .OrderBy(value => value)
    96	            .ToArray();
    97	
    98	    internal bool CanDirectAttackBarrel(
    99	        EntityId barrelId,
   100	        CellId workerCell,
   101	        out CellId workPosition)
   102	    {
   103	        workPosition = default;
   104	        BarrelSnapshot? barrel = _barrelRepository?.Get().Get(barrelId);
   105	        return barrel != null
   106	            && barrel.IsAttackable
   107	            && TryResolveBarrelWorkPosition(barrel.Cell, workerCell, out workPosition);
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    internal Result StartDirectBarrelAttack(
   111	        EntityId barrelId,
   112	        EntityId workerId,
   113	        CellId workerCell,
   114	        long tick)
   115	    {
   116	        EnsureBarrelsInitialized();
   117	        if (!CanDirectAttackBarrel(barrelId, workerCell, out CellId workPosition))
   118	        {
   119	            return Result.Failure(new DomainError(
   120	                "barrel.direct_target_unavailable",
   121	                "The barrel is unavailable or has no reachable attack position."));
   122	        }
   123	
   124	        Result prepared = PrepareResidentsForDirectCommand(
   125	            new[] { workerId.ToString() },
   126	            tick);
   127	        if (prepared.IsFailure)
   128	        {
   129	            return prepared;
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        EntityId jobId = DemoId('c', checked(++_nextBarrelJobSequence));
   133	        Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult> started = _startBarrelAttack!.Handle(
   134	            new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
   135	                jobId,
   136	                barrelId,
   137	                workerId,
   138	                workPosition,
   139	                BarrelDirectPriority,
   140	                tick));
   141	        return started.IsSuccess ? Result.Success() : Result.Failure(started.Error!);
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    internal Result AdvanceBarrels(long tick, IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   145	    {
   146	        if (_barrelRepository == null)
   147	        {
   148	            return Result.Success();
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
   152	            value => value.Id,
   153	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
   154	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
   155	        {
   156	            if (job.Definition is not BarrelAttackJobDefinition definition
   157	                || !IsActive(job)
   158	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   159	                || !agentsById.TryGetValue(
   160	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   161	                    out AgentViewModel? worker))
   162	            {
   163	                continue;
   164	            }
   165	
   166	            if (worker.CellX != definition.WorkPosition.X
   167	                || worker.CellY != definition.WorkPosition.Y
   168	                || worker.CellZ != definition.WorkPosition.Z)
   169	            {
   170	                continue;
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            Result result = AdvanceBarrelAtWorkPosition(job, tick);
   174	            if (result.IsFailure)
   175	            {
   176	                return result;
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	
   180	        return Result.Success();
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    internal Result SettleUnsupportedBarrels(long tick)
   184	    {
   185	        if (_barrelRepository == null)
   186	        {
   187	            return Result.Success();
   188	        }
   189	
   190	        foreach (BarrelSnapshot barrel in LoadBarrels())
   191	        {
   192	            if (barrel.Lifecycle != BarrelLifecycle.Supported
   193	                || HasSolidSupport(barrel.Cell)
   194	                || !TryResolveBarrelLanding(barrel.Cell, out CellId landing))
   195	            {
   196	                continue;
   197	            }
   198	
   199	            Result settled = _settleBarrel!.Handle(
   200	                new SettleBarrelAfterSupportLossCommand(barrel.BarrelId, landing, tick));
   201	            if (settled.IsFailure)
   202	            {
   203	                return settled;
   204	            }
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return Result.Success();
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    private Result AdvanceBarrelAtWorkPosition(JobSnapshot job, long tick)
   211	    {
   212	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
   213	        {
   214	            return _arriveAtBarrel!.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(job.Id, tick));
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.PerformWork)
   218	        {
   219	            if (tick % 2 != 0)
   220	            {
   221	                return Result.Success();
   222	            }
   223	
   224	            Result hit = _completeBarrelHit!.Handle(
   225	                new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(job.Id, tick));
   226	            if (hit.IsFailure)
   227	            {
   228	                return hit;
   229	            }
   230	        }
   231	
   232	        JobSnapshot? updated = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id);
   233	        if (updated?.Stage != JobStageKind.Finalize)
   234	        {
   235	            return Result.Success();
   236	        }
   237	
   238	        EntityId outputId = DemoId('d', checked(++_nextBarrelOutputSequence));
   239	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> completion = _completeBarrelDestruction!.Handle(
   240	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(job.Id, outputId, tick));
   241	        _routePlans.Remove(job.Id);
   242	        if (completion.IsSuccess)
   243	        {
   244	            return Result.Success();
   245	        }
   246	
   247	        if (completion.Error == BarrelApplicationErrors.GenerationConflict
   248	            || completion.Error == BarrelErrors.NotAttackable
   249	            || completion.Error == BarrelErrors.VersionConflict)
   250	        {
   251	            Result cancelled = _cancelBarrelAttack!.Handle(new CancelBarrelAttackCommand(
   252	                job.Id,
   253	                "barrel_destroyed_by_concurrent_attack",
   254	                tick));
   255	            return cancelled.IsSuccess ? Result.Success() : cancelled;
   256	        }
   257	
   258	        return Result.Failure(completion.Error!);
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    private ItemId SelectBarrelContents(EntityId barrelId)
   262	    {
   263	        RandomStreamCatalog streams = new RandomStreamCatalog(
   264	            unchecked((ulong)(uint)_worldSession.MiningOutputWorldSeed));
   265	        DeterministicRandomStream stream = streams.GetOrCreate(
   266	            $"barrel.contents.{barrelId}");
   267	        return stream.NextInt(2) == 0 ? BarrelStoneItemId : BarrelOreItemId;
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    private void EnsureBarrelsInitialized()
   271	    {
   272	        if (_barrelRepository == null
   273	            || _startBarrelAttack == null
   274	            || _completeBarrelDestruction == null)
   275	        {
   276	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Barrel demo is not initialized.");
   277	        }
   278	    }
   279	}
   280	
   281	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.BuildingWorkPositions.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	using Dig.Presentation.World;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Unity
     9	{
    10	
    11	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    12	{
    13	    private DemoBuildingPlacement FindDemoBuildingPlacement(
    14	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    15	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> excludedCells)
    16	    {
    17	        WorldCellViewModel[] openCells = _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
    18	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    19	            .Where(value => !value.IsSolid)
    20	            .ToArray();
    21	        HashSet<CellId> open = new HashSet<CellId>(openCells.Select(
    22	            value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z)));
    23	        HashSet<CellId> solid = new HashSet<CellId>(_worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
    24	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    25	            .Where(value => value.IsSolid)
    26	            .Select(value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z)));
    27	        HashSet<CellId> excluded = new HashSet<CellId>(excludedCells);
    28	        foreach (WorldCellViewModel candidate in openCells
    29	            .Where(value => solid.Contains(new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z)))
    30	            .OrderByDescending(value => value.X)
    31	            .ThenByDescending(value => value.Y)
    32	            .ThenBy(value => value.Z))
    33	        {
    34	            CellId origin = new CellId(candidate.X, candidate.Y, candidate.Z);
    35	            if (excluded.Contains(origin))
    36	            {
    37	                continue;
    38	            }
    39	
    40	            CellId? workPosition = definition.ResolveWorkPositions(
    41	                    origin,
    42	                    BuildingOrientation.North)
    43	                .Where(value => value.Y == origin.Y && value.Z == origin.Z)
    44	                .Where(value => !excluded.Contains(value)
    45	                    && open.Contains(value)
    46	                    && solid.Contains(new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z)))
    47	                .OrderBy(value => Math.Abs(value.X - origin.X))
    48	                .ThenBy(value => value)
    49	                .Cast<CellId?>()
    50	                .FirstOrDefault();
    51	            if (workPosition.HasValue)
    52	            {
    53	                return new DemoBuildingPlacement(origin, workPosition.Value);
    54	            }
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        throw new InvalidOperationException(
    58	            "The demo world has no supported same-plane building and side-work pair.");
    59	    }
    60	
    61	
    62	}
    63	
    64	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.Buildings.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
    11	using Dig.Domain.World;
    12	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    13	using Dig.Presentation.Buildings;
    14	using Dig.Presentation.World;
    15	
    16	namespace Dig.Unity
    17	{
    18	
    19	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    20	{
    21	    private static readonly ItemId DemoBuildingBoxItemId =
    22	        new ItemId("demo.building_box.workshop");
    23	    private static readonly ItemId DemoResidentToolItemId =
    24	        new ItemId("demo.tool.pickaxe");
    25	    private static readonly ItemId DemoResidentHammerItemId =
    26	        new ItemId("demo.tool.hammer");
    27	    private static readonly ItemId DemoBasketItemId =
    28	        ResidentInventoryExpansionContent.BasketItemId;
    29	    private static readonly ItemId DemoLargeBasketItemId =
    30	        ResidentInventoryExpansionContent.LargeBasketItemId;
    31	    private static readonly ItemId DemoScabbardItemId =
    32	        ResidentInventoryExpansionContent.SheathItemId;
    33	    private static readonly ItemId DemoHarnessItemId =
    34	        ResidentInventoryExpansionContent.WeaponHarnessItemId;
    35	    private static readonly DomainError BuildingsNotInitialized = new DomainError(
    36	        "unity.buildings.not_initialized",
    37	        "The demo building runtime is not initialized.");
    38	
    39	    private InMemoryBuildingsRepository? _buildingsRepository;
    40	    private InMemoryInventoryRepository? _buildingInventoryRepository;
    41	    private BuildingWorldPresenter? _buildingPresenter;
    42	    private BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter? _buildingCommands;
    43	    private long _nextPackingSequence;
    44	
    45	    public void InitializeBuildingDemo(InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
    46	    {
    47	        if (journal == null)
    48	        {
    49	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(journal));
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        if (_buildingsRepository != null)
    53	        {
    54	            return;
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        BuildingDefinition workshopDefinition = CreateDemoBuildingDefinition();
    58	        BuildingDefinition campfireDefinition =
    59	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.Definition.Building;
    60	        BuildingCatalog catalog = new BuildingCatalog(
    61	            new[] { workshopDefinition }
    62	                .Concat(CampfireProductionContent.CreateBuildings())
    63	                .GroupBy(value => value.Id)
    64	                .Select(group => group.First()));
    65	
    66	        DemoBuildingPlacement workshopPlacement = FindDemoBuildingPlacement(
    67	            workshopDefinition,
    68	            Array.Empty<CellId>());
    69	        BuildingSnapshot workshop = CreateCompletedDemoBuilding(
    70	            catalog.Get(workshopDefinition.Id),
    71	            DemoId('b', 1),
    72	            DemoId('c', 1),
    73	            DemoId('d', 1),
    74	            workshopPlacement.Origin,
    75	            workshopPlacement.WorkPosition,
    76	            journal);
    77	
    78	        DemoBuildingPlacement campfirePlacement = FindSurfaceCampfirePlacement(
    79	            campfireDefinition,
    80	            workshop.Footprint);
    81	        BuildingSnapshot campfire = CreateCompletedDemoBuilding(
    82	            catalog.Get(campfireDefinition.Id),
    83	            DemoId('b', 2),
    84	            DemoId('c', 2),
    85	            DemoId('d', 2),
    86	            campfirePlacement.Origin,
    87	            campfirePlacement.WorkPosition,
    88	            journal);
    89	
    90	        BuildingsState buildings = BuildingsState.RestoreWithPacking(
    91	            new[] { workshop, campfire }).Value;
    92	        _buildingsRepository = new InMemoryBuildingsRepository(buildings);
    93	        _buildingInventoryRepository = _inventoryRepository;
    94	
    95	        BuildingFunctionsPresenter functions = new BuildingFunctionsPresenter();
    96	        _buildingPresenter = new BuildingWorldPresenter(functions);
    97	        _buildingCommands = new BuildingFunctionsCommandAdapter(
    98	            functions,
    99	            new StartBuildingBoxPackingHandler(
   100	                _buildingsRepository,
   101	                _buildingInventoryRepository,
   102	                _jobRepository,
   103	                journal));
   104	        InitializeBuildingPackingExecution(journal);
   105	        InitializeBuildingBoxWorldInput(catalog, workshopDefinition, journal);
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingWorldViewModel> LoadBuildings()
   109	    {
   110	        if (_buildingsRepository == null || _buildingPresenter == null)
   111	        {
   112	            return Array.Empty<BuildingWorldViewModel>();
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        return _buildingPresenter.Load(_buildingsRepository.Get().GetAll());
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    public Result StartBuildingPacking(string buildingId, long tick)
   119	    {
   120	        if (_buildingsRepository == null || _buildingCommands == null)
   121	        {
   122	            return Result.Failure(BuildingsNotInitialized);
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(buildingId))
   126	        {
   127	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id is required.", nameof(buildingId));
   128	        }
   129	
   130	        EntityId id = EntityId.Parse(buildingId);
   131	        BuildingSnapshot? snapshot = _buildingsRepository.Get().Get(id);
   132	        if (snapshot == null)
   133	        {
   134	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        long sequence = checked(_nextPackingSequence + 1);
   138	        _nextPackingSequence = sequence;
   139	        return _buildingCommands.StartPacking(
   140	            snapshot,
   141	            DemoId('e', sequence),
   142	            DemoId('f', sequence),
   143	            priority: 650,
   144	            tick: tick);
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    private BuildingSnapshot CreateCompletedDemoBuilding(
   148	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   149	        EntityId buildingId,
   150	        EntityId sourceStackId,
   151	        EntityId assemblyJobId,
   152	        CellId origin,
   153	        CellId workPosition,
   154	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
   155	    {
   156	        CreateCompletedAssemblyJob(
   157	            buildingId,
   158	            sourceStackId,
   159	            assemblyJobId,
   160	            origin,
   161	            workPosition,
   162	            journal);
   163	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   164	            buildingId,
   165	            definition,
   166	            origin,
   167	            BuildingOrientation.North,
   168	            definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, BuildingOrientation.North),
   169	            workPosition,
   170	            BuildingStatus.Completed,
   171	            definition.RequiredWork,
   172	            definition.MaximumDurability,
   173	            version: 1,
   174	            diagnosticReason: null,
   175	            boxPlan: new BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot(
   176	                sourceStackId,
   177	                assemblyJobId,
   178	                BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed));
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    private void CreateCompletedAssemblyJob(
   182	        EntityId buildingId,
   183	        EntityId sourceStackId,
   184	        EntityId assemblyJobId,
   185	        CellId origin,
   186	        CellId workPosition,
   187	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
   188	    {
   189	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
   190	        BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition definition =
   191	            new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition(
   192	                assemblyJobId,
   193	                buildingId,
   194	                sourceStackId,
   195	                origin,
   196	                workPosition,
   197	                priority: 600,
   198	                createdTick: 0,
   199	                retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   200	        Require(jobs.Add(definition));
   201	        Require(jobs.MakeAvailable(assemblyJobId, tick: 0));
   202	        Require(jobs.Claim(assemblyJobId, DemoId('a', 1), tick: 0));
   203	        Require(jobs.Start(assemblyJobId, tick: 0));
   204	        Require(jobs.Complete(assemblyJobId, tick: 0));
   205	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   206	        journal.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    private DemoBuildingPlacement FindSurfaceCampfirePlacement(
   210	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   211	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> excludedCells)
   212	    {
   213	        TunnelDemoLayout layout = _worldSession.CreateTunnelNavigationVolume().DemoLayout
   214	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The demo tunnel layout is required.");
   215	        Dictionary<CellId, WorldCellViewModel> cells = _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
   216	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
   217	            .ToDictionary(value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z));
   218	        HashSet<CellId> occupied = new HashSet<CellId>(excludedCells);
   219	        foreach (ItemStackSnapshot stack in _inventoryRepository.Get().CreateSnapshot().Stacks)
   220	        {
   221	            if (stack.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.World && stack.Location.HasCell)
   222	            {
   223	                occupied.Add(stack.Location.CellId);
   224	            }
   225	        }
   226	
   227	        CellId origin = new CellId(
   228	            layout.ShaftX - 2,
   229	            layout.SurfaceY,
   230	            layout.ShaftZ);
   231	        CellId[] footprint = definition.ResolveFootprint(
   232	                origin,
   233	                BuildingOrientation.North)
   234	            .ToArray();
   235	        bool validFootprint = footprint.All(value =>
   236	        {
   237	            CellId support = new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z);
   238	            return !occupied.Contains(value)
   239	                && cells.TryGetValue(value, out WorldCellViewModel footprintCell)
   240	                && !footprintCell.IsSolid
   241	                && cells.TryGetValue(support, out WorldCellViewModel supportCell)
   242	                && supportCell.IsSolid;
   243	        });
   244	        if (!validFootprint)
   245	        {
   246	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   247	                "The surface campfire anchor two cells left of the shaft is invalid.");
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        CellId? workPosition = definition.ResolveWorkPositions(
   251	                origin,
   252	                BuildingOrientation.North)
   253	            .Where(value => !occupied.Contains(value)
   254	                && !footprint.Contains(value)
   255	                && value.Y == origin.Y
   256	                && value.Z == origin.Z
   257	                && cells.TryGetValue(value, out WorldCellViewModel workCell)
   258	                && !workCell.IsSolid
   259	                && cells.TryGetValue(
   260	                    new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z),
   261	                    out WorldCellViewModel supportCell)
   262	                && supportCell.IsSolid)
   263	            .OrderBy(value => Math.Abs(value.X - origin.X))
   264	            .ThenBy(value => value)
   265	            .Select(value => (CellId?)value)
   266	            .FirstOrDefault();
   267	        if (!workPosition.HasValue)
   268	        {
   269	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   270	                "The surface campfire has no valid work position.");
   271	        }
   272	
   273	        return new DemoBuildingPlacement(origin, workPosition.Value);
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    private readonly struct DemoBuildingPlacement
   277	    {
   278	        internal DemoBuildingPlacement(CellId origin, CellId workPosition)
   279	        {
   280	            Origin = origin;
   281	            WorkPosition = workPosition;
   282	        }
   283	
   284	        internal CellId Origin { get; }
   285	        internal CellId WorkPosition { get; }
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    private static BuildingDefinition CreateDemoBuildingDefinition()
   289	    {
   290	        return new BuildingDefinition(
   291	            new BuildingDefinitionId("demo.workshop.box"),
   292	            "Box Workshop",
   293	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0) },
   294	            new[]
   295	            {
   296	                new CellOffset(0, -1),
   297	                new CellOffset(-1, 0),
   298	                new CellOffset(1, 0),
   299	                new CellOffset(0, 1),
   300	            },
   301	            Array.Empty<BuildingMaterialRequirement>(),
   302	            requiredWork: 3,
   303	            maximumDurability: 100,
   304	            boxPolicy: new BuildingBoxPolicy(DemoBuildingBoxItemId, packingWork: 4));
   305	    }
   306	
   307	    private static EntityId DemoId(char prefix, long value)
   308	    {
   309	        return EntityId.Parse(prefix + value.ToString("x31"));
   310	    }
   311	}

===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.Composition.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Agents;

    27	    public static DigTerrainWorkSession CreateDemo(
    28	        DigWorldSession worldSession,
    29	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
    30	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal,
    31	        IAgentSkillGrantService skillGrants,
    32	        MiningOutputCommitState? miningOutputCommits = null)
    33	    {
    34	        if (worldSession == null || agents == null)
    35	        {
    36	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(worldSession));
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        WorldCellViewModel[] targets = worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
    40	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    41	            .Where(cell => cell.IsDesignated)
    42	            .OrderBy(cell => cell.Y)
    43	            .ThenBy(cell => cell.X)
    44	            .ToArray();
    45	        if (agents.Count == 0)
    46	        {
    47	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
    48	                "The terrain work demo requires at least one resident.");
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs = new InMemoryJobRepository();
    52	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates = new InMemoryJobCandidateProvider();
    53	        Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId> outputStackIds =
    54	            CreateInitialDigJobs(
    55	                worldSession,
    56	                targets,
    57	                agents,
    58	                jobs,
    59	                candidates,
    60	                journal);
    61	        AssignInitialDigJobs(jobs, candidates, journal);
    62	        AdvanceJobHandler advance = CreateAdvanceHandler(jobs, journal);
    63	        AgentViewModel firstResident = agents[0];
    64	        InventoryState inventory = CreateDemoResidentInventory(
    65	            worldSession.TerrainDepositDefinitions,
    66	            new CellId(
    67	                firstResident.CellX,
    68	                firstResident.CellY,
    69	                firstResident.CellZ));
    70	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository =
    71	            new InMemoryInventoryRepository(inventory);
    72	        TraversalProfile profile = TraversalProfile.CreateFreeMover();
    73	        InMemoryNavigationRepository navigation = BuildNavigation(
    74	            worldSession,
    75	            profile);
    76	        worldSession.DrainDirtyChunks();
    77	        return Compose(
    78	            worldSession,
    79	            journal,
    80	            skillGrants,
    81	            jobs,
    82	            advance,
    83	            inventoryRepository,
    84	            navigation,
    85	            profile,
    86	            outputStackIds,
    87	            miningOutputCommits);
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    private static Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId> CreateInitialDigJobs(
    91	        DigWorldSession worldSession,
    92	        IReadOnlyList<WorldCellViewModel> targets,
    93	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
    94	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs,
    95	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates,
    96	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
    97	    {
    98	        Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId> outputIds =
    99	            new Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId>();
   100	        CreateDigJobHandler create = new CreateDigJobHandler(jobs, journal);
   101	        for (int index = 0; index < targets.Count; index++)
   102	        {
   103	            EntityId jobId = EntityId.Parse(
   104	                $"400000000000000000000000000000{index + 1:D2}");
   105	            EntityId stackId = EntityId.Parse(
   106	                $"500000000000000000000000000000{index + 1:D2}");
   107	            WorldCellViewModel target = targets[index];
   108	            CellId targetCell = new CellId(target.X, target.Y, target.Z);
   109	            DigJobDefinition definition = new DigJobDefinition(
   110	                jobId,
   111	                new DigJobTarget(targetCell),
   112	                DefaultExcavationPriority,
   113	                createdTick: 0,
   114	                new JobRetryPolicy(2, 3),
   115	                skillGrantProfile: worldSession
   116	                    .ResolveExcavationSkillGrantProfile(targetCell));
   117	            Require(create.Handle(new CreateDigJobCommand(definition, makeAvailable: true)));
   118	            outputIds.Add(jobId, stackId);
   119	            candidates.SetCandidates(jobId, CreateCandidates(agents, target));
   120	        }
   121	
   122	        return outputIds;
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    private static void AssignInitialDigJobs(
   126	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs,
   127	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates,
   128	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
   129	    {
   130	        new AssignAvailableJobsHandler(
   131	            jobs,
   132	            candidates,
   133	            journal,
   134	            assignmentReportSink: journal)
   135	            .Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick: 0));
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    private static AdvanceJobHandler CreateAdvanceHandler(
   139	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs,
   140	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal)
   141	    {
   142	        AdvanceJobHandler advance = new AdvanceJobHandler(
   143	            jobs,
   144	            journal,
   145	            new SuggestedToolJobExecutionReadinessPolicy(journal));
   146	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in jobs.Get().GetAll())
   147	        {
   148	            if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed)
   149	            {
   150	                Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick: 0)));
   151	            }
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        return advance;
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    private static InMemoryNavigationRepository BuildNavigation(
   158	        DigWorldSession worldSession,
   159	        TraversalProfile profile)
   160	    {
   161	        InMemoryNavigationRepository navigation = new InMemoryNavigationRepository();
   162	        Result<NavigationUpdateDiagnostics> rebuild =
   163	            new RebuildNavigationCommandHandler(navigation).Handle(
   164	                new RebuildNavigationCommand(
   165	                    profile,
   166	                    worldSession.LoadSnapshot(),
   167	                    Array.Empty<TraversalLink>()));
   168	        if (rebuild.IsFailure)
   169	        {
   170	            throw new InvalidOperationException(rebuild.Error!.ToString());
   171	        }
   172	
   173	        return navigation;
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    private static DigTerrainWorkSession Compose(
   177	        DigWorldSession world,
   178	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal,
   179	        IAgentSkillGrantService skills,
   180	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs,
   181	        AdvanceJobHandler advance,
   182	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventory,
   183	        InMemoryNavigationRepository navigation,
   184	        TraversalProfile profile,
   185	        Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId> outputIds,
   186	        MiningOutputCommitState? miningOutputCommits)
   187	    {
   188	        return new DigTerrainWorkSession(
   189	            advance,
   190	            new CompleteTerrainWorkCommandHandler(
   191	                jobs, world.Repository, inventory, journal, skills),
   192	            new CompletePartialTerrainWorkCommandHandler(
   193	                jobs, world.Repository, journal, skills),
   194	            new JobOverlayPresenter(
   195	                new GetJobsHandler(jobs),
   196	                new GetJobReservationsHandler(jobs)),
   197	            journal,
   198	            new InventoryWorldPresenter(
   199	                new GetInventorySnapshotQueryHandler(inventory),
   200	                new Dictionary<ItemId, WorldItemInteractionKind>
   201	                {
   202	                    [DemoBuildingBoxItemId] = WorldItemInteractionKind.BuildingBox,
   203	                    [CampfireBuildingBoxContent.CampfireBoxItemId] =
   204	                        WorldItemInteractionKind.BuildingBox,
   205	                },
   206	                WorldItemInteractionKind.Pickup),
   207	            new NavigationRoutePresenter(),
   208	            jobs,
   209	            inventory,
   210	            navigation,
   211	            world,
   212	            new TerrainWorkRoutePlanner(new NavigationPathfinder()),
   213	            new UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner(new NavigationPathfinder()),
   214	            profile,
   215	            outputIds,
   216	            skills,
   217	            miningOutputCommits);
   218	    }
   219	}

===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.DirectCommands.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Application.Ecology;
     4	using Dig.Application.WorldObjects;
     5	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    12	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    13	
    14	namespace Dig.Unity
    15	{
    16	    internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    17	    {
    18	        internal Result PrepareResidentsForDirectCommand(
    19	            IReadOnlyList<string> residentIds,
    20	            long tick)
    21	        {
    22	            if (residentIds == null)
    23	            {
    24	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(residentIds));
    25	            }
    26	
    27	            JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    28	            InventoryState terrainInventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
    29	            for (int residentIndex = 0; residentIndex < residentIds.Count; residentIndex++)
    30	            {
    31	                EntityId residentId = EntityId.Parse(residentIds[residentIndex]);
    32	                Result interrupted = InterruptFoodMealForDirectCommand(residentId, tick);
    33	                if (interrupted.IsFailure)
    34	                {
    35	                    return interrupted;
    36	                }
    37	
    38	                CancelManualQuarterExcavation(residentId.ToString());
    39	                JobSnapshot[] assigned = CollectAssignedActiveJobs(jobs, residentId);
    40	                for (int jobIndex = 0; jobIndex < assigned.Length; jobIndex++)
    41	                {
    42	                    JobSnapshot job = assigned[jobIndex];
    43	                    Result released = job.Definition switch
    44	                    {
    45	                        WorldItemPickupJobDefinition =>
    46	                            CancelPickupForDirectCommand(jobs, job, tick),
    47	                        MushroomChopJobDefinition =>
    48	                            CancelMushroomForDirectCommand(job, tick),
    49	                        BarrelAttackJobDefinition =>
    50	                            CancelBarrelForDirectCommand(job, tick),
    51	                        BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition =>
    52	                            CancelBuildingBoxForDirectCommand(job, tick),
    53	                        BuildingBoxPickupJobDefinition relocation when relocation.IsRelocation =>
    54	                            CancelBuildingBoxForDirectCommand(job, tick),
    55	                        _ => ReleaseDigWorkForDirectCommand(job, tick),
    56	                    };
    57	                    if (released.IsFailure)
    58	                    {
    59	                        return released;
    60	                    }
    61	
    62	                    RemoveAllRoutePlans(job.Id);
    63	                }
    64	            }
    65	
    66	            _inventoryRepository.Save(terrainInventory);
    67	            _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
    68	            return Result.Success();
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        private Result InterruptFoodMealForDirectCommand(EntityId residentId, long tick)
    72	        {
    73	            AgentState? resident = _productionAgents?.Get(residentId);
    74	            if (resident == null || !resident.HasActiveFoodMeal)
    75	            {
    76	                return Result.Success();
    77	            }
    78	
    79	            Result interrupted = resident.InterruptFoodMeal(
    80	                "direct_command_replaced",
    81	                tick);
    82	            if (interrupted.IsFailure)
    83	            {
    84	                return interrupted;
    85	            }
    86	
    87	            _productionAgents!.Save(resident);
    88	            _journal.Append(resident.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
    89	            return Result.Success();
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        private static JobSnapshot[] CollectAssignedActiveJobs(
    93	            JobSystem jobs,
    94	            EntityId residentId)
    95	        {
    96	            List<JobSnapshot> assigned = new List<JobSnapshot>();
    97	            foreach (JobSnapshot job in jobs.GetAll())
    98	            {
    99	                if (!job.IsTerminal
   100	                    && job.AssignedAgentId == residentId)
   101	                {
   102	                    assigned.Add(job);
   103	                }
   104	            }
   105	
   106	            return assigned.ToArray();
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        private Result CancelPickupForDirectCommand(
   110	            JobSystem jobs,
   111	            JobSnapshot job,
   112	            long tick)
   113	        {
   114	            if (job.Definition is not WorldItemPickupJobDefinition pickup)
   115	            {
   116	                return Result.Failure(WorldItemPickupErrors.JobTypeMismatch);
   117	            }
   118	
   119	            InMemoryInventoryRepository? repository = ResolveWorldItemRepository(
   120	                pickup.StackId);
   121	            if (repository == null)
   122	            {
   123	                return Result.Failure(WorldItemPickupErrors.StackMissing);
   124	            }
   125	
   126	            Result cancelled = jobs.Cancel(
   127	                job.Id,
   128	                new JobBlockReason(
   129	                    "world_item_pickup_direct_command",
   130	                    "Pickup was cancelled by a direct resident command."),
   131	                tick);
   132	            if (cancelled.IsFailure)
   133	            {
   134	                return cancelled;
   135	            }
   136	
   137	            InventoryState inventory = repository.Get();
   138	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(job.Id, tick);
   139	            repository.Save(inventory);
   140	            _journal.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   141	            return Result.Success();
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        private Result CancelMushroomForDirectCommand(JobSnapshot job, long tick)
   145	        {
   146	            return _cancelMushroomChop == null
   147	                ? Result.Success()
   148	                : _cancelMushroomChop.Handle(new CancelMushroomChopCommand(
   149	                    job.Id,
   150	                    "mushroom_direct_command_replaced",
   151	                    tick));
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        private Result CancelBarrelForDirectCommand(JobSnapshot job, long tick)
   155	        {
   156	            return _cancelBarrelAttack == null
   157	                ? Result.Success()
   158	                : _cancelBarrelAttack.Handle(new CancelBarrelAttackCommand(
   159	                    job.Id,
   160	                    "barrel_direct_command_replaced",
   161	                    tick));
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        private Result ReleaseDigWorkForDirectCommand(JobSnapshot job, long tick)
   165	        {
   166	            if (_releaseAssignment == null)
   167	            {
   168	                return Result.Success();
   169	            }
   170	
   171	            return _releaseAssignment.Handle(
   172	                new ReleaseJobAssignmentCommand(job.Id, tick));
   173	        }
   174	    }
   175	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.MushroomNavigation.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	using Dig.Presentation.Agents;
    10	using Dig.Presentation.World;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Unity
    13	{
    14	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    15	{
    16	    private bool TryPlanMushroomMovement(
    17	        JobSnapshot job,
    18	        AgentViewModel agent,
    19	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
    20	        IDictionary<string, CellId> movement)
    21	    {
    22	        if (job.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition)
    23	        {
    24	            return false;
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        CellId start = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
    28	        PathResult path = new NavigationPathfinder().FindPath(
    29	            navigation,
    30	            new PathRequest(start, definition.WorkPosition, navigation.NavigationVersion));
    31	        if (!path.Succeeded || path.Path == null)
    32	        {
    33	            return true;
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        _routePlans[job.Id] = new TerrainWorkRoutePlan(
    37	            job.Id,
    38	            definition.TargetCell,
    39	            definition.WorkPosition,
    40	            path,
    41	            candidateCount: 1);
    42	        movement[agent.Id] = path.Path.Cells.Count > 1
    43	            ? path.Path.Cells[1]
    44	            : definition.WorkPosition;
    45	        return true;
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    private bool TryResolveMushroomWorkPosition(
    49	        CellId target,
    50	        CellId workerCell,
    51	        out CellId workPosition)
    52	    {
    53	        workPosition = default;
    54	        Result refresh = RefreshNavigation();
    55	        if (refresh.IsFailure)
    56	        {
    57	            return false;
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        NavigationMap? map = _navigationRepository.Get(_profile.Id);
    61	        if (map == null)
    62	        {
    63	            return false;
    64	        }
    65	
    66	        Result<NavigationSnapshot> snapshotResult = map.GetSnapshot();
    67	        if (snapshotResult.IsFailure)
    68	        {
    69	            return false;
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        NavigationSnapshot navigation = snapshotResult.Value;
    73	        CellId[] candidates =
    74	        {
    75	            new CellId(target.X - 1, target.Y, target.Z),
    76	            new CellId(target.X + 1, target.Y, target.Z),
    77	            new CellId(target.X, target.Y - 1, target.Z),
    78	            new CellId(target.X, target.Y + 1, target.Z),
    79	        };
    80	        PathResult? selectedPath = null;
    81	        CellId selectedCell = default;
    82	        foreach (CellId candidate in candidates
    83	            .Where(navigation.IsWalkable)
    84	            .Distinct()
    85	            .OrderBy(value => value))
    86	        {
    87	            PathResult path = new NavigationPathfinder().FindPath(
    88	                navigation,
    89	                new PathRequest(workerCell, candidate, navigation.NavigationVersion));
    90	            if (!path.Succeeded || path.Path == null)
    91	            {
    92	                continue;
    93	            }
    94	
    95	            if (selectedPath == null
    96	                || path.Path.TotalCost < selectedPath.Path!.TotalCost)
    97	            {
    98	                selectedPath = path;
    99	                selectedCell = candidate;
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	
   103	        if (selectedPath == null)
   104	        {
   105	            return false;
   106	        }
   107	
   108	        workPosition = selectedCell;
   109	        return true;
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    private CellId FindMushroomDemoCell(bool surface, CellId? excluded)
   113	    {
   114	        WorldCellViewModel[] cells = _worldSession.LoadView().Chunks
   115	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
   116	            .ToArray();
   117	        Dictionary<CellId, WorldCellViewModel> byId = cells.ToDictionary(
   118	            value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z));
   119	        HashSet<CellId> occupied = _buildingsRepository == null
   120	            ? new HashSet<CellId>()
   121	            : new HashSet<CellId>(_buildingsRepository.Get().GetOccupiedCells());
   122	        foreach (Dig.Domain.Inventory.ItemStackSnapshot stack
   123	            in _inventoryRepository.Get().CreateSnapshot().Stacks)
   124	        {
   125	            if (stack.Location.Kind == Dig.Domain.Inventory.ItemLocationKind.World
   126	                && stack.Location.HasCell)
   127	            {
   128	                occupied.Add(stack.Location.CellId);
   129	            }
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        IEnumerable<CellId> candidates = cells
   133	            .Where(value => !value.IsSolid)
   134	            .Select(value => new CellId(value.X, value.Y, value.Z))
   135	            .Where(value => !excluded.HasValue || value != excluded.Value)
   136	            .Where(value => !occupied.Contains(value))
   137	            .Where(value => byId.TryGetValue(
   138	                new CellId(value.X, value.Y + 1, value.Z),
   139	                out WorldCellViewModel below)
   140	                && below.IsSolid);
   141	        candidates = surface
   142	            ? candidates.Where(value => value.Z == 0)
   143	                .OrderBy(value => value.Y)
   144	                .ThenBy(value => value.X)
   145	            : candidates.Where(value => value.Z > 0)
   146	                .OrderByDescending(value => value.Z)
   147	                .ThenByDescending(value => value.Y)
   148	                .ThenByDescending(value => value.X);
   149	        CellId? selected = candidates.Cast<CellId?>().FirstOrDefault();
   150	        if (!selected.HasValue)
   151	        {
   152	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   153	                surface
   154	                    ? "The demo has no supported surface cell for a mushroom."
   155	                    : "The demo has no supported lower-cave cell for a mushroom.");
   156	        }
   157	
   158	        return selected.Value;
   159	    }
   160	
   161	
   162	}
   163	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.Mushrooms.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Application.Ecology;
     6	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
