    17	namespace Dig.Unity
    18	{
    19	internal sealed partial class DigTerrainWorkSession
    20	{
    21	    private const int MushroomDirectPriority = 900;
    22	    private static readonly MushroomDefinitionId DemoMushroomDefinitionId =
    23	        new MushroomDefinitionId("ecology.mushroom.common");
    24	    private static readonly ItemId MushroomCapItemId =
    25	        new ItemId("material.mushroom_cap");
    26	    private static readonly ItemId MushroomLegItemId =
    27	        new ItemId("material.mushroom_leg");
    28	
    29	    private InMemoryMushroomRepository? _mushroomRepository;
    30	    private AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommandHandler? _advanceMushroomGrowth;
    31	    private StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler? _startMushroomChop;
    32	    private ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler? _arriveAtMushroom;
    33	    private CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler? _completeMushroomSwing;
    34	    private CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler? _completeMushroomChop;
    35	    private CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler? _cancelMushroomChop;
    36	    private long _nextMushroomJobSequence;
    37	    private long _nextMushroomOutputSequence;
    38	
    39	    internal void InitializeMushroomDemo(long tick)
    40	    {
    41	        if (_mushroomRepository != null)
    42	        {
    43	            return;
    44	        }
    45	
    46	        MushroomDefinition definition = new MushroomDefinition(
    47	            DemoMushroomDefinitionId,
    48	            stageDurationTicks: 1,
    49	            capItemId: MushroomCapItemId,
    50	            legItemId: MushroomLegItemId);
    51	        MushroomState state = new MushroomState(new MushroomCatalog(new[] { definition }));
    52	        CellId surface = FindMushroomDemoCell(surface: true, excluded: null);
    53	        CellId cave = FindMushroomDemoCell(surface: false, excluded: surface);
    54	        Require(state.AddSite(DemoId('8', 1), definition.Id, surface, MushroomStage.Tiny, tick));
    55	        Require(state.AddSite(DemoId('8', 2), definition.Id, cave, MushroomStage.Tiny, tick));
    56	        _mushroomRepository = new InMemoryMushroomRepository(state);
    57	
    58	        if (_buildingInventoryRepository == null)
    59	        {
    60	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
    61	                "Mushroom drops require the shared production inventory.");
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        IAgentSkillLevelReader skillReader = _skillGrants as IAgentSkillLevelReader
    65	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(
    66	                "The mushroom demo requires a readable authoritative skill service.");
    67	        MushroomSwingRandom random = new MushroomSwingRandom(new RandomStreamCatalog(1337));
    68	        _advanceMushroomGrowth = new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommandHandler(
    69	            _mushroomRepository,
    70	            _journal);
    71	        _startMushroomChop = new StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler(
    72	            _mushroomRepository,
    73	            _jobRepository,
    74	            skillReader,
    75	            random,
    76	            _journal);
    77	        _arriveAtMushroom = new ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler(
    78	            _jobRepository,
    79	            _journal);
    80	        _completeMushroomSwing = new CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler(
    81	            _mushroomRepository,
    82	            _jobRepository,
    83	            _journal);
    84	        _completeMushroomChop = new CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler(
    85	            _mushroomRepository,
    86	            _jobRepository,
    87	            _buildingInventoryRepository,
    88	            _skillGrants,
    89	            _journal);
    90	        _cancelMushroomChop = new CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler(
    91	            _mushroomRepository,
    92	            _jobRepository,
    93	            _journal);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    internal IReadOnlyList<MushroomSiteSnapshot> LoadMushrooms()
    97	    {
    98	        return _mushroomRepository?.Get().GetAll()
    99	            ?? Array.Empty<MushroomSiteSnapshot>();
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    internal IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> MushroomBuildingBlockedCells =>
   103	        _mushroomRepository?.Get().GetBuildingBlockedCells()
   104	        ?? Array.Empty<CellId>();
   105	
   106	    internal bool CanDirectChopMushroom(
   107	        EntityId siteId,
   108	        CellId workerCell,
   109	        out CellId workPosition)
   110	    {
   111	        workPosition = default;
   112	        MushroomSiteSnapshot? site = _mushroomRepository?.Get().Get(siteId);
   113	        return site != null
   114	            && site.IsVisible
   115	            && TryResolveMushroomWorkPosition(site.Cell, workerCell, out workPosition);
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    internal Result StartDirectMushroomChop(
   119	        EntityId siteId,
   120	        EntityId workerId,
   121	        CellId workerCell,
   122	        long tick)
   123	    {
   124	        EnsureMushroomsInitialized();
   125	        if (!CanDirectChopMushroom(siteId, workerCell, out CellId workPosition))
   126	        {
   127	            return Result.Failure(new DomainError(
   128	                "mushroom.direct_target_unavailable",
   129	                "The mushroom is absent or has no reachable work position."));
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        Result prepared = PrepareResidentsForDirectCommand(
   133	            new[] { workerId.ToString() },
   134	            tick);
   135	        if (prepared.IsFailure)
   136	        {
   137	            return prepared;
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        EntityId jobId = DemoId('9', checked(++_nextMushroomJobSequence));
   141	        Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> started = _startMushroomChop!.Handle(
   142	            new StartDirectMushroomChopCommand(
   143	                jobId,
   144	                siteId,
   145	                workerId,
   146	                workPosition,
   147	                MushroomDirectPriority,
   148	                tick));
   149	        return started.IsSuccess
   150	            ? Result.Success()
   151	            : Result.Failure(started.Error!);
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    internal Result AdvanceMushrooms(long tick, IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   155	    {
   156	        if (_mushroomRepository == null)
   157	        {
   158	            return Result.Success();
   159	        }
   160	
   161	        Result growth = _advanceMushroomGrowth!.Handle(
   162	            new AdvanceMushroomGrowthCommand(tick));
   163	        if (growth.IsFailure)
   164	        {
   165	            return growth;
   166	        }
   167	
   168	        Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
   169	            value => value.Id,
   170	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
   171	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
   172	        {
   173	            if (job.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition
   174	                || !IsActive(job)
   175	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   176	                || !agentsById.TryGetValue(
   177	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   178	                    out AgentViewModel? worker))
   179	            {
   180	                continue;
   181	            }
   182	
   183	            bool atWork = worker.CellX == definition.WorkPosition.X
   184	                && worker.CellY == definition.WorkPosition.Y
   185	                && worker.CellZ == definition.WorkPosition.Z;
   186	            if (!atWork)
   187	            {
   188	                continue;
   189	            }
   190	
   191	            Result result = AdvanceMushroomJob(job, definition, tick);
   192	            if (result.IsFailure)
   193	            {
   194	                return result;
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	
   198	        return Result.Success();
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    private Result AdvanceMushroomJob(
   202	        JobSnapshot job,
   203	        MushroomChopJobDefinition definition,
   204	        long tick)
   205	    {
   206	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed
   207	            || job.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
   208	        {
   209	            return _arriveAtMushroom!.Handle(
   210	                new ArriveAtMushroomCommand(job.Id, tick));
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.PerformWork)
   214	        {
   215	            MushroomSiteSnapshot? site = _mushroomRepository!.Get().Get(definition.SiteId);
   216	            if (site == null)
   217	            {
   218	                return Result.Failure(MushroomErrors.NotFound);
   219	            }
   220	
   221	            if (site.CompletedSwings < site.RequiredSwings)
   222	            {
   223	                Result<bool> swing = _completeMushroomSwing!.Handle(
   224	                    new CompleteMushroomSwingCommand(job.Id, tick));
   225	                if (swing.IsFailure)
   226	                {
   227	                    return Result.Failure(swing.Error!);
   228	                }
   229	
   230	                if (!swing.Value)
   231	                {
   232	                    return Result.Success();
   233	                }
   234	            }
   235	            else
   236	            {
   237	                Result advanced = _advanceHandler.Handle(
   238	                    new AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
   239	                if (advanced.IsFailure)
   240	                {
   241	                    return advanced;
   242	                }
   243	            }
   244	
   245	            job = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id)
   246	                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(
   247	                    "The active mushroom job disappeared before completion.");
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        if (job.Stage != JobStageKind.Finalize)
   251	        {
   252	            return Result.Success();
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        long sequence = checked(_nextMushroomOutputSequence + 1);
   256	        _nextMushroomOutputSequence = sequence;
   257	        Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult> completed = _completeMushroomChop!.Handle(
   258	            new CompleteMushroomChopCommand(
   259	                job.Id,
   260	                DemoId('7', sequence),
   261	                tick));
   262	        return completed.IsSuccess
   263	            ? Result.Success()
   264	            : Result.Failure(completed.Error!);
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    private void EnsureMushroomsInitialized()
   268	    {
   269	        if (_mushroomRepository == null)
   270	        {
   271	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Mushroom demo is not initialized.");
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	}
   275	}

   142	            residentStartCell.X - 1,

   153	    private static void AddResidentUnit(

===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigTerrainWorkSession.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;

    46	        new Dictionary<EntityId, TerrainWorkRoutePlan>();
    47	    private bool _worldChanged;
    48	
    49	    private DigTerrainWorkSession(
    50	        AdvanceJobHandler advanceHandler,
    51	        CompleteTerrainWorkCommandHandler completionHandler,
    52	        CompletePartialTerrainWorkCommandHandler partialCompletionHandler,
    53	        JobOverlayPresenter jobPresenter,
    54	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal,
    55	        InventoryWorldPresenter inventoryPresenter,
    56	        NavigationRoutePresenter routePresenter,
    57	        InMemoryJobRepository jobRepository,
    58	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    59	        InMemoryNavigationRepository navigationRepository,
    60	        DigWorldSession worldSession,
    61	        TerrainWorkRoutePlanner routePlanner,
    62	        UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner supportRecoveryPlanner,
    63	        TraversalProfile profile,
    64	        Dictionary<EntityId, EntityId> outputStackIds,
    65	        IAgentSkillGrantService skillGrants,
    66	        MiningOutputCommitState? miningOutputCommits)

    71	        _jobPresenter = jobPresenter;
    72	        _journal = journal ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(journal));
    73	        _inventoryPresenter = inventoryPresenter;
    74	        _routePresenter = routePresenter;
    75	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
    76	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
    77	        _navigationRepository = navigationRepository;
    78	        _worldSession = worldSession;
    79	        _routePlanner = routePlanner;
    80	        _supportRecoveryPlanner = supportRecoveryPlanner;
    81	        _profile = profile;
    82	        _outputStackIds = outputStackIds;
    83	        _skillGrants = skillGrants ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(skillGrants));
    84	        _quarterCommitHandler = new CommitExcavationQuarterCommandHandler(worldSession.Repository, _skillGrants, _journal);
    85	        _miningOutputCommits = miningOutputCommits ?? new MiningOutputCommitState();
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    internal MiningOutputCommitState MiningOutputCommits => _miningOutputCommits;
    89	
    90	    internal bool HasWorldChanged => _worldChanged;
    91	
    92	    internal void MarkAuthoritativeWorldChanged()
    93	    {
    94	        _worldChanged = true;
    95	    }

    97	    public IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> LoadJobs()
    98	    {

   100	    }

   106	
   107	    public Result Advance(long tick, IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   108	    {
   109	        Dictionary<string, AgentViewModel> agentsById = agents.ToDictionary(
   110	            agent => agent.Id,
   111	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
   112	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in _jobRepository.Get().GetAll())
   113	        {
   114	            if (!IsActive(job)
   115	                || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   116	                || !agentsById.TryGetValue(
   117	                    job.AssignedAgentId.Value.ToString(),
   118	                    out AgentViewModel? agent))
   119	            {
   120	                continue;
   121	            }
   122	
   123	            if (job.Definition is MushroomChopJobDefinition
   124	                || job.Definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition)
   125	            {
   126	                continue;
   127	            }
   128	
   129	            Result result;
   130	            if (job.Definition is HaulJobDefinition)
   131	            {
   132	                result = AdvanceHaulingAtTarget(job, agent, tick);
   133	            }
   134	            else if (_routePlans.TryGetValue(
   135	                    job.Id,
   136	                    out TerrainWorkRoutePlan? route)
   137	                && route.Succeeded
   138	                && route.WorkCell.HasValue
   139	                && agent.CellX == route.WorkCell.Value.X
   140	                && agent.CellY == route.WorkCell.Value.Y
   141	                && agent.CellZ == route.WorkCell.Value.Z)
   142	            {
   143	                result = AdvanceAtWorkCell(job, agent, route, tick);
   144	            }
   145	            else
   146	            {
   147	                continue;
   148	            }
   149	
   150	            if (result.IsFailure)
   151	            {
   152	                return result;
   153	            }
   154	        }
   155	
   156	        return Result.Success();
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    internal void ReconcileChangedTerrain(
   160	        long tick,
   161	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents)
   162	    {
   163	        if (agents == null)
   164	        {
   165	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(agents));
   166	        }
   167	
   168	        if (_worldChanged)
   169	        {
   170	            // Rebuild frontier jobs once after every resident has advanced. Doing this
   171	            // from per-resident Advance calls used a one-agent candidate set and could
   172	            // leave the remaining tunnel cells without a worker after the first dig.
   173	            SynchronizeDesignations(tick, agents, DefaultExcavationPriority);
   174	        }
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    public bool ConsumeWorldChanged()
   178	    {
   179	        bool changed = _worldChanged;
   180	        _worldChanged = false;
   181	        return changed;
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    private Result AdvanceAtWorkCell(
   185	        JobSnapshot job,
   186	        AgentViewModel agent,
   187	        TerrainWorkRoutePlan route,
   188	        long tick)
   189	    {
   190	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed
   191	            || job.Stage == JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
   192	        {
   193	            return _advanceHandler.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
   194	        }
   195	
   196	        if (job.Stage == JobStageKind.Finalize)
   197	        {
   198	            return CompleteTerrainJobAtWorkCell(job, tick);
   199	        }
   200	
   201	        if (job.Stage != JobStageKind.PerformWork)
   202	        {
   203	            return Result.Success();
   204	        }
   205	
   206	        DigJobDefinition definition = (DigJobDefinition)job.Definition;
   207	        EntityId workerId = job.AssignedAgentId!.Value;
   208	        CellId residentCell = new CellId(agent.CellX, agent.CellY, agent.CellZ);
   209	        bool quartersComplete = AdvanceExcavationQuarterWork(
   210	            workerId,
   211	            new ExcavationWorkTarget(
   212	                definition.Target.CellId,
   213	                definition.Target.CellId.Z),
   214	            residentCell,
   215	            route.Posture,
   216	            tick);
   217	        if (!quartersComplete)
   218	        {
   219	            return Result.Success();
   220	        }
   221	
   222	        Result advanced = _advanceHandler.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(job.Id, tick));
   223	        if (advanced.IsFailure)
   224	        {
   225	            return advanced;
   226	        }
   227	
   228	        JobSnapshot? updated = _jobRepository.Get().Get(job.Id);
   229	        if (updated == null)
   230	        {
   231	            return Result.Failure(JobErrors.NotFound);
   232	        }
   233	
   234	        return updated.Stage == JobStageKind.Finalize
   235	            ? CompleteTerrainJobAtWorkCell(updated, tick)
   236	            : Result.Success();
   237	    }
   238	
   239	    private Result CompleteTerrainJobAtWorkCell(JobSnapshot job, long tick)
   240	    {
   241	        DigJobDefinition terrainJob = (DigJobDefinition)job.Definition;
   242	        CellId targetCell = terrainJob.Target.CellId;
   243	        if (_worldSession.TryGetCaveRoomExcavationTarget(
   244	                targetCell,
   245	                out CaveRoomExcavationTarget roomTarget)
   246	            && !roomTarget.IsFullCell)
   247	        {
   248	            return CompletePartialTerrainJobAtWorkCell(
   249	                job,
   250	                roomTarget,
   251	                tick);
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        MiningOutputPlan output;
   255	        try
   256	        {
   257	            output = _miningOutputResolver.Resolve(
   258	                _worldSession.MiningOutputWorldSeed,
   259	                _worldSession.MiningOutputGeneratorVersion,
   260	                targetCell,
   261	                _worldSession.ResolveTerrainMaterial(targetCell),
   262	                _worldSession.TerrainDeposits);
   263	            _miningOutputCommits.Validate(
   264	                output,
   265	                _outputStackIds[job.Id],
   266	                _inventoryRepository.Get(),
   267	                _worldSession.TerrainDeposits);
   268	        }
   269	        catch (Exception error)
   270	        {
   271	            return Result.Failure(new DomainError(
   272	                "terrain_work.mining_output_invalid",
   273	                error.Message));
   274	        }
   275	
   276	        CompleteTerrainWorkCommand command = output.IsEmpty
   277	            ? CompleteTerrainWorkCommand.WithoutOutput(
   278	                job.Id,
   279	                _worldSession.EmptyMaterialId,
   280	                tick)
   281	            : new CompleteTerrainWorkCommand(
   282	                job.Id,
   283	                _outputStackIds[job.Id],
   284	                output.ItemId,
   285	                output.Quantity,
   286	                _worldSession.EmptyMaterialId,
   287	                tick);
   288	        Result<TerrainWorkCompletionResult> completion = _completionHandler.Handle(command);
   289	        if (completion.IsFailure)
   290	        {
   291	            return Result.Failure(completion.Error!);
   292	        }
   293	
   294	        // The terrain commit above is already authoritative. Mark the world dirty
   295	        // before any derived navigation refresh so Presentation cannot miss the open
   296	        // cell when navigation fails and the tick returns a recoverable warning.
   297	        MarkAuthoritativeWorldChanged();
   298	        if (output.SourceKind == MiningOutputSourceKind.Deposit)
   299	        {
   300	            _worldSession.DepleteTerrainDeposit(targetCell, tick);
   301	        }
   302	
   303	        _miningOutputCommits.Record(output, _outputStackIds[job.Id]);
   304	        _worldSession.RevealTerrainDepositsAdjacentTo(targetCell, tick);
   305	        CompleteExcavationQuarterTarget(targetCell);
   306	        _routePlans.Remove(job.Id);
   307	        MarkTemplateCellExcavated(targetCell);
   308	        PublishTerrainCompletionEffects(job.Id, targetCell, tick, !output.IsEmpty);
   309	
   310	        Result refresh = RefreshNavigation();
   311	        return refresh.IsFailure ? refresh : Result.Success();
   312	    }
   313	
   314	
   315	    private static bool IsActive(JobSnapshot job)
   316	    {
   317	        return job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed
   318	            || job.Status == JobStatus.InProgress;
   319	    }
   320	
   321	    private static IReadOnlyList<JobCandidate> CreateCandidates(
   322	        IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents,
   323	        WorldCellViewModel target)
   324	    {
   325	        JobCandidate[] values = new JobCandidate[agents.Count];
   326	        for (int index = 0; index < agents.Count; index++)
   327	        {
   328	            AgentViewModel agent = agents[index];
   329	            int distance = Math.Abs(agent.CellX - target.X)
   330	                + Math.Abs(agent.CellY - target.Y);
   331	            values[index] = new JobCandidate(
   332	                EntityId.Parse(agent.Id),
   333	                skillLevel: 5_000 - (index * 250),
   334	                distanceCost: distance,
   335	                isAvailable: agent.IsAvailableForAutomaticPlanning);
   336	        }
   337	
   338	        return values;
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    private static void Require(Result result)
   342	    {
   343	        if (result.IsFailure)
   344	        {
   345	            throw new InvalidOperationException(result.Error!.ToString());
   346	        }
   347	    }
   348	}
   349

    29	        private void Awake()
    30	        {
    31	            DigHudOverlay hud = GetOrAdd<DigHudOverlay>(gameObject);
    32	            hud.SetStatus("Starting runtime...");
    33	            DigGameHudCanvas gameHud = CreateStartupGameHud(hud);
    34	            try
    35	            {
    36	                StartRuntime(hud, gameHud);
    37	            }
    38	            catch (Exception exception)
    39	            {
    40	                DisableRuntimeDrivers();
    41	                string message = $"STARTUP FAILED [{_startupStage}] "
    42	                    + $"{exception.GetType().Name}: {exception.Message}";
    43	                hud.SetStatus(message);
    44	                Debug.LogException(exception, this);
    45	            }
    46	        }

    48	        private void StartRuntime(DigHudOverlay hud, DigGameHudCanvas gameHud)
    49	        {
    50	            _startupStage = "validating demo configuration";
    51	            ClampDemoConfiguration();
    52	            _startupStage = "configuring side-view world root";
    53	            ConfigureSideViewRoot();
    54	
    55	            _startupStage = "creating world";
    56	            DigWorldSession worldSession = DigWorldSession.CreateDemo(
    57	                demoWidth, demoHeight, chunkSize);
    58	            WorldViewModel world = worldSession.LoadView();
    59	
    60	            _startupStage = "creating residents";
    61	            DigAgentSession agentSession = DigAgentSession.CreateDemo(
    62	                world,
    63	                worldSession.CreateTunnelNavigationVolume(),
    64	                worldSession.Journal);
    65	            agentSession.InitializeHudSchedule(worldSession.Journal);
    66	            IReadOnlyList<AgentViewModel> agents = agentSession.LoadView();
    67	
    68	            _startupStage = "creating work systems";
    69	            DigTerrainWorkSession terrainSession = DigTerrainWorkSession.CreateDemo(
    70	                worldSession, agents, worldSession.Journal, agentSession.SkillGrants);
    71	            terrainSession.InitializeDynamicDesignations(worldSession.Journal);
    72	            terrainSession.BindExcavationSkillSource(agentId =>
    73	                agentSession.GetSkillLevel(
    74	                    agentId,
    75	                    Dig.Domain.Agents.AgentSkillCatalog.Stonework)
    76	                / Dig.Domain.Agents.AgentSkillCatalog.UnitsPerPoint);
    77	            terrainSession.InitializeHauling(worldSession.Journal);
    78	            terrainSession.PlanMovement(agents, tick: 0);
    79	            terrainSession.InitializeBuildingDemo(worldSession.Journal);
    80	            terrainSession.InitializeBuildingProductionDemo(
    81	                agentSession.Repository,
    82	                worldSession.Journal);
    83	            terrainSession.InitializeMushroomDemo(agentSession.Tick);
    84	            terrainSession.InitializeBarrelDemo(agentSession.Tick);
    85	            terrainSession.InitializeToolAwareJobAssignment(worldSession.Journal);
    86	            Result settledItems = terrainSession.SettleWorldItems(agentSession.Tick);
    87	            if (settledItems.IsFailure)
    88	            {
    89	                throw new InvalidOperationException(settledItems.Error!.ToString());
    90	            }
    91	
    92	            IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> jobs = terrainSession.LoadJobs();
    93	            IReadOnlyList<WorldItemViewModel> items = terrainSession.LoadAllWorldItems();
    94	            IReadOnlyList<RouteViewModel> routes = terrainSession.LoadRoutes();
    95	            IReadOnlyList<BuildingWorldViewModel> buildings = terrainSession.LoadBuildings();
    96	            DigStorageStatus storage = terrainSession.GetStorageStatus();
    97	
    98	            _startupStage = "creating Unity adapters";
    99	            Camera targetCamera = EnsureCamera();
   100	            GetOrAdd<DigRenderMaterialLibrary>(gameObject);
   101	            DigPresentationEffectBridge effectBridge =
   102	                GetOrAdd<DigPresentationEffectBridge>(gameObject);
   103	            DigPresentationEffectRuntime effectRuntime =
   104	                GetOrAdd<DigPresentationEffectRuntime>(gameObject);
   105	            effectRuntime.Initialize(
   106	                worldSession,
   107	                agentSession,
   108	                terrainSession,
   109	                effectBridge,
   110	                targetCamera);
   111	            terrainSession.BindPresentationEffectSink(
   112	                effectRuntime.Publish);
   113	            DigWorldRenderer worldRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigWorldRenderer>(gameObject);
   114	            DigOverlayManager overlayManager = GetOrAdd<DigOverlayManager>(gameObject);
   115	            DigWorldOverlayRenderer worldOverlayRenderer =
   116	                GetOrAdd<DigWorldOverlayRenderer>(gameObject);
   117	            DigAgentRenderer agentRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigAgentRenderer>(gameObject);
   118	            DigCreatureRenderer creatureRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigCreatureRenderer>(gameObject);
   119	            DigMushroomRenderer mushroomRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigMushroomRenderer>(gameObject);
   120	            DigBarrelRenderer barrelRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigBarrelRenderer>(gameObject);
   121	            DigJobRenderer jobRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigJobRenderer>(gameObject);
   122	            DigBuildingRenderer buildingRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigBuildingRenderer>(gameObject);
   123	            DigBuildingInternalStockRenderer buildingInternalStockRenderer =
   124	                GetOrAdd<DigBuildingInternalStockRenderer>(gameObject);
   125	            DigWorldItemRenderer itemRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigWorldItemRenderer>(gameObject);
   126	            DigBuildingBoxGhostRenderer ghostRenderer =
   127	                GetOrAdd<DigBuildingBoxGhostRenderer>(gameObject);
   128	            DigStockpileRenderer stockpileRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigStockpileRenderer>(gameObject);
   129	            DigNavigationRouteRenderer routeRenderer =
   130	                GetOrAdd<DigNavigationRouteRenderer>(gameObject);
   131	            DigTunnelDemoRenderer tunnelRenderer = GetOrAdd<DigTunnelDemoRenderer>(gameObject);
   132	            DigCaveRoomPreviewRenderer caveRoomPreviewRenderer =
   133	                GetOrAdd<DigCaveRoomPreviewRenderer>(gameObject);
   134	            DigCaveRoomFloorRenderer caveRoomFloorRenderer =
   135	                GetOrAdd<DigCaveRoomFloorRenderer>(gameObject);
   136	            DigWorldInteraction interaction = GetOrAdd<DigWorldInteraction>(gameObject);
   137	            DigAgentSimulationDriver simulation = GetOrAdd<DigAgentSimulationDriver>(gameObject);
   138	            DigCameraController cameraController =
   139	                GetOrAdd<DigCameraController>(targetCamera.gameObject);
   140	            interaction.enabled = false;
   141	            simulation.enabled = false;
   142	
   143	            _startupStage = "framing camera and HUD";
   144	            cameraController.Initialize(targetCamera, world);
   145	            hud.SetWorld(world);
   146	            hud.SetAgents(agents, agentSession.Tick);
   147	            hud.SetJobs(jobs);
   148	            hud.SetStorageStatus(storage);
   149	            hud.SetSimulationControls(simulation);
   150	            hud.SetToolAssignmentControls(terrainSession, jobRenderer);
   151	            hud.SetBuildingControls(terrainSession, buildingRenderer, jobRenderer);
   152	            hud.SetStatus("Binding runtime controls...");
   153	
   154	            _startupStage = "initializing interaction and simulation";
   155	            worldOverlayRenderer.Initialize(
   156	                overlayManager,
   157	                agentRenderer,
   158	                buildingRenderer,
   159	                worldRenderer);
   160	            jobRenderer.Initialize(agentRenderer);
   161	            interaction.Initialize(
   162	                targetCamera, cameraController, worldSession, worldRenderer,
   163	                agentRenderer, creatureRenderer, mushroomRenderer, barrelRenderer,
   164	                jobRenderer, buildingRenderer, buildingInternalStockRenderer, itemRenderer,
   165	                ghostRenderer, terrainSession, stockpileRenderer, agentSession,
   166	                simulation, hud);
   167	            interaction.SetTunnelMovement(tunnelRenderer);
   168	            interaction.SetCaveRoomRenderers(caveRoomPreviewRenderer, caveRoomFloorRenderer);
   169	            simulation.Initialize(
   170	                worldSession, worldRenderer, agentSession, agentRenderer,
   171	                terrainSession, mushroomRenderer, barrelRenderer, jobRenderer,
   172	                buildingRenderer, buildingInternalStockRenderer, itemRenderer,
   173	                stockpileRenderer, routeRenderer, worldOverlayRenderer, hud);
   174	
   175	            _startupStage = "binding uGUI game HUD";
   176	            gameHud.Initialize(
   177	                terrainSession, agentRenderer, jobRenderer, buildingRenderer,
   178	                interaction, simulation, hud, targetCamera, world);
   179	
   180	            List<string> visualWarnings = new List<string>();
   181	            RenderSettings.ambientLight = new Color(0.58f, 0.60f, 0.66f, 1f);
   182	            RunPresentationStage("rendering world terrain", visualWarnings, () =>
   183	            {
   184	                worldRenderer.SetProtectedCells(worldSession.ProtectedCells);
   185	                worldRenderer.SetTunnelCutaway(agentSession.TunnelVolume);
   186	                worldRenderer.SetTerrainDeposits(worldSession.LoadTerrainDeposits());
   187	                worldRenderer.Render(world);
   188	            });
   189	            RunPresentationStage("rendering layered tunnels", visualWarnings,
   190	                () => tunnelRenderer.Initialize(agentSession.TunnelVolume));
   191	            RunPresentationStage("clearing cave preview", visualWarnings,
   192	                caveRoomPreviewRenderer.Clear);
   193	            RunPresentationStage("rendering residents", visualWarnings,
   194	                () => agentRenderer.Render(agents, movementDuration: 0f));
   195	            RunPresentationStage("rendering creatures", visualWarnings, () =>
   196	                creatureRenderer.Render(
   197	                    Array.Empty<CreatureVisualSnapshot>(), targetCamera, movementDuration: 0f));
   198	            RunPresentationStage("rendering mushrooms", visualWarnings, () =>
   199	                mushroomRenderer.Render(terrainSession.LoadMushrooms()));
   200	            RunPresentationStage("rendering jobs", visualWarnings,
   201	                () => jobRenderer.Render(jobs));
   202	            RunPresentationStage("rendering buildings", visualWarnings,
   203	                () => buildingRenderer.Render(buildings));
   204	            RunPresentationStage("rendering internal building stock", visualWarnings, () =>
   205	                buildingInternalStockRenderer.Render(
   206	                    terrainSession.LoadAllBuildingProduction(),
   207	                    buildings));
   208	            RunPresentationStage("rendering world items", visualWarnings,
   209	                () => itemRenderer.Render(items));
   210	            RunPresentationStage("clearing building ghost", visualWarnings, ghostRenderer.Clear);
   211	            RunPresentationStage("rendering stockpile", visualWarnings,
   212	                () => stockpileRenderer.Render(storage));
   213	            RunPresentationStage("rendering navigation routes", visualWarnings,
   214	                () => routeRenderer.Render(routes));
   215	            RunPresentationStage("rendering world overlays", visualWarnings, () =>
   216	            {
   217	                worldOverlayRenderer.RenderWorld(
   218	                    world,
   219	                    worldSession.LoadTerrainDeposits());
   220	                worldOverlayRenderer.RenderDynamic(buildings, storage, routes);
   221	            });
   222	            RunPresentationStage("rendering ambient effects", visualWarnings,
   223	                () => effectRuntime.Flush(agentSession.Tick));
   224	
   225	            interaction.enabled = true;
   226	            simulation.enabled = true;
   227	            _startupStage = "running";
   228	            hud.SetStatus(visualWarnings.Count == 0
   229	                ? "Select a dwarf, building, or job. Tunnel planning is the default mode."
   230	                : $"Runtime started with {visualWarnings.Count} visual warning(s): "
   231	                    + visualWarnings[0]);
   232	            if (logStartup)
   233	            {
   234	                Debug.Log(
   235	                    $"Dig Unity runtime started with {agents.Count} residents, "
   236	                    + $"{jobs.Count} jobs, {buildings.Count} buildings and a "
   237	                    + $"{world.Width}x{world.Height}x4 rock volume. "
   238	                    + $"Visual warnings: {visualWarnings.Count}.", this);
   239	            }
   240	        }
   241	
   242	        private void RunPresentationStage(
   243	            string stage, ICollection<string> warnings, Action action)
   244	        {
   245	            _startupStage = stage;
   246	            try
   247	            {
   248	                action();
   249	            }
   250	            catch (Exception exception)
   251	            {
   252	                string warning = $"{stage}: {exception.GetType().Name}: {exception.Message}";
   253	                warnings.Add(warning);
   254	                Debug.LogException(exception, this);
   255	            }
   256	        }
   257	
   258	        private static DigGameHudCanvas CreateStartupGameHud(DigHudOverlay hud)
   259	        {
   260	            GameObject canvasObject = new GameObject(
   261	                "Dig Game HUD Canvas", typeof(RectTransform));
   262	            DigGameHudCanvas gameHud = canvasObject.AddComponent<DigGameHudCanvas>();
   263	            gameHud.InitializeStartup(hud);
   264	            hud.AttachGameHudCanvas(gameHud);
   265	            return gameHud;
   266	        }
   267	
   268	        private void ConfigureSideViewRoot()
   269	        {
   270	            transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f);
   271	        }
   272	
   273	        private void DisableRuntimeDrivers()
   274	        {
   275	            DigWorldInteraction interaction = GetComponent<DigWorldInteraction>();
   276	            if (interaction != null)
   277	            {
   278	                interaction.enabled = false;
   279	            }
   280	            DigAgentSimulationDriver simulation = GetComponent<DigAgentSimulationDriver>();
   281	            if (simulation != null)
   282	            {
   283	                simulation.enabled = false;
   284	            }
   285	        }
   286	
   287	        private static Camera EnsureCamera()
   288	        {
   289	            Camera camera = Camera.main;
   290	            if (camera == null)
   291	            {
   292	                GameObject cameraObject = new GameObject("Main Camera");
   293	                cameraObject.tag = "MainCamera";
   294	                camera = cameraObject.AddComponent<Camera>();
   295	                cameraObject.AddComponent<AudioListener>();
   296	            }
   297	            camera.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
   298	            camera.backgroundColor = new Color(0.10f, 0.13f, 0.17f, 1f);
   299	            camera.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
   300	            camera.farClipPlane = 200f;
   301	            return camera;
   302	        }
   303	
   304	        private static T GetOrAdd<T>(GameObject target) where T : Component
   305	        {
   306	            T component = target.GetComponent<T>();
   307	            return component == null ? target.AddComponent<T>() : component;
   308	        }
   309	
   310	        private void ClampDemoConfiguration()
   311	        {
   312	            demoWidth = Mathf.Clamp(demoWidth, MinimumDemoDimension, MaximumDemoDimension);
   313	            demoHeight = Mathf.Clamp(demoHeight, MinimumDemoDimension, MaximumDemoDimension);
   314	            chunkSize = Mathf.Clamp(chunkSize, 1, Mathf.Min(demoWidth, demoHeight));
   315	        }
   316	
   317	        private void OnValidate()
   318	        {
   319	            ClampDemoConfiguration();
   320	        }
   321	    }
   322	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigWorldInteraction.BuildingInternalStock.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     2	using Dig.Domain.World;
     3	using Dig.Presentation.Input;
     4	using UnityEngine;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Unity
     7	{
     8	
     9	public sealed partial class DigWorldInteraction
    10	{
    11	    private bool TryHandleBuildingInternalStockPointerInput(RaycastHit[] hits)
    12	    {
    13	        if (_agentRenderer!.SelectedCount == 0
    14	            || !TryResolveBuildingInternalStockHit(
    15	                hits,
    16	                out DigBuildingInternalStockVisual stock))
    17	        {
    18	            return false;
    19	        }
    20	
    21	        CancelResidentMarquee();
    22	        DisableExcavationDrawing();
    23	        DisableCaveRoomPlanning();
    24	        if (!_terrainSession!.TryResolveBuildingInternalStockPickup(
    25	                stock.BuildingId,
    26	                stock.ItemId,
    27	                out string stackId,
    28	                out CellId workPosition))
    29	        {
    30	            _hud!.SetStatus("Internal stock is reserved or empty.");
    31	            return true;
    32	        }
    33	
    34	        ContextPointerTarget target = new ContextPointerTarget(
    35	            ContextWorldTargetKind.GenericItem,
    36	            EntityId.Parse(stackId),
    37	            workPosition,
    38	            reachable: true,
    39	            supportsAltInteraction: true);
    40	        ApplyDecision(_inputRouter.Route(
    41	            Pointer(PointerButtonKind.Left),
    42	            BuildState(PointerButtonKind.Left),
    43	            target));
    44	        return true;
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    private bool TryResolveBuildingInternalStockHit(
    48	        RaycastHit[] hits,
    49	        out DigBuildingInternalStockVisual stock)
    50	    {
    51	        for (int index = 0; index < hits.Length; index++)
    52	        {
    53	            if (_itemRenderer != null
    54	                && _itemRenderer.TryGetItem(hits[index], out _))
    55	            {
    56	                stock = null!;
    57	                return false;
    58	            }
    59	
    60	            if (_buildingInternalStockRenderer != null
    61	                && _buildingInternalStockRenderer.TryGetStock(hits[index], out stock))
    62	            {
    63	                return true;
    64	            }
    65	        }
    66	
    67	        stock = null!;
    68	        return false;
    69	    }
    70	}
    71	
    72	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigWorldInteraction.Decisions.cs =====
     1	using Dig.Presentation.Input;
     2	using UnityEngine;
     3	
     4	namespace Dig.Unity
     5	{
     6	
     7	public sealed partial class DigWorldInteraction
     8	{
     9	    private void ApplyDecision(
    10	        ContextInputDecision decision,
    11	        DigAgentVisual? agent = null,
    12	        DigCellVisual? cell = null,
    13	        DigBuildingVisual? building = null,
    14	        DigWorldItemVisual? item = null)
    15	    {
    16	        ApplyEffects(decision, agent, building, item);
    17	        if (!decision.HasApplicationCommand)
    18	        {
    19	            return;
    20	        }
    21	
    22	        switch (decision.CommandKind)
    23	        {
    24	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.ConfirmBuildingPlacement:
    25	                ConfirmBuildingPlacement();
    26	                break;
    27	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.UseInventoryItem:
    28	                ApplyResidentInventoryUse(decision);
    29	                break;
    30	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.DropInventoryStack:
    31	                ApplyResidentInventoryDrop(decision);
    32	                break;
    33	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.PickupBuildingBox:
    34	                CreateBuildingBoxPickup(decision);
    35	                break;
    36	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.PickupWorldItem:
    37	                CreateWorldItemPickup(decision);
    38	                break;
    39	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.EatWorldItem:
    40	                CreateWorldFoodPickup(decision);
    41	                break;
    42	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.MoveResident:
    43	                ApplyMove(decision);
    44	                break;
    45	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.AttackTarget:
    46	                ApplyAttack(decision);
    47	                break;
    48	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.AttackBarrel:
    49	                ApplyBarrelAttack(decision);
    50	                break;
    51	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.ChopMushroom:
    52	                ApplyMushroomChop(decision);
    53	                break;
    54	            case ApplicationInputCommandKind.ApplyExcavation:
    55	                ApplyExcavation(decision, cell);
    56	                break;
    57	            default:
    58	                _hud!.SetStatus(
    59	                    $"Input command '{decision.CommandKind}' is not wired in this demo slice.");
    60	                break;
    61	        }
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    private void ApplyEffects(
    65	        ContextInputDecision decision,
    66	        DigAgentVisual? agent,
    67	        DigBuildingVisual? building,
    68	        DigWorldItemVisual? item)
    69	    {
    70	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.CancelBuildingPlacement))
    71	        {
    72	            CancelBuildingPlacement();
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.StartBuildingPlacement))
    76	        {
    77	            StartBuildingPlacement(decision, item);
    78	        }
    79	
    80	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.SelectBuildingBox))
    81	        {
    82	            if (item != null && item.Model.IsBuildingBox)
    83	            {
    84	                SelectBuildingBox(item.Model, item);
    85	            }
    86	            else if (decision.TargetEntityId.HasValue)
    87	            {
    88	                SelectBuildingBoxFromHud(decision.TargetEntityId.Value.ToString());
    89	            }
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.DeselectResident))
    93	        {
    94	            _agentRenderer!.ClearSelection();
    95	            ClearSelectedInventoryStack();
    96	            _hud!.SetAgentSelection(null, 0);
    97	        }
    98	
    99	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.SelectResident))
   100	        {
   101	            ClearBuildingBoxSelection();
   102	            DisableExcavationDrawing();
   103	            DisableCaveRoomPlanning();
   104	            ClearSelectedInventoryStack();
   105	            DigAgentVisual? selected = agent;
   106	            if (selected == null && decision.TargetEntityId.HasValue)
   107	            {
   108	                selected = _agentRenderer!.SelectById(
   109	                    decision.TargetEntityId.Value.ToString());
   110	            }
   111	            else
   112	            {
   113	                _agentRenderer!.Select(selected);
   114	            }
   115	
   116	            _selectedCell = null;
   117	            _renderer!.Select(null);
   118	            _jobRenderer!.Select(null);
   119	            _buildingRenderer!.Select(null);
   120	            _hud!.SetAgentSelection(
   121	                selected?.Model,
   122	                _agentRenderer.SelectedCount);
   123	            _hud.ActivateResidentRosterForSelection();
   124	        }
   125	
   126	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.SelectBuilding))
   127	        {
   128	            ClearBuildingBoxSelection();
   129	            ClearSelectedInventoryStack();
   130	            DigBuildingVisual? selected = building;
   131	            if (selected == null && decision.TargetEntityId.HasValue)
   132	            {
   133	                selected = _buildingRenderer!.SelectById(
   134	                    decision.TargetEntityId.Value.ToString());
   135	            }
   136	            else
   137	            {
   138	                _buildingRenderer!.Select(selected);
   139	            }
   140	
   141	            _selectedCell = null;
   142	            _renderer!.Select(null);
   143	            _agentRenderer!.ClearSelection();
   144	            _jobRenderer!.Select(null);
   145	            _hud!.SetBuildingSelection(selected?.Model);
   146	            _hud.ActivateBuildingRosterForSelection();
   147	        }
   148	
   149	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.FocusResident)
   150	            && decision.TargetEntityId.HasValue
   151	            && _agentRenderer!.TryGetWorldPosition(
   152	                decision.TargetEntityId.Value.ToString(),
   153	                out Vector3 position))
   154	        {
   155	            _cameraController!.Focus(position);
   156	        }
   157	
   158	        if (decision.Effects.HasFlag(PresentationInputEffect.ShowReason)
   159	            && decision.ReasonCode != null)
   160	        {
   161	            _hud!.SetStatus(decision.ReasonCode);
   162	        }
   163	    }
   164	}
   165	
   166	}
===== unity/Dig.Unity/Assets/Dig.Unity/Runtime/DigWorldInteraction.DirectCommandCursor.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.World;
     3	using UnityEngine;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Unity
     6	{
     7	    public sealed partial class DigWorldInteraction
     8	    {
     9	        private const float CommandCursorFrameSeconds = 0.11f;
    10	        private const float MovementCursorDurationSeconds = 0.85f;
    11	        private static readonly Vector2 ShovelCursorHotspot = new Vector2(16f, 27f);
    12	        private static readonly Vector2 PickupCursorHotspot = new Vector2(16f, 27f);
    13	        private static readonly Vector2 DropCursorHotspot = new Vector2(16f, 5f);
    14	        private static readonly Vector2 MovementCursorHotspot = new Vector2(16f, 27f);
    15	        private static readonly Vector2 AxeCursorHotspot = new Vector2(11f, 27f);
    16	        private static readonly Vector2 EatCursorHotspot = new Vector2(16f, 16f);
    17	        private static readonly Vector2 SwordCursorHotspot = new Vector2(12f, 28f);
    18	
    19	        private Texture2D[]? _shovelCursorFrames;
    20	        private Texture2D[]? _pickupCursorFrames;
    21	        private Texture2D[]? _dropCursorFrames;
    22	        private Texture2D[]? _movementCursorFrames;
    23	        private Texture2D[]? _axeCursorFrames;
    24	        private Texture2D[]? _eatCursorFrames;
    25	        private Texture2D[]? _swordCursorFrames;
    26	        private DirectCommandCursorKind _commandCursorKind;
    27	        private int _commandCursorFrame = -1;
    28	        private float _commandCursorAnimationStartedAt;
    29	        private float _movementCursorExpiresAt;
    30	
    31	        private enum DirectCommandCursorKind
    32	        {
    33	            Default = 0,
    34	            Shovel = 1,
    35	            Pickup = 2,
    36	            Movement = 3,
    37	            Axe = 4,
    38	            Sword = 5,
    39	            Eat = 6,
    40	            Drop = 7,
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        private void UpdateSelectedResidentCommandCursor()
    44	        {
    45	            _barrelRenderer?.SetHighlighted(null);
    46	            SetInteractionHighlightedItem(null);
    47	            DirectCommandCursorKind kind = ResolveCommandCursorKind();
    48	            ApplyCommandCursor(kind);
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        private DirectCommandCursorKind ResolveCommandCursorKind()
    52	        {
    53	            if (Time.unscaledTime < _movementCursorExpiresAt)
    54	            {
    55	                return DirectCommandCursorKind.Movement;
    56	            }
    57	
    58	            if (!IsInitialized() || _hud == null || _buildingPlacementMode.HasValue)
    59	            {
    60	                return DirectCommandCursorKind.Default;
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            if (_hud.ContainsScreenPoint(Input.mousePosition))
    64	            {
    65	                return ResolveInventoryHoverCursorKind();
    66	            }
    67	
    68	            RaycastHit[] hits = GetPointerHits();
    69	            if (_agentRenderer != null && _agentRenderer.SelectedCount > 0)
    70	            {
    71	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
    72	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
    73	                    && TryResolveMushroomHoverTarget(hits))
    74	                {
    75	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Axe;
    76	                }
    77	
    78	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
    79	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
    80	                    && TryResolveHostileCombatHoverTarget(hits))
    81	                {
    82	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Sword;
    83	                }
    84	
    85	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
    86	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
    87	                    && TryResolveBarrelHoverTarget(hits))
    88	                {
    89	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Sword;
    90	                }
    91	
    92	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
    93	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
    94	                    && TryResolveFoodItemHoverTarget(hits, out DigWorldItemVisual food))
    95	                {
    96	                    SetInteractionHighlightedItem(food);
    97	                    return IsAltPressed()
    98	                        ? DirectCommandCursorKind.Eat
    99	                        : DirectCommandCursorKind.Pickup;
   100	                }
   101	
   102	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
   103	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
   104	                    && IsAltPressed()
   105	                    && TryResolveBuildingBoxHoverTarget(
   106	                        hits,
   107	                        out DigWorldItemVisual buildingBox))
   108	                {
   109	                    SetInteractionHighlightedItem(buildingBox);
   110	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Pickup;
   111	                }
   112	
   113	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
   114	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
   115	                    && TryResolvePickableItemHoverTarget(
   116	                        hits,
   117	                        out DigWorldItemVisual item))
   118	                {
   119	                    SetInteractionHighlightedItem(item);
   120	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Pickup;
   121	                }
   122	
   123	                if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.None
   124	                    && !_caveRoomPreset.HasValue
   125	                    && TryResolveExplicitExcavationHoverTarget(hits))
   126	                {
   127	                    return DirectCommandCursorKind.Shovel;
   128	                }
   129	            }
   130	
   131	            if (_excavationMode == DigExcavationDrawingMode.Depth
   132	                && ResolveTunnelDepthSource().HasValue)
   133	            {
   134	                return DirectCommandCursorKind.Shovel;
   135	            }
   136	
   137	            return DirectCommandCursorKind.Default;
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        private bool TryResolveBarrelHoverTarget(RaycastHit[] hits)
   141	        {
   142	            if (!TryResolveBarrelHit(hits, out DigBarrelVisual barrel))
   143	            {
   144	                return false;
   145	            }
   146	
   147	            Dig.Presentation.Agents.AgentViewModel? selected =
   148	                _agentRenderer!.SelectedModel;
   149	            bool reachable = selected != null
   150	                && _terrainSession!.CanDirectAttackBarrel(
   151	                    barrel.Model.BarrelId,
   152	                    new CellId(selected.CellX, selected.CellY, selected.CellZ),
   153	                    out _);
   154	            if (reachable)
   155	            {
   156	                _barrelRenderer!.SetHighlighted(barrel.Model.BarrelId);
   157	            }
   158	
   159	            return reachable;
   160	        }
   161	
   162	        private bool TryResolveMushroomHoverTarget(RaycastHit[] hits)
   163	        {
   164	            return TryResolveReachableMushroomHit(hits, out _);
   165	        }
   166	
   167	        private bool TryResolveExplicitExcavationHoverTarget(RaycastHit[] hits)
   168	        {
   169	            if (hits == null)
   170	            {
   171	                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(hits));
   172	            }
   173	
   174	            for (int index = 0; index < hits.Length; index++)
   175	            {
   176	                RaycastHit hit = hits[index];
   177	                if (_renderer!.TryGetDepthDesignation(hit, out _))
   178	                {
   179	                    return true;
   180	                }
   181	
   182	                if (_agentRenderer!.TryGetAgent(hit, out _)
   183	                    || (_buildingRenderer != null
   184	                        && _buildingRenderer.TryGetBuilding(hit, out _))
   185	                    || (_itemRenderer != null
   186	                        && _itemRenderer.TryGetItem(hit, out _)))
   187	                {
   188	                    continue;
   189	                }
   190	
   191	                if (ResolveExcavationTarget(hit).HasValue)
   192	                {
   193	                    return true;
   194	                }
   195	            }
   196	
   197	            return false;
   198	        }
   199	
   200	        private void PlayMovementCursorFeedback()
   201	        {
   202	            _movementCursorExpiresAt = Time.unscaledTime + MovementCursorDurationSeconds;
   203	            BeginCommandCursorAnimation(DirectCommandCursorKind.Movement);
   204	        }
   205	
   206	        private void ApplyCommandCursor(DirectCommandCursorKind kind)
   207	        {
   208	            if (kind == DirectCommandCursorKind.Default)
   209	            {
   210	                if (_commandCursorKind != DirectCommandCursorKind.Default
   211	                    || _commandCursorFrame != -1)
   212	                {
   213	                    Cursor.SetCursor(null, Vector2.zero, CursorMode.Auto);
   214	                }
   215	
   216	                _commandCursorKind = DirectCommandCursorKind.Default;
   217	                _commandCursorFrame = -1;
   218	                return;
   219	            }
   220	
   221	            if (_commandCursorKind != kind)
   222	            {
   223	                BeginCommandCursorAnimation(kind);
   224	            }
   225	
   226	            Texture2D[] frames = ResolveCommandCursorFrames(kind);
   227	            int frame = Mathf.FloorToInt(
   228	                (Time.unscaledTime - _commandCursorAnimationStartedAt)
   229	                / CommandCursorFrameSeconds) % frames.Length;
   230	            if (_commandCursorFrame == frame)
   231	            {
   232	                return;
   233	            }
   234	
   235	            _commandCursorFrame = frame;
   236	            Cursor.SetCursor(
   237	                frames[frame],
   238	                ResolveCommandCursorHotspot(kind),
   239	                CursorMode.Auto);
   240	        }
   241	
   242	        private void BeginCommandCursorAnimation(DirectCommandCursorKind kind)
   243	        {
   244	            _commandCursorKind = kind;
   245	            _commandCursorAnimationStartedAt = Time.unscaledTime;
   246	            _commandCursorFrame = -1;
   247	        }
   248	
   249	        private Texture2D[] ResolveCommandCursorFrames(DirectCommandCursorKind kind)
   250	        {
   251	            switch (kind)
   252	            {
   253	                case DirectCommandCursorKind.Shovel:
   254	                    return _shovelCursorFrames ??= CreateShovelCursorFrames();
   255	                case DirectCommandCursorKind.Pickup:
   256	                    return _pickupCursorFrames ??= CreatePickupCursorFrames();
